### [1] [Latent Cache Flow: Model-to-Model Communication Without Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22863)
*Maximillian Rossi,Prajwal Raghunath,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Latent Cache Flow (LCF)，通过压缩和联合翻译KV缓存，实现高效、低延迟的LLM智能体间通信，在13MB适配器下比956MB的C2C更准确，且支持不同上下文场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过文本通信存在延迟高和信息损失的问题；C2C方法虽试图交换KV缓存，但适配器过大且要求目标上下文完全相同，不适用于智能体通信场景。

Method: 观察到键和值可以联合翻译与压缩，将适配器大小降至C2C的约4%；设计适配器传输目标模型所缺失的新信息摘要，以应对不同上下文的问题。

Result: 13MB的LCF适配器在共享上下文设定下比956MB的C2C适配器更准确；在不同上下文场景中，LCF比文本通信准确率高23%，速度快8.5倍。

Conclusion: LCF通过压缩KV缓存适配器并支持不同上下文下的选择性信息传输，显著提升了LLM智能体间通信的效率和准确性。

Abstract: LLM agents today communicate via text, which incurs considerable latency and information loss due to the need to autoregressively decode the sharer model's state and encode at the receiver model. Recent work such as Cache-to-Cache (C2C; Fu et al., 2026) seeks to exchange KV caches by learning adapters that translate sharer KV matrices to the receiver model. However, the adapters are large and expensive to train, and translate individual tokens, which requires the target context to be identical. This is unsuitable for agent communication, where the LLMs have differing context.
  We introduce Latent Cache Flow (LCF). To address efficiency, we observe that keys and values can be jointly translated and compressed, reducing the adapter to about 4% of C2C's size. To address differing context, we design the adapter to transmit a summary of new information that the target model does not have. Our early experiments show that a 13 MB LCF adapter can be more accurate than a 956 MB C2C adapter in shared-context settings; for different contexts, LCF is 23% more accurate and 8.5x faster than text-based communication.

</details>


### [2] [Reading Calibrated Uncertainty from Language Model Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22864)
*Aliai Eusebi,Alexander Herzog,Xiaoyu Liang,Marie Vasek,Enrico Mariconti,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过提取每层MLP更新的尺度不变几何特征（共11种），训练稀疏线性探针来量化语言模型生成结构化输出时的不确定性。该方法在选择性弃权任务中优于最大softmax概率（MSP），AURC提升最高达21个点，且探针系数具有可解释的几何意义，能揭示误差在模型深度上的形成位置与方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大softmax概率（MSP）虽计算廉价但校准性差；现有探针方法将隐状态作为静态快照输入黑箱分类器，忽略了表示在层间形成的轨迹信息。相似的终点可能源于不同的路径，而证据在深度上的累积、增强或反转过程可能揭示最终概率所掩盖的不确定性。

Method: 提取11种尺度不变的几何特征，追踪逐层MLP更新的累积路径，将这些特征输入稀疏线性探针进行不确定性评估。

Result: 在选择性弃权任务中，该探针优于MSP，性能提升与基线校准误差成正比，AURC最高提升21个点。

Conclusion: 每个特征具有闭合形式的几何含义，探针系数可追溯误差在深度上的形成过程——哪些层过早做出决策、哪些层与当前状态矛盾、轨迹何时偏离终点，从而实现可解释的不确定性量化。

Abstract: The maximum softmax probability (MSP) represents a default approach when evaluating uncertainty quantification for language model generation with structured output. Although cheap, it is often miscalibrated. Methods that probe the model's internal activations feed raw hidden states into opaque classifiers, reading activations as static snapshots and leaving implicit the layer-wise trajectory by which a representation is formed. Yet, similar endpoints can arise from very different paths, and how evidence accumulates, reinforces, or reverses across depth might reveal uncertainty that final probabilities obscure. We extract eleven scale-invariant geometric features, tracing the cumulative path of per-layer MLP updates, and feed them to a sparse linear probe. The probe outperforms MSP under selective abstention, with gains scaling with baseline miscalibration up to 21 AURC points. Because every feature has a closed-form geometric meaning, the probe's coefficients trace how and where along depth errors take shape -- which layers commit prematurely, which contradict the running state, where trajectories drift away from their endpoint.

</details>


### [3] [FusionSense: Tri-Stage Near-Sensor Learning for Runtime-Adaptive Multimodal Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22868)
*Sanggeon Yun,Ryozo Masukawa,Minhyoung Na,Hyunwoo Oh,Yoshiki Yamaguchi,Wenjun Huang,SungHeon Jeong,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FusionSense是一种面向多模态传感器（如RGB+Depth/LiDAR）的融合感知框架，通过在近传感器端部署轻量级分类器，利用服务器端融合模型生成"过滤安全"标签来量化各模态必要性，从而在边缘端实现联合计算与通信缩减，实验中在SynDrone数据集上实现了高达33倍能效提升和92.3%质量损失降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么在服务器端融合多模态数据（能耗与延迟高），要么在近传感器端应用单模态滤波器（忽略跨模态依赖，导致冗余传输或漏检），缺乏融合感知的边缘端自适应决策机制。

Method: 三步训练流程：(i)服务器端训练融合模型学习下游任务；(ii)生成"过滤安全"(FoS)标签量化各模态相对于融合决策的必要性；(iii)通过注入近传感器预测作为辅助信号，压缩边缘端融合模型。运行时决策层联合减少计算与通信，且复杂度随传感器数量线性扩展。

Result: 在RGB+Depth/LiDAR双模态SynDrone平台上：1%感兴趣出现率下能耗降低33倍，10%下降低11倍；固定30%数据缩减时质量损失降低92.3%；能效比最佳基线方法高约1.5倍。

Conclusion: FusionSense证明了融合感知的边缘端数据缩减方法在多模态自主系统中能同时实现高能效和任务质量保持，为资源受限的边缘智能系统提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous systems and smart-industry deployments increasingly split computation across near-sensor, edge, and cloud resources, where tight energy, latency, and reliability budgets demand run-time adaptivity. In practice, deciding what to compute and transmit at each point is pivotal; yet as multimodal sensor suites (cameras, LiDAR/depth, etc.) proliferate at the edge, most prior approaches either (i) fuse modalities on powerful servers or (ii) apply uni-modal near-sensor filters that ignore cross-modal dependencies, leading to redundant transmissions or missed events. We present FusionSense, a fusion-aware intelligent sensing framework for energy-constrained autonomous edge systems. Lightweight near-sensor classifiers are trained via a three-step procedure: (i) a server-side fusion model learns the downstream task, (ii) filter-out-safe (FoS) labels quantify each modality's necessity relative to the fused decision, and (iii) an edge-side fusion model is compacted by injecting near-sensor predictions as auxiliary signals. The result is a run-time decision layer that jointly reduces compute and communication while scaling linearly with sensor count. On a dual-modality (RGB+Depth/LiDAR) setup with SynDrone, FusionSense sustains task quality at substantially higher data-reduction rates than uni-modal filters and delivers large end-to-end gains: up to 33x lower energy at 1% FoI prevalence, 11x at 10%, a 92.3% reduction in quality loss at a fixed 30% data reduction, and roughly 1.5x higher energy savings than the best prior filtering baseline.

</details>


### [4] [FuRA: Full-Rank Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning with Spectral Preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22869)
*Yequan Zhao,Ruijie Zhang,Liyan Tan,Niall Moran,Tong Qin,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FuRA（全秩适应）框架，通过块张量-列车分解 W=LSR，冻结预训练的奇异值分解（SVD）基 L，仅优化紧凑核 R 和块奇异值 S，实现全秩谱预条件优化。该方法在保持参数效率（与 LoRA 相当）的同时，在 LLM 微调、数学推理强化学习及 VLM 视觉指令微调中均超越全参数微调（Full FT），其 4-bit 量化变体 QFuRA 也优于 QLoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调（Full FT）和 LoRA 等参数高效微调方法在更新权重时未考虑预训练建立的谱结构，导致有限微调数据的噪声梯度可能扰动鲁棒的预训练特征。作者识别出谱预条件（spectral preconditioning）是缺失的关键要素。

Method: 提出 FuRA 框架，基于块张量-列车分解 W=LSR：固定大型核 L（预训练块 SVD 基），仅优化紧凑核 R 和块奇异值 S。该分解提供全秩谱预条件，保持全秩更新表达能力，并在参数、内存和步长时间效率上与 LoRA 相当。

Result: 在多项任务上 FuRA 一致优于 Full FT：LLaMA-3-8B 常识推理 +1.37；LLM 数学推理强化学习中的表现更优；VLM 视觉指令微调效果更好。量化变体 QFuRA 也超过 QLoRA。

Conclusion: 谱预条件是微调中关键但缺失的成分，FuRA 通过冻结预训练 SVD 基实现全秩谱预条件，证明了在参数高效前提下达到甚至超越全参数微调效果的可行性。

Abstract: Both full fine-tuning (Full FT) and parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods such as LoRA introduce weight updates without accounting for the spectral structure established during pretraining. As a result, noisy gradients from limited fine-tuning data can perturb robust pretrained features. We identify spectral preconditioning as the missing ingredient: reparameterizing each weight matrix through its full-rank singular value decomposition (SVD) and freezing one singular basis constrains updates to the pretrained column space, yielding a preconditioned optimization scheme that outperforms unconstrained Full FT at the same trainable parameter count. Building on this insight, we propose FuRA (Full-Rank Adaptation), an efficient full-rank adaptation framework based on a block tensor-train factorization W = LSR, where the large core L is fixed to the pretrained block-wise SVD basis, while only the compact core R and the block-wise singular values S are optimized. This design simultaneously provides full-rank spectral preconditioning, preserves full-rank update expressivity, and achieves parameter, memory, and step-time efficiency comparable to LoRA. FuRA consistently outperforms Full FT across multiple settings, including LLM fine-tuning (+1.37 on LLaMA-3-8B commonsense reasoning), LLM reinforcement learning for mathematical reasoning, and visual instruction tuning for VLMs. Furthermore, the 4-bit quantized variant, QFuRA, also surpasses QLoRA. Code is available at https://github.com/olokevin/FuRA-NIPS

</details>


### [5] [The Readout Shortcut: Positional Number Copying Dominates Arithmetic CoT Readout in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22870)
*Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 小语言模型在算术推理中依赖链式思维提示中的位置捷径：模型直接复制答案分隔符前的最后一个数字，而非进行真正的逻辑推理。这种复制机制解释了89-92%的性能，且优先于真实计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明打乱CoT步骤顺序仍能保持大部分性能，质疑CoT的实际贡献是否来自逻辑序列性。需要探究小模型在算术任务中究竟利用CoT的何种特性。

Method: 在三个1-3B指令微调的语言模型上，通过前缀补全隔离答案读出阶段，分析位置捷径。进行干扰数字替换、移除实验，头级消融分析，并在GSM-Symbolic和BBH任务上验证。

Result: 答案分隔符前最后一个数字被复制占性能54-92pp（89-92%教师强制上限）；错误样本中95-96%匹配最后数字；替换为错误值后准确率归零；移除后恢复5-32pp；Qwen/Llama复制新干扰项87-95%；非算术任务中打乱效果消失。

Conclusion: CoT步骤级忠实性评估可能混淆位置性答案传输与真实计算，这是基于CoT的监督的一个失败模式。模型并非逻辑推理，而是利用快捷位置复制机制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting is necessary for arithmetic in small language models, yet shuffling its steps preserves most performance. What does CoT contribute if not logical sequencing? In three 1-3B instruction-tuned LMs on GSM8K, we isolate the answer-readout stage via prefix completion and identify a positional shortcut: the model copies whichever number occupies the trailing position before the answer delimiter, regardless of intermediate reasoning. Gold-answer presence accounts for 54-92 pp of accuracy (89-92% of each model's teacher-forcing ceiling); even on incorrect items, the final answer matches the last CoT number 95-96% of the time. The copy channel takes precedence over retained-context completion: replacing the trailing number with a wrong value collapses accuracy to near-zero despite correct intermediates, yet removing it recovers 5-32 pp above that floor--even single-step arithmetic the model can otherwise perform is suppressed when a copyable number is present. Qwen and Llama copy novel distractors 87-95% of the time; Gemma gates selectively. Head-level ablation implicates architecture-specific head sets; the effect replicates on GSM-Symbolic. On non-arithmetic BBH tasks, shuffle retention drops sharply; at 7-8B, content-selective gating emerges. Step-level faithfulness evaluations risk conflating positional answer transport with genuine computation--a failure mode for CoT-based oversight.

</details>


### [6] [Approximate Machine Unlearning through Manifold Representation Forgetting Guided by Self Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22871)
*Weiqi Wang,Zhiyi Tian,Chenhan Zhang,Luoyu Chen,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ManiF-SMC方法，通过将机器遗忘问题重构为将擦除样本推离其原始流形表示中心并靠近保留数据中的语义最近邻，利用基于边界的三元组损失和自模式连通性模块，在表示空间内实现高效遗忘，无需依赖标签和任务梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于标签操纵或任务梯度反转的遗忘方法效果有限，且可能损害原始学习目标，无法保证等价于重新训练的标准遗忘。

Method: 提出ManiF-SMC方法：1) 将近似遗忘重新定义为将每个擦除样本推离其原始流形表示中心，移向其保留数据中的语义最近邻；2) 采用基于边界的三元组损失同时实现遗忘和表示保留；3) 设计自模式连通性模块快速重建局部流形，为每个遗忘案例生成自适应边界。

Result: 在四个代表性数据集上的实验表明，ManiF-SMC在仅操作模型表示空间的条件下，取得了与最先进近似遗忘方法相当的遗忘效果。

Conclusion: ManiF-SMC通过流形表示空间的遗忘策略，有效弥合了近似遗忘与重训练遗忘之间的差距，且减少了对标签和任务特定梯度的依赖。

Abstract: Machine unlearning is a fundamental mechanism that enforces the right to be forgotten. Existing unlearning studies that rely on label manipulation or task-gradient reversal often deliver limited unlearning effectiveness. Moreover, they can undermine the original learning objective and typically do not guarantee equivalence to standard unlearning by retraining.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{ManiF-SMC} (\textbf{Mani}fold \textbf{F}orgetting with \textbf{S}elf \textbf{M}ode \textbf{C}onnectivity), motivated by the observation that a model retrained on the remaining data tends to classify erased samples by their semantic similarity to the retained data. We begin with systematically recasting the approximate unlearning as pushing each erased sample away from its original learned manifold representation centroid toward its nearest semantic neighbors in the retained data. This reformulation aligns unlearning with retraining behavior and operates purely in representation space, reducing reliance on labels and task-specific gradients. To tackle the manifold representation-based unlearning problem, ManiF-SMC encapsulates the unlearning and representation preservation goals in a margin-based triplet loss. Because finding a suitable margin for unlearning is challenging, we propose a self-mode-connectivity module that rapidly reconstructs the local manifold to guide the adaptive margins generation for each unlearning case. Extensive experiments on four representative datasets show that ManiF-SMC achieves unlearning effectiveness comparable to state-of-the-art approximate methods while operating solely within the model's representation space.

</details>


### [7] [MedExpMem: Adapting Experience Memory for Differential Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22872)
*Qianhan Feng,Zhongzhen Huang,Yakun Zhu,Yannian Gu,Winnie Chiu Wing Chu,Xiaofan Zhang,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MedExpMem经验记忆框架，让医学视觉语言模型通过积累诊断失败经验来提升鉴别诊断能力，在放射学基准上最高提升7.0%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学视觉语言模型（VLM）的参数编码的是静态知识，无法像经验丰富的医生那样通过临床实践积累鉴别诊断经验，缺乏区分易混淆疾病的能力。

Method: 提出MedExpMem经验记忆框架，采用两阶段构建过程（初始实践暴露知识缺口 + 反思性再诊断优化理解），将模型自身的诊断失败经验组织为成对鉴别笔记，编码关键鉴别特征、可操作决策规则和推理错误模式；新病例到来时检索经验记忆引导鉴别推理。

Result: 在涵盖11个亚专科的放射学基准上评估，在多种模型和规模上实现一致的准确率提升，最高提升7.0%；分析实验验证了经验质量与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MedExpMem是一种有竞争力的方法，能够解决参数化学习无法触及的医学适应性需求，弥合了静态VLM知识与动态临床经验积累之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Experienced physicians develop diagnostic expertise through clinical practice, acquiring not only disease knowledge but also the ability to differentiate confusable conditions. Current medical vision-language models (VLMs) lack this capability -- their parameters encode static knowledge that does not evolve across diagnostic encounters. We propose MedExpMem, an experience memory framework enabling VLM-based diagnostic agents to accumulate differential diagnosis expertise. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation, which retrieves encyclopedic disease descriptions, MedExpMem memorizes discriminative experience derived from the agent's own diagnostic failures and organizes them as pairwise differential notes encoding key discriminators, actionable decision rules and reasoning error patterns. The framework adopts a two-phase construction process mirroring physician learning: initial practice exposes knowledge gaps, and reflective re-diagnosis refines understanding. When encountering new cases, the agent retrieves experience memory to guide differential reasoning. We evaluate MedExpMem on a radiology benchmark spanning 11 subspecialties. Results demonstrate consistent accuracy improvements, maximum 7.0%, across diverse models and scales. Analytical experiments validate experience quality and robustness, demonstrating MedExpMem as a competitive method addresses medical adaptation needs beyond the reach of parameteric learning.

</details>


### [8] [When Do LLMs Reason? A Dynamical Systems View via Entropy Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22873)
*Wei Xia,Haoqing Wang,Zhi-Hong Deng,Yehui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EDRM框架，通过早期解码熵动态自适应选择推理策略，在保持或提升准确率的同时显著减少token消耗（数据集级41-55%，实例级27-45%），揭示了推理应选择性调用而非默认启用的核心观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-thought推理虽被广泛使用，但在事实性和开放式任务中常带来边际甚至负收益，同时大幅增加token消耗。因此需要回答一个根本问题：何时显式推理真正有益？

Method: 提出EDRM（Entropy Dynamics-based Reasoning Manifold），一种轻量级、无需训练的框架。通过分析早期解码阶段的熵动态作为推理状态的可靠信号，将熵轨迹嵌入紧凑可解释的流形表示，实现零样本部署和细粒度实例级自适应策略选择。

Result: 在15个基准测试和4种不同规模/架构的LLM上，EDRM一致优于静态基线。数据集级：41-55% token减少同时提升准确率（仅需50个校准样本）；实例级：准确率提升达4.7%，同时保持27-45% token节省。

Conclusion: 推理应被选择性调用而非默认启用，熵驱动的解码控制是实现高效自适应LLM推理的有效方法。LLM的推理行为是一个动态解码状态，早期熵模式可作为其可靠信号。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become the default strategy for enhancing LLM capabilities, yet its application raises a fundamental question: when is explicit reasoning actually beneficial? Empirical evidence reveals a striking paradox: CoT often provides marginal or even negative gains on factual and open-ended tasks while multiplying token consumption. In this work, we show that LLM reasoning is not a static property of tasks or models, but a \emph{dynamic decoding state} that emerges during generation. Through systematic analysis, we find early-stage entropy dynamics provide a reliable signal of this state: tasks benefiting from CoT exhibit consistent entropy reduction, while others display unstable or increasing patterns. This behavior can be interpreted as a phase-transition-like shift from a high-entropy exploratory regime to a low-entropy structured reasoning regime. Based on these insights, we propose \textbf{EDRM} (Entropy Dynamics-based Reasoning Manifold), a lightweight and training-free routing framework that leverages early decoding entropy to adaptively select inference strategies. EDRM embeds entropy trajectories into a compact and interpretable manifold representation, enabling both zero-shot deployment and fine-grained instance-level adaptation. Across 15 benchmarks and 4 LLMs of varying scales and architectures, EDRM consistently outperforms static baselines. At the dataset level, EDRM achieves \textbf{41--55\%} token reduction while improving accuracy with as few as 50 calibration samples. At the instance level, it further improves accuracy by up to \textbf{4.7\%} while maintaining \textbf{27--45\%} token savings. These results suggest that reasoning should be invoked selectively rather than by default, and demonstrate the effectiveness of entropy-driven decoding control for efficient and adaptive LLM inference.

</details>


### [9] [Symbolic Density Estimation for Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21813)
*Ziwen Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出符号密度估计（SDE）框架，通过组合初等解析运算自动恢复离散概率质量函数的闭式表达式，集成领域结构先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推理，扩展至零膨胀和有限混合模型，在基准数据集和真实数据上均取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散概率分布家族长期依赖逐案例数学推导，可解释分布的目录扩展缓慢，缺乏自动化发现新分布的方法。

Method: 提出符号密度估计（SDE）无监督框架，在结构化搜索空间中组合初等解析运算，集成领域特定结构先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推理阶段，并扩展至零膨胀和有限混合等丰富分布家族。

Result: 在贡献的基准数据集上，SDE恢复了所有基准分布家族并给出准确参数估计；在真实数据应用中，识别出简洁可解释的混合模型，拟合优度优于标准模型。

Conclusion: SDE能够自动化发现闭式离散概率质量函数，扩展了可解释分布目录，兼具恢复已知分布和发现新分布的能力。

Abstract: Discrete probability laws underpin statistical modeling, yet the catalog of interpretable distributions has expanded only gradually through centuries of case-by-case mathematical derivations. We introduce symbolic density estimation (SDE), an unsupervised framework that automatically recovers closed-form probability mass functions by composing elementary analytic operations within a structured search space. Our method integrates domain-specific structural priors with evolutionary search and a validity-aware inference stage, and it extends to richer distribution families such as zero inflation and finite mixtures. To support systematic evaluation and future research, we contribute a benchmark dataset spanning a broad collection of commonly used discrete distributions. The proposed algorithm recovers all benchmark families with accurate parameter estimates. A real data application shows that it identifies concise and interpretable mixture models that improve goodness-of-fit over standard models.

</details>


### [10] [WeCon: An Efficient Weight-Conditioned Neural Solver for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22876)
*Xuan Wu,Jinbiao Chen,Yang Li,Lijie Wen,Chunguo Wu,Yuanshu Li,Yubin Xiao,Chunyan Miao,You Zhou,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Weight-Conditioned神经求解器WeCon，通过门控残差融合模块实现实例特征与权重的和谐交互，结合高效偏好优化策略，在保持与SOTA相当的超体积指标下减少40%推理时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOCOP神经求解器存在两个问题：(1) 权重仅单次注入解码阶段或主要在编码阶段使用导致权重信号稀释；(2) 偏好优化方法依赖纯随机采样构建解对，产生信息量不足的训练样本，训练效率低下。

Method: (1) 编码器：设计三层注意力块+Gated Residual Fusion (GRF)模块，实现实例特征与权重的和谐交互；(2) 解码器：引入插拔式Residual Fusion (RF)模块缓解权重信号稀释；(3) 训练：提出Efficient Preference Optimization (EPO)，构建高质量解对提升训练效率。

Result: 在四种MOCOP变体、不同问题规模和分布模式上，WeCon达到与SOTA求解器POCCO-W相当的HV值，同时推理时间减少40%。消融实验验证了各设计组件的有效性。

Conclusion: WeCon通过改进权重与实例特征的交互机制和偏好优化策略，在保持求解质量的同时显著提升了推理效率，为MOCOP神经求解器提供了更高效的设计范式。

Abstract: Existing neural solvers for Multi-Objective Combinatorial Optimization Problems (MOCOPs) commonly adopt decomposition-based strategies that scalarize an MOCOP into multiple subproblems associated with distinct weight vectors. However, they either inject weights only once during decoding, limiting weight-conditioned context modeling, or primarily during encoding, causing weight-signal dilution during decoding. Moreover, preference optimization methods rely on purely random sampling to construct solution pairs for training solvers, which often produces less informative pairs and thus leads to low training effectiveness. To better address these limitations, we propose an efficient Weight-Conditioned neural solver (WeCon). Specifically, we design an encoder layer with three attention blocks and our proposed Gated Residual Fusion (GRF) block to facilitate harmonious interaction between instance features and weights, thereby generating informative weight-conditioned context. We further introduce a plug-and-play Residual Fusion (RF) block in the decoder to alleviate weight-signal dilution. Finally, we propose Efficient Preference Optimization (EPO), which constructs high-quality solutions, thereby generating more informative pairs to improve training effectiveness. Experiments on four MOCOP variants across different problem scales and distribution patterns demonstrate that WeCon achieves HyperVolume (HV) values comparable to SOTA solver POCCO-W, while reducing inference time by 40%. Ablation studies validate the contributions of all designs.

</details>


### [11] [Partial Fusion of Neural Networks: Efficient Tradeoffs Between Ensembles and Weight Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22350)
*Fabian Morelli,Stephan Eckstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经网络的部分融合方法，通过神经元的相似性匹配与部分最优传输，在集成模型与权重聚合之间实现计算成本与性能的灵活折中，并进一步推广为集成模型的广义剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络集成虽然性能优异但计算成本高昂，而权重聚合虽然成本低但精度下降。现有方法无法在计算成本与性能之间灵活调节，需要一种可插值的折中方案。

Method: 1) 基于神经元级相似性，扩展现有权重聚合方法，仅聚合最相似神经元的权重；2) 利用部分最优传输联合识别与匹配相似神经元；3) 将权重聚合与部分融合推广为集成模型的广义剪枝，神经元可被删除或线性组合；4) 对单一网络应用广义剪枝，通过隔离、删除和线性组合神经元实现类似收益。

Result: 部分融合方法在集成模型与权重聚合之间实现了灵活的性能-成本折中，广义剪枝对单一网络也能带来类似收益。

Conclusion: 部分融合提供了一种可调节计算成本与性能权衡的有效策略，广义剪枝视角统一了多种模型压缩与融合方法。

Abstract: Ensembles of neural networks typically outperform individual networks but incur large computational costs, whereas weight aggregation produces less costly, yet also less accurate, aggregate models. We introduce partial fusion of networks, which interpolates between ensembles and weight aggregation and thus allows for a flexible tradeoff between computational cost and performance. A direct way to achieve this is to extend existing weight aggregation methods based on neuron-level similarity between different networks, where partial fusion then only aggregates weights of neurons which are most similar. We showcase one particular method to jointly identify which neurons are most similar and match them via partial optimal transport. Further, we consider the more general perspective of weight aggregation and partial fusion as generalized pruning of ensemble models, where neurons cannot just be deleted, but also linearly combined. Finally, we show that generalized pruning applied to a single network yields similar benefits as partial fusion by allowing for a tradeoff between isolating, deleting, and linearly combining neurons based on similarity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Fabian-Mor/partial_fusion_nn.

</details>


### [12] [Tensor Cache: Eviction-conditioned Associative Memory for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22884)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tensor Cache，一种双层KV缓存机制：L1为滑动窗口精确注意力，L2为外积快速权重记忆（由窗口驱逐的KV对压缩而成）。通过标量门控融合两层输出，并发现chunked-mean训练捷径存在平方项伪外积问题，提出了替代的加权求和扫描方法。在多个任务上提升了内存-质量权衡边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer的KV缓存随上下文长度线性增长；滑动窗口缓存虽限制了内存但完全丢弃被驱逐的token，导致窗口外的相关证据不可访问。需要一种既能限制内存增长又能保留长程信息的缓存机制。

Method: 1) 双层缓存架构：L1为滑动窗口softmax注意力（精确局部注意力），L2为固定大小的外积快速权重记忆（由窗口驱逐的KV对压缩为每层矩阵A）；2) 通过线性注意力恒等式q_t(k_i⊗v_i)=⟨q_t,k_i⟩v_i实现单次矩阵乘法读取；3) 可学习的标量门控融合L1和L2输出，每头的衰减和写入率参数端到端训练；4) 发现chunked-mean训练捷径引入每个chunk内C²-C个虚假跨token外积，提出并行加权求和扫描替代方案，达到float32精度下的per-token写入等价效果。

Result: 在系统规模扩展、受控联想回忆、长上下文语言建模和记忆容量诊断等多个任务上，Tensor Cache在内存-质量权衡曲线上优于有界状态基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将滑动窗口驱逐的KV对外积压缩为快速权重记忆，并解决训练中的伪外积问题，Tensor Cache在有限内存预算下实现了更好的长程依赖捕获能力，是一种有效的两层级联缓存方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive Transformer KV caches grow linearly with context length; sliding-window caching bounds memory but discards evicted tokens entirely, so relevant evidence outside the window becomes inaccessible. We introduce \emph{Tensor Cache}, a two-level cache that pairs sliding-window softmax attention as a first-level cache (L1) with a fixed-size outer-product fast-weight memory as a second-level cache (L2) fed by KV pairs evicted from the window. Recent tokens remain in exact local attention; evicted pairs are compressed into a per-layer matrix $A$ and read by future queries through a single matrix multiplication, exploiting the linear-attention identity $q_t(k_i \otimes v_i)=\langle q_t,k_i\rangle v_i$. A learned scalar gate fuses the L1 and L2 outputs, and per-head decay and write-rate parameters are trained end-to-end. The outer-product memory and the read identity are well-known; our contribution is their use as an L2 cache fed exclusively by sliding-window evictions, plus identifying that the common chunked-mean training shortcut $A\!\leftarrow\!λA\!+\!η(\bar k\!\otimes\!\bar v)$ silently introduces $C^2{-}C$ spurious cross-token outer products per chunk, and closing the gap with a parallel weighted-sum scan equivalent to per-token writes within float32 epsilon. Across systems scaling, controlled associative recall, long-context language modeling, and memory-capacity diagnostics, Tensor Cache improves the memory--quality frontier over bounded-state baselines.

</details>


### [13] [Pointwise Metrics Mislead: An Evaluation Protocol for Multimodal Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22891)
*Mads H. Baattrup,Jörn Bach,Laurids Jeppe,Finn Labe,Alexander Grohsjean,Christian Schwanenberger,Peer Stelldinger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了在具有多模态后验的逆问题中，基于点估计的评估指标（如RMSE、MAE）存在结构性缺陷，会导致边际谱压缩。提出了一套三部分评估协议（CRPS分布精度、频谱保真度、覆盖校准），在粒子物理等领域中显示模型排名会因评估指标而反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学重建中广泛使用点状指标（RMSE、MAE）来评估重建质量，但其隐含假设——更低误差意味着更好重建——在多模态后验的逆问题中结构性失效。这种失效会压缩边际谱中的尾部、模态和形状等关键特征，影响下游科学测量。

Method: 通过全方差定律证明，最小化MSE或MAE的点估计器产生的边际谱严格窄于真实后验。提出三部分评估协议：(1) 逐事件分布精度用CRPS衡量；(2) 总体边际精度用频谱保真度诊断；(3) 不确定性可信度用覆盖校准检验。

Result: 在合成基准和粒子物理逆问题中，点状指标与分布指标的模型排名完全相反；校准进一步区分了CRPS无法分辨的架构。评估协议决定了科学结论。

Conclusion: 评估协议比模型本身更能决定科学结论，当前基于点状指标的评估范式需要被更全面的分布级评估替代。

Abstract: Evaluation in scientific reconstruction is dominated by pointwise metrics - RMSE, MAE, per-event resolution - under the implicit assumption that lower error means better reconstruction. We show that this assumption fails structurally for inverse problems with multimodal posteriors. By the law of total variance, point estimators trained to minimize MSE or MAE produce a marginal spectrum strictly narrower than the truth whenever the posterior has nonzero width. The resulting bias is independent of architecture, training, and dataset size, and it compresses precisely the spectral features - tails, modes, shapes - that downstream scientific measurements rely on. We propose a three-part evaluation protocol where each step targets a failure mode the others miss: per-event distributional accuracy via CRPS, population-level marginal accuracy via a spectrum-fidelity diagnostic, and uncertainty trustworthiness via coverage-based calibration. On a synthetic benchmark with an analytic posterior and on a realistic many-to-one inverse problem from particle physics, model rankings reverse between pointwise and distributional metrics, and calibration further separates architectures indistinguishable under CRPS. The evaluation protocol, not the model, determines the scientific conclusion.

</details>


### [14] [Human-Centered Learning Mechanics: A Dynamical Framework for Entropy-Regulated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22940)
*Kim Phuc Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出人本学习力学（HCLM）框架，将深度学习视为开放、受控的动力学系统，核心观点是熵正则化只有在其代理项产生非退化信息力时才有用，否则会退化为普通损失最小化。引入有效熵概念，研究了几何熵代理（方差、对数行列式协方差），并给出了收敛性、熵流、Wasserstein梯度流和泛化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习理论多将训练视为封闭优化系统，无法解释模型在不确定性、资源约束、分布偏移、下游决策风险和人类反馈等真实场景中的行为，需要一种开放、受控的学习动力学框架。

Method: 提出有效熵与信息力概念，形式化熵正则化的信息力条件；推导收敛性、熵流、Wasserstein梯度流和带噪表示泛化结果；给出缩放律行为的条件动力学解释；在控制性表示学习实验上验证几何熵代理（尤其对数行列式协方差熵）优于softmax归一化熵。

Result: 几何熵代理，尤其对数行列式协方差熵，能诱导更强、更稳定的信息力；建立了熵正则化有效性的条件（非退化信息力）；给出了缩放律行为的动力学解释（信息注入、熵耗散与残差风险的平衡）。

Conclusion: 熵正则化的效用取决于能否产生非退化信息力；几何熵代理优于传统softmax熵；缩放律行为可被动力学解释但非无条件推导。

Abstract: Deep learning is increasingly viewed as a dynamical process in parameter space, yet many existing theories still treat training as a closed optimization system. This view is limited for real-world AI, where models operate under uncertainty, resource constraints, distribution shift, downstream decision risks, and human feedback. We propose Human-Centered Learning Mechanics (HCLM), a dynamical and information-theoretic framework for open and controlled learning systems. The central idea is that entropy regularization is useful only when the chosen entropy surrogate generates a non-degenerate information force along the optimization trajectory. Otherwise, entropy terms may produce weak, unstable, or misaligned gradients, causing the dynamics to collapse toward ordinary loss minimization. We introduce the notion of effective entropy and study tractable geometric entropy surrogates, including variance-based and log-determinant covariance proxies. The paper makes three contributions. First, it formalizes entropy regularization through effective information force and characterizes degenerate entropy regimes. Second, it derives convergence, entropy-flow, Wasserstein-gradient-flow, and noisy-representation generalization results under explicit assumptions. Third, it offers a conditional dynamical interpretation of scaling-law-like behavior as a balance between information injection, entropy dissipation, and residual risk, without claiming an unconditional derivation of empirical neural scaling laws. Controlled representation-learning experiments support the hypothesis that geometric entropy surrogates, especially log-determinant covariance entropy, induce stronger and more stable information forces than softmax-normalized entropy.

</details>


### [15] [From Residuals to Reasons: LLM-Guided Mechanism Inference from Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22897)
*Mohammad R. Rezaei,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多智能体残差上下文学习(MARICL)框架，利用LLM分析基础模型预测残差、生成显式修正项，并通过多轮文本梯度优化进行迭代改进。在9个基准测试中均优于基础模型，冻结的修正公式在同一生化协议下92%情况有效，不同协议下则失效，证明了机制泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在科学应用中面临预测与理解兼顾的挑战：统计模型是黑箱，现有可解释性方法只能回答"哪些特征重要"而非"特征如何交互"，且无法与人进行迭代式的解释优化。

Method: MARICL框架：先用基础模型做预测，多个LLM智能体分析残差较大的样本，在高残差示例的上下文提示下假设缺失的结构，生成显式的修正项，再通过多轮文本梯度优化进行细化。

Result: 在9个科学、生物医学、社会经济和合成基准上均一致优于基础模型。冻结从Cell-Free Protein数据集一个实验批次学到的公式，在同一试剂协议下92%的留出批次有效，不同协议下则系统性失效。

Conclusion: MARICL能发现可迁移的机制性结构而非批次特定噪声，其成功边界由生化原理而非批次数量决定，这直接证明了机制泛化能力。

Abstract: A persistent challenge in machine learning for scientific applications is jointly achieving prediction and understanding. Statistical models excel on structured data but operate as black boxes, while existing interpretability methods are largely inspective: they answer "which features matter?" but do not articulate how features interact or refine explanations iteratively alongside human understanding. Asking an LLM to predict the target directly forces it to search the entire output space; we instead anchor predictions with a base model and ask the LLM the narrower question of what that model is missing. We introduce Multi-Agent Residual In-Context Learning (MARICL), an agentic framework in which LLM agents analyze where a base-model fails, hypothesize missing structure from high-residual examples provided in context, and produce explicit correction terms refined through multi-turn textual gradient optimization. Across nine benchmarks spanning scientific, biomedical, socioeconomic, and synthetic settings, MARICL improves consistently over its base model on all datasets. To test whether these corrections reflect real structure or batch-specific noise, we freeze formulas learned on one experimental batch of the Cell-Free Protein dataset and apply them (with no retraining and no further LLM calls) to held-out batches. Within the same reagent protocol, the frozen formulas improve predictions in over 92% of cases; across a different protocol, they fail systematically. The success boundary aligns with the biochemistry, not the batch count; direct evidence of mechanistic generalization.

</details>


### [16] [Anytime Training with Schedule-Free Spectral Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23061)
*Anuj Apte,Pranav Deshpande,Niraj Kumar,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Junhyung Lyle Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SF-NorMuon，一种无调度谱优化器，在无预先指定训练步数的情况下，在125M和772M参数语言模型上匹配或超越调优后的AdamW，并具备理论收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络训练依赖于固定步数的学习率调度，导致路径依赖严重且数据量变化时需重新调参。Schedule-Free (SF) 方法虽去除了显式调度，但现有SF-AdamW始终不如调优后的AdamW，存在性能差距。

Method: 提出SF-NorMuon，一种无调度的谱优化器。理论方面，证明了无调度谱动力学的驻点保证，并指出在快迭代上施加权重衰减对长程稳定性至关重要。

Result: 在125M和772M参数语言模型上，在1-8倍Chinchilla训练步数范围内，SF-NorMuon以单一超参数配置匹配或超越调优后的AdamW，消除了SF方法与传统方法的性能差距。

Conclusion: SF-NorMuon使无调度优化更实用，用户无需预先指定训练步数即可在任何时刻获得高质量检查点，向真正的开放式持续学习迈进一步。

Abstract: Standard neural network training relies on learning-rate schedules tied to a fixed horizon, leading to strong path dependence and costly re-tuning as data availability changes. Schedule-Free (SF) methods address this by removing explicit schedules, yet SF-AdamW, the current state-of-the-art anytime optimizer, consistently underperforms well-tuned AdamW baselines. We propose SF-NorMuon, a schedule-free spectral optimizer that closes this gap: with a single hyperparameter configuration, SF-NorMuon matches or exceeds tuned AdamW on 125M and 772M parameter language models across $1$--$8\times$ Chinchilla horizons. On the theoretical side, we prove a stationarity guarantee for schedule-free spectral dynamics and identify weight decay at the fast iterate as essential for long-horizon stability. SF-NorMuon enables practitioners to obtain high-quality checkpoints at any point during training without committing to a horizon in advance. By closing the performance gap with tuned baselines, SF-NorMuon makes horizon-free optimization more practical, taking a step towards truly open-ended, continual learning.

</details>


### [17] [FIRMA: FIbonacci Ring Model Aggregation for Privacy-preserving Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22898)
*Rachid Hedjam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRMA系列协议，通过斐波那契加权环形聚合、精度门控邻居抑制和2-opt环排列，实现了无服务器、永久私有分类头、环形拓扑和非对称邻居加权，在28种配置下优于FedAvg等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习协议面临结构性三难困境：基于服务器的聚合存在单点故障和梯度反演风险；去中心化环形聚合将分类头暴露给半诚实节点；个性化方法重新引入中心聚合。尚无协议能同时实现无服务器、永久私有分类头、环形拓扑和原则性非对称邻居加权。

Method: FIRMA包含三个逐步增强的协议：1) FIRMA-Base：基于斐波那契加权的邻居融合与永久私有分类头的无服务器环形聚合；2) FIRMA-Plus：增加精度门控邻居抑制，选择性降低收敛不佳节点的权重；3) FIRMA-Pro：完整系统，引入2-opt环排列最大化相邻客户端类别多样性、全局环覆盖（Kg=⌈N/2⌉轮传播）和余弦退火自保留校准。

Result: 跨28种配置（4个基准×7种异构性）的系统实验表明：FIRMA-Pro在全部12种标签偏斜配置中超越FedAvg，在CIFAR-10上K=1时峰值优势达+20.7个百分点；在Dirichlet异构性下，FIRMA-Pro是所有无服务器协议中Pareto最优的方法，在28种配置中17个达到最高准确率。

Conclusion: FIRMA系列协议成功解决了联邦学习的结构性三难困境，建立了收敛率界及四个支持性命题（归一化、覆盖、保留和多样性最优性），在标签偏斜和Dirichlet异构性下均表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Federated learning protocols face a structural trilemma: canonical server-based aggregation~\cite{mcmahan2017} creates a single point of failure and gradient inversion risk; decentralised ring-gossip alternatives~\cite{hu2019segmented} expose classification heads to semi-honest peers via uninformed uniform weights; and personalised methods~\cite{collins2021exploiting} reintroduce central aggregation. No existing protocol simultaneously achieves server-free operation, permanently private heads, ring topology, and principled asymmetric neighbour weighting. We propose FIRMA (\textbf{FI}bonacci \textbf{R}ing \textbf{M}odel \textbf{A}ggregation), a family of three progressively enhanced federated learning protocols: 1) \fibfl\ establishes the foundation: server-free ring aggregation with Fibonacci-weighted neighbour blending and permanently private classification heads. 2) \fibflp\ augments this with accuracy-gated neighbour suppression, selectively down-weighting poorly-converged peers while preserving the Fibonacci directional bias. 3) \fibflpp, the full system, completes the family with a 2-opt ring permutation that maximises adjacent-client class diversity, global ring coverage via $K_g{=}\lceil N/2\rceil$ gossip passes, and cosine-annealed self-retention calibration. We establish a convergence rate bound and three supporting propositions governing normalisation, coverage, retention, and diversity optimality. Systematic experiments across 28 configurations -- four benchmarks crossed with seven heterogeneity regimes -- demonstrate that \fibflpp\ surpasses \fedavg\ in all 12 label-skew configurations, with a peak advantage of $+20.7$\,pp on CIFAR-10 at $K{=}1$. Under Dirichlet heterogeneity, \fibflpp\ is the Pareto-dominant method among all server-free protocols, achieving the highest accuracy in 17 of 28 configurations.

</details>

*Yiming Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gen-ROTDA框架，通过鲁棒最优传输引导的残差域自适应方法，有效解决了共享单车需求预测中因出行模式变化导致的时间域漂移问题，在2025-2026年主任务上取得最低MAE，并在噪声环境下展现出优异稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于历史站点小时数据训练的共享单车需求预测模型在后续年份部署时会因出行模式随时间变化而发生性能退化，现有方法未能有效处理这种时间域自适应问题。

Method: 提出Gen-ROTDA框架：(1) 利用少量标注目标子集拟合目标域站点时间锚点；(2) 预测残差而非原始需求；(3) 设计确定性标签保持的残差特征生成器；(4) 在训练最终残差预测器前修剪高成本传输匹配对。

Result: Gen-ROTDA在2025-2026年主任务上取得最低MAE，在多年份任务平均上是最优的OT系列方法；在异常无标注目标数据下，Gen-ROTDA相比非鲁棒OT变体具有显著更高的稳定性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒最优传输对于共享单车需求预测中的噪声时间迁移是有效的，但微调和MMD自适应仍是整体上强劲的基线方法。

Abstract: Bike-sharing models trained on historical station-hour data may degrade when deployed in later years because travel patterns change over time. This paper studies March Citi Bike demand prediction from 2021 to 2026 as a temporal domain adaptation problem and proposes Gen-ROTDA, a robust optimal transport-guided residual domain adaptation framework. The method fits a target-domain station-time anchor with a small labeled target subset, transfers residual rather than raw demand, applies a deterministic label-preserving residual feature generator, and trims high-cost transport matches before training the final residual predictor. Experiments compare Gen-ROTDA with anchor-only, source-only, target-only, fine-tuning, MMD adaptation, Sinkhorn OTDA, ROTDA, and Gen-OTDA. Gen-ROTDA achieves the lowest MAE on the main 2025 to 2026 task and is the best OT-family method on average across multi-year tasks, although fine-tuning and MMD adaptation remain strong overall baselines. Under abnormal target-unlabeled records, Gen-ROTDA is much more stable than non-robust OT variants, suggesting that robust transport is useful for noisy temporal transfer in bike-sharing demand prediction.

</details>


### [19] [Transcoders Trace Visual Grounding and Hallucinations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22902)
*Dimitrios Damianos,Leon Voukoutis,Georgios Skyrianos,Vassilis Katsouros,Georgios Paraskevopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transcoder（MLP稀疏近似）的功能中心化可解释性框架，用于分解视觉语言模型（VLM）的跨模态计算路径。在Gemma 3-4B-IT上的实验表明，相比稀疏自编码器（SAE），Transcoder归因在视觉标记上产生更强更稳定的因果效应，并能构建幻觉预测模型（AUC 0.68）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）的可解释性工作只能分解静态残差表示，无法捕捉驱动跨模态交互的功能更新。需要一种功能中心化的方法来理解视觉输入在VLM中如何被转化为文本。

Method: 采用Transcoder（MLP层的稀疏近似）作为功能中心化的因果代理，将模型分解为可解释的计算路径，连接图像块到令牌生成方向。通过补丁消融、反事实分析（False Visual Grounding）和电路痕迹的图结构分析来验证和利用这些路径。

Result: 1) Transcoder归因在视觉接地令牌上比SAE归因产生更强更稳定的效果；2) 与语义相关图像区域的对齐更好；3) 反事实分析确认恢复的路径是视觉-语言交互特有的；4) 基于电路图特征的逻辑回归分类器预测幻觉的AUC达到0.68。

Conclusion: 功能中心化的电路分解能够为VLM中的多模态计算提供可解释且可预测的说明，为理解跨模态交互和检测生成幻觉提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Generative Vision-Language Models (VLMs) perform well on multimodal reasoning, but how visual inputs are transformed to text remains poorly understood. Existing interpretability work on VLMs uses Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), which decompose static residual representations and miss the functional updates that drive cross-modal interaction. We adopt a function-centric framework based on Transcoders, sparse approximations of MLP sublayers that act as a causal proxy for layer-wise computation. Applied to Gemma 3-4B-IT, the framework decomposes the model into interpretable computational pathways linking image patches to directions in token generation. Transcoder attributions produce stronger and more stable effects on visually grounded tokens under patch ablation than SAE attributions, and align better with semantically relevant image regions. A False Visual Grounding counterfactual analysis confirms that the recovered pathways are specific to vision-language interaction.Finally, we perform a structural analysis of hallucinated generations, by extracting graph-based indicators from circuit traces produced by the transcoders. A logistic classifier over these mechanistic graph features predicts hallucinations at AUC $0.68$. These results show that function-centric circuit decomposition yields interpretable and predictive accounts of multimodal computation in VLMs.

</details>


### [20] [Any-Dimensional Invariant Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23156)
*Shengtai Yao,Eitan Levin,Mateo Díaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对任意维度输入（如图、点云）的机器学习模型的通用性（universality）问题，提出了一种系统性的分析方法。通过将任意维度函数映射到无限维极限空间，并结合对称性与不同维度间的关系，建立了一种新的拓扑框架，在该框架下可以证明任意维度模型的通用性。作者还展示了现有架构的不足，并提出了简单改进方案以恢复通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用性研究局限于固定尺寸输入的模型，而对接受任意尺寸输入（如图、点云）的模型的通用性缺乏系统性理解。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，为任意维度模型的通用性建立严谨的分析框架。

Method: 将任意维度函数唯一地映射到无限维极限空间中的函数，该空间包含所有有限尺寸输入及其极限；利用输入对称性及不同尺寸输入之间的关系，为极限空间构造合适的拓扑结构，使得在该拓扑下存在丰富的紧集族，从而可以在这些紧集上建立任意维度通用性。

Result: 成功建立了任意维度通用性的系统分析方法；证明了若干现有架构在该框架下不具通用性；提出了简单的架构修改方案，能够恢复其通用性。

Conclusion: 本文为任意维度机器学习模型的通用性研究提供了理论基础和分析工具，揭示了传统方法在任意维度场景下的局限性，并给出了可行的改进方向。

Abstract: Several machine learning models are defined for inputs of any size, such as graphs with different numbers of nodes and point clouds containing varying numbers of points. The universality properties of such any-dimensional models remain poorly understood, as universality is traditionally studied for models accepting inputs of a fixed size, defined on a compact subset of their domain. In sharp contrast, any-dimensional models can be viewed as sequences of functions defined on growing-sized inputs, and it is not clear in which sense they can be universal. We develop a systematic approach to establish any-dimensional universality, by identifying any-dimensional functions with a unique function taking inputs in a suitable infinite-dimensional limit space containing inputs of all finite sizes as well as their limits. Using the symmetries of these inputs and relations between inputs of different sizes, we show that this limit space admits a natural topology with rich families of compact sets on which any-dimensional universality can be established. We illustrate our approach by showing that several existing architectures fail to be universal, and we propose simple modifications that restore universality.

</details>


### [21] [Building a privacy-preserving Federated Recommender system for mobile devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22924)
*Aasheesh Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段联邦推荐系统流水线，实现非敏感偏好数据与敏感移动上下文数据的分离，第一阶段在云端协同过滤生成候选列表，第二阶段在设备端利用敏感信号重排序，仅模型梯度离开设备，并在多个数据集上验证后以Kotlin多平台库形式部署于Android和iOS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统移动端个性化内容推荐需要将用户敏感数据集中到服务器，这种做法与日益严格的隐私期望和地理法规相冲突，因此需要一种既能保护用户隐私又能实现个性化推荐的方案。

Method: 两阶段联邦推荐系统：第一阶段在云端基于非敏感应用-上下文数据运行协同过滤模型生成候选列表；第二阶段在设备端利用敏感移动信号对候选进行重排序，仅模型更新/梯度离开设备，实现敏感数据不离开设备的隐私保护。

Result: 在MovieLens、UCI Human Activity Recognition以及一个专有试点数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并交付了基于Kotlin Multiplatform的生产级实现，可在Android和iOS上部署。

Conclusion: 通过在云端和设备端之间进行原则性的数据分离，两阶段联邦推荐流水线能够在保护用户隐私的同时实现有效的个性化推荐，并且具备跨平台的生产级部署能力。

Abstract: Serving personalized content on mobile devices has traditionally required pooling sensitive user data on centralized servers, a practice increasingly at odds with modern privacy expectations and geographical regulations. We present a two-stage federated recommendation system pipeline for mobile devices, built around a principled separation between non-sensitive user preference data and sensitive mobile context data that never leaves the device. The first stage runs a collaborative filtering model on non-sensitive app-context data in the cloud to generate a shortlist of relevant items. The second stage re-ranks these candidates on-device using sensitive mobile signals, with only model updates/gradients ever leaving the device. We validate the approach on MovieLens, UCI Human Activity Recognition, and a proprietary pilot dataset, and deliver a production-ready implementation as a Kotlin Multiplatform library deployable on Android and iOS.

</details>


### [22] [Understanding and Improving Noisy Embedding Techniques in Instruction Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23171)
*Abhay Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymNoise方法，在指令微调中使用对称噪声替代NEFTune的均匀噪声，通过更严格地约束局部曲率来提升模型性能。在LLaMA-2-7B上，SymNoise在AlpacaEval上达到69.04%，比NEFTune（64.69%）提升6.7%，并在多个模型和数据集上持续超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEFTune发现均匀噪声优于高斯噪声但原因不明，本文旨在从理论和实证角度澄清不同噪声类型性能可比，并探索更优的噪声策略。

Method: 提出SymNoise方法，在语言模型微调的嵌入层引入对称噪声，通过更严格地约束模型局部曲率来增强泛化能力。

Result: LLaMA-2-7B微调Alpaca，AlpacaEval得分从标准方法的29.79%提升至69.04%，相比NEFTune（64.69%）提升6.7%；在Evol-Instruct、ShareGPT、OpenPlatypus等更强基线数据集及多种模型上均一致优于NEFTune。

Conclusion: SymNoise是噪声策略在语言模型微调中的重要进展，显著超越了当前最先进的NEFTune方法。

Abstract: Recent advancements in instructional fine-tuning have injected noise into embeddings, with NEFTune (Jain et al., 2024) setting benchmarks using uniform noise. Despite NEFTune's empirical findings that uniform noise outperforms Gaussian noise, the reasons for this remain unclear. This paper aims to clarify this by offering a thorough analysis, both theoretical and empirical, indicating comparable performance among these noise types. Additionally, we introduce a new fine-tuning method for language models, utilizing symmetric noise in embeddings. This method aims to enhance the model's function by more stringently regulating its local curvature, demonstrating superior performance over the current method, NEFTune. When fine-tuning the LLaMA-2-7B model using Alpaca, standard techniques yield a 29.79% score on AlpacaEval. However, our approach, SymNoise, increases this score significantly to 69.04%, using symmetric noisy embeddings. This is a 6.7% improvement over the state-of-the-art method, NEFTune (64.69%). Furthermore, when tested on various models and stronger baseline instruction datasets, such as Evol-Instruct, ShareGPT, OpenPlatypus, SymNoise consistently outperforms NEFTune. The current literature, including NEFTune, has underscored the importance of more in-depth research into the application of noise-based strategies in the fine-tuning of language models. Our approach, SymNoise, is another significant step towards this direction, showing notable improvement over the existing state-of-the-art method.

</details>


### [23] [Instance-Optimal Estimation with Multiple LLM Judges on a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23362)
*Junghyun Lee,Sanghwa Kim,Yassir Jedra,Alexandre Proutière,Se-Young Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定预算下，如何将评估查询分配给不同成本和可靠性的法官，以最准确地估计大语言模型评分。提出了EST-IVWE自适应算法，并证明了其达到Oracle分配的最优率，同时通过局部扰动型Assouad论证给出了匹配的极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge评估协议成本高昂，不同法官的成本和可靠性各异，且不同提示-响应对的难度差异很大。如何在固定预算下最优分配评估查询，以最小化评分估计误差，是一个尚未解决的基本分配问题。

Method: 形式化为预算异方差多法官估计问题；分析逆方差加权估计量(IVWE)并推导出Oracle分配；提出EST-IVWE自适应算法，利用乐观有偏方差估计来稳定经验分配；采用Assouad型期望论证（而非Fano型高概率论证）推导局部极小极大下界。

Result: EST-IVWE在预算范围内匹配Oracle IVWE的误差率（仅差低阶项）；建立了匹配的局部极小极大下界，证明算法实例最优性；数值实验在合成数据和HelpSteer2数据集上优于朴素均匀分配。

Conclusion: 该工作为预算受限下的异质法官评估提供了理论最优分配方案，关键技术贡献在于使用Assouad型期望论证保留了局部方差结构，从而得到尖锐的分配依赖下界。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models increasingly relies on LLM-as-a-judge protocols, but such evaluations remain costly: different judges have different prices and reliabilities, and the difficulty of each prompt-response pair can vary substantially. This raises a basic allocation question: under a fixed budget, how should one distribute evaluation queries across heterogeneous judges and instances to obtain the most accurate score estimates? We formalize this question as *budgeted heteroskedastic multi-judge estimation*. Given $K$ prompt-response pairs, $J$ judges with known costs, and unknown query-judge variances, the goal is to estimate a bounded score vector while minimizing an $\ell_p$-error. Our first contribution is to analyze the inverse-variance weighted estimator (IVWE) and to derive the oracle allocation that minimizes its error rate. Since this allocation depends on the unknown variances, we then address the practical unknown-variance setting by proposing EST-IVWE, an adaptive algorithm that constructs and leverages *optimistically biased* variance estimates to stabilize the empirical allocation. We prove that EST-IVWE matches the oracle IVWE rate up to lower-order terms in the budget. Our second and central theoretical contribution is a matching *local* minimax lower bound, which establishes the instance-optimality of the proposed algorithms. A key technical insight is that Fano-type high-probability arguments are too coarse for this problem: their packing construction loses the local variance structure that governs the optimal allocation. We instead use an Assouad-type in-expectation argument, based on local perturbations, which preserves this structure and yields the sharp allocation-dependent lower bound. Finally, we numerically validate the superiority of our approach over naïve uniform allocation on synthetic and HelpSteer2 datasets.

</details>


### [24] [MARGIN: Runtime Confidence Calibration for Multi-Agent Foundation Model Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22949)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MARGIN在线校准方法，无需模型访问或重训练，在多智能体系统中通过对称指数加权移动平均和贝叶斯收缩混合计算每智能体每置信度带的校准因子，在19个基础模型、8个基准测试中校准误差降低3-6倍，多智能体选择任务中成对分辨率从45-56%提升至70-89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型智能体的置信度系统性地误校准，且在困难任务上置信度与准确率呈负相关；传统设计时校准方法（温度缩放、Platt缩放、直方图分箱）在分布偏移下性能严重退化，无法解决在线场景中的校准问题。

Method: 提出MARGIN，一种在线校准方法：使用对称指数加权移动平均（SEWMA）结合贝叶斯收缩混合，从任务流中在线学习每个智能体在每个置信度带上的校准因子，无需模型访问、无需留出数据、无需重训练，仅需三个超参数且具有鲁棒的默认值。

Result: 在19个基础模型、8个基准测试、5万+观测中，MARGIN在分布偏移下校准误差比最优设计时基线低3-6倍；多智能体选择中，将原始语言化置信度的成对分辨率从45-56%（低于随机）提升至70-89%，在四个基准测试中的三个上超越了始终选择最佳模型的Oracle。

Conclusion: MARGIN有效解决了基础模型在线校准问题，通过六个形式化命题从理论上保证了收敛性、跟踪速度和对称更新的最优性，实验验证了所有理论预测。

Abstract: Foundation model agents increasingly operate in multi-agent deployments where a coordinator must decide which agent's response to trust. The standard approach weights agents by their self-reported confidence, but recent evidence shows that foundation model confidence is systematically mis-calibrated and, on hard tasks, inversely correlated with accuracy. Design-time calibration methods (temperature scaling, Platt scaling, histogram binning) cannot address this problem because they fit a fixed correction to held-out data and degrade under distribution shift.
  We present MARGIN (Multi Agent Runtime Grading via Incremental Normalization), an online calibration method that learns per-agent, per-confidence-band calibration factors from the task stream itself, requiring no model access, no held-out data, and no retraining. MARGIN uses symmetric exponentially weighted moving averages with Bayesian shrinkage blending, and has three hyperparameters with robust defaults. Across 19 foundation models, 8 benchmarks, and over 50,000 observations, MARGIN achieves 3-6x lower calibration error than the best design-time baseline under distribution shift. In multi-agent selection, raw verbalized confidence produces pairwise resolution worse than random (45-56%) on hard benchmarks. MARGIN corrects this completely, raising pairwise resolution to 70-89% and surpassing the always-best-model oracle on three of four benchmarks. Six formal propositions characterize convergence, tracking speed, and the optimality of symmetric updates for non-strategic agents, with all predictions illustrated empirically.

</details>


### [25] [Optimal Dimension-Free Sampling for Regularized Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23726)
*Meysam Alishahi,Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对一类Lipschitz连续分类损失函数（包括逻辑损失、sigmoid损失、hinge损失和ReLU损失）在不同正则化项下，证明了达到$(1\pm\varepsilon)$相对误差的最优采样界。具体地，对$\|\cdot\|_2/k$正则化得到$k^2/\varepsilon^2$上下界，对$\|\cdot\|_1/k$正则化得到$k/\varepsilon^2$上下界，对$\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$正则化则取决于有界导数性质。所有上界均有匹配的下界（至多相差多对数项），且本文采用简单均匀或（平方）范数采样，改进了近期灵敏度采样的$k^3/\varepsilon^2$界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Lipschitz连续分类损失函数在不同正则化条件下建立最优（匹配上下界）的采样复杂度理论，填补现有研究在采样界紧性上的空白，并克服VC维度和灵敏度框架中过度计数的缺陷。

Method: 采用简单均匀采样或（平方）范数采样，结合高阶矩界和经验过程分析，替代传统的VC维度和灵敏度框架，避免过度计数问题。

Result: 1) $\|\cdot\|_2/k$正则化: $k^2/\varepsilon^2$上下界；2) $\|\cdot\|_1/k$正则化: $k/\varepsilon^2$上下界；3) $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$正则化: 若$|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$且$g(0)>0$且$g$单调或凸，则线性$k$复杂度；否则$k^2/\varepsilon^2$；4) 若$g(0)=0$，则无维度无关界；5) 所有上界均有匹配下界（至多对数因子）。

Conclusion: 本文通过精细的理论分析，为一大类Lipschitz连续损失函数在不同正则化下的采样复杂度提供了完整的最优刻画，在理论上改进了近期灵敏度采样的结果，并建立了简单采样策略的紧性保证。

Abstract: We prove optimal sampling bounds achieving $(1\pm\varepsilon)$-relative error for a broad class of Lipschitz continuous classification loss functions under various regularization terms. This includes important functions such as logistic and sigmoid loss, hinge loss, and ReLU loss, as prominent and popular representative examples. In particular, we prove $k^2/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_2/k$ regularization, and $k/\varepsilon^2$ upper and lower bounds for $\|\cdot\|_1/k$ regularization. For $\|\cdot\|_2^2/k$ regularization, the sampling complexity depends mainly on a bounded derivative property: if $|g'(x)|\leq g(x)$, and $g(0)>0$, and $g$ is monotonic or convex, then it admits linear in $k$ sampling complexity; otherwise the general bound is $k^2/\varepsilon^2$. However, if $g(0)=0$, our results indicate that no dimension-free bounds are possible, and even sublinear bounds are ruled out. All upper bounds are complemented by matching lower bounds up to polylogarithmic terms. Moreover, our work relies conceptually and algorithmically on simple uniform or (squared) norm sampling and hereby improves over recent cubic $k^3/\varepsilon^2$ sensitivity sampling bounds of (Alishahi and Phillips, ICML'24). This is achieved by refined arguments involving higher moment bounds and empirical process analyses to avoid overcounting that appears in the de-facto standard VC-dimension and sensitivity framework.

</details>


### [26] [FederatedRSF : Federated Random Survival Forests for Partially Overlapping Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22954)
*Maryam Moradpour,Jonas Harriehausen,Amirreza Aleyasin,Lion Philipp Wolf,Youngjun Park,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FederatedRSF，一个实现联邦随机生存森林的Python包，通过聚合本地训练的生存树并仅向各站点分发特征兼容的树，在特征部分重叠的情况下实现多中心生存预测，无需共享原始数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心生存预测能提升鲁棒性和泛化性，但隐私法规和机构治理阻碍了跨机构共享患者级临床和基因组数据；此外，不同站点收集的协变量或测序panel不同导致特征空间异质性，仅有部分特征重叠，增加了实际部署难度。

Method: 提出FederatedRSF联邦学习框架：各站点本地训练随机生存森林，聚合后仅向各站点分发特征兼容的生存树，实现部分特征重叠下的推理，无需共享原始数据。在GBSG2乳腺癌队列上模拟特征异质性进行验证。

Result: 在模拟特征异质性的跨站点联邦场景下，FederatedRSF的C-Index性能与集中式训练模式相当。

Conclusion: FederatedRSF能够在保护隐私和应对特征空间异质性的前提下，实现与集中式训练可比的多中心生存预测性能。

Abstract: Multi-center survival prediction can improve robustness and generalizability, yet privacy regulations and institutional governance often prevent pooling patient-level clinical and genomic data across institutions. In practice, deployment is further complicated by feature-space heterogeneity, in which sites collect different covariates or use different sequencing panels, resulting in only partially overlapping feature sets. We present FederatedRSF, a Python package that implements federated random survival forests, aggregating locally trained survival trees and redistributing only feature-compatible trees to each site, enabling inference with partial overlap without sharing raw data. We evaluate FederatedRSF on the GBSG2 breast cancer cohort distributed with the scikit-survival package, simulating feature heterogeneity across clients by withholding subsets of features, and assessing discrimination using Harrell's concordance index (C-Index) under repeated cross-validation and site-splits. The results demonstrated that the federated model can achieve performance comparable to that of the centralized training setting.

</details>


### [27] [Entrywise Error Bounds for Spectral Ranking with Semi-Random Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23854)
*Dongmin Lee,Anuran Makur,Japneet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了半随机对抗性设置下Bradley-Terry-Luce（BTL）模型排序的谱方法，发现未加权谱方法的性能严重依赖于图的谱特性，并证明通过对观测边进行适当重加权以恢复谱隙，可以接近均匀采样图下的渐近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然BTL模型在均匀采样图下的理论性能已被充分研究，但这些结果难以推广到更广泛的随机图类别中。本文旨在探索在半随机对抗性环境下（攻击者可任意提升某些边的采样概率）谱算法的表现。

Method: 本文采用半随机对抗模型（攻击者可任意提升某些边的采样概率），分析未加权谱方法的逐元素误差，并提出一种通过重加权观测边来对抗攻击者并恢复谱隙的方法。

Result: 未加权谱方法的性能高度依赖于生成图的谱特性；通过适当重加权观测边可以恢复谱隙，使渐近性能接近均匀采样图下的水平；数值模拟支持了理论发现。

Conclusion: 在半随机对抗性设置下，通过合适的边重加权策略，谱方法可以恢复到接近均匀采样图的性能水平，这为BTL模型在更广泛图结构下的应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Bradley-Terry-Luce (BTL) model estimation is a well-established strategy to rank a collection of items given a dataset of pairwise comparisons. Although the theoretical performance of BTL estimation methods, such as spectral and maximum likelihood estimation, is well studied in the regime of uniformly sampled graphs, generalizing such results to a wider class of random graphs has proved challenging. In this work, we investigate the entry-wise error of spectral algorithms against a semi-random adversary that can arbitrarily boost the sampling probabilities of certain edges. We find that the performance of the unweighted spectral method is heavily dependent on the spectral properties of the generated graph. Furthermore, we show that asymptotic performance approaching that of uniformly sampled graphs can be recovered by appropriately reweighting the observed edges to counteract the adversary and restore the spectral gap. Finally, we provide numerical simulations that support our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [28] [Certification from Examples is Hard for Circuits and Transformers under Minimal Overparametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22964)
*Artur Back de Luca,Kimon Fountoulakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经网络在推理和算法任务中的精确验证问题，发现即使平均精度很高，最小过参数化也会导致验证所需的标签样本数量呈指数级增长。对于深度≥2的阈值电路，额外增加一个门就可使验证集大小随输入维度指数增长；对数精度Transformer也存在类似困难。近似验证同样面临指数级障碍，实验表明不完美模型可通过大规模均匀采样避开检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着神经网络在推理和算法任务中部署，精确性保证变得日益重要。然而高平均精度无法保证模型行为的一致性，因此需要精确验证（exact certification）方法。本文旨在探究验证一个学习到的假设是否与目标函数完全相同所需的最小标注样本数量，并揭示过参数化对验证复杂度的影响。

Method: 理论分析结合实验验证。理论上，研究深度≥2的阈值电路和对数精度Transformer的精确验证复杂度，分析过参数化对证书大小的影响；同时研究近似验证（允许多项式级错误或常数级相对误差）的复杂度。实验上，构建特定电路和训练用于识别二进制加法的Transformer，测试验证困难与检测逃逸现象。

Result: 深度≥2的阈值电路中，额外增加一个门就可使精确验证所需标签数随输入维度指数增长；对数精度Transformer中，仅需常数级架构开销即可实现类似的指数级验证困难。近似验证方面，允许多项式级错误仍需指数级证书，而常数级相对误差保证可隐藏指数级错误。实验证实了构造电路的指数级验证障碍，并发现不完美Transformer模型可通过大规模均匀采样候选者逃避检测。

Conclusion: 精确验证在过参数化模型中面临根本性的指数级复杂度障碍，近似验证也未能完全规避这一问题。这提示我们，在追求模型精确性保证时，需要重新思考验证策略和复杂度理论基础。

Abstract: As state-of-the-art neural networks are deployed on reasoning and algorithmic tasks, exactness guarantees become increasingly important. However, high average-case accuracy can still mask inconsistent behaviors. This motivates exact certification, which asks for the smallest set of labeled examples needed to certify that a learned hypothesis equals the target. We show that while some hypotheses are easy to certify, even minimal overparametrization can make certification exponentially hard across several hypothesis classes. For threshold circuits of depth $\ge 2$, adding a single extra gate can force certificate sizes exponential in the input dimension. We show an analogous hardness result for log-precision Transformers with only constant architectural overhead. We also characterize approximate certification, showing that allowing only polynomially many mistakes still requires exponentially large certificates, whereas constant relative-error guarantees can hide exponentially many mistakes. Empirically, we study certification for constructed circuits and trained Transformers for recognizing binary addition. While the constructed circuits instantiate the exponential barrier for certification, the trained Transformer analysis shows that imperfect models can evade detection by large uniformly sampled certificate candidates.

</details>


### [29] [Training-Free Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23872)
*Lizhang Chen,Jonathan Li,Chen Liang,Ni Lao,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的循环变压器方法，通过在推理时循环冻结检查点的连续中间块层，无需微调或架构修改，即可提升大模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往循环变压器方法需要端到端训练，且直接复现块通常会导致性能下降。本文旨在探索如何在不额外训练的情况下，利用预训练模型的冻结检查点在推理时实现循环，从而提升模型性能。

Method: 将预归一化变压器块视为ODE的前向欧拉步，将循环视为同一近似过程的细化，用较小的阻尼子步替代单次大更新。在推理时对冻结的预训练模型进行轻量级包装，循环连续中间块层。

Result: 在7种密集、稀疏MoE和MLA+MoE模型家族上，Qwen3-4B-Instruct在MMLU-Pro上提升+2.64pp，Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct在CommonsenseQA上提升+1.14pp，Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct在OpenBookQA上提升+1.20pp。

Conclusion: 无需训练的循环变压器能够有效提升预训练模型性能，循环应用策略至关重要，将单个块重应用替换为阻尼子步可避免性能退化。

Abstract: We introduce training-free looped transformers, in which a lightweight inference-time wrapper loops a contiguous mid-stack block of layers of a frozen checkpoint without additional fine-tuning, continued training, or architectural changes. Unlike prior looped transformer methods that train with the looped structure end-to-end, we retrofit recurrence onto pretrained models at test time. We show that naive block reapplication usually degrades performance, highlighting the importance of the loop application strategy. Motivated by viewing a pre-norm transformer block as a forward Euler step on an ODE, we instead treat looping as a refinement of the same approximation, replacing one large update with smaller damped sub-steps. Across seven dense, sparse MoE, and MLA+MoE model families, our method improves Qwen3-4B-Instruct by +2.64 pp on MMLU-Pro, Qwen3-30B-A3B-Instruct by +1.14 pp on CommonsenseQA, and Moonlight-16B-A3B-Instruct by +1.20 pp on OpenBookQA.

</details>


### [30] [Non-normal spectral signatures of instability in neural network training dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23476)
*Souvik Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从算子理论角度证明了深度学习优化器（Adam、带动量的SGD）的线性化更新算子本质上是非正规的，非正规性会导致瞬态放大进而引发训练不稳定。发现谱半径ρ(J)无法区分稳定与不稳定训练阶段，而条件数κ(V)能够实现约一个数量级的有效区分，为自适应优化稳定性提供了新的诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练不稳定性（loss尖峰、振荡收敛、梯度病态）普遍存在，但缺乏严格的算子理论解释。现有理论工具（如谱半径）无法有效预测和区分训练稳定性状态。

Method: 1. 证明Adam和带动量SGD的线性化更新算子本质上是非正规算子；2. 利用非正规稳定性理论推导出基于κ(V)的伪谱前兆界，作为瞬态放大的早期预警指标；3. 建立异常点（exceptional points）作为κ(V)→∞的极限情形；4. 在两层网络上进行数值实验验证。

Result: 谱半径ρ(J)无法区分稳定与不稳定训练阶段，而条件数κ(V)能够以约一个数量级的差距有效区分；κ(V)可作为连续的非正规放大严重性度量，补充了经典sharpness准则。

Conclusion: 非Hermitian算子理论是理解神经网络优化稳定性的有用且未被充分探索的框架，为自适应优化稳定性提供了诊断语言和概念验证基准。

Abstract: Training instabilities in deep networks - loss spikes, oscillatory convergence, and gradient pathologies - are empirically prevalent but lack a rigorous operator-theoretic explanation. We show that the linearized update operators for practically used optimizers are generically non-normal: for Adam, non-normality is controlled by the commutator [H, M] between the Hessian and the diagonal adaptive preconditioner, while for SGD with momentum it arises from the augmented state-space structure of the update map. Applying non-normal stability theory to these operators, we derive a conservative pseudospectral precursor bound in which κ(V) serves as an early-warning indicator of transient amplification even when the spectral radius remains below one, and we establish that exceptional points of the update operator appear as the κ(V) -> \infty limiting case of this framework. Numerical experiments on two-layer networks confirm that the spectral radius ρ(J) provides no separation between stable and unstable training phases while κ(V) separates them by approximately one order of magnitude, complementing the classical sharpness criterion with a continuous severity measure of non-normal amplification. These results establish non-Hermitian operator theory as a useful and underexplored framework for neural network optimization stability, offering a diagnostic language and proof-of-concept benchmark for understanding adaptive optimization stability.

</details>


### [31] [Learned Relay Representations for Forward-Thinking Discrete Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22967)
*Benjamin Rozonoyer,Jacopo Minniti,Dhruvesh Patel,Neil Band,Avishek Joey Bose,Tim G. J. Rudner,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Learned Relay Representations (Relay)方法，通过在掩码扩散模型（MDMs）的降噪步骤之间显式传递潜在信息，避免传统方法中每步重新计算导致的内部表示浪费，在保持或提升性能的同时将推理延迟降低高达32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MDMs在生成序列时，每一步降噪都会丢弃前一步掩码位置上的内部计算（模型表示），导致后续步骤需要重新计算这些有价值的信息，造成了计算资源的浪费和推理效率的降低。

Method: Relay引入一个可微分的每令牌通道，在MDMs的前向传播之间传递潜在信息，并通过截断式时间反向传播（BPTT）进行训练。该方法与块扩散和KV缓存等技术无缝兼容。

Result: 在Sudoku规划任务上验证了设计选择的有效性；在Fast-dLLM v2上，Relay在编码任务上优于标准监督微调，并将推理延迟降低高达32%，推进了性能-延迟帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 最先进的扩散语言模型可以通过显式训练在解码步骤间中继传递潜在信息，从而在推理效率与生成性能之间实现更优的权衡。

Abstract: When Masked Diffusion Models (MDMs) generate sequences through iterative refinement, the rich internal computation over masked positions is discarded, forcing every subsequent refinement step to recompute the valuable internal information stored as model representations. To avoid a hard reset between denoising rounds, we propose Learned Relay Representations (Relay), a method that allows MDMs to be forward-thinking when denoising by explicitly learning how to propagate latent information for the benefit of future denoising steps. Relay introduces a differentiable per-token channel that passes information between forward passes and is trained via truncated backpropagation through time (BPTT). We show that this framework can be scaled to state-of-the-art Diffusion Language Models (DLMs), and is seamlessly compatible with techniques like block diffusion and KV caching. We first provide a thorough justification of the design choices in Relay on a challenging Sudoku-based planning task. We then scale Relay to Fast-dLLM v2, a state-of-the-art DLM, outperforming standard supervised finetuning on coding tasks while reducing inference latency by up to 32%. Our empirical results demonstrate that state-of-the-art DLMs can be explicitly trained to relay latent information forward across decoding steps, advancing the performance-latency Pareto frontier. We provide code for all our experiments.

</details>


### [32] [A mathematical theory of balancing relational generalization and memorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22972)
*Luke Cheng,Samuel Lippl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出带例外的传递推理任务（Transitive Inference with Exceptions），通过核岭回归理论分析和预训练语言模型实验，揭示了学习系统在关系泛化与异常记忆之间的权衡机制，并指出该任务对表征几何结构高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏合适的任务范式来系统探究学习系统如何在学习通用规则与记忆规则例外之间取得平衡，作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 设计"带例外的传递推理"新任务；2) 使用核岭回归（Kernel Ridge Regression）对各类表征和任务参数进行理论刻画；3) 在微调预训练语言模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 模型能在关系泛化与异常记忆之间取得平衡，但对表征几何结构敏感；2) 理论揭示了该任务比无异常的传递推理更具挑战性的机制原因；3) 预训练语言模型能按传递规则泛化，但会出现理论预测的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 学习系统可以平衡关系泛化与异常记忆，但表征几何结构是关键影响因素；需要新的任务范式来深入探究这一核心能力。

Abstract: Humans, animals, and modern machine learning models exhibit impressive abilities to learn complex behaviors and generalize these behaviors to unseen situations. This ability requires us to learn rules and regularities that allow for such generalizations. At the same time, in most complex environments, any rule will have its exceptions. How do learning systems balance between learning general regularities and memorizing exceptions? We argue that a lack of task paradigms has hindered the study of this essential ability. To address this gap, we introduce a novel task, transitive inference with exceptions, that tests for relational generalization and memorization of an exception to the relational rule. We then analytically characterize the behavior of a simple, theoretically tractable model of neural network learning (kernel ridge regression) across a broad family of representations and task parameters. We find that these models can balance between relational generalization and memorization, but unlike for transitive inference without an exception, successful generalization is sensitive to the specific representational geometry. We explain why this task is more challenging mechanistically by drawing on our analytical theory. Finally, we validate our theoretical insights in pretrained language models that are finetuned on ordered relations, finding that these models successfully generalize according to the transitive rule, but also make the kinds of systematic mistakes predicted by our theory. Overall, our theory shows how learning systems can balance between relational generalization and memorization, explains how this can go wrong, and emphasizes the need for new task paradigms designed to probe this ability.

</details>


### [33] [Worse than Random: The Importance of a Baseline for Unsupervised Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22973)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Michael E. Houle,Oussama Chelly,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将随机特征选择作为无监督特征选择方法评估的基线，并实验证明许多现有最优方法在性能和效率上均不如随机选择，强调新方法必须严格优于随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督特征选择方法的评估缺乏统一基线，难以判断新方法相较于已有文献的实际价值，因此需要建立一个简单有效的评估基准。

Method: 提出使用随机特征选择作为评估基线，通过实验比较多种现有无监督特征选择方法与随机特征选择在性能（如聚类精度）和效率（如运行时间）上的表现。

Abstract: Many novel unsupervised feature selection methods are proposed each year, yet their empirical evaluation is limited to supervised and unsupervised evaluation metrics computed on selected datasets, along with comparisons to existing methods.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) is an emerging paradigm that enables models to adapt their parameters during inference, improving performance on tasks such as few-shot learning, retrieval-augmented generation, and complex reasoning. However, this dynamic adaptation introduces new vulnerabilities that adversaries can exploit to jailbreak models. We identify three threat models for TTT and demonstrate how attackers can leverage them to bypass safety filters. Our results show that TTT can significantly increase the Attack Success Rate (ASR) and the ASR over 10 generation trials (ASR@10). For example, under LoRA, the few-shot and generation-phase threat models achieve an average ASR@10 of 95% and 93% respectively, across models from different families and scales. These vulnerabilities transfer to production fine-tuning APIs. We also show that TTT-induced overfitting can produce degenerate outputs that inflate ASR under standard judges, and propose a validity-aware evaluation to correct for this. Our findings suggest that TTT exposes a new attack surface, strengthens attacks, and undermines existing safety guardrails. As a first step toward defense, we propose a lightweight provider-side detector that flags TTT requests via the perplexity shift on a private harmful holdout, but robust deployment will ultimately require dynamic alignment.

Abstract: One prominent method of evaluating machine learning model trustworthiness is the notion of calibration. In the binary outcome setting, a probabilistic predictor is calibrated if outcomes are realized according to a model's distributional prediction, conditioned on this prediction. Straightforward extensions of binary calibration definitions to probabilistic multiclass classifiers suffer from an exponential complexity blowup as the space of predictions grows exponentially in the number of classes $n$. As a remedy, Noarov and Roth (2023) propose multiclass calibration with predictions that are properties of the outcome distribution, reducing complexity from growing in the number of classes $n$ to the dimension $d$ of the property, called its elicitation complexity. Previous work on approximate property calibration is generally limited to continuous scalar properties, despite many relevant properties of interest being discrete, like the mode or rankings. We characterize the approximate property calibration of discrete properties which are strongly orderable by using Lipschitz continuous properties as an intermediary. This work is the first to our knowledge to provide approximate calibration results for discrete properties. Along the way, we characterize the Lipschitz elicitation complexity of strongly orderable discrete properties by constructing algorithms for designing these Lipschitz properties, which we prove can be post-processed to obtain the original discrete property.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型智能体在生产部署中需要大量计算和人工调优提示、解析器、验证器等组件。自我进化虽为有前景的替代方案，但多数现有框架假设可访问能可靠诊断故障、提出修订并判断自身更新的前沿模型。本文研究在资源受限条件下，冻结的小语言模型能否作为有效的自我进化智能体。

Method: 提出PACE（Prompt And Control Logic Evolution）两时间尺度框架：低风险提示精炼（在固定控制逻辑下持续优化提示直至收益饱和）与高风险控制逻辑更新（通过留出验证集约束的受控更新）。使用三个冻结SLM骨干（4B-14B参数）在四个受控基准上评估。

Result: 在全部12个骨干网络-基准组合上取得最佳性能，相比原始SLM智能体相对提升最高+9.2%，相比更强的单模式进化基线相对提升最高+5.4%。tau-bench案例研究显示PACE在多轮工具使用成功率上优于原始和仅提示进化方法。

Abstract: Deploying language-model agents in production often requires substantial compute and human effort to tune prompts, parsers, validators, and other components of the agent pipeline. Self-evolution offers a promising alternative, but most existing frameworks assume access to frontier models that can reliably diagnose failures, propose revisions, and judge their own updates. We study whether frozen small language models (SLMs) can serve as effective self-evolving agents under resource constraints. We propose PACE (Prompt And Control Logic Evolution), a two-timescale framework that coordinates low-risk prompt refinement with higher-risk control-logic updates. PACE evolves prompts under fixed control logic until prompt-level gains saturate, then considers constrained control-logic updates that are accepted through held-out validation. Across three frozen SLM backbones ranging from 4B to 14B parameters and four controlled benchmarks, PACE achieves the best performance on all 12 backbone--benchmark combinations, improving over vanilla SLM agents by up to +9.2% relative improvement and over the stronger single-mode evolution baseline by up to +5.4% relative improvement. A tau-bench case study further shows that PACE improves multi-turn tool-use success over vanilla and prompt-only evolution. These results suggest that reliable SLM agent self-evolution is possible without updating model weights or relying on frontier-model teachers, and that the key benefit is not any single final solver pattern but autonomous, validated discovery of task-appropriate inference strategies.

</details>

Conclusion: World Machine作为一种生成式世界建模架构，通过潜在状态机制有效解决了传统Transformer在时间序列建模中的计算瓶颈，为构建更高效、可泛化的世界模型提供了新方向。

Abstract: World models represent a paradigm shift in generative AI, pursuing predictive understanding and controllable simulation of environments in a structured and generalizable way. We present World Machine, a generative world-modeling architecture for time series. It is a transformer-based architecture with latent states that enables adaptation to different amounts of observed data and contexts. This shows an improvement over traditional transformers, which have a computational and memory cost that scales quadratically with the context. Experiments on a proposed synthetic dataset, Toy1D, validate the approach's feasibility, demonstrate capabilities not found in conventional transformers, and highlight the contributions of each component of the training protocol.

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RADAR指标，通过分析基础模型中表示空间的层间几何演化（角度对齐和距离变化）来估计跨域迁移性，在多模态任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强策略可能导致负迁移，缺乏有效评估跨域迁移性的几何度量方法。

Abstract: Machine learning methods rely on data. However, gathering suitable data can be challenging due to availability constraints, cost, or the need for domain expertise. Expanding datasets with additional sources is a common response to limited data, yet this practice does not always improve downstream performance and can sometimes lead to a loss of performance, known as negative transfer. We propose RADAR, a simple, geometrically grounded metric for estimating cross-domain transferability in foundation models. RADAR analyzes the layer-wise evolution of representations by measuring angular alignments and relative changes in distance along layer-to-layer displacement trajectories, and by comparing empirical distributions of within-domain and cross-domain dynamics. We hypothesize that domain transferability is related to the divergence between these trajectory distributions. We evaluate the metric across multiple modalities, including cross-lingual sentiment classification with text embedding models and cross-domain image classification with foundation vision models. Across several settings, RADAR provides competitive predictive performance relative to existing transferability metrics on several vision and text benchmarks, with particularly strong results when domain transitions are smooth or cleanly separated. Our ablations further suggest that the effectiveness of transferability estimation depends on the geometry of the model's internal representation space, with different modalities favoring different topological formulations.

</details>
