Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LOES方法（基于几何和光谱分析的最优嵌入层选择）和GeoReg损失（几何正则化），证明深度模型的不同层以非单调方式分布任务相关信息，通过识别任务判别性子空间并稳定微调过程中的表示几何，可显著提升迁移学习性能，模型越深效果越明显，并支持跨模态可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习实践中普遍仅使用最后一层或浅层混合的嵌入表示进行迁移学习，但本文发现任务相关信息非单调地分布于各层，简单聚合无法有效恢复，因此需要一种系统性的方法来识别哪些层编码了任务判别性结构。

Method: 提出Layer-wise Optimal Embedding Selection (LOES)，一种基于谱分析的构造性方法，通过正交性和各向同性约束下最小化残差误差来识别任务判别性子空间；同时提出Geometric Regularization Loss (GeoReg)，在微调过程中对类别流形施加单纯形结构以稳定表示几何。

Result: 跨多种架构、深度、模态和数据规模，LOES一致优于标准基线，且模型深度越大增益越明显；此外该方法还能揭示语义因子在各层的分布，支持跨语言和跨模态的可解释性分析。

Conclusion: 层间嵌入几何不是深度模型中的偶然现象，而是模型表示与迁移知识的核心机制，本文的方法和结论有力支持了这一观点。

Abstract: Foundational Models pretrained on huge amount of data learn representations that evolve across depth, forming a hierarchy of embeddings with distinct semantic content and geometric structure. Contrary to the widespread practice of using only the final layer or shallow mixtures, we show that task-relevant information is distributed non-monotonically across layers and cannot be recovered by naïve aggregation. Through a geometric and empirical study across multiple modalities, we show that effective transfer depends on identifying which layers encode task-discriminative structure and how their embeddings are geometrically organized. We introduce Layer-wise Optimal Embedding Selection (LOES), a constructive spectral method that identifies task-discriminative subspaces by minimizing residual error under orthogonality and isotropy constraints. To align fine-tuning with this selection principle, we further propose Geometric Regularization Loss (GeoReg), which enforces a simplicial structure on class manifolds and stabilizes representation geometry during fine-tuning. Across a wide range of architectures, depths, modalities, and data regimes, LOES consistently outperforms standard baselines, with gains that grow as model depth increases. Beyond accuracy, our method reveals how semantic factors are distributed across layers, thereby enabling cross-lingual and cross-modal interpretability analyses. Together, our results provide strong evidence that layerwise embedding geometry is not incidental but central to how deep models represent and transfer knowledge.

</details>

Abstract: Data-driven modeling is becoming central to multiphase transport, electronics cooling, acoustic diagnostics, and thermal-fluid digital twins, but progress is limited by fragmented datasets and raw instrument files that are difficult to decode, reuse, or benchmark. This paper presents an open ecosystem of multimodal datasets and open-source software packages developed by the Nano Energy and Data-Driven Discovery (NED3) Laboratory for reproducible AI-enabled thermal-fluid research. We introduce a spatial-plus-temporal dimensionality framework, denoted S+TD, to classify datasets by the dimensionality of measured or simulated fields, including 0+0D point values, 0+1D time series, 1+0D profiles, 2+0D images, 2+1D videos, 3+0D volumetric fields, and multimodal combinations. We organize public NED3 datasets spanning boiling images, acoustic and thermal measurements, high-speed videos, infrared thermography, thermal-resistance measurements, CFD-generated fields, design files, and acoustic-emission data. We also describe complementary software packages, including BubbleID, SeqReg, CFDTwin, IRISApp, decode-wfs, AELab, and FlowLab, which support computer vision, sequence regression, surrogate modeling, infrared analysis, waveform decoding, acoustic-emission analysis, and multimodal diagnostics. Particular emphasis is placed on SeqReg, a general sequence-regression library for 0+1D, 1+1D, and 2+1D data, with applications such as nonintrusive heat-flux estimation. Finally, we discuss future community efforts to build interoperable thermal-fluid databanks and curated AI/ML tool libraries that connect datasets, metadata, decoders, baselines, benchmarks, and physically interpretable models.

</details>

Method: 1) 在注意力查询激活上应用稀疏自编码器（SAE）分解为可解释特征；2) 推理时进行基于梯度的优化，将稀疏表示与目标行为的类原型对齐；3) 在Textualized Gridworld（验证规划约束）和教育领域（控制Bloom认知复杂度）两个场景中验证。

Conclusion: 稀疏查询表征能够实现对逻辑规划和风格细节的统一、可解释控制，验证了注意力查询激活作为高保真引导位点的有效性。

Abstract: Latent steering exploits internal representations of Large Language Models (LLMs) to guide generation, yet interventions on dense states can entangle distinct semantic features. In this paper, we investigate attention query activations as a high-fidelity site for precise control, hypothesizing that manipulating the attention mechanism itself offers sharper steerability than general state interventions. We introduce Prototype-Based Sparse Steering, a framework that applies Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) specifically to query activations, to decompose them into interpretable features, then apply gradient-based optimization during inference to align the sparse representation with class prototypes of target behaviors. To validate this architectural insight, we first analyze the mechanism in Textualized Gridworld, a controlled environment for verifiable planning constraints. We demonstrate that optimizing sparse query features enables effective navigation of rigid planning requirements (i.e., safe vs. short paths), confirming the method's ability to satisfy objective rules. We then demonstrate the framework's versatility by training SAEs on a high-dimensional educational domain, where the framework steers the cognitive complexity of feedback (i.e., Bloom's Taxonomy). Our experiments establish that sparse query representations provide the necessary disentanglement for unified, interpretable control over both logical planning and stylistic nuance.

</details>

*Changling Li,Terry Jingchen Zhang,Jie Zhang,Zhijing Jin,Sahar Abdelnabi,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出评估意识（evaluation awareness）的框架，将其分解为环境组件（任务可识别性）和模型组件（识别与行为倾向），通过8类触发因素和链式思维监控研究9个前沿模型在4个基准测试上的表现。结果表明识别率取决于模型与基准的特定配对，识别很少导致行为改变，且方向取决于评估类型。作者还构建了EvalAwareBench基准（100个安全-能力配对任务），发现叠加因素可逐步提升所有模型的评估意识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型有时能识别自己正在被评估并调整行为，从而破坏基准测试结果的有效性。然而该领域缺乏共同的研究基础，经常混淆评估属性与模型属性，以及检测与行为反应。

Method: 1) 基于社会心理学将评估意识分解为环境组件（8类触发因素）和模型组件（识别与行为倾向）；2) 通过链式思维监控研究识别与行为；3) 构建EvalAwareBench基准，包含100个安全-能力配对任务，独立控制8类因素。

Result: 1) 识别率取决于模型与基准的特定配对，而非任何一方独立决定；2) 识别很少导致行为改变；3) 行为改变方向取决于评估类型；4) 模型对安全评估比能力评估更敏感；5) 无单一因素统一影响所有模型，但叠加因素可逐步提升评估意识。

Conclusion: 提供了衡量、归因和缓解评估意识的工具，指出在识别状态下保持行为一致性是未来有前景的方向。

Abstract: Frontier language models sometimes recognize that they are being evaluated and adjust their behavior, undermining validity of benchmark results. Yet the field studies it without a shared foundation, conflating properties of the evaluation with properties of the model, and detection with behavioral response. We ground evaluation awareness in social psychology, decomposing it into an environment component (how recognizable the task is) and a model component that separates recognition from propensity to act on it. We operationalize the environment component through eight categorized trigger factors, such as placeholder entities and grading-style output formats, and study recognition and behavior through chain-of-thought monitoring. Across nine frontier models and four benchmarks, recognition rates depend on the specific pairing of model and benchmark rather than on either in isolation. Recognition rarely leads to behavioral change, and when it does, the direction depends on the type of evaluation perceived. Models are also more sensitive to safety than capability evaluations, placing safety benchmark validity at greater risk. To study which factors each model is sensitive to and how they interact, we propose \textbf{EvalAwareBench}, a factor-controlled benchmark of 100 paired safety-capability tasks where each of the eight factors can be independently toggled, varying evaluative signals while holding the underlying request fixed. Through EvalAwareBench, we find that no single factor uniformly affects all models, but stacking factors progressively raises evaluation awareness across all of them. Our framework and EvalAwareBench provide the tools to measure, attribute, and mitigate evaluation awareness, pointing to behavioral consistency under recognition as a promising path forward.

</details>


### [43] [ModeSwitch-LLM: A Lightweight Phase-Aware Controller for Cross-Mode LLM Inference on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23057)
*Aman Sunesh,Ali Alshehhi,Hivansh Dhakne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModeSwitch-LLM是一个轻量级请求边界控制器，通过将每个请求路由到合适的固定推理模式（FP16、量化、推测解码、混合模式等），在单GPU大语言模型推理中实现2.10倍平均延迟加速和51.7%更低每token能耗，且精度几乎无损（平均+0.17个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型推理服务通常采用单一静态配置，无法针对不同请求特性动态选择最优推理模式，导致效率低下。作者希望在不重新训练模型或改变架构的前提下，通过简单的请求感知路由来恢复现有推理模式的效率潜力。

Method: 提出ModeSwitch-LLM，一个轻量级请求边界控制器，利用廉价的 workload 级特征（如请求长度、内容特性等），为每个请求动态选择最合适的推理模式。支持的模式包括FP16、量化模式（如GPTQ、INT8）、推测解码以及混合模式（如GPTQ+前缀缓存、INT8+连续批处理）。控制器采用基于规则的在线调度策略，同时对比了轻量级学习路由器的效果。

Result: 在Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct单A100 GPU上，合成工作负载下在线控制器实现2.10倍平均延迟加速、0.48倍平均能耗比（降低51.7%每token能耗）。自动基准测试中精度平均提升0.17个百分点。轻量级学习路由器因路由开销和更频繁违反约束，未明显优于基于规则的控制器。

Conclusion: 简单的请求感知路由可以在不重训练模型或改变架构的前提下，从现有推理模式中恢复可观的效率收益。基于规则的控制器在实际部署中优于学习路由方案，兼顾了延迟、能耗和精度约束。

Abstract: ModeSwitch-LLM is a lightweight request-boundary controller for improving single-GPU large language model inference efficiency by routing each request to an appropriate fixed inference mode. Instead of relying on one static serving configuration, the system selects among FP16, quantized modes, speculative decoding, and hybrid modes such as GPTQ plus prefix caching and INT8 plus continuous batching using cheap workload-level features. We evaluate ModeSwitch-LLM on Meta-Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct served on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU. On deployment-style synthetic workloads, the online controller achieves a 2.10x mean latency speedup over FP16 and a 0.48x mean energy ratio, corresponding to 51.7% lower energy per token. On automatic benchmarks used as a quality gate, accuracy remains close to FP16 with a mean delta of +0.17 percentage points. We also evaluate lightweight learned routers, but find that they do not clearly outperform the rule-based controller because they add routing overhead and more often select modes that violate quality, energy, or memory constraints. These results show that simple request-aware routing can recover substantial efficiency from existing inference modes without retraining the model or changing its architecture.

</details>


### [44] [GEMQ: Global Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23078)
*Jianing Deng,Song Wang,Dongwei Wang,Zijie Liu,Tianlong Chen,Huanrui Yang,Jingtong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GEMQ，一种面向MoE-LLMs的全局专家级混合精度量化方法，通过线性规划建模全局专家重要性和路由器微调来适应量化专家，在极低比特量化下实现精度-内存Pareto最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE-LLMs混合精度量化方法依赖逐层重要性估计，忽视了量化引起的路由器偏移，导致比特分配和路由决策次优。

Method: (1) 基于量化误差分析的全局线性规划建模模型级专家重要性；(2) 高效的路由器微调使路由适应量化专家；两者集成到渐进式量化框架中迭代优化。

Result: 显著降低内存占用并加速推理，精度损失极小。

Conclusion: GEMQ克服了现有方法在专家级重要性估计和路由适应性上的局限，实现了更优的混合精度量化效果。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models (MoE-LLMs) achieve strong performance but incur substantial memory overhead due to massive expert parameters. Mixed-precision quantization mitigates this cost by allocating expert-wise bit-widths based on their importance, approaching the accuracy-memory Pareto frontier and enabling extreme low-bit quantization. However, existing methods rely on layer-wise importance estimation and overlook router shifts induced by quantization, resulting in suboptimal allocation and routing. In this work, we propose Global Expert-level Mixed-precision Quantization (GEMQ) to overcome these limitations via (1) a global linear-programming formulation that captures model-wide expert importance based on quantization error analysis, and (2) efficient router fine-tuning to adapt routing to quantized experts. These components are integrated into a progressive quantization framework that iteratively refines importance estimation and allocation. Experiments demonstrate that GEMQ significantly reduces memory and accelerates inference with minimal accuracy degradation. Source code is available at https://github.com/jndeng/GEMQ .

</details>


### [45] [The Attribution Contract: Feature Attribution for Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23080)
*Giang Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出将分类器时代的特征归因方法直接迁移到生成式语言模型存在概念性局限，提出"归因契约"（Attribution Contract）规范，明确归因声明中需指定解释对象、可归因特征、生成过程假设、固定因素及归因分数，通过自回归与扩散模型案例说明归因方法应作为"方法-契约"配对进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式语言模型中特征的定义存在歧义：自回归模型中早期生成token既是输出又是输入，扩散模型中归因可能指向扩散状态而非下一token。作者认为这不是实现细节，而是分类器时代特征归因直接迁移到生成式语言建模时的概念性局限。

Method: 提出"归因契约"规范，包含五个要素：被解释的输出、可接受归因的特征集、假设的生成过程、固定不变的因素、被归因的模型分数。通过自回归和扩散语言模型作为案例，展示同一归因方法在不同契约下回答不同问题，以及不同归因阶段的信息性与误导性。

Result: 阐明许多关于生成式语言模型特征归因的分歧并非归因算法的差异，而是未明确陈述的解释性契约之间的差异。特征归因方法应作为"方法-契约"配对进行评估。

Conclusion: 特征归因方法在生成式语言模型中必须明确其归因契约，否则容易产生误导；评估时应将方法与契约视为整体，而非孤立地评判归因算法。

Abstract: Feature attribution methods promise to identify which input features matter for a model output. In generative language models, however, it is often unclear what should count as a feature in the first place. In autoregressive language models, earlier generated tokens are both outputs of the model and inputs to later predictions. In diffusion language models, generation proceeds through iterative denoising or unmasking rather than fixed left-to-right prediction, so local explanation may target a state of diffusion rather than a next token. We argue that this ambiguity is not merely an implementation detail, but a conceptual limitation of carrying classifier-era feature attribution directly into generative language modeling. We introduce the Attribution Contract, a specification for feature-attribution claims that names what output is being explained, which features are eligible to receive attribution, what generative process is assumed, what is held fixed, and what model score is being attributed. The contract clarifies why the same attribution method can answer different questions depending on how it is instantiated. We argue that many disagreements about feature attribution in generative language models are not disagreements about attribution algorithms, but about unstated explanatory contracts. Using autoregressive and diffusion language models as case studies, we show when attribution to earlier generated tokens, intermediate states, or denoising stages is informative, when it is misleading, and why feature-attribution methods in generative language models should be evaluated as method-contract pairs.

</details>


### [46] [ThriftAttention: Selective Mixed Precision for Long-Context FP4 Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23081)
*Joe Sharratt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThriftAttention提出了一种混合精度注意力机制，通过快速识别重要的查询-键块对进行FP16计算（仅占5%），其余块使用FP4计算，从而在长上下文场景下达到接近FP16的质量和FP4的推理效率，平均恢复89.1%的FP4到FP16性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Blackwell GPU的块级量化注意力机制在长上下文场景下存在显著的质量下降问题，量化误差的影响高度非均匀且随查询-键交互重要性增加而加剧，需要一种能在保持FP4推理效率的同时接近FP16质量的方法。

Method: 两阶段方法：1) 启发式方法快速选择少量重要的查询-键块对用于FP16精度；2) 选定块以FP16计算，其余块以FP4计算，通过在线softmax将两条路径合并为单一输出。

Result: 仅计算5%的查询-键块为FP16即可平均恢复89.1%的FP4到FP16性能差距，优势随序列长度增长而增加，缓解了长上下文下FP4的系统性质量退化。

Conclusion: ThriftAttention在长上下文基准测试和多种模型家族上验证了混合精度注意力策略的有效性，实现了接近FP16质量与FP4效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Efficient attention algorithms are critical to mitigate the quadratic cost of attention in long-context workloads. Prior work utilises block-scaled quantisation techniques on Blackwell GPUs to move attention computation to 4-bit precision to accelerate inference. However, these techniques result in significant quality degradation in long-context settings. We show that the output impact of quantisation error is highly non-uniform and increases with the importance of each query-key interaction, concentrating functionally relevant error in a small number of attention blocks that contain the most important tokens. We propose ThriftAttention, a low-bit attention variant that delivers near-FP16 long-context quality at FP4 inference efficiency. This approach proceeds in two stages. First, a heuristic rapidly selects a small number of important query-key block pairs for FP16 precision. Second, the selected blocks are computed in FP16 and the remaining blocks in FP4, with both paths merged via online softmax into a single output. We demonstrate across long-context benchmarks and model families that by computing only 5% of query-key blocks in FP16, ThriftAttention recovers on average 89.1% of the FP4-to-FP16 performance gap. We show ThriftAttention's advantage grows with sequence length, mitigating the systematic FP4 quality degradation observed at longer contexts. The code is available at https://github.com/joesharratt1229/ThriftAttention.

</details>


### [47] [The Implicit Bias of Depth: From Neural Collapse to Softmax Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23087)
*Connall Garrod,Jonathan P. Keating,Christos Thrampoulidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度无约束特征模型（UFM）中，未使用L2正则化时，梯度下降和深度如何单独塑造神经网络几何结构。发现深度会引入隐式的低秩偏置，促进softmax编码的涌现，并分析了奇异值排斥动力学以及宽度对秩的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期理论表明神经坍缩（NC）在深层架构中可能是次优的，这被归因于L2正则化带来的显式低秩偏置。本文希望在不使用正则化的情况下，单独研究梯度下降和深度对特征几何的影响，以厘清深度本身是否以及如何产生隐式偏置。

Method: 采用深度无约束特征模型（UFM，等价于具有正交输入的深度线性网络），在不加L2正则化的情况下训练；通过理论分析低秩矩阵在多次乘法中的范数传播效率，研究训练动力学；利用谱初始化分析奇异值之间的早期排斥效应；通过控制网络宽度对比随机初始化下的行为。

Result: 深度诱导了隐式低秩偏置，促使网络收敛到softmax编码（即最大间隔解）；谱初始化下早期奇异值间的排斥驱动了低秩结构的涌现；增加深度缩小了神经坍缩的吸引域；而增加宽度则向相反方向偏置，促进更高秩的解。

Conclusion: 本文首次在深度UFM上（无正则化、多类交叉熵损失）给出了隐式偏置的渐近和动力学刻画，揭示了深度与宽度对秩结构的竞争性影响。

Abstract: Neural collapse (NC) describes the structured geometry that emerges in the features and weights of trained classifiers. Recent theory suggests NC can be suboptimal in deep architectures, attributing this to an explicit low-rank bias from L2 regularization. We study the deep unconstrained feature model (UFM)-equivalent to a deep linear network with orthogonal inputs-trained without regularization, to isolate how gradient descent and depth alone shape NC. We show that depth induces an implicit low-rank bias: low-rank matrices propagate norm more efficiently through successive multiplications, promoting low-rank alternatives to NC. These alternatives, we argue, correspond to softmax codes: max-margin solutions previously found in width-bottlenecked networks. Analyzing training dynamics under spectral initialization, we identify an early-time repulsion among singular values that drives low-rank emergence, and characterize how depth shrinks NC's basin of attraction. Finally, we show that some effects act in the opposite direction: for randomly initialized networks, increasing width biases training toward higher-rank solutions. Our results provide the first asymptotic and dynamic characterization of implicit bias in deep UFMs trained with unregularized multiclass cross-entropy.

</details>


### [48] [Dreaming Smoothly and Sample Efficiently with Gradient Penalized Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23089)
*Romil V. Sonigra,P. R. Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GPLD，一种对DreamerV3潜变量世界模型施加梯度惩罚以鼓励局部平滑过渡动力学学习的方法，在DeepMind Control任务上提升了样本效率，尤其在复杂运动控制任务上表现显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜世界模型（如DreamerV3）未显式强制过渡动力学的局部平滑性，而这种归纳偏置有助于提升学习效率，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 对DreamerV3的后验潜变量分布施加逐行雅可比惩罚，该惩罚可视为离散嵌入状态MDP中有限差分平滑的连续潜变量对应物，并使用Hutchinson风格随机探针高效估计。

Result: 在DeepMind Control本体感受任务上，GPLD提升了整体样本效率，在高复杂度运动环境中增益尤为显著；在更 challenging 的四足机器人任务上，GPLD更早达到高回报行为，并在长期训练中保持更一致的学习效果。

Conclusion: 显式局部平滑正则化是一种简单有效的方法，可用于提升面向平滑连续控制环境的潜世界模型。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning improves sample efficiency by learning a world model. However, existing latent world models such as DreamerV3 do not explicitly enforce local smoothness in their learned transition dynamics, leaving a useful inductive bias for transition dynamics learning unexploited. We propose GPLD, a gradient-penalized latent dynamics regularizer for DreamerV3 that applies a row-wise Jacobian penalty to the posterior latent distribution to encourage locally smooth transition learning. We show that this penalty can be interpreted as the continuous-latent analog of finite-difference smoothing of transition laws in discrete embedded-state MDPs, and estimate it efficiently using Hutchinson-style stochastic probes. Empirically, across DeepMind Control proprioceptive tasks, GPLD improves aggregate sample efficiency, with particularly strong gains on higher-complexity locomotion environments. On more challenging quadruped tasks, GPLD reaches high-return behavior earlier and exhibits more consistent late-stage learning over longer horizons. Explicit local smoothness regularization is a simple and effective way to improve latent world models for smooth continuous control environments. Code for GPLD is available at github.com/romils9/gpld-mbrl .

</details>


### [49] [When Determinants Are Not Enough: Private Rare Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23131)
*Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了Codex如何帮助作者将稀有切换（rare switching）技术适配到差分隐私线性bandit场景。标准行列式更新规则因设计矩阵单调性失效，Codex提出基于广义瑞利商的切换规则，恢复了对数策略更新和置信宽度比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差分隐私线性bandit和RL中，添加高斯噪声破坏了设计矩阵的单调性，导致标准的基于行列式的稀有切换规则分析失效，需要新的切换机制来适配隐私设定。

Method: 提出基于广义瑞利商（generalized Rayleigh quotient）的稀有切换规则，替代传统的行列式更新规则，以恢复对数级别的策略更新次数并维持置信宽度比较。

Result: 新的切换规则成功实现了对数级别的策略更新，并且置信宽度比较仅差一个常数因子，解决了隐私噪声破坏单调性的问题。

Conclusion: Codex辅助生成了有效的解决方案，展示了AI在算法设计中提供insight的潜力；基于广义瑞利商的切换规则是隐私线性bandit中稀有切换的正确适配方式。

Abstract: In this note, I would like to share a small research moment where Codex helped me find the right way to adapt rare switching to the private setting. The standard determinant-based update rule in linear bandits and RL works beautifully because the design matrix grows monotonically. But once Gaussian noise is added for privacy, this monotonicity can fail, and the usual analysis no longer goes through. The key reason is that determinant growth controls volume, while regret analysis needs control of the worst direction. To address this, Codex comes up with a different rare-switching rule based on the generalized Rayleigh quotient, which restores logarithmic policy updates and the desired confidence-width comparison up to a constant factor. I present my manually clean-up version of the proof here as well as some personal reflection on this example.

</details>


### [50] [Archimedean Copula Inference via Taylor-Mode AD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23134)
*Cambridge Yang,Dongdong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: acopula是一个基于JAX的框架，能够处理任意嵌套阿基米德Copula在任意逐变量右删失、任意嵌套树结构下的精确似然计算与参数梯度，通过泰勒模式自动微分的多项式幂运算替代传统手工推导的偏Bell多项式表，在多项式时间内完成计算，并在高维（d=53至d=8000）数据上实现了比现有工具数千倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌套阿基米德Copula工具无法同时处理三个关键需求：生存分析中的任意逐变量右删失、任意嵌套树结构、以及精确参数梯度。已有实现仅限于二元问题、低维（d≤10）情况、两层嵌套或仅限手工推导的Copula嵌套，亟需一个统一的、可扩展的高效框架。

Method: 提出acopula框架，基于JAX实现。核心机制是将泰勒模式自动微分输出进行多项式幂运算，用单一可微计算替代传统逐族手工推导的偏Bell多项式表，从而支持任意用户定义的阿基米德生成元（经典或神经网络），在多项式时间内计算任意删失掩码下的精确嵌套Copula似然和参数梯度。

Result: 通过广泛仿真验证正确性。在四个真实场景中展示效果：(a) MIMIC-IV ICU数据（85229条记录，d=53）的逐变量删失建模；(b) S&P 500日回报率（d=98）的11部门层次模型；(c) 视网膜病变研究中跨十个族（其中五个无先前实现）的无关删失MLE；(d) 在d=35时每密度计算速度比R的nacLL快约650倍，且可二次缩放至d=8000。

Conclusion: acopula提供了首个同时支持任意逐变量删失、任意嵌套树结构和精确梯度计算的嵌套阿基米德Copula框架，在高维和复杂删失场景下显著超越现有工具，且通过可微分编程范式消除了对手工推导的依赖，为Copula建模在生存分析和金融等领域的应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: No existing nested Archimedean copula tool handles all three of (a) arbitrary per-variable (right-)censoring in survival analysis, (b) arbitrary nesting trees, and (c) exact parameter gradients. Existing implementations handle only bivariate problems, low dimensional (i.e., $d \leq 10$) cases, two layers of nesting, or only hand-derived copula nestings. We present \textsc{acopula}, a JAX-native framework that, given any Archimedean generator -- classical or neural -- evaluates exact nested-copula likelihoods and parameter gradients under arbitrary censoring masks in polynomial time. The mechanism is polynomial powering of Taylor-mode automatic differentiation output, which replaces per-family hand-derived partial Bell polynomial tables with a single differentiable computation that any user-defined generator can drive. We conduct extensive simulations to verify the correctness of \textsc{acopula}. We then demonstrate (a) per-variable censoring on $85{,}229$ MIMIC-IV ICU admissions in high dimensions with $d{=}53$, fit by both classical Archimedean families and nested neural Archimedean copulas; (b) an 11-sector hierarchical model on S\&P~500 daily returns at $d{=}98$; (c) family-agnostic censored MLE across ten families, five of them with no prior implementation, on a retinopathy study; and (d) a ${\sim}650\times$ per-density speedup over R's \texttt{nacLL} at $d{=}35$, scaling quadratically to $d{=}8{,}000$.

</details>


### [51] [CALAD: Channel-Aware contrastive Learning for multivariate time series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23139)
*Jaehyeop Hong,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CALAD框架，利用通道相关性估计指导对比学习中的正负样本构建，结合重建头实现多元时间序列异常检测，在多个真实数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督多元时间序列异常检测方法通常平等对待所有通道，导致异常相关信号被稀释，忽略了不同通道对异常检测贡献不均衡的问题。

Method: 1) 使用Transformer自编码器的重建误差估计通道相关性；2) 基于通道相关性设计通道级增强策略，保留异常相关通道、扰动无关通道来构建正负样本；3) 联合对比学习与辅助重建头进行联合优化。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上持续优于现有方法，尤其在分布偏移场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通道感知的对比学习能有效区分异常相关通道与无关通道，从而提升多元时间序列异常检测性能。

Abstract: Multivariate time series anomaly detection has become increasingly important in real-world applications, where labeled data are often scarce. Many existing approaches rely on unsupervised learning to model normal patterns, but they often treat all channels equally. This design can dilute anomaly-relevant signals, since not all channels contribute equally to anomaly detection. In this paper, we propose CALAD, a channel-aware contrastive learning framework for multivariate time series anomaly detection. CALAD governs the construction of contrastive samples using estimated channel relevance, allowing the learning process to reflect anomaly semantics rather than generic similarity. Channel relevance is estimated from reconstruction errors of a transformer-based autoencoder and is used to distinguish channels that are more influential to anomalous behaviors. Using this information, we design a channel-wise augmentation strategy in which positive and negative samples are constructed based on whether anomaly-relevant channels are preserved or perturbed. This encourages invariance to changes in irrelevant channels while being sensitive to changes in anomaly-relevant channels. Furthermore, CALAD combines contrastive learning and an auxiliary reconstruction head, allowing the model to learn discriminative representations while retaining normal structures. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets shows that CALAD consistently outperforms existing methods, particularly under distribution shift scenarios. We provide the code for reproducibility at https://github.com/hirundo1218/CALAD

</details>


### [52] [Infra-Bayesian Reinforcement Learning Agents Outperform Classical RL For Worst-Case Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23146)
*Manish Aryal,Faiyaz Azam,Agnivo Banerjee,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Allegra Laro,Clément Legentilhomme,Andrew Lin,Florian Lorkowski,Radman Rakhshandehroo,Patric Rommel,Emanuel Ruzak,Nathan Theng,Paul Yushin Rapoport*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个infra-Bayesian强化学习架构的验证性实现，用于有限结果的无状态决策问题。该智能体维护一组不精确假设，通过infra-Bayesian条件化更新，并选择最大化最坏情况期望值的动作。在存在Knightian不确定性的环境中，该智能体相比经典强化学习智能体表现出更低的最坏情况遗憾，并在Newcomb问题中选择了最优策略，优于经典决策理论智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习假设环境是固定的且不依赖智能体的策略，但在非可实现环境中（如智能体与预测者、人类、其他AI智能体和机构交互时），该假设不成立。在这种设定下，模型类无法捕捉真实世界，经典的贝叶斯方法会产生自信但错误的 posterior、不可靠的决策和无界遗憾。因此，需要一种能够在模型误规范和策略依赖性不确定性下保持鲁棒的决策框架。

Method: 采用infra-Bayesianism决策理论框架，区分普通概率不确定性（可合理选择先验）和Knightian不确定性（无法构建先验）。智能体维护一组不精确假设（hypotheses set），通过infra-Bayesian conditioning进行更新，并采用最大化最坏情况期望值（maximin）的策略选择动作。该方法应用于有限结果的无状态决策问题。

Result: 在存在Knightian不确定性的环境中，infra-Bayesian智能体的最坏情况遗憾低于经典强化学习智能体。在Newcomb问题中，infra-Bayesian智能体选择了最优策略，优于经典决策理论智能体。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建在模型误规范和策略依赖性不确定性下保持鲁棒的强化学习智能体提供了初步步骤，证明了infra-Bayesian方法在处理非可实现环境中的有效性。

Abstract: Classical reinforcement learning assumes the agent interacts with a fixed environment whose behavior does not depend on the agent's policy. This assumption breaks down in non-realizable settings where other actors might anticipate the agent's behavior, including environments crucial to AI safety, where the agent interacts with predictors, humans, other AI agents, and institutions. In such settings, the agent's model class fails to capture the world in which it operates. Under such misspecification, classical Bayesian methods can produce confidently wrong posteriors, unreliable decisions, and unbounded regret, as realizability fails to obtain. Infra-Bayesianism is a decision-theoretic framework that addresses these failures by distinguishing ordinary probabilistic uncertainty, where priors can be reasonably chosen, from Knightian uncertainty, where no grounds exist for the construction of such a prior. It does so by evaluating actions on their worst-case outcomes, rather than from posterior expectations or weighted averaging. We present the first proof-of-concept implementation of an infra-Bayesian reinforcement learning architecture for finite-outcome stateless decision problems. Our agent maintains a set of imprecise hypotheses, updates them using infra-Bayesian conditioning, and selects actions by maximizing worst-case expected value. We apply this implementation of the infra-Bayesian maximin decision process to an environment with Knightian uncertainty, and demonstrate a lower worst-case regret as compared to classical reinforcement learning agents. We also investigate Newcomb's problem and show that the infra-Bayesian agent picks the optimal strategy, outperforming classical decision theory agents. Our results provide a step towards reinforcement learning agents that remain robust under model misspecification and policy-dependent uncertainty.

</details>


### [53] [Pure Exploration for a Good Policy in Reinforcement Learning with Bandit Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23182)
*Zitian Li,Wang Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 episodic 强化学习中的"好策略识别"(GPI)问题，提出了 BEE-GPI 算法，在固定置信度设置下，该算法能以高概率识别出期望奖励是否达到给定阈值 μ₀ 的策略，并理论分析了其样本复杂度上界，证明了其在正实例情况下与动作和状态空间规模无关，且达到了近最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中通常只需要一个"足够好"的策略而非最优策略，传统最优策略识别(BPI)的样本复杂度依赖于状态和动作空间规模，在复杂环境中成本过高。GPI 旨在降低样本复杂度，只要求判断是否存在期望奖励不低于给定阈值的策略。

Method: 提出了 BEE-GPI 算法，在固定置信度设置下，以概率 ≥ 1-δ 正确输出结果，并最小化期望样本复杂度（探索的 episode 数）。算法针对正实例和负实例分别推导了理论上有保证的样本复杂度上界。

Result: 正实例情况下样本复杂度上界中 log(1/δ) 的系数为 O(H²/(V* - μ₀)²)，其中 H 为 episode 长度，V* 为最优期望奖励，该系数不依赖于动作和状态空间规模，与 BPI 形成鲜明对比。同时建立了下界结果，证明了 BEE-GPI 的近最优性以及 1/(V* - μ₀)² 项的必要性。

Conclusion: BEE-GPI 在好策略识别问题上实现了高效的样本复杂度，数值实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Pure exploration in episodic Reinforcement Learning has primarily focused on Best Policy Identification (BPI), which seeks to identify a (near)-optimal policy with high confidence. Motivated by practical settings where a ``good enough'' policy suffices, we study an alternate objective of Good Policy Identification (GPI). For a given reward threshold $μ_0$, GPI only requires identifying a policy with expected reward in an episode at least $μ_0$ if such a policy exists (positive instance), or declaring None if no such policy exists (negative instance). We formalize GPI under the fixed-confidence setting. We require the output to be correct with probability $\geq 1-δ$, and seek to minimize the expected sample complexity, which is the expected number of episodes explored for the output. We propose a novel algorithm BEE-GPI, and derive theoretically-grounded upper bounds on its sample complexity for positive and negative instances. Notably, for positive instances, the coefficient of $\log 1/δ$ in our upper bound is $O(H^2/(V^* - μ_0)^2)$, where $H$ is the episode length and $V^*$ is the optimal expected reward in an episode. The coefficient does not depend on the action and state space sizes otherwise, in sharp contrast to the sample complexity in BPI. We further establish lower bound results to show the near-optimality of BEE-GPI and the necessity of the $1/(V^* -μ)^2$ term. Numerical experiments further validate the efficiency of our approach.

</details>


### [54] [Empirical Bayes Conformal Prediction for Vision and Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23189)
*Jiapeng Zeng,Yogesh Prabhu,Zhanpeng Zeng,Michael A. Newton,Vikas Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于经验贝叶斯的共形预测框架，利用r值将分数变异性转化为不确定性感知的非一致性分数，在保持目标覆盖的同时减少高方差虚假候选的包含，提升排序稳定性并缩小预测集大小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测使用单一非一致性分数进行排名决策，忽略了分数变异性所蕴含的不确定性信息，导致弱答案可能因单次采样而进入共形集。作者旨在利用变异性区分稳定信号与噪声驱动的波动，从而改进预测集的可靠性。

Method: 提出经验贝叶斯共形预测框架，引入r值作为非一致性分数。r值通过候选潜在分数属于排名靠前组的概率来评估，同时考虑其均值和不确定性。包含闭合形式的Normal-Normal经验贝叶斯估计器和非参数后验采样估计器两种实现方式。

Result: 在图像分类、CLIP多模态基准和LLM任务上，r值共形预测在保持目标覆盖的同时提高了排序稳定性，减少了预测集大小。当变异性具有信息量时表现更优，变异性消失时退化为标准CP行为。

Conclusion: r值共形预测能有效将分数变异性纳入共形预测框架，在温和正则条件下理论上可证明减少高方差虚假候选的包含，是一种更鲁棒的共形预测方法。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) gives distribution-free coverage for modern vision and language models, but it is often forced to make a ranking decision from a single unstable nonconformity score. Standard CP uses one realization, while average-then-calibrate variants smooth multiple realizations into a point estimate. Both options discard the inconsistency that can help identify whether a candidate is indeed stable. A weak answer can enter the conformal set even if the evidence is not strong, simply because one posterior sample or prompt phrasing made it look strong. But variability can help distinguish a stable signal from noise-driven fluctuations. We describe an empirical Bayes conformal prediction framework that uses $r$-values to convert score variability into an uncertainty informed nonconformity score. The resulting $r$-value estimates how likely a candidate's latent score belongs to the top-ranked group after accounting for both its mean score and its uncertainty. It admits both a closed-form Normal-Normal empirical Bayes estimator and a nonparametric posterior-sampling estimator. Using the $r$-value as the nonconformity score preserves the target conformal coverage while provably reducing the inclusion of high variance false candidates under mild regularity conditions. Across image classification, CLIP-based VLM benchmarks, and LLMs, we show that $r$-value conformal prediction preserves target coverage while improving ranking stability and reducing set size when variability is informative, and reverting to CP-like behavior when variability vanishes.

</details>


### [55] [Expand More, Shrink Less: Shaping Effective-Rank Dynamics for Dense Scaling in Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23191)
*Guoming Li,Shangyu Zhang,Junwei Pan,Wentao Ning,Jin Chen,Gengsheng Xue,Chao Zhou,Shudong Huang,Haijie Gu,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对RankMixer模型的嵌入坍塌问题（表示的有效秩低，限制了表达能力），提出RankElastor架构，通过参数化全混合和GLU改进的P-FFN模块，有效缓解嵌入坍塌，提升推荐性能并展现鲁棒扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RankMixer虽在统一token表示下通过token混合和P-FFN实现了可扩展推荐性能，但存在嵌入坍塌问题——学习到的表示有效秩低，限制了表达力并未充分利用扩展的表示空间。

Method: RankElastor引入两个核心组件：(i)参数化全混合（parameterized full mixing），实现更具表达力的token混合并提升谱鲁棒性；(ii)GLU改进的P-FFN（GLU-improved P-FFNs），通过GLU风格的FFN模块稳定表示谱。从经验分析和理论洞见出发，识别出刚性token混合和P-FFN模块是导致有效秩演化的阻尼振荡轨迹的根本原因。

Result: 在大规模工业数据集上的实验表明，RankElastor持续提升推荐性能，有效缓解嵌入坍塌，并展现出鲁棒的扩展行为。

Conclusion: RankElastor通过谱鲁棒的表示设计和嵌入坍塌的理论缓解，为推荐模型的可扩展性提供了一种有效的架构方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Scaling recommendation models is a central challenge in recommender systems. Recently, RankMixer has emerged as an effective solution, operating on a unified token representation and alternating between token mixing and per-token feedforward networks (P-FFNs) to achieve scalable performance. However, RankMixer suffers from \textit{embedding collapse}, where learned representations have low effective rank, limiting expressivity and underutilizing the expanded representation space. Through empirical analysis and theoretical insights, we identify rigid token mixing and P-FFN modules as the primary causes of this phenomenon, jointly inducing a \textbf{damped oscillatory trajectory} in effective-rank evolution across layers. To address it, we propose RankElastor, a novel architecture that produces spectrum-robust representations with provable collapse mitigation. RankElastor introduces two components: (i) \textbf{parameterized full mixing}, which enables expressive token mixing with improved spectral robustness; and (ii) \textbf{GLU-improved P-FFNs}, which stabilize representation spectra through GLU-style FFN modules. Extensive experiments on large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that RankElastor consistently improves recommendation performance, mitigates embedding collapse, and exhibits robust scaling behavior. Code is available at this GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/RankElastor

</details>


### [56] [Scalable Heterogeneous Graph Foundation Models for Data-Driven Optimal Power Flow in Smart Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23194)
*Massimiliano Lupo Pasini,Yijiang Li,Kibaek Kim,Teja Kuruganti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于HydraGNN的可扩展异构图神经网络工作流，用于最优潮流（OPF）的替代建模和OPF图基础模型（GFM）开发。该工作流保留了电网中多种节点和边类型的异构结构，支持分布式预处理、训练、超参数优化和下游微调。在Frontier超级计算机上对300万个异构图实例进行HPO，识别出紧凑模型（约1.6-1.7M参数）；下游实验表明，预训练OPF GFM的微调能提高低数据精度、稳定训练、加速收敛并降低适应成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的OPF近似方法存在三个主要问题：一是许多方法扁平化处理电力网络的异构结构；二是只针对有限数量的电网拓扑；三是缺乏用于图基础模型（GFM）训练的可扩展基础设施。

Method: 提出一个基于HydraGNN的可扩展异构GNN工作流，保留电网中多种节点和边类型（母线、发电机、负荷、并联设备、交流线路、变压器、设备到母线耦合）；支持分布式预处理、训练、HPO和下游微调；使用DeepHyper在Frontier超级计算机上进行HPO，训练包含300万个异构图实例、覆盖10个PGLib-OPF案例（14到13,659个母线）。

Result: HPO识别出紧凑模型（约1.6-1.7M参数）且验证损失最低；下游实验表明，预训练OPF GFM的微调（部分微调或仅头部微调）能提高低数据精度、稳定训练、加速收敛并降低适应成本。

Conclusion: 该异构GNN工作流可有效用于OPF替代建模和GFM开发，预训练GFM的微调策略在数据有限场景下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Fast and reliable optimal power flow (OPF) approximation is essential for reliable smart-grid operation, yet many learning-based surrogates either flatten the native heterogeneous structure of power networks, target a limited set of grid topologies, or lack scalable infrastructure for graph foundation model (GFM) training. This paper presents a scalable heterogeneous graph neural network (GNN) workflow, built on HydraGNN, for data-driven OPF surrogate modeling and OPF-GFM development. The workflow preserves the distinct node and edge types of power grids -- buses, generators, loads, shunts, AC lines, transformers, and device-to-bus couplings -- and supports distributed preprocessing, training, hyperparameter optimization (HPO), and downstream fine-tuning on leadership-class supercomputers. Using three million heterogeneous graph instances spanning ten PGLib-OPF cases, from 14 to 13,659 buses, we conduct DeepHyper-driven HPO on the ORNL Frontier supercomputer. The campaign identifies compact models ($\sim$1.6--1.7M parameters) with the lowest validation losses. Downstream experiments on feasibility classification and N-1 contingency regression show that fine-tuning pretrained OPF GFM improves low-data accuracy, stabilizes training, accelerates convergence, and reduces adaptation cost when partial or head-only fine-tuning is used.

</details>


### [57] [Label-Efficient Dataset Pruning via Semi-Supervised Pseudo-Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23198)
*Yeseul Cho,Baekrok Shin,Changmin Kang,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemiPrune框架利用少量随机标注数据和半监督学习生成伪标签，使现有监督剪枝方法能应用于无标签数据，通过伪标签诱导的训练动态估计样本难度并选择核心集，在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法大多需要全标注数据，而实际场景中无标签数据丰富且标注成本高。已有的无标签剪枝方法依赖预训练模型特征估计样本难度，当目标数据集与预训练分布差异大时不可靠。

Method: 使用少量随机标注子集，通过半监督学习为无标签数据生成伪标签，使需要标签信息的监督剪枝方法能应用于伪标签训练池；从伪标签诱导的训练动态中估计样本难度并选择核心集。

Result: 在领域特定、图像损坏和长尾数据集上达到无标签和标签高效剪枝方法中的最先进性能，在标准基准上也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过直接学习目标数据集，SemiPrune能更好地捕捉目标分布，为难度估计和核心集选择提供更可靠的信号。

Abstract: Dataset pruning reduces the storage and training costs of deep learning by selecting an informative subset from a large dataset. However, most existing pruning methods require fully labeled data, which limits their applicability in realistic settings where unlabeled data are abundant and annotation is costly. Recent label-free pruning methods address this issue, but they rely on features from pretrained models to estimate example difficulty. This dependence can be unreliable when the target dataset differs substantially from the pretraining distribution. We propose SemiPrune, a label-efficient dataset pruning framework, using only a small randomly labeled subset, that uses semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled data, allowing existing supervised pruning methods that require label information to be seamlessly applied to the resulting pseudo-labeled training pool. We then estimate example difficulty from pseudo-label-induced training dynamics and select a coreset. By learning directly from the target dataset, our method better captures the target distribution and provides more reliable signals for difficulty estimation and coreset selection. We validate our approach on domain-specific, image-corrupted, and long-tailed datasets, where it achieves state-of-the-art performance among label-free and label-efficient baselines, while also demonstrating competitive performance on standard benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [Adaptive Mass-Segmented KV Compression for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23200)
*Junzhe Yang,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AMS KV压缩通过区域感知配额分配替代全局Top-k选择，解决长上下文LLM推理中KV缓存线性增长导致的结构性推理片段被过度驱逐（Region Wipe-out）问题，是一种即插即用、与现有评分方法和vLLM兼容的通用框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV压缩方法依赖全局Top-k选择会触发Region Wipe-out，即连续推理块被严重驱逐，破坏逻辑连贯性。

Method: 提出Adaptive Mass-Segmented (AMS) KV Compression，将范式从token级竞争转向区域感知配额分配：根据注意力质量的空间分布自适应划分KV缓存，确保关键推理段获得保证内存配额；采用EMA平滑机制防止迭代解码中分段边界抖动；是一种与现有评分器正交的即插即用层，兼容vLLM等分页KV服务框架。

Result: 在数学推理（MATH500、AIME、GSM8K）、代码补全、开放域QA和稀疏检索等任务上，AMS一致缓解了结构性碎片化并提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: AMS通过区域配额分配机制有效解决了全局Top-k选择导致的结构片段驱逐问题，可作为通用插件集成到多种现有KV压缩方法中，且系统开销低。

Abstract: The linear growth of the Key-Value (KV) cache is a critical bottleneck in long-form LLM inference. Existing KV compression methods mitigate this by evicting tokens based on importance scores. However, we show that their reliance on global Top-k selection triggers Region Wipe-out: the severe eviction of contiguous reasoning blocks that derails logical coherence. To address this, we propose Adaptive Mass-Segmented (AMS) KV Compression, a framework that shifts the paradigm from token-level competition to region-aware quota allocation. AMS adaptively partitions the KV cache based on the spatial distribution of attention mass, ensuring structurally vital reasoning segments receive guaranteed memory quotas. To ensure stability during iterative decoding, an EMA-based smoothing mechanism is incorporated to prevent jitter in segment boundaries. Crucially, AMS is a universal plug-and-play layer that is orthogonal to existing scorers. It can be seamlessly integrated into representative methods such as TOVA, Expected Attention, KeyDiff, R-KV and TriAttention. AMS is also system-compatible with modern paged-KV serving frameworks such as vLLM, supporting efficient gather-and-compact KV execution without introducing additional steady-state attention overhead. Extensive experiments across a diverse suite of tasks, including mathematical reasoning (MATH500, AIME, GSM8K), code completion, open-domain QA, and sparse retrieval, demonstrate that AMS consistently mitigates structural fragmentation and boosts model performance.

</details>


### [59] [FastKernels: Benchmarking GPU Kernel Generation in Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23215)
*Gabriele Oliaro,Yichao Fu,May Jiang,Owen Lu,Junli Wang,Zhihao Jia,Hao Zhang,Samyam Rajbhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FastKernels，一个由46个代表性架构组成的GPU kernel基准测试，它同时也是一个生产级推理框架。该框架揭示了现有基准与生产环境之间的严重脱节：最强的kernel agent在FastKernels上仅达到生产基线的0.94倍加速，而现有基准给出的高分无法转化为实际生产收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU kernel基准测试与生产推理框架严重脱节：它们在单GPU上使用合成输入评估kernel，忽略编译堆栈，奖励复现已知优化而非发现新优化。这导致agent学会生成在沙盒中得分高但与实际系统集成时出现接口不兼容、编译冲突和正确性退化问题的kernel。

Method: 构建了一个包含46个代表性架构（覆盖8个类别）的最小化基准测试，这些架构的kernel覆盖了HuggingFace Transformers中96.2%的架构。同时FastKernels本身也是一个轻量级生产级推理框架，每个任务的接口与相应架构族的最先进库对齐，可直接将优化kernel部署到生产代码中。

Result: 最强的kernel agent在FastKernels上仅达到0.94倍聚合加速比，较弱的agent分别为0.78倍和0.53倍，远低于现有基准所声称的性能。FastKernels在主流LLM服务上与vLLM和SGLang等成熟系统持平，在欠服务架构上大幅超越上游参考实现。

Conclusion: 基准-生产之间的不对齐是当前领域的关键瓶颈。FastKernels旨在成为桥梁，使kernel agent的基准收益能够直接转化为生产吞吐量的实际提升。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for GPU kernel generation are advancing rapidly, yet their progress is fundamentally constrained by the benchmarks they optimize against. Existing benchmarks are poorly aligned with production inference frameworks: they evaluate kernels on a single GPU with synthetic inputs, ignore the surrounding compilation stack, and reward replicating known optimizations rather than discovering new ones. The resulting reward signals are misleading: agents learn to generate kernels that score well in sandboxes but introduce interface incompatibilities, compilation-stack conflicts, and silent correctness degradation when integrated into real systems. We introduce FastKernels, a kernel benchmark built around a minimal set of 46 representative architectures spanning 8 categories, whose kernels collectively subsume those of 96.2% (409/425) of HuggingFace Transformers architectures. FastKernels doubles as a minimalistic, production-grade inference framework that runs at parity with hardened systems such as vLLM and SGLang on mainstream LLM serving and substantially exceeds upstream references on under-served architectures; each task's interface mirrors the corresponding module in the state-of-the-art library for its architecture family, enabling direct deployment of optimized kernels into production codebases. Evaluating state-of-the-art kernel agents on FastKernels, we find that even the strongest agent achieves only 0.94$\times$ aggregate speedup over production baselines, with weaker agents at $0.78\times$ and $0.53\times$ -- confirming that benchmark-production misalignment is a critical bottleneck for the field. We release FastKernels as a stepping stone toward kernel agents whose benchmark gains translate directly into production throughput improvements. Code is available at https://github.com/Snowflake-AI-Research/fastkernels

</details>


### [60] [PaP-NF: Probabilistic Long-Term Time Series Forecasting via Prefix-as-Prompt Reprogramming and Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23219)
*Minju Kim,Youngbum Hur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PaP-NF框架，通过Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与大语言模型对齐，并利用归一化流解码器进行概率预测，在长期预测基准上有效捕捉多模态不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中的时间序列表现出固有的不确定性和复杂未来行为，现有确定性单点预测方法不足以量化不确定性，亟需概率预测方法。

Method: 采用Prefix-as-Prompt机制将连续时间序列表示与冻结的大语言模型对齐，并以LLM提取的全局上下文为条件，驱动归一化流解码器生成预测分布。

Result: 在多个长期预测基准上，PaP-NF稳健地捕捉多模态不确定性，同时保持具有竞争力的点预测精度。

Conclusion: PaP-NF通过结合LLM的语义理解能力与归一化流的分布建模能力，在概率时间序列预测任务中实现了不确定性量化与点预测性能的平衡。

Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a central role in many real-world applications and has been extensively studied. Most existing approaches rely on deterministic models. However, real-world environments exhibit inherently uncertain and complex future behaviors, making single-point predictions insufficient. This highlights the need for probabilistic forecasting methods that can quantify and represent uncertainty. In this work, we propose PaP-NF, a probabilistic forecasting framework that aligns continuous time series representations with a frozen large language model (LLM) using a Prefix-as-Prompt mechanism, and conditions a normalizing flow decoder on the global context extracted by the LLM. The quality of the resulting predictive distributions is evaluated using the Continuous Ranked Probability Score (CRPS), a standard metric in probabilistic forecasting. Across a variety of long-term forecasting benchmarks, PaP-NF robustly captures multi-modal uncertainty while maintaining competitive point forecasting accuracy. The official implementation is available at: https://github.com/democracy04/PaP-NF

</details>


### [61] [WMAttack: Automated Attack Search for Adversarial Evaluation of World-Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23220)
*Zhixiang Guo,Siyuan Liang,Shi Fu,Cheng Guo,Andras Balogh,Mark Jelasity,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WMAttack，一个针对世界模型智能体对抗鲁棒性的自动化攻击搜索框架。它通过自校正攻击搜索（SCAS）和表征引导攻击检索（RGAR）两个模块，在有限预算下高效发现强攻击。实验表明，在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack显著优于基线方法，归一化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034（DreamerV3 Atari）和从0.319提升至0.682（DMC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型作为决策智能体日益普及，但其对抗鲁棒性因缺乏专用自动化评估方法而研究不足。手动调参攻击可能高估鲁棒性，而穷举超参数搜索因需闭环滚动生成而计算成本过高。

Method: WMAttack将鲁棒性评估形式化为有限预算的对抗配置搜索问题，包含攻击家族、扰动预算、优化步数、重启次数和分配规则等维度。SCAS利用奖励下降、动作不稳定性、运行时成本和滚动变异性等反馈，迭代优化攻击提议分布；RGAR从表征相似任务中检索历史有效配置，为未见环境提供热启动。理论证明提议细化在有限预算搜索中通过将概率质量向高效用攻击转移来提升效果。

Result: 在Atari和DeepMind Control任务上，WMAttack持续发现比基线更强的攻击：DreamerV3 Atari归一化奖励下降从0.497提升至1.034，DMC从0.319提升至0.682。消融实验表明RGAR提升初始候选质量，SCAS在固定评估预算下提升最终攻击效用。

Conclusion: WMAttack为世界模型智能体提供了自动化、准确且高效的对抗鲁棒性评估方法，解决了手动调参不准确和穷举搜索成本过高的关键矛盾。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of world models as decision-making agents, their adversarial robustness remains underexplored due to the lack of dedicated automated evaluation methods. A key obstacle is that attack evaluation must be both accurate and efficient: weak manually tuned attacks can overestimate robustness, while exhaustive hyperparameter search is prohibitively expensive because each candidate requires closed-loop rollouts through learned latent dynamics. We introduce WMAttack, an automated attack-search framework for adversarial evaluation of world-model agents. WMAttack formulates robustness evaluation as a finite-budget search over attack configurations, including attack families, perturbation budgets, optimization steps, restarts, and allocation rules. To improve search accuracy, Self-Correcting Attack Search (SCAS) refines the attack proposal distribution using feedback from reward degradation, action instability, runtime cost, and rollout variability. To improve search efficiency, Representation-Guided Attack Retrieval (RGAR) retrieves effective historical configurations from representation-similar tasks, providing a warm start for unseen environments. We provide a theoretical explanation showing that proposal refinement improves finite-budget search when it shifts probability mass toward high-utility attacks. Across Atari and DeepMind Control tasks, WMAttack consistently discovers stronger attacks than the evaluated baselines, improving normalized reward drop from 0.497 to 1.034 on DreamerV3 Atari and from 0.319 to 0.682 on DMC. Ablations further show that RGAR improves initial candidate quality and SCAS improves final attack utility under fixed evaluation budgets.

</details>


### [62] [Enhancing Deep Neural Network Reliability with Refinement and Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23249)
*Ramya Hebbalaguppe,Ajay Shastry,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Chetan Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RefCal框架，通过联合优化校准、精炼（sharpness）和准确率，解决现有校准方法牺牲精炼度的问题，在CIFAR-100-LT上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有校准方法（如后处理技术）虽能改善置信度校准，但往往以降低精炼度（即模型对正确与错误预测的区分能力）为代价，导致模型可靠性提升有限。

Method: 提出一种通过监督对比学习显式提升精炼度的损失函数，并构建联合训练框架RefCal，同时优化准确率、校准误差（ECE）和精炼度。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-LT（10%不平衡率）上，RefCal取得(准确率58.81, 精炼度95.67, ECE 0.08)，全面优于Correctness Ranking Loss的(46.27, 93.7, 0.22)。

Conclusion: 联合优化校准与精炼是提升DNN可靠性的有效路径，监督对比学习可用于显式提升精炼度。

Abstract: Although deep neural networks (DNNs) achieve high predictive accuracy, their confidence estimates are often unreliable, potentially compromising user trust in their decisions. This has motivated research on calibrated models, where calibration measures how well a model's predicted confidence aligns with the empirical probability of correctness. However, calibration metrics can often be improved through post-processing techniques that merely mimic training-time uncertainty without genuinely improving the model's understanding. For this reason, statisticians recommend that models be not only calibrated but also refined. Intuitively, a model is considered more refined if it assigns significantly different confidence scores to correct and incorrect predictions, a property also referred to as sharpness. We observe that many existing calibration methods improve calibration at the cost of reduced refinement. To address this limitation, we propose: (1) a novel loss function that explicitly promotes refinement and can be optimized through supervised contrastive learning; and (2) a unified training framework, RefCal, that jointly optimizes calibration, refinement, and accuracy to improve DNN reliability. On the CIFAR-100-LT dataset with 10 percent class imbalance, RefCal achieves (accuracy, refinement, ECE) of (58.81, 95.67, 0.08), substantially outperforming the widely used Correctness Ranking Loss, which achieves (46.27, 93.7, 0.22).

</details>


### [63] [Assessing Predictive Models for Fairness Based on Movement Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23234)
*Francesco Lettich,Mario A. Nascimento,Chiara Pugliese,Chiara Renso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将空间公平性从单一居住地扩展到个体移动轨迹，并设计了一种基于多分辨率空间分区和扫描统计的方法来检测预测模型对移动模式的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间公平性研究假设个体仅关联单一地理位置（如居住地），但个体访问过的多个位置（移动模式）同样可能受到预测模型的不公平对待，因此需要将空间公平性概念泛化到包含移动轨迹。

Method: 首先将个体移动轨迹关联到不同分辨率和对齐方式的空间分区，然后采用合适的空间扫描统计量来评估预测模型是否基于移动模式存在不公平性。

Result: 在数千个合成不公平数据集上的实验表明，该方法能有效检测这种新类型的不公平性，并能检索出受到不公平对待的个体集合，但定位性能存在一致的多分辨率权衡。

Conclusion: 将空间公平性扩展到移动模式是必要且有意义的，提出的方法能够有效检测基于移动轨迹的模型不公平性，但多分辨率的选择需要在检测精度和定位精度之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Assessing the spatial fairness of predictive models involves establishing whether they are statistically penalizing (favoring) individuals associated with certain geographical locations. Literature on this topic makes the fundamental assumption that each individual is assigned to a single geographical location (e.g., place of residence). However, fairness with respect to the set of locations where one has been, i.e., their movement patterns over different regions, also matters when fairness is considered. Consequently, we argue that it is necessary to generalize the notion of spatial fairness to also include movement patterns, leading to the novel problem of assessing predictive models for fairness relative to the movements of individuals. To deal with this problem, we propose an approach that first associates the movements of individuals to certain geographic regions, considering multiple spatial partitions with different resolutions and alignments, and then employs a suitable spatial scan statistic to assess whether a predictive model is fair based on movement patterns. In the experimental evaluation, we study the performance of our approach over thousands of synthetic unfair datasets, showing that it is effective at detecting this new type of unfairness and at retrieving the set of objects treated unfairly, while localization performance exhibits a consistent multi-resolution trade-off.

</details>


### [64] [Multi-Gate Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23259)
*Zhizhan Zheng,Feiyun Zhang,Shuchun Liu,Tian Xia,Xi Liu,Dasheng Hu,Hongquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Multi-Gate Residuals (MGR)，通过简单的评分和门控机制稳定激活尺度，无需额外通信开销，在大型模型训练和部署中优于现有架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Attention Residuals虽能缓解深层残差网络中激活值无界增长的问题，但带来了显著的通信开销，亟需一种既能稳定激活尺度又不增加通信负担的方法。

Method: 提出Multi-Gate Residuals (MGR)，利用简单的评分和门控机制维持多流上下文，并结合注意力池化从流状态中提取隐藏状态。

Result: 实验证明MGR适用于大规模训练和部署，在性能上优于现有架构。

Conclusion: MGR在实际的大规模训练和部署中是可行的，能够在不增加通信开销的前提下提升模型性能。

Abstract: While Attention Residuals has shown some effectiveness in addressing the widespread issue of unbounded activation growth across deep residual layers, it inevitably incurs significant communication overhead. To circumvent this bottleneck, we propose Multi-Gate Residuals (MGR), which stabilizes activation scales without additional communication burden. It utilizes a straightforward scoring and gating mechanism to maintain multi-stream context, coupled with Attention Pooling to extract hidden states from the stream states. Empirical experiments demonstrate that MGR is practical for large-scale training and deployment, offering tangible performance improvements over existing architectures.

</details>


### [65] [Convex Low-resource Accent-Robust Language Detection in Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23235)
*Miria Feng,William Tan,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Convex Language Detection (CLD)框架，利用凸优化技术（ADMM在JAX中实现）解决低资源方言和口音下的口语对话系统语言检测问题，在低资源场景下达到97-98%准确率，并提供全局最优性保证和特征扰动鲁棒性理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语对话系统在处理低资源方言和口音时频繁失败，常误识别输入语言导致下游级联故障；标准微调计算成本高且在高维语音数据上容易过拟合。

Method: 提出Convex Language Detection (CLD)框架，将凸优化技术集成到口语对话系统管道中；通过多GPU ADMM算法在JAX中高效实现，提供全局最优性保证和多项式时间快速训练。

Result: 在低资源场景下达到97-98%准确率，具有样本效率和输入方言变异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CLD框架在理论上证明了凸目标函数可诱导认证边界稳定性并提供特征扰动保证，在实证上对低资源方言和口音具有高效性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Globalization and multiculturalism continue to produce increasingly diverse speech varieties. Yet current spoken dialogue systems frequently fail on under-represented dialects and accents, often misidentifying the input language and causing cascading failures in downstream dialogue tasks. Addressing this dialectal variance under low-resource constraints remains an open challenge, as standard fine-tuning is computationally expensive and prone to overfitting on high-dimensional speech data. We propose Convex Language Detection (CLD), a novel framework that integrates theoretically grounded convex optimization techniques into the spoken dialogue systems pipeline. Our method is efficiently implemented via multi-GPU Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) in JAX, thus providing global optimality guarantees and fast training in polynomial time. Theoretically, we prove that our convex objective induces certified margin stability and provide guarantees against feature perturbations. Empirically, we demonstrate sample efficiency and robustness to input dialectical variation, achieving 97-98% accuracy in challenging low-resource regimes. Our open-source package is available at https://pypi.org/project/jaxcld/

</details>


### [66] [When Good Equations Get Bad Scores: Improving Symbolic Regression Through Better Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23272)
*Boxiao Wang,Kai Li,Zhiwei Chen,Yang Huang,Runxiang Wang,Ziwen Zhang,Yifan Zhang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE-Fit框架，利用符号表达式的结构和语义先验来解决符号回归中“好结构、差分数”的优化瓶颈，作为即插即用模块显著提升评估保真度和各类SR系统的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归中非线性算子导致内循环参数优化高度非凸，依赖快速局部求解器（如BFGS）容易陷入不良局部最小值，使得正确结构获得低估的分数，即“好结构、差分数”现象，严重降低搜索效率和误导外循环搜索方向。

Method: 提出SAGE-Fit框架，利用符号表达式的双重原生先验（结构先验和语义先验），针对每种属性设计定制化模块，从而缓解参数优化中的非凸瓶颈。

Result: 作为即插即用模块，SAGE-Fit显著提升了评估保真度，并在多种符号回归系统上实现了普适性的性能提升。

Conclusion: SAGE-Fit有效解决了符号回归中因参数优化不佳导致的结构误判问题，为提升SR系统的搜索效率和发现正确方程的能力提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Symbolic Regression (SR) plays a central role in scientific knowledge discovery by distilling mathematical equations from observational data. Most existing SR methods function within a bi-level optimization framework: an outer loop that searches for the discrete equation structure, and an inner loop that optimizes the continuous parameters of that structure. Crucially, parameter-fitting quality directly determines a structure's score and thus the outer-loop search. However, nonlinear operators make the inner loop highly non-convex, and budget-driven reliance on fast local solvers (e.g., BFGS) often yields poor local minima and underestimated scores for correct structures. This ``Good Structure, Bad Score'' phenomenon becomes a key bottleneck, degrading efficiency and misguiding the search away from the true equation. To resolve this, we propose SAGE-Fit (Structure-Aware and Semantics-Guided Evaluator for Symbolic Regression), an SR-native fitting framework that exploits the dual native priors of symbolic expressions. By capitalizing on the structural and semantic priors unique to SR, we design tailored modules for each property, thereby effectively mitigating this optimization bottleneck. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach, as a plug-and-play module, significantly enhances evaluation fidelity and universally improves the performance of various SR systems.

</details>


### [67] [Self-supervised Adversarial Purification for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23239)
*Woohyun Lee,Hogun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GPR-GAE，一种基于自监督图自编码器的对抗净化框架，通过多步净化过程从图结构中分离鲁棒性，实现即插即用的GNN防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗训练方法将准确性与鲁棒性纠缠在同一分类器中，难以平衡两者间的权衡。

Method: 设计GPR-GAE图自编码器作为专用净化器，采用自监督训练策略，利用多个广义PageRank滤波器捕获多样化结构表示，并通过多步净化实现精确图恢复。

Result: 在多种数据集和攻击场景下达到最先进的鲁棒性，可作为独立即插即用的净化器。

Conclusion: GPR-GAE有效分离了鲁棒性与分类器，通过自适应图结构净化实现对抗防御，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Defending Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) against adversarial attacks requires balancing accuracy and robustness, a trade-off often mishandled by traditional methods like adversarial training that intertwine these conflicting objectives within a single classifier. To overcome this limitation, we propose a self-supervised adversarial purification framework. We separate robustness from the classifier by introducing a dedicated purifier, which cleanses the input data before classification. In contrast to prior adversarial purification methods, we propose GPR-GAE, a novel graph auto-encoder (GAE), as a specialized purifier trained with a self-supervised strategy, adapting to diverse graph structures in a data-driven manner. Utilizing multiple Generalized PageRank (GPR) filters, GPR-GAE captures diverse structural representations for robust and effective purification. Our multi-step purification process further facilitates GPR-GAE to achieve precise graph recovery and robust defense against structural perturbations. Experiments across diverse datasets and attack scenarios demonstrate the state-of-the-art robustness of GPR-GAE, showcasing it as an independent plug-and-play purifier for GNN classifiers. Our code can be found at https://github.com/woodavid31/GPR-GAE.

</details>


### [68] [Reinforcement Learning for Microcanonical Graph Ensemble with Assortativity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23285)
*Hoyun Choi,Junghyo Jo,Deok-Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出深度微正则图生成器（DMGG），一种基于强化学习的框架，通过保度重连精确调控图的同配性，相比传统指数随机图模型（ERGM）无需参数调优且生成速度快一个数量级，同时保持构型多样性，为硬约束图生成提供了实用新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络结构如何决定功能是根本问题，但现有方法存在局限：规范系综（ERGM）仅期望约束，允许波动；微正则系综可实现硬约束，但实际采样方法局限于度序列固定，无法有效约束更复杂的结构属性（如同配性）。

Method: 提出深度微正则图生成器（DMGG），利用强化学习框架，通过保度重连操作将任意图精确变换到指定同配性。采用策略引导搜索（而非ERGM的Metropolis-Hastings熵主导动力学），最大程度改变联合度矩阵，无需穷举参数调优。

Result: DMGG生成速度比ERGM快至少一个数量级，保持构型多样性，可泛化至不同图规模、稀疏度和拓扑结构，提供精确零模型以定量分离聚类系数等次级可观测量。

Conclusion: 建立了强化学习作为生成硬约束图的实用且强大范式，为研究结构-功能关系提供了免于系综假象的精确方法。

Abstract: How network structure determines function is a fundamental question, and it can be investigated by graph ensembles with precisely controlled structural properties. Canonical approaches, formulated as exponential random graph models (ERGMs), enforce constraints only in expectation, allowing individual realizations to fluctuate around the target. Conversely, microcanonical ensembles impose hard constraints exactly, but practical sampling methods beyond fixing the degree sequence have remained out of reach. Here we introduce the Deep Microcanonical Graph Generator (DMGG), a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that transforms any given graph through degree-preserving rewirings to exactly reach a prescribed assortativity, which characterizes the degree--degree correlation of adjacent nodes. Instead of relying on the entropically dominated Metropolis--Hastings dynamics of the ERGM, DMGG employs a policy-guided search that maximally alters the joint-degree matrix. This eliminates exhaustive parameter tuning and accelerates generation by at least an order of magnitude while preserving configurational diversity. As DMGG generalizes across various graph sizes, sparsities, and topologies, it provides exact null models that allow for the quantitative isolation of secondary observables, such as the clustering coefficient. These results establish RL as a practical and powerful paradigm for generating hard-constrained graphs, opening avenues to investigate structure-function relationships free from ensemble artifacts.

</details>


### [69] [RelPrism: A Multi-Faceted Pre-training Framework with Self-Generated Tasks for Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23241)
*Jinyu Yang,Cheng Yang,Junze Chen,Zedi Liu,Muhan Zhang,Hanyang Peng,Chuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RelPrism是一个面向关系数据库的多面自监督学习框架，通过构建内在、关系和混合三种视角的属性，并应用多粒度聚类形成伪任务池进行预训练，在分类和回归任务上分别提升了4.15%的ROC-AUC和降低了10.75%的MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关系数据库自监督学习方法通常仅从单一视角（如节点级内在属性或子图级关系结构）派生监督信号，适应性有限。然而RDB任务需要跨不同视角和粒度的多方面信息，现有方法无法充分覆盖下游任务所需的综合信息。

Method: RelPrism从不同视角构建内在属性、关系属性和混合属性，对每种属性应用多粒度聚类形成对应的伪任务池，通过在多个伪任务池上进行预训练，使表示学习能够暴露于更广泛的视角和粒度层次。

Result: 在5个真实数据集的14个任务上，RelPrism在分类任务中提升ROC-AUC 4.15%，在回归任务中降低MAE 10.75%，超越了当前最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 多面自监督预训练能够为关系数据库的下游任务提供更强的表示基础，有效解决了异构任务对多视角多粒度信息的需求问题。

Abstract: Relational databases (RDBs) remain the cornerstone of modern data systems and support diverse predictive tasks. Recent relational deep learning (RDL) methods enable end-to-end prediction by converting RDBs into graphs, where rows are represented as nodes and inter-table interactions are represented as edges, and then applying graph-based models for representation learning. Despite the strong capability of RDL, effective self-supervised pre-training for RDBs remains non-trivial. RDB tasks often require multi-faceted information across different perspectives and granularities. For example, user churn classification may rely more on interaction patterns, whereas consumption value prediction requires both user-item behaviors and intrinsic user attributes for fine-grained regression. Such heterogeneous needs challenge RDB representation learning, as pre-training objectives should cover comprehensive information for downstream adaptation. However, existing SSL methods typically derive supervision from a single facet, such as node-level intrinsic attributes or subgraph-level relational structures, providing limited adaptability. To this end, we propose RelPrism, a multi-faceted self-supervised learning framework for RDBs. RelPrism constructs intrinsic, relational, and hybrid attributes from distinct perspectives, and applies multi-granularity clustering to each perspective to form corresponding pseudo-task pools. Pre-training over these pools exposes representations to broader perspectives and granularity levels, yielding a stronger basis for downstream adaptation. Experiments on 14 tasks across 5 real-world datasets show that RelPrism improves ROC-AUC by 4.15% for classification and reduces MAE by 10.75% for regression over state-of-the-art baselines. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RelPrism.

</details>


### [70] [Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23365)
*Kyungyoon Kim,Donghyeon Ki,Hee-Jun Ahn,Byung-Jun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SOM算法，通过从Q函数直接构造目标速度场，实现单步生成的在线强化学习策略，在运动控制任务上达到SOTA性能并大幅降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型和流匹配策略在RL中虽有效但多步去噪推理成本高，MeanFlow虽能单步生成但需要目标分布样本（在线RL中不可得），因此需要一种无需目标分布样本的单步生成策略。

Method: 基于Actor-Critic框架，通过分数估计和概率流ODE从Q函数直接构造目标速度场，从而将概率质量集中到高值模态上。

Result: 在完全在线RL设置下，SOM在运动控制任务上达到SOTA性能，同时相比先前基于扩散和流匹配的策略大幅减少训练和推理时间。

Conclusion: SOM有效解决了MeanFlow在在线RL中无法获取目标分布样本的局限，实现了高效的单步生成策略，为扩散/流匹配策略在RL中的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow matching have emerged as expressive policy classes in reinforcement learning, but their reliance on multi-step denoising imposes substantial computational overhead at inference time, which is particularly problematic in online RL. MeanFlow offers a promising alternative by learning an average velocity field that maps noise to data in a single network evaluation. However, MeanFlow typically requires samples from the target distribution to construct its target velocity field, which are unavailable in online RL. We propose Score-Based One-step MeanFlow Policy Optimization (SOM), an actor-critic algorithm that resolves this by constructing the target velocity field directly from the Q-function via score estimation and a probability flow ODE, thereby concentrating probability mass on high-value modes. In the fully online RL setting, SOM achieves state-of-the-art performance on locomotion tasks with a single generation step, while substantially reducing both training and inference time compared to prior diffusion- and flow-matching-based policies.

</details>


### [71] [Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning on a Single GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23244)
*Miria Feng,Mert Pilanci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COALA算法，利用凸优化对神经网络进行重新表述，消除了对参考模型的需求，在单GPU上实现高效的大语言模型偏好微调，仅需DPO约17.6%的TFLOPs，并在多个数据集和模型上展现了竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好对齐方法如RLHF计算复杂且昂贵，DPO虽更简单但存在排序精度不一致、GPU资源依赖高、超参数调优成本高等局限性。为克服这些问题，作者寻求一种更轻量、高效且具有理论保证的替代方案。

Method: 利用神经网络的凸优化重新表述，消除对参考模型的需求；提出COALA（Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning Algorithm），一种轻量级、具有强理论保证的偏好对齐策略。

Result: 在四个数据集（含26621样本的合成教育反馈数据集）和六个模型（含Llama-3.1-8B）上，COALA实现了竞争性性能与效率，仅消耗DPO约17.6%的总TFLOPs；表现出稳定、单调递增的奖励，并在显著更短时间内达到峰值边际。

Conclusion: 首次将凸优化有效应用于大语言模型的偏好微调，提供了一种高效、可单GPU训练且理论完备的新范式。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) to align with human preferences has driven the success of systems such as Gemini and ChatGPT. However, approaches like Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) remain computationally expensive and complex. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) offers a simpler alternative but has limitations such as inconsistent ranking accuracy, high dependence on GPU resources, and expensive hyperparameter tuning. We propose the Convex Optimization for Alignment and Preference Learning Algorithm (COALA): a novel lightweight strategy with strong theoretical guarantees. By leveraging the convex optimization reformulation of neural networks, COALA eliminates the need for a reference model and obtains significant reduction in both training time and VRAM consumption, thus enabling efficient training on a single GPU. Experiments across four datasets--including a 26621-sample synthetic Educational Feedback dataset--and six models (including Llama-3.1-8B) demonstrate COALA's competitive performance and efficiency while utilizing as little as ~17.6% of DPO's total TFLOPs. COALA exhibits stable, monotonically increasing rewards and reaches peak margins in significantly shorter time in comparison to traditional methods such as DPO and ORPO. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time convex optimization has been effectively applied to preference fine-tuning of LLMs.

</details>


### [72] [Curriculum reinforcement learning with measurable task representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23372)
*Yongyan Wen,Siyuan Li,Mingjian Fu,Yiqin Yang,Xun Wang,Peng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可度量任务表示学习的自动课程生成方法，通过变分自编码器将任务空间映射到潜在空间以衡量任务相似性，并基于此自动生成与目标任务越来越相似的课程任务，在复杂导航任务中超越了现有最先进的插值和GAN方法的CRL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在课程强化学习中，插值范式依赖于任务空间中的欧几里得距离度量来衡量任务相似性。然而，在复杂的导航任务中，非欧几里得上下文（任务）空间使得这一假设失效，因此需要一种无需依赖欧几里得距离假设即可自动生成课程的方法。

Method: 提出基于可度量任务表示学习的自动课程生成方法：1）利用变分自编码器（VAE）对奖励和状态转移进行编码，将任务空间映射到潜在空间，获得具有任务相似性度量属性的潜在表示；2）基于该潜在表示，自动生成与目标任务越来越相似的课程任务。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的导航任务上的实验结果表明，该方法在性能上超越了基于插值和生成对抗网络的最先进课程强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 通过可度量的任务表示学习，有效解决了非欧几里得任务空间中自动课程生成的难题，推动了课程强化学习在复杂任务中的应用。

Abstract: In curriculum reinforcement learning (CRL), an agent incrementally accumulates knowledge over a sequence of tasks (i.e., a curriculum), and the learning process is aimed at using the accumulated knowledge to finally solve a challenging target task. While early CRL works focus on sequencing candidate tasks, recent research explores automatic curriculum generation. Among the rich CRL literature, the interpolation-based CRL paradigm is a main body, which automatically generates intermediate tasks by interpolating between the initial task distribution and the target task distribution in task space with meaningful distance metrics (i.e., can measure the task similarity). However, in challenging navigation tasks, the non-Euclidean context (task) space invalidates this assumption. To achieve automatic curriculum generation in complex task, we propose a novel automatic curriculum generation approach based on measurable task representation learning. To better measure the similarity, we propose to transform the task space to a latent space. Through a variational autoencoder structure that encodes the reward and the state transitions, we achieve a latent task representation with a task similarity measurement property, and two close task embeddings correspond to two similar tasks in terms of rewards and state transitions. Based on the learned task representation, we further develop an automatic curriculum generation scheme, which can effectively generate new tasks more and more similar to the target task. We evaluate our method in a variety of challenging navigation tasks, and the experiment results indicate that the proposed approach surpasses state-of-the-art CRL approaches based on interpolation and generative adversarial networks.

</details>


### [73] [Accelerating Divisible Load Processing Through Machine Learning: A Practical Framework for Large-Scale Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23247)
*Bharadwaj Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于机器学习的框架，使用前馈神经网络（FNN）和16个工程特征，在无需显式求解方程的情况下预测可分割负载理论（DLT）中单层树形网络（SLTN）的最优处理时间，模型在100,000个合成配置上达到97-99%的R²精度和1-5%的平均绝对百分比误差，推理时间低于1毫秒，相比传统方法实现10-100倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统DLT计算需要显式求解复杂方程，计算开销大，难以应用于实时调度、设计空间探索和云资源分配等场景，因此需要一种快速且精度接近最优的替代方法。

Method: 构建前馈神经网络（FNN），设计16个工程特征，在100,000个合成生成的系统配置上训练模型，预测SLTN架构中的最优处理时间，无需显式推导DLT方程。

Result: 模型R²因子达到97-99%，平均绝对百分比误差为1-5%，推理时间低于1毫秒，相比传统DLT计算实现10-100倍加速；在n=3到20、负载大小1到100 GB的多样化配置上表现一致，但在超大或高度异构系统中性能略有下降。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了使用机器学习加速分布式计算优化的可行性，能够在保持接近最优精度的同时显著提升计算速度，适用于实时调度、设计空间探索和云资源分配等应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce the first machine learning framework for predicting optimal processing times in Single-Level Tree Network (SLTN) architectures for the Divisible Load Theory (DLT) paradigm. Using a feedforward neural network(FNN) with 16 engineered features, we train a model on 100,000 synthetically generated configurations to predict optimal processing times without explicit formulation of DLT equations. The model achieves 97-99% accuracy (R-square factor) with mean absolute percentage error of 1-5%, demonstrating that neural networks can effectively learn complex load distribution relationships. Feature importance analysis reveals that the model implicitly captures DLT mathematical structure, including load conservation and simultaneous finishing constraints. With inference times under 1 millisecond, the approach provides 10-100x speedup over traditional DLT computation, enabling applications in real-time scheduling, design space exploration, and cloud resource allocation. The method generalizes well across diverse system configurations (n=3 to 20, load size =1 to 100 GB) with consistent accuracy, though performance degrades slightly for very large or highly heterogeneous systems. This work demonstrates the feasibility of using machine learning to accelerate distributed computing optimization while maintaining near-optimal accuracy.

</details>


### [74] [Every Component is a Lookup: Token Attribution and Composition from a Single Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23393)
*Po-Kai Chen,Niki van Stein,Aske Plaat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Unpack方法，利用Transformer中注意力与MLP共享的键值模板$\phi(S)U$，通过后向递归在不依赖干预、梯度或辅助训练的情况下，分解任意两个组件之间的端到端路径及其K/Q/V组合标签，并实现单次前向传播的逐token归因。在间接宾语识别任务中，该方法恢复了GPT-2 small上的三种组合连接及其模式特异性路由，并在Pythia家族（160M-6.9B参数）中一致复现了重复姓名抑制模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer机制可解释性方法仅能识别哪些组件重要，但无法揭示它们如何组合成产生预测的计算路径。缺乏一种无需干预、梯度或额外训练，就能在单次前向传播中分解组件间交互强度并标注K/Q/V组合标签的方法。

Method: 利用注意力与MLP共享的键值模板$\phi(S)U$结构，设计Unpack后向递归算法，将信用分配反向分解穿过两个子层，产生任意两个组件间的端到端路径及其K/Q/V组合标签，同时实现逐token归因。

Result: 在GPT-2 small上恢复了Wang et al. (2023)描述的全部三种组合连接及每种连接的K、Q、V模式特异性路由。在Pythia家族（160M至6.9B参数）上一致复现了重复姓名抑制模式：首次提及时保持强信用，重复检测位置被抑制，且控制提示中无此模式。

Conclusion: Unpack方法能够在不依赖真实电路标签的情况下，有效追踪Transformer的机制性结构，为大规模模型的 mechanistic interpretability 提供了一种高效、无干预的逆向分解工具。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of transformers requires identifying not just which components matter but how they compose into the computational route that produced a prediction. Both attention and MLP follow a shared key-value template $φ(S)U$. We exploit this structure to develop Unpack, a backward recursion that decomposes credit through both sublayers, producing interaction strengths between any two components, named end-to-end paths with K/Q/V composition labels, and per-token attribution from a single forward pass, without intervention, gradients, or auxiliary training. We evaluate on the indirect object identification task. On GPT-2 small, the method recovers all three composition connections described by Wang et al. (2023), including the mode-specific routing of each connection (K, Q, or V). To test token-level attribution beyond trivial copying, we compare two occurrences of the same name in the same decomposition: the first mention retains strong credit while the duplicate-detection position is suppressed, a pattern absent in matched control prompts. Across the Pythia family from 160M to 6.9B parameters, this suppression pattern is consistently recovered at every scale, demonstrating that the method tracks mechanistic structure without ground-truth circuit labels. Code is available at https://github.com/Fun-Cry/unpacklm.

</details>


### [75] [Parametric Prior Mapping Framework for Non-stationary Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23402)
*Jinglin Li,Jun Tan,QI Fang,Ning Gui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出参数先验映射(PPM)框架，通过将参数化结构先验注入生成建模过程，在多元时间序列预测中平衡表达能力和鲁棒性，在非平稳动态建模上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化方法具有强归纳偏置但缺乏灵活性，而深度生成模型虽表达力强但难以捕捉复杂时间依赖且需要大量数据和计算。两者在非平稳MTS预测中均存在不足，需要一种既能保持参数方法效率又能利用生成模型表达能力的新方法。

Method: 提出Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM)框架，使用参数化估计器生成动态自适应先验，通过可学习的映射将该先验注入生成模型的学习过程中，并采用混合目标函数进行训练。

Result: PPM在非平稳数据上优于现有基线，在预测精度和不确定性校准方面表现良好，实现了准确性与计算效率之间的优越权衡。

Conclusion: PPM成功融合了参数化方法和深度生成模型的优势，为非平稳多元时间序列预测提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Effectively modeling non-stationary dynamics in probabilistic multivariate time series(MTS) forecasting requires balancing expressiveness with robustness. Existing parametric approaches benefit from strong inductive biases but lack flexibility, whereas deep generative models struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies without extensive data and computation. We introduce Parametric Prior Mapping (PPM), a framework that injects parametric structural priors into a generative modeling process. Specifically, PPM utilizes a parametric estimator to derive a dynamic, adaptive prior that guides the learning of a complex predictive distribution via a learnable mapping.
