This design allows the model to retain the efficiency of parametric methods while exploiting the expressive power of generative models. Trained with a hybrid objective, PPM yields precise forecasts with well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. Empirical results show that PPM outperforms existing baselines in handling non-stationary data, offering a superior trade-off between accuracy and computational efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/ljl8336/PPM.

</details>


### [76] [Learning-Augmented Online Scheduling with Parsimonious Preemption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23255)
*Mugen Blue,Sungjin Im,Alexander Lindermayr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了学习增强调度中限制抢占次数与优化延迟的权衡，提出了在单机与无关并行机上仅需O(1)次抢占/作业、延迟O(1)-竞争的算法，且开销随预测误差对数增长，并通过实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习增强在线调度算法虽然优化了作业延迟，但依赖频繁的“盲目”抢占，忽略了算法性能与抢占复杂性之间的根本权衡。本文旨在弥合理论延迟界与抢占开销之间的差距。

Method: 建立学习增强调度框架，在单机和无关并行机场景下设计算法，在准确预测时实现O(1)次抢占/作业和O(1)-竞争比，开销随预测误差对数增长；并首次将有限抢占保证拓展至无关机和可塑机。

Result: 在单机和无关并行机上实现了O(1)-竞争比与O(1)次抢占/作业（预测准确时），抢占开销随预测误差对数增长；首次给出了无关机和可塑机的有限抢占保证。

Conclusion: 理论延迟界与抢占开销之间的差距可以通过系统分析弥合，学习增强框架可扩展至更受限且更实际的机器模型（如无关机和可塑机），实验验证了算法的有效性。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms have emerged as a powerful paradigm to surpass traditional worst-case lower bounds by integrating potentially noisy predictions. While this framework has seen success in online scheduling, existing work primarily optimizes job latency while relying on frequent, ``blind'' preemptions. This ignores the fundamental trade-off between algorithmic performance and preemption complexity. We provide the first systematic study of learning-augmented scheduling that curbs preemption while optimizing latency. We establish that the gap between theoretical latency bounds and preemption overhead can be bridged with solid analytical foundations. Our results include $O(1)$-competitive algorithms for single and unrelated parallel machines with only $O(1)$ preemptions per job under accurate predictions, with overhead scaling logarithmically with the prediction error. By providing the first bounded-preemption guarantees for unrelated and malleable machines, we extend the theoretical reach of the learning-augmented framework to more constrained and realistic settings. Finally, our algorithms are validated through experiments.

</details>


### [77] [Reflex: Reinforcement Learning with Reflection Symmetry Exploitation in State-Based Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23415)
*Shuai Zhen,Yifan Zhang,Yuling Wang,Yanhua Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflex范式，利用反射对称性（轴向反射和双边反射）提升基于状态的连续控制任务的样本效率，通过对称性正则化机制与PPO、SAC等算法结合，在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习长期面临样本效率低下的问题，现有利用群不变MDP的工作主要关注图像RL和旋转对称性（如SO(2)），而基于状态的RL和反射对称性尚未充分探索。

Method: 形式化定义了两种反射类型（轴向反射和双边反射）及其变换；在理论分析对称性保持最优值函数和策略的基础上，通过原则性的对称性正则化机制将反射对称性融入策略学习；与PPO和SAC算法无缝集成。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym和DeepMind Control基准测试中，Reflex优于标准基线方法，同时提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 反射对称性可以有效提升基于状态的连续控制任务的样本效率，Reflex范式可适用于on-policy和off-policy两类RL算法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has long struggled with poor sample efficiency. One promising approach to mitigate this problem is leveraging group-invariant Markov Decision Processes ($G$-invariant MDPs). Existing works in this direction have primarily focused on image-based RL and rotational symmetry such as $\mathrm{SO(2)}$, leaving state-based RL and reflection symmetry largely underexplored. In this work, we focus on state-based continuous control tasks and exploit reflection symmetry by introducing Reflex, a paradigm that seamlessly integrates with both on-policy and off-policy RL algorithms. We formalize two types of reflection-axial reflection and bilateral reflection, and characterize their corresponding transformations. Building on a theoretical analysis of symmetry-preserving optimal value functions and policies, Reflex integrates reflection symmetry into policy learning through principled symmetry regularization mechanisms. We integrate Reflex with PPO and SAC, and evaluate it on a suite of OpenAI Gym and DeepMind Control benchmarks, demonstrating superior performance over standard baselines while improving sample efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/TonyStark042/Reflex.

</details>


### [78] [A Simple Plug-in for Improving Eviction-Based KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23258)
*Yuping Lin,Jiayuan Ding,Yue Xing,Pengfei He,Jiliang Tang,Subhabrata Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VECTOR是一种即插即用的KV缓存压缩增强方法，通过引入保留、近似、驱逐三种令牌路由策略，利用可重构性信号恢复有价值的向量信息，在中等至高压缩率下显著改善了质量-内存权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存增长成为主要瓶颈，现有方法主要采用二元驱逐或表示近似，未能充分利用那些虽非关键保留但可重构的令牌，导致有价值信息不可逆丢失。

Method: 提出VECTOR方法，通过结合基础评分器的重要性信号与离线校准的回归值估计的可重构性信号，实现三种路由策略：保留（关键向量）、近似（可重构向量）和驱逐（不重要向量）。

Result: 在中等至高压缩率下，VECTOR改善了质量-内存权衡，特别是在严格预算场景下增益更为明显。

Conclusion: VECTOR作为即插即用的增强模块，通过可重构性信号恢复了二元驱逐下不可逆丢失的有价值信息，同时保留关键向量保证注意力路由稳定性，有效提升了KV缓存压缩性能。

Abstract: KV cache growth is a major bottleneck for long-context inference in large language models. Existing methods are often dominated by binary eviction or representation approximation, which may underutilize tokens that are not critical for exact retention but are still reconstructable. We present VECTOR, a plug-and-play augmentation for eviction-based pipelines that introduces three-way token routing: retention, approximation, and eviction. VECTOR combines an importance signal from the base scorer with a reconstructability signal from an offline-calibrated regression-based value estimation. By leveraging reconstructability, VECTOR recovers useful value information that would otherwise be irreversibly lost under binary eviction, while preserving key vectors for attention routing stability. Experimental results show that VECTOR improves quality-memory trade-offs under medium-to-high compression, with especially clear gains in stricter budget regimes.

</details>


### [79] [Learning Individual Dynamics from Sparse Cross-Sectional Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23470)
*Christian Lagemann,Kai Lagemann,Steven L. Brunton,Sach Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CADENCE提出了一种从稀疏快照中恢复连续个体轨迹的概率框架，通过将潜在动态锚定到静态个体上下文，在仅使用横截面数据训练的情况下，性能可媲美甚至超越需要密集轨迹数据的序列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在稀疏或横截面数据下存在根本性局限：序列模型（如潜变量ODE）需要密集纵向数据，而横截面方法（如最优传输、流匹配）只能描述群体分布，无法重建个体化连续轨迹。作者旨在打破这种二分法。

Method: 提出CADENCE框架：(1) 将潜在动态锚定到静态个体级上下文；(2) 利用基于分数的空间编码器（双射概率流ODE）消除微分同胚歧义；(3) 引入Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE)路由器，联合识别个体动力学参数和路由函数。

Result: 在物理系统到真实生物数据的多个基准测试中，CADENCE仅使用带有上下文结构的极稀疏快照训练，其性能匹配或超越了使用密集全轨迹数据训练的先进序列模型。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了从单时间点横截面数据推断个体连续轨迹的可能性，提供了首个单时间点轨迹推断的可辨识性保证，为纵向数据稀缺场景下的动态系统建模开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Predicting how a dynamical unit evolves over time - how an individual ages, an epidemic spreads, or a physical system degrades - typically requires dense longitudinal tracking. When only extremely sparse or entirely cross-sectional data is available, inferring individualized, continuous-time trajectories is fundamentally ill-posed. Existing methods force a strict compromise: sequence models (e.g. latent ODEs) require dense longitudinal data, while cross-sectional methods (e.g. optimal transport, flow matching-based) map aggregate populations, losing individual dynamics. In this paper, we demonstrate that this dichotomy can be broken. We introduce CADENCE, a principled probabilistic framework that recovers continuous individual trajectories from isolated snapshots by anchoring latent dynamics to static, individual-level contexts. We provide novel identifiability guarantees for single-timepoint trajectory inference. By combining a score-based spatial encoder (bijective Probability Flow ODE) to eliminate diffeomorphic ambiguities with a Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) router, we show that individual dynamical parameters and routing function are jointly identifiable. Across a suite of benchmarks spanning physical systems to real-world biological data, CADENCE, trained strictly on extremely sparse snapshots with context structure, matches or exceeds the performance of state-of-the-art sequential models trained on dense, full-trajectory data.

</details>


### [80] [CBANet: A Compact Attention-Based CNN-BiLSTM Network for Aggressive Driving Event Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23471)
*Hanadi Alhamdan,Ghadah Alosaimi,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei,Farshad Arvin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种增强型深度学习框架CBANet，通过构建工程化动态特征、结合SMOTE过采样与类别加权损失的稳定训练策略，以及基于类别特定阈值校准的安全导向决策策略，有效提升了自然驾驶数据中极端不平衡场景下的激进驾驶行为检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进驾驶是交通事故的主要原因，但现有深度学习方法在真实场景中受限于数据严重不平衡、驾驶员间差异大以及缺乏物理可解释的车辆动力学表征。

Method: 1) 构建工程化动态特征（转向、加速、制动行为）；2) 结合受控SMOTE过采样与类别加权损失的稳定训练策略，并评估focal loss变体处理不平衡；3) 基于类别特定阈值校准的安全导向决策策略，以平衡漏检与误报的非对称风险。

Result: 在新采集的自然驾驶数据集上，该方法在少数类召回率和安全关键F-score指标上显著优于标准深度学习基线，同时保持实际计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了真实驾驶数据中的不平衡与可解释性问题，为实际道路安全应用提供了可靠且高效的激进驾驶检测方案。

Abstract: Aggressive driving is a major cause of traffic accidents and poses a serious threat to road safety. Although deep learning methods have shown promising results in detecting risky driving behaviours from vehicle sensor data, their performance in real-world conditions is often limited by severe data imbalance, large variability between drivers, and the lack of physically interpretable vehicle dynamics representations. In this paper, we propose an enhanced deep learning framework for aggressive driving detection using multivariate vehicle dynamics signals. Instead of relying solely on raw measurements, the proposed approach constructs engineered dynamic features that capture steering, acceleration, and braking behaviour. To address the extreme rarity of aggressive events in naturalistic driving data, we introduce a stable training strategy that combines controlled SMOTE-based oversampling with a class-weighted loss formulation, and evaluates focal loss variants for imbalance handling. Furthermore, a safety-oriented decision strategy based on class-specific threshold calibration is adopted to better reflect the asymmetric risks of missed detections and false alarms in real-world applications. The proposed framework is evaluated on a newly collected naturalistic driving dataset. Extensive experiments show that the proposed method consistently outperforms standard deep learning baselines with significant improvements in minority-class recall and safety-critical F-score metrics while maintaining practical computational efficiency. Code: \url {https://github.com/halhamdan/CBANet}

</details>


### [81] [Automated Random Embedding for Practical Bayesian Optimization with Unknown Effective Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23473)
*Hong Qian,Xiang Shu,Xiang Xia,Xuhui Liu,Yangde Fu,Bei Liang,Huibin Wang,Liang Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动态共享嵌入贝叶斯优化（DSEBO），一种针对有效维度未知的高维贝叶斯优化自动随机嵌入方法。DSEBO从低维子空间开始，当当前子空间解出现初步收敛时自动切换到更高维子空间，并基于不同子空间中的解质量动态确定下一子空间维度，同时共享已查询的解以初始化新子空间。理论推导了遗憾界，实验表明该方法在优化遗憾和时间上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在高维空间中面临维度诅咒问题。随机嵌入作为降维策略虽有效，但需要预先知道任务的有效维度，而实际中有效维度难以确定。传统方法依赖专家给定固定子空间维度或通过试错法估算，资源消耗大且性能受限。

Method: DSEBO从低维子空间开始优化，监测当前子空间解是否出现初步收敛；一旦收敛，自动切换到更高维子空间。通过评估不同子空间中解的质量动态确定下一子空间的维度，并将已查询的解共享给新子空间以实现更好的初始化。理论部分推导了DSEBO的遗憾界，证明其能更好地平衡逼近误差和优化误差。

Result: 在多种维度的函数和实际任务上，与现有方法相比，DSEBO在不同子空间间交替优化在优化遗憾和计算时间上均取得了显著提升。

Conclusion: DSEBO通过动态确定子空间维度和共享历史查询解，有效解决了有效维度未知时高维贝叶斯优化中的维度选择难题，实现了逼近误差与优化误差的良好平衡，显著提升了高维优化的性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is widely employed for optimizing complex black-box functions but struggles with the curse of dimensionality. Random embedding, as a dimension reduction strategy, simplifies tasks that possess the effective dimension by optimizing within a low-dimensional subspace. However, determining the effective dimension of a task in advance remains a significant challenge, which influences the selection of the subspace dimensionality and the optimization performance. Traditional methods use fixed subspace dimensions provided by experts or rely on trial and error to estimate subspace dimensions with resources consumed. To this end, this paper proposes an automated random embedding for high-dimensional Bayesian optimization with unknown effective dimension, called Dynamic Shared Embedding Bayesian Optimization (DSEBO). DSEBO starts with a low dimension and switches to a higher subspace if the solutions in the current subspace show preliminary convergence. DSEBO dynamically determines the dimension of the next subspace based on the quality of the solutions in different subspaces and shares the queried solutions with the new subspace for a better initialization. Theoretically, we derive a regret bound for DSEBO and demonstrate that DSEBO can better balance approximation and optimization errors. Extensive experiments on functions with dimensionality of varying magnitudes and real-world tasks with unknown effective dimensions reveal that, compared with state-of-the-art methods, alternating optimization across different subspaces results in significant improvements in high-dimensional optimization, both in terms of optimization regret and time.

</details>


### [82] [Diffusion Domain Expansion: Learning to Coordinate Pre-trained Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23275)
*Egor Lifar,Semyon Savkin,Timur Garipov,Shangyuan Tong,Tommi Jaakkola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出扩散域扩展(DDE)方法，通过紧凑可训练协调网络扩展预训练扩散模型，使其能生成更大物体和处理更复杂条件，在长音频生成和条件图像生成任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型在处理超出其原始训练域的大尺寸对象和复杂条件时存在局限性，现有方法效率低下。

Method: 采用紧凑可训练协调网络，协调预训练扩散模型的去噪输出，该协调器结构简单且能泛化到比训练时更大的域。

Result: 在长音频轨道生成和条件图像生成任务上，DDE在定性和定量评估中均优于其他协调式扩散生成方法。

Conclusion: DDE能够高效扩展预训练扩散模型的能力边界，且协调器具有通用简单性和域外泛化能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Diffusion Domain Expansion (DDE), a method that efficiently extends pre-trained diffusion models to generate larger objects and handle more complex conditioning beyond their original capabilities. Our method employs a compact trainable network designed to coordinate the denoised outputs of pre-trained diffusion models. We demonstrate that the coordinator can be universally simple while being capable of generalizing to domains larger than those observed during its training time. We evaluate DDE on long audio track generation and conditional image generation, demonstrating its applicability across domains. DDE outperforms other approaches to coordinated generation with diffusion models in qualitative and quantitative evaluations.

</details>


### [83] [CoSPlay: Cooperative Self-Play at Test-Time with Self-Generated Code and Unit Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23491)
*Zhangyi Hu,Chenhui Liu,Tian Huang,Jindong Li,Yang Yang,Jiemin Wu,Zining Zhong,Menglin Yang,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoSPlay提出了一种无Ground-Truth、无需训练的协同自博弈框架，通过代码与单元测试的迭代共同进化，显著提升了LLM代码生成的性能，在多个基准上超越了依赖GT的RLVR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖Ground-Truth单元测试进行训练，成本高昂；而现有的Test-Time Scaling方法在缺乏GT时性能下降。关键挑战在于代码和自生成单元测试的质量相互耦合，无法独立验证，需要同时改进两者。

Method: CoSPlay框架包含三个步骤：1) 探索多样化解决方案并识别其潜在失败模式以生成有区分力的UT想法；2) 利用代码-UT执行矩阵中的双向通过计数信号，迭代剪枝/修复弱代码并刷新/替换不可靠UT，使两者共同进化；3) 当多个代码通过数相同时，从最大输出一致性聚类中选择最终代码。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上，平均BoN从22.1%提升至33.2%，UT准确率从14.6%提升至78.3%，匹配或超越CURE-7B。应用于CURE-7B时进一步提升BoN 5.7%。在多种骨干模型上均表现优异，优于同类GT-free TTS基线。

Conclusion: CoSPlay证明了在没有任何GT数据的情况下，通过可扩展的推理策略可以实现具有竞争力的代码生成，且随着推理预算增加性能持续提升。

Abstract: Recently, Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and Test-Time Scaling (TTS) have advanced LLM code generation through executable verification. Yet Ground-Truth Unit Tests (GT UTs) remain a bottleneck: SOTA RLVR methods require them for costly training, while existing TTS methods lose competitiveness without them. This motivates GT-free TTS, where existing methods directly use self-generated UTs to refine and select code candidates. Yet such UTs are often noisy or spuriously coupled with wrong code, and UT quality in turn cannot be validated without reliable code. The key challenge is therefore to jointly improve both. To this end, we present CoSPlay, a GT-free, training-free framework that jointly improves codes and UTs through cooperative self-play. It first explores diverse solution ideas and identifies their potential failure modes to produce discriminative UT ideas. It then uses bidirectional pass-count signals from the Code-UT execution matrix to iteratively prune or fix weak codes and refresh or replace unreliable UTs, letting the two pools co-evolve. Finally, when multiple codes remain tied at the highest pass count, it picks the final code from the largest output-consensus cluster, since correct codes agree on the same inputs while wrong codes diverge. Experiments on four challenging benchmarks show that CoSPlay on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct improves average BoN from 22.1% to 33.2% and UT accuracy from 14.6% to 78.3%, matching or surpassing the RLVR model CURE-7B. When applied to CURE-7B, it further improves BoN by 5.7%. CoSPlay also generalizes across diverse backbones and outperforms GT-free TTS baselines under comparable token budgets, with continued gains as the budget scales up. These results suggest a scalable inference strategy for competitive code generation without any GT data.

</details>


### [84] [VACE: Learning Geometrically Structured Representations for Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23504)
*Alberto D. Cencillo,Leonardo Concepción,Isaac Triguero,Julián Luengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VACE提出了一种基于速度一致性自监督学习的多元时间序列异常检测方法，通过构建紧凑且方向一致的正常行为表征空间，结合马氏距离位置评分和速度库方向评分，在TSB-AD-M基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比自监督方法通过配对采样启发式间接塑造嵌入空间，无法显式控制距离评分所需的几何结构（如正常表征的紧凑性和距离的方向意义），导致异常检测精度受限。

Method: VACE：1）通道感知编码器通过速度一致性目标训练（无负样本、无合成异常）；2）正常轨迹在嵌入空间中保持局部平滑和对齐；3）测试时联合使用马氏距离位置评分和速度库方向评分（乘积形式），标记同时偏离分布和动态异常的点。

Result: 在TSB-AD-M基准上达到最先进性能，显著优于使用更大预算训练的复杂方法。

Conclusion: VACE以简洁的设计实现了优异的异常检测效果，验证了显式控制嵌入空间几何结构对于距离评分的重要性。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series is a critical task across a wide range of real-world applications, where abnormal behaviour is rare, labels are unavailable, and the cost of a miss is high. The central challenge is learning a characterisation of normality precise enough to flag deviations. Representation self-supervised learning, typically through contrastive approaches, addresses this by embedding temporal patches into a latent space where normality occupies a well-defined region, with anomalies detected by geometric deviation. However, contrastive approaches shape this space indirectly through pair-sampling heuristics, providing no explicit control over the geometric structure that distance-based scoring requires. This means how tightly normal representations are grouped, and whether distances are directionally meaningful. We present VACE (Velocity-Aligned Channel Embeddings), a self-supervised anomaly detection method that represents normality as a compact, directionally coherent region in the embedding space. To this end, VACE trains a channel-aware encoder through a velocity-consistency objective, with no negatives and no synthetic anomalies, so that normal trajectories are locally smooth and aligned. At test time, a Mahalanobis positional score and a velocity-bank directional score are combined multiplicatively, flagging points that are simultaneously off-distribution and dynamically atypical. Despite its simplicity, VACE achieves state-of-the-art performance on TSB-AD-M under rigorous evaluation, significantly outperforming more complex methods trained on substantially larger budgets.

</details>


### [85] [Contrastive Distribution Matching for Amortized Sequential Monte Carlo in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23346)
*Jaihoon Kim,Taehoon Yoon,Prin Phunyaphibarn,Seungjun Kim,Morteza Mardani,Minhyuk Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对比分布匹配（CDM）框架，通过学习参数化扭曲函数来摊销离散扩散模型中奖励倾斜分布的序贯蒙特卡洛采样成本，在仅增加不到5%计算开销的情况下优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在生成结构化分类数据方面效果显著，但高效地从奖励倾斜分布中采样仍是一大挑战。Twisted SMC虽然可以提供渐近精确的采样，但在离散状态空间中估计最优扭曲函数需要昂贵的蒙特卡洛近似，导致推理阶段出现严重的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出对比分布匹配（CDM）框架，利用正负样本学习参数化的扭曲函数，从而摊销SMC推理的计算成本。为高效训练，重新设计了梯度估计器，利用离散扩散模型的闭式前向核。学得的扭曲函数在推理时仅增加不到5%的计算开销。

Result: 在匹配实际运行时间的条件下，CDM在多个任务（有毒文本生成、调控DNA序列设计、蛋白质可设计性、扩散大语言模型对齐）中一致优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: CDM通过参数化扭曲函数有效解决了离散扩散模型中奖励倾斜采样的计算瓶颈问题，兼具计算效率与性能优势，在多种实际应用中展示了有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models have emerged as powerful frameworks for generating structured categorical data. However, efficiently sampling from reward-tilted distributions remains a fundamental challenge. While Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) offers asymptotic exactness for this task, estimating the optimal twist function in discrete state spaces necessitates costly Monte Carlo approximations, resulting a severe computational bottleneck at inference. To overcome this limitation, we introduce Contrastive Distribution Matching (CDM), a novel framework that amortizes the cost of SMC inference by learning a parameterized twist function via positive and negative samples. For efficient training, we reformulate the gradient estimator to leverage the closed-form forward kernels of discrete diffusion models. In practice, evaluating our learned twist function incurs less than 5% additional computational overhead compared to a single forward pass of the base model. Through extensive empirical evaluations, we demonstrate that CDM consistently outperforms existing baselines under matched wall-clock time. We validate the effectiveness and versatility of our approach across a diverse range of applications, including toxic text generation, regulatory DNA sequence design, protein designability, and diffusion large language model alignment.

</details>


### [86] [Precise: SDE-Consistent Stochastic Sampling for RL Post-Training of Flow-Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23522)
*Jade Zou,Tao Huang,Weijie Kong,Junzhe Li,Yue Wu,Qi Tian,Jiangfeng Xiong,Jianwei Zhang,Liefeng Bo,Zhao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出名为Precise的新型随机采样器，通过平衡探索与稳定性、保持SDE一致性，显著提升了扩散/流匹配生成器中强化学习奖励优化的效率与效果，在减少13.1-53.2%训练时间的同时达到SOTA对齐分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将在线强化学习应用于流匹配生成器时，需要将确定性ODE采样轨迹转化为随机SDE策略。采样器的设计（包含探索行为与去噪动力学）直接影响奖励优化性能，但现有采样器存在探索与稳定性之间的固有矛盾，且在少步数离散化时引入过多噪声或依赖启发式规则，无法保证收敛到数据分布。

Method: 将采样器设计分解为两个相互依赖的组件：选择适当的随机探索量，以及对所得SDE进行高保真离散化。通过分析去噪中探索与稳定性的内在张力，推导出平衡二者的SDE调度。针对离散化挑战，提出Precise采样器，通过冻结干净潜变量后验均值的新近似方法保持去噪轨迹的SDE一致性，解决标准采样器的过量噪声问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，Precise采样器在强化学习奖励优化中速度更快、训练更稳定，在PickScore、HPSv2.1等对齐指标上达到SOTA，同时相比先前最佳采样器节省13.1-53.2%的挂钟训练时间。

Conclusion: 通过解耦采样器设计中的探索量与离散化精度两个关键因素，Precise采样器有效平衡了探索与稳定性，为流匹配生成器中的在线RL对齐提供了一种更高效、更稳定的训练范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become an effective way to improve prompt alignment and perceptual quality in diffusion and flow-matching generators. A critical step for applying online RL to flow matching is turning the deterministic sampling trajectory into a stochastic policy, typically by replacing the reverse-time Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) with a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE). The stochastic sampler, controlling the exploration behavior and denoising dynamics, is thus part of the policy, and its design can significantly affect the reward optimization performance. We break down the sampler design into two interdependent components: choosing the right amount of stochastic exploration, and discretizing the resulting SDE faithfully at the small step counts used in RL. To address the first component, we analyze the inherent tension between exploration and stability in denoising and derive an SDE schedule that balances the two. Turning to the discretization challenge, we use a toy example to show that existing samplers can deviate from the flow-matching process, either by introducing excessive discretization noise or by relying on heuristic rules that do not guarantee convergence to the data distribution. To address these issues, we propose Precise, a new stochastic sampler that balances effective exploration with stability. Crucially, Precise keeps the denoising trajectory SDE-consistent through a novel approximation that freezes the clean-latent posterior mean, resolving the excess noise issue in standard samplers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that this formulation leads to significantly faster and more stable reward optimization via reinforcement learning, achieving state-of-the-art alignment scores (e.g., PickScore, HPSv2.1) while requiring 13.1-53.2% less wall-clock training time to match the best in-domain performance of prior samplers.

</details>


### [87] [Prudent-Banker: No Extra Fees for Baseline Safety in Adversarial Bandits With and Without Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23351)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prudent-Banker算法，首次在对抗性多臂老虎机中同时实现延迟反馈下的最优安全-鲁棒性权衡：达到$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$的伪遗憾和$\widetilde{O}(1)$的安全比较器遗憾，并证明该下界不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法在处理延迟反馈时，保守策略与探索之间的转换时机可能因延迟而错位，从而危及安全保证。本文旨在弥合这一差距，在延迟反馈场景下实现安全与鲁棒性的最优平衡。

Method: 提出了Prudent-Banker算法，将延迟适应的在线镜像下降变体与改进的分阶段攻击机制相结合。关键技术贡献是延迟校准的重启阈值，严格考虑了未观测反馈导致的最坏情况失真，并可靠检测比较器次优性。

Result: 建立了安全约束对抗性延迟老虎机的新下界，证明Prudent-Banker的遗憾保证在基线安全要求下（对数因子内）不可改进。伪遗憾$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$，安全比较器遗憾$\widetilde{O}(1)$。

Conclusion: Prudent-Banker是首个在有无延迟下均实现最优安全-鲁棒性权衡的算法，实验表明其在不同延迟分布下能有效平衡安全与学习。

Abstract: We study adversarial multi-armed bandits with and without delayed feedback under a safety-aware goal: achieving minimax-optimal worst-case regret while keeping nearly constant regret relative to a designated "safe" baseline policy. Existing approaches can balance this trade-off with immediate feedback for smooth comparators, but arbitrary delays can mistime transitions between conservatism and exploration, endangering the safety guarantee. To bridge this gap, we propose Prudent-Banker, a novel algorithm that combines a delay-adapted variant of Online Mirror Descent with a modified phased-aggression mechanism. Its key technical contribution is a delay-calibrated restart threshold that rigorously accounts for the worst-case distortion induced by unobserved feedback and reliably detects comparator suboptimality. We also establish new lower bounds for safety-constrained adversarial delayed bandits, showing that the regret guarantees of Prudent-Banker are unimprovable, up to logarithmic factors, under the baseline-safety requirement. To the best of our knowledge, Prudent-Banker is the first algorithm to achieve the optimal safety--robustness trade-off: pseudo-regret $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T}+\sqrt{D})$ together with $\widetilde{O}(1)$ regret against the safe comparator, both with and without delays. Experiments across diverse delay distributions show that, unlike standard delay-robust baselines, Prudent-Banker effectively balances safety and learning.

</details>


### [88] [Goal-Conditioned Agents that Learn Everything All at Once](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23551)
*Michael Matthews,Matthew Jackson,Michael Beukman,Thomas Foster,Alistair Letcher,Scott Fujimoto,Cédric Colas,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEO方法，通过一次性并行输出所有目标的值和动作，实现高效的全部目标学习（all-goals learning），在目标条件Craftax任务上显著优于其他方法，在连续控制任务上与基线相当，同时相比传统的全部目标重标注方法实现>250倍加速，并可作为教师网络进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在目标条件强化学习中，智能体在轨迹中观察到丰富信息，但仅对当前命令目标做on-policy更新会丢弃大量信息。全部目标学习（all-goals learning）虽能最大化信息利用，但通过朴素重标注实现时计算不可行。

Method: 提出LEO（Learning Everything all at Once）方法，通过一次性联合输出所有目标的值和动作，以单次网络前向传播实现高效的并行全部目标更新，避免传统重标注的计算开销。同时探索将LEO作为教师网络而非直接策略网络的用法。

Result: 在目标条件Craftax任务上显著优于其他方法；在连续控制环境上与现有基线竞争相当；相比全部目标重标注方法实现>250倍加速。

Conclusion: LEO解锁了大规模场景下的全部目标学习，为复杂环境中的强化学习实践者提供了有用工具；开源代码供社区使用。

Abstract: A goal-conditioned reinforcement learning agent exploring an environment will see a wealth of information throughout a trajectory, most of which is discarded when only performing on-policy updates with respect to the commanded goal. All-goals learning, where each transition is used for learning off-policy with respect to every goal, allows agents to extract maximal information, however it is usually computationally infeasible when done via naive relabelling. This can be overcome by jointly outputting values and actions for every goal at once, allowing for efficient, parallel all-goals updates with a single pass through the network, in a process we call Learning Everything all at Once (LEO). We show that this approach significantly outperforms other methods on goal-conditioned Craftax and is competitive with existing baselines on continuous control environments, while achieving a >250x speed-up compared to all-goals relabelling. We then go on to show that this approach can be made even more powerful by using LEO as a teacher network, rather than a direct actor. We hope that, by unlocking all-goals learning at scale, LEO can serve as a useful tool for RL practitioners in complex environments. We open source our code.

</details>


### [89] [Understanding Goal Generalisation in Sequential Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23565)
*Jason Ross Brown,Edward James Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强化学习代理在训练分布外环境中的泛化行为，发现显著特征驱动泛化且早期学习目标会持续影响后续目标，并提出了潜在策略梯度方法（latent policy gradients）来预测训练流程可能引发的分布外行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习代理经常在训练分布之外的环境中表现出非预期的目标导向行为，但当前缺乏对其基于训练历史如何泛化到新环境的原则性理解。

Method: 研究了超过100个顺序训练流程，在250多个分布外环境中评估行为；提出潜在策略梯度方法，通过模拟训练过程中低维潜在变量根据训练目标获得高奖励的演化过程，来预测分布外行为。

Result: 发现显著特征驱动泛化，早期学习目标会持续并影响后续目标；潜在策略梯度方法具有强预测准确性，能泛化到未见过的训练流程类型，且具有可解释性。

Conclusion: 分布外RL代理行为依赖于整个训练流程，但这种依赖存在可捕捉的底层结构，为从发展视角理解目标泛化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents often exhibit unintended goal-directed behaviour outside their training distribution, but we currently lack a principled understanding of how such agents will generalise to novel environments based on their training history. We address this gap for agents trained sequentially on one or more tasks. We study over 100 sequential training pipelines, evaluating behaviour across over 250 out-of-distribution environments. We find that salient features drive generalisation, and that goals learnt early in training can persist and influence those acquired later. To explain these phenomena, we introduce latent policy gradients, a method that predicts what out-of-distribution behaviour a training pipeline will likely induce. Our method simulates the evolution of low-dimensional latent variables during training according to what would achieve high reward on the training objective with respect to a simple model of how the latent variables map to behaviour. It achieves strong predictive accuracy, generalises to unseen types of training pipeline, and is interpretable. Our findings demonstrate that while out-of-distribution RL agent behaviour is dependent on the whole training pipeline, this dependence has an underlying structure we can capture, laying groundwork for understanding goal generalisation from a developmental perspective.

</details>


### [90] [Cost-Effective Model Evaluation with Meta-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23595)
*Trinh Pham,Viet Huynh,Hongzhi Yin,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Thanh Tam Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MetaEvaluator，一个基于元学习的模型无关框架，能在无标注数据上高效评估任意新模型（跨架构、跨模态），通过参考模型池的元学习获得可迁移初始化，大幅降低评估成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型快速增长，传统评估方法依赖昂贵标注、重复微调或窄假设，难以扩展到未见模型和无标签数据的可靠性验证。

Method: 利用元学习在参考模型池上学习可迁移的初始化参数，使框架在无需针对每个新模型重新训练的情况下，快速评估模型在无标签数据上的性能。

Result: 在大量实验中，MetaEvaluator能以远低于传统方法的成本，产生稳定且准确的性能估计，使得在无标签数据上规模化评估新模型成为可行。

Conclusion: MetaEvaluator是首个能在完全无标注数据集上评估新模型的模型无关框架，实现了低成本、跨架构/模态的可扩展模型评估。

Abstract: The rapid growth of machine learning has produced an ever-expanding ecosystem of models, making it increasingly challenging to verify the reliability of newly released models on unseen, unlabeled data. Conventional evaluation pipelines depend on expensive annotation, repeated fine-tuning, or narrow assumptions that fail to transfer across model families. We present MetaEvaluator, a cost-effective, model-agnostic framework for rapid, label-free assessment of unseen models spanning diverse architectures and modalities. MetaEvaluator leverages meta-learning over a pool of reference models to obtain a transferable initialization, enabling accurate evaluation of new models while amortizing cost across the pool and removing the need for per-model retraining. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first model-agnostic framework capable of evaluating new models on entirely unlabeled datasets. Extensive experiments show that MetaEvaluator produces stable and accurate performance estimates at substantially reduced cost compared to conventional approaches, making scalable benchmarking of emerging models on unlabeled data practical.

</details>


### [91] [Coupling-Robust Accuracy in Multiphysics Physics Informed Neural Networks via Kronecker-Preconditioned Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23391)
*Youngjae Park,Jaemin Kim,Junghwa Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对物理信息神经网络（PINN）在多物理场耦合系统中随耦合强度增大而精度退化的问题，通过神经正切核（NTK）理论证明：标准NTK谱半径随耦合强度γ呈Ω(γ²)增长，导致稳定学习率收缩；而块对角高斯-牛顿（GN）预条件处理后的预条件NTK谱半径被限制为网络数S，与γ无关。结合Kronecker预条件优化器SOAP与逆梯度范数损失平衡（SOAP+GN），在234个实验中保持最终周期L₂退化比≤1.1倍，而Adam+GN超过10²倍，并成功扩展至2D六偏微分方程电渗流系统（Adam+GN完全失效）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物理场耦合PINN存在系统性的精度退化问题，即方程间耦合越强，精度越差，该现象缺乏理论解释与有效解决方案。

Method: 1) 通过NTK理论分析，证明标准NTK谱半径随耦合强度γ呈Ω(γ²)增长；2) 提出块对角GN预条件处理，使预条件NTK谱半径有界于网络数S；3) 结合SOAP优化器与逆梯度范数损失平衡（SOAP+GN）。

Result: NTK谱半径Ω(γ²)增长在对称、非对称及非线性耦合PDE系统中得到数值验证；预条件NTK最大特征值恒等于S。SOAP+GN在234个实验中保持耦合鲁棒性（L₂退化比≤1.1×），而Adam+GN退化比>10²×；SOAP+GN成功求解2D六PDE电渗流系统（Adam+GN L₂>0.9完全失效）。

Conclusion: 多物理场耦合PINN的精度退化根源在于NTK谱半径随耦合强度增长，块对角GN预条件可消除该依赖，SOAP+GN是耦合鲁棒的优化方法。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for coupled multiphysics systems suffer systematic accuracy degradation as inter-equation coupling strengthens. We provide a theoretical explanation for this phenomenon through neural tangent kernel (NTK) analysis: for linearly coupled systems, we prove that the standard NTK's spectral radius grows as $Ω(γ^2)$ with coupling strength $γ$, shrinking the stable learning rate, while block-diagonal Gauss--Newton (GN) preconditioning yields a preconditioned NTK $K_P = J H^{+} J^\top$ (where $H$ is the block-diagonal GN Hessian) whose spectral radius is bounded by $S$ ($S$ = number of networks), independent of $γ$. We verify the $Ω(γ^2)$ growth numerically across symmetric, asymmetric, and nonlinear coupled PDE systems, and confirm $λ_{\max}(K_P) = S$ with equality in all cases. Combining the Kronecker-preconditioned optimizer SOAP with inverse-gradient-norm loss balancing (SOAP+GN) yields coupling-robust accuracy: across 234 experiments spanning three 1D systems of increasing nonlinearity and a 2D electroosmotic flow benchmark, SOAP+GN maintains final-epoch $L_2$ degradation $\leq 1.1\times$ (ratio of strong- to weak-coupling error) even as coupling parameters vary over one to two orders of magnitude, compared with $> 10^2\times$ for Adam+GN. SOAP+GN further scales to a 2D, 6-PDE electroosmotic flow system at EDL-resolved conditions -- a regime that all prior PINN electrokinetics studies have avoided through simplified physics -- where Adam+GN fails entirely ($L_2 > 0.9$).

</details>


### [92] [Preisach Attention: A Hysteretic Model of Sequential Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23603)
*Piotr Frydrych*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Preisach注意力层(PAL)，一种基于Preisach磁滞算子的序列建模架构。PAL用二元继电算子替代softmax注意力，通过维护局部极值堆栈作为内部状态，实现O(1)深度下的图灵完备性（模拟双栈下推自动机），与标准硬注意力Transformer的O(log n)深度形成对比。PAL与Transformer的函数类不可比较：PAL以O(1)层计算历史范围统计（Transformer需O(log n)），而Transformer支持随机访问检索。PAL的分离属性是率无关性——仅响应局部极值序列。极值堆栈是率无关泛函历史的最简充分统计量。PAL适用于长情节记忆和弱位置依赖任务，推理成本O(n log n) vs O(n²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer的softmax注意力机制在长序列任务中计算复杂度高（O(n²)），且对绝对位置和时间间隔敏感，不适用于仅依赖局部极值序列的率无关任务。现有架构缺乏对这类记忆模式的显式建模。

Method: PAL用学习激活/去激活阈值的二元继电算子替换softmax注意力，维护局部极值堆栈作为内部状态。通过模拟双栈下推自动机实现Turing完备性。PAL的率无关性使其仅响应局部极值序列，忽略绝对位置和时间间隔。

Result: PAL在O(1)深度下模拟双栈下推自动机实现Turing完备（标准硬注意力Transformer需O(log n)）。PAL以O(1)层计算历史范围统计（Transformer需O(log n)）。PAL的极值堆栈是率无关泛函历史的最简充分统计量。推理成本O(n log n) < O(n²)。

Conclusion: PAL是一种高效的长序列建模架构，适用于依赖局部极值序列且位置不敏感的任务（如时间序列分析、物理系统模拟），计算复杂度显著低于标准注意力机制。

Abstract: We introduce the Preisach Attention Layer (PAL), a novel sequence modelling architecture grounded in the classical Preisach hysteresis operator from mathematical physics. PAL replaces the softmax attention mechanism with a binary relay operator parameterised by learned activation and deactivation thresholds, maintaining a stack of local extrema as its internal state. A single-layer PAL-Transformer with O(1) depth is Turing-complete under arbitrary precision arithmetic, achievable through simulation of a two-stack pushdown automaton -- in contrast to the O(log n) depth required by standard hard-attention transformers. Second, we prove that the function classes computable by PAL and by the transformer are incomparable: PAL computes historical range statistics in O(1) layers that require O(log n) layers for transformers, while transformers support random-access retrieval that PAL cannot perform without auxiliary state. The separating property is rate-independence -- PAL responds only to the sequence of local extrema, not to absolute token positions or temporal spacing. Third, we show that the extremum stack constitutes a minimal sufficient statistic of the input history for all rate-independent functionals, providing a formal analogue of the wiping property in classical hysteresis theory. PAL is thus an efficient architecture for tasks with long episodic memory and weak positional dependence, with O(n log n) total inference cost versus O(n^2) for standard attention.

</details>


### [93] [DiLaDiff: Distilled Latent-Augmented Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23605)
*Jean-Marie Lemercier,Tomas Geffner,Karsten Kreis,Morteza Mardani,Arash Vahdat,Ante Jukić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiLaDiff，通过连续潜空间、潜扩散模型和一致性蒸馏解决扩散语言模型在解码token间相关性捕获失败导致的采样质量与吞吐量权衡问题，在加速推理的同时超越掩码扩散基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型本质上无法捕获已解码token之间的相关性，导致采样质量与吞吐量之间存在严重权衡。

Method: 提出DiLaDiff，包含三个组件：(1) 通过从现有掩码扩散语言模型微调的自编码器学习具有语义能力的连续潜空间；(2) 在编码器分布上学习先验的潜扩散模型；(3) 将学得的先验蒸馏为少步潜生成模型的一致性模型。

Result: 即使不进行蒸馏，潜引导扩散模型也优于掩码扩散基线并显著加速推理。一致性蒸馏进一步降低连续扩散的计算开销，使潜生成时间相较于离散解码可忽略不计。

Conclusion: DiLaDiff有效解决了扩散语言模型中解码token间相关性缺失的问题，实现了采样质量与推理速度的双重提升。

Abstract: Diffusion language models intrinsically fail to capture correlations between decoded tokens, which leads to a harsh trade-off between sampling quality and throughput. To solve this issue, we propose DiLaDiff, a variant of masked diffusion language models with three components: (1) a continuous latent space with semantic capabilities, learned by an auto-encoder fine-tuned from an existing masked diffusion language model; (2) a latent diffusion model learning the prior over the encoder distribution; (3) a consistency model distilling the learned prior into a few-step latent generative model. We show that, even without distillation, our latent-guided diffusion model outperforms the masked diffusion baseline while significantly accelerating inference. Consistency distillation further lowers the computational overhead of continuous diffusion, such that the latent is generated in negligible time compared to discrete decoding.

</details>


### [94] [Convex Compositional Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23395)
*Meir Roketlishvili,Semyon Semenov,Maksim Bobrin,Viktor Kovalchuk,Albert Baichorov,Abduragim Shtanchaev,Fakhri Karray,Dmitry V. Dylov,Martin Takáč,Arip Asadulaev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Convex Compositional Energy Minimization (CCEM)框架，通过将每个因子参数化为输入凸神经网络，并在可行集的紧凸松弛上优化组合能量，解决了组合推理中非凸能量景观的关键瓶颈问题。该方法在小规模子问题或单一问题规模上训练后，可直接迁移到更大实例上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合能量模型在泛化到更大组合推理问题时，主要瓶颈并非组合本身，而是学习到的能量景观的非凸几何结构导致的优化困难。

Method: 提出CCEM框架：1) 每个因子用输入凸神经网络参数化；2) 在可行集的紧凸松弛上优化组合能量（凸性在求和下保持，全局松弛目标仍为凸）；3) 两阶段训练：因子级对比学习塑造局部能量盆地，然后通过展开的投影求解器进行端到端精炼。

Result: 在小规模子问题或单一问题大小上训练的模型，无需重新训练即可直接迁移到更大规模的实例上。

Conclusion: 组合推理的关键瓶颈不是组合本身，而是能量景观的非凸几何；通过凸松弛和凸神经网络参数化，可以有效解决该问题并实现零样本规模泛化。

Abstract: Compositional energy-based models can generalize to larger combinatorial reasoning problems by reusing a learned factor energy across many local constraints. In our paper, we show that a key bottleneck in compositional reasoning is not composition itself, but the non-convex geometry of the learned energy landscape. To solve this problem, we introduce Convex Compositional Energy Minimization (CCEM), a framework that parameterizes each factor with an input-convex neural network and optimizes the composed energy over a tight convex relaxation of the feasible set. Because convexity is preserved under summation, the global relaxed objective remains convex, enabling deterministic projected first-order optimization. CCEM is trained in two stages: factor-level contrastive learning to shape local energy basins, followed by end-to-end refinement through an unrolled projected solver. Our experiments show that our models trained on small subproblems or a single problem size transfer to larger instances without retraining.

</details>


### [95] [Learning Through Noise: Why Subliminal Learning Works and When It Fails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23645)
*Vincent C. Brockers,Roman D. Ventzke,Valentin Neuhaus,Belén Hidalgo-Ogalde,Viola Priesemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络中的"潜意识学习"现象，发现其并非依赖于师生模型的初始化匹配，而是由兼容的输出头（auxiliary head和class head）所驱动。即使在隐藏层随机初始化、增减层或改变架构的情况下，兼容的输出头仍能实现任务无关数据上的知识迁移，并推导出该机制的理论上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究将潜意识学习归因于师生模型初始化的高度匹配，但本文作者质疑这一解释是否完备，意图揭示其真正的核心机制。

Method: 在MNIST上设计可控实验，将网络输出分为auxiliary head（处理任务无关噪声）和class head（处理分类任务），通过随机初始化隐藏层、移除/添加层、改变架构（MLP→CNN）等方式验证潜意识学习的发生条件，并给出理论分析。

Result: 发现潜意识学习的关键在于兼容的输出头，而非初始化匹配。兼容的auxiliary head能够传递可恢复的教师信号，使学生表征向教师靠拢；当class head也兼容时，仅在噪声上训练的学生能达到甚至匹配教师的任务性能。

Conclusion: 将潜意识学习从一种令人惊讶的迁移效应转变为具有可预测界限的理论化机制，为该领域提供了更清晰的理论基础。

Abstract: In the context of artificial neural networks, subliminal learning refers to the transfer of task-relevant knowledge or unintended biases from teacher to student models through distillation on task-unrelated input$\unicode{x2013}$output pairs. Prior explanations tie this effect to shared or closely matched teacher$\unicode{x2013}$student initialization. We show that a closely matched initialization is not necessary. Instead, subliminal learning is governed by compatible output heads. Using a controlled MNIST setting, we split outputs into an auxiliary head (for auxiliary, task-unrelated noise signals) and a class head (for classification) to demonstrate subliminal learning occurs$\unicode{x2014}$even when we randomly initialize hidden layers and remove layers, add new layers, or change the architecture (MLP-to-CNN). Compatible auxiliary heads enable transfer of a recoverable teacher signal, bringing the student's representations closer to the teacher's. When the class heads remain compatible as well, students trained only on task-unrelated noise can approach, and in favorable regimes match, teacher-level task performance. Our setting enables us to develop a theory that explains the mechanism of subliminal learning and to derive upper bounds on when subliminal learning fails. Together, our results turn subliminal learning from a surprising transfer effect into a theoretically grounded mechanism with predictable limits.

</details>


### [96] [It's the humans, not the data: Geopolitical bias in LLMs originates in post-training, amplified by the language of the prompt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23825)
*Stuart Bladon,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过测试7个开源LLM的基础版和聊天版在28个国家配对上的表现，发现地缘政治偏见主要源于后训练阶段而非预训练阶段。6个模型在post-training后向开发者所在国家/地区偏移，其中阿里Qwen2.5的中国偏好从-0.15变为+2.91（18倍变化），Mistral仅在使用法语提示时出现亲法偏见。结论：地缘政治偏好不是简单继承自训练数据，而是由对齐过程主动塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"语言模型地缘政治偏见源自预训练数据"的普遍假设，系统性地探究偏见究竟在模型开发的哪个阶段（预训练 vs 后训练）产生。

Method: 使用配对场景二选一探测方法，在28个国家对上测试7个实验室的7对开源LLM（基础版和聊天版），涉及英语、法语和中文三种语言，通过log-odds统计量量化偏好变化。

Result: 后训练导致6/7的模型向开发者所在国家偏移；阿里Qwen2.5基础版对中国偏好为-0.15（p=0.15，中性），聊天版变为+2.91（p<10^-4），偏好增加18倍；Mistral仅在法语提示下出现亲法偏见（法语-英语差异+1.91，p<10^-4）。

Conclusion: 地缘政治偏好不是从互联网数据中被动继承，而是被后训练过程主动塑造，呼吁对模型对齐过程提高透明度、加强审计和监督。

Abstract: It has generally been assumed that geopolitical bias in language models originates from the training data used during the pre-training phase. We tested seven open-weight LLM pairs consisting of the base model (pre-training only) and the chat model (pre-training and post-training) from seven labs on a paired-scenario forced-choice probe over 28 country pairs in English, French, and Chinese, and found that geopolitical bias originates in post-training rather than in pre-training. Across seven AI labs, six showed shifts in the direction associated with the country or region of the model developer after post-training. This shift is strongest in Alibaba's Qwen 2.5: while the base is neutral on China-favourability (-0.15 log-odds, p=0.15), the post-trained chat variant is at +2.91 (p<10^-4), an 18x shift in odds. We also observe shifts in biases toward other countries across all models. Additionally, the magnitude of this shift depends on the language used to prompt the model: the French-made Mistral becomes pro-France only under French prompting (FR-EN shift +1.91, p<10^-4). These findings suggest that geopolitical preferences in language models are not simply inherited from large-scale internet data but are actively shaped during post-training, highlighting the need for greater transparency, auditing, and oversight of alignment processes that influence how models represent nations, cultures, and political perspectives.

</details>


### [97] [Hybrid Quantum-Classical Corrective Diffusion Modeling for Meteorological Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23403)
*Rui Wang,Edoardo Pasetto,Amer Delilbasic,Morris Riedel,Kristel Michielsen,Gabriele Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合量子-经典校正扩散模型，用于气象场的概率统计降尺度。该模型在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈层插入变分量子电路层，保持回归分支完全经典，以测试量子电路作为紧凑非线性特征图进行潜通道混合的能力。在10米风场成分上评估了通道内和跨通道的量子电路设计。2020验证集上，混合模型保持稳定，保留了生成风场的大尺度空间结构，并在多个配置下相对于经典校正扩散模型提升了MAE和CRPS。结构诊断表明混合变体保持了与经典模型相似的动能谱和风速分布，同时在尾行为、极端风速定位和联合风场分量结构上产生可控变化。后端研究表明在测试电路规模下模拟设备噪声影响可忽略，但真实硬件部署仍受限于量子比特可用性和执行保真度。2021年分布外测试显示分布内增益在时间偏移下未能均匀迁移，揭示了泛化差距。结果表明瓶颈级量子混合可为气象统计降尺度做出非平凡贡献，同时也凸显了电路规模和硬件部署仍是关键限制因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气象统计降尺度需要从粗分辨率输入重建高分辨率输出，传统方法计算成本高昂。本文探索利用量子计算在深度学习模型瓶颈层中作为紧凑非线性特征映射的潜力，以提升统计降尺度性能并降低计算开销。

Method: 提出混合量子-经典校正扩散模型，在扩散UNet的最压缩瓶颈层插入变分量子电路（VQC）层，回归分支保持完全经典。设计了通道内和跨通道两种量子电路ansätze，并在10米风场成分上进行评估。

Result: 2020验证集上混合模型在多个配置下优于经典基线，提升了MAE和CRPS；保持了动能谱和风速分布相似性；尾行为和极端风速定位产生可控变化；模拟噪声影响可忽略但真实硬件受限；2021分布外测试显示泛化差距。

Conclusion: 瓶颈级量子混合可为气象统计降尺度做出非平凡贡献，但电路规模限制和硬件部署保真度仍是关键限制因素，未来需要稳定化和正则化来弥补泛化差距。

Abstract: Statistical downscaling is a crucial component of the weather modeling field, where high-resolution outputs must be reconstructed from coarse-resolution inputs with the full cost of dynamical refinement. In this work, we investigate a hybrid quantum-classical corrective diffusion model for probabilistic statistical downscaling of weather fields. The proposed model inserts variational quantum circuit layers into the most compressed bottleneck of the diffusion UNet while leaving the regression branch fully classical. This placement tests whether quantum circuits can act as compact nonlinear feature maps for latent-channel mixing. We evaluate intra-channel and cross-channel ansätze on 10m wind components. On the 2020 validation set, the hybrid models remain stable, preserve the large-scale spatial organization of the generated wind fields, and improve both MAE and CRPS relative to a classical corrective diffusion model in several configurations. Structural diagnostics further show that the hybrid variants preserve kinetic-energy spectra and windspeed distributions similar to its classical counterpart while producing controlled changes in tail behavior, extreme-windspeed localization, and joint wind field components structure. Backend studies on the 2020 validation set show negligible impact from simulated device noise at the tested circuit scale, whereas real-hardware deployment remains limited by qubit availability and execution fidelity. The 2021 out-of-distribution test shows that these in-distribution gains do not transfer uniformly under temporal shift, revealing a generalization gap that motivates future mitigation through stabilization and regularization. These results show that bottleneck-level quantum hybridization can make a nontrivial contribution to weather statistical downscaling, while also highlighting that circuit scale and hardware deployment remain key limiting factors.

</details>


### [98] [Leveraging Foundation Models for Causal Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23861)
*Aneesh Komanduri,Xintao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FM-CGM是一个模块化框架，利用预训练基础模型（大语言模型+文本到图像扩散模型）实现零样本因果发现、干预和反事实生成，并通过因果语义引导（CSG）机制确保干预语义正确传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果生成模型缺乏统一框架来利用预训练基础模型的零样本推理能力，难以实现端到端的视觉因果推理。

Method: 提出FM-CGM框架，包含三个核心组件：概念提取器、概念操作器和反事实生成器；使用大语言模型进行因果推理，文本到图像扩散模型进行生成；开发因果语义引导（CSG）机制，基于交叉注意力确保语义干预正确传播到后代概念，同时保持不变区域。

Result: 该方法能够识别合理的因果结构，并生成保真的反事实图像。

Conclusion: FM-CGM通过模块化设计和预训练基础模型的零样本能力，实现了有效的视觉因果推理和保真反事实图像生成。

Abstract: Causal generative modeling is essential for developing reliable and transparent AI systems capable of counterfactual reasoning. While existing approaches focus on integrating causal constraints during the training of generative models, they often lack a unified framework to leverage the zero-shot reasoning capabilities of pretrained foundation models. We introduce FM-CGM, a modular framework for end-to-end visual causal reasoning using pretrained foundation models. FM-CGM formalizes the causal pipeline through three core components: a concept extractor, a concept manipulator, and a counterfactual generator. By leveraging a large reasoning model for causal inference and a text-to-image diffusion model for generation, our approach enables zero-shot causal discovery, intervention, and counterfactual generation. We then develop Causal Semantic Guidance (CSG), a cross-attention-based mechanism that ensures semantic interventions propagate to descendant concepts while preserving invariant regions. We empirically show that our approach can identify plausible causal structures and is suitable for faithful counterfactual image generation.

</details>


### [99] [What Linear Probes Miss: Multi-View Probing for Weight-Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23410)
*Eunwoo Heo,Kyeongkook Seo,Jaejun Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MVProbe提出了一种多视角探测框架，通过融合一阶信号和基于Gram矩阵的交互感知视图，在模型丛林基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探测方法采用单视图设计，只能捕获一阶结构，无法编码权重空间中行-列交互的高阶相关模式，限制了模型分析的表达能力。

Method: MVProbe是一种多视角探测框架，通过合成一阶信号与交互感知的Gram矩阵视图来实现。理论上分析了不同探测阶次的标度律，推导出原理性的标准化与融合策略，确保各分支贡献平衡。

Result: 在Model Jungle基准测试中，MVProbe在多种架构上持续优于现有最优方法ProbeX，包括判别式骨干网络（ResNet、SupViT、MAE、DINO）和大规模生成式LoRA适配器（Stable Diffusion LoRA）。

Conclusion: 通过引入多视角融合策略，MVProbe有效弥补了单视图探测方法在高阶交互建模上的不足，为无文档/元数据的模型仓库中的模型识别与分析提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: The explosive growth of open-source model repositories has created a Model Jungle, where checkpoints are frequently shared without adequate documentation or metadata. While weight-space learning offers a pathway to identify and analyze these models directly from their parameters, processing full-scale weights is computationally prohibitive. Probing-based methods have emerged as a lightweight alternative, extracting permutation-equivariant representations via learnable probe vectors. However, existing probing methods are limited by a single-view design: they capture first-order structures but fail to encode the rich, higher-order correlation patterns inherent in row-column interactions. To bridge this gap, we introduce MVProbe, a multi-perspective probing framework that synthesizes first-order signals with interaction-aware (Gram-based) views. Our approach is theoretically grounded; we analyze the scaling laws of different probing orders to derive a principled standardization and fusion strategy that ensures balanced contributions from all branches. On the Model Jungle benchmark, MVProbe consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art ProbeX across diverse architectures, including discriminative backbones (ResNet, SupViT, MAE, DINO) and large-scale generative LoRA adapters (Stable Diffusion LoRA).

</details>


### [100] [LLMs as Noisy Channels: A Shannon Perspective on Model Capacity and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23901)
*Xu Ouyang,Deyi Liu,Yuhang Cai,Jing Liu,Yuan Yang,Chen Zheng,Thomas Hartvigsen,Yiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出香农缩放定律（Shannon Scaling Law），将LLM训练建模为噪声信道上的信息传输，揭示了模型缩放中的U型性能退化现象，并通过实验验证了该理论在预测能力和拟合优度上优于传统单调幂律缩放定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM缩放定律（单调幂律）无法解释灾难性过训练和量化退化等非单调现象，即增加计算量反而导致性能下降。

Method: 基于香农-哈特利定理，将LLM训练建模为噪声信道上的信息传输：模型参数映射为信道带宽，训练token映射为信号功率，通过信噪比（SNR）刻画学习信号与内在噪声的相互作用，推导出LLM的香农容量。

Result: 在Pythia和OLMo2上的实验（高斯噪声、量化、SFT等扰动）表明：香农缩放定律在R²分数上优于经典缩放定律和扰动感知定律；在≤6.9B/≤180B tokens上拟合后，可预测12B模型在307B tokens的表现（R²=0.847），而单调基线模型失效。

Conclusion: 香农缩放定律统一解释了单调和非单调缩放现象，指出盲目扩展模型或数据而不维持足够SNR会放大噪声，导致性能从单调提升转为U型退化，为LLM的理性缩放提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Existing scaling laws for Large Language Models (LLMs), predominantly monotonic power laws, fail to explain emerging non-monotonic phenomena such as catastrophic overtraining and quantization-induced degradation, where performance deteriorates despite increased compute.
  We propose the Shannon Scaling Law, a unified theoretical framework that models LLM training as information transmission over a noisy channel, grounded in the Shannon-Hartley theorem. By mapping model parameters to channel bandwidth and training tokens to signal power, our formulation explicitly captures the interaction between learning signal and intrinsic noise. This perspective reveals a fundamental Shannon capacity for LLMs: scaling model size or data without preserving a sufficient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) inevitably amplifies noise, inducing a transition from monotonic improvement to U-shaped performance degradation.
  We validate our theory through experiments on Pythia and OLMo2 under perturbations, including Gaussian noise, quantization and supervised fine-tuning on math, QA and code tasks. The Shannon Scaling Law consistently outperforms classical scaling laws and recent perturbation-aware laws, achieving strong $R^2$ scores and accurately capturing loss basins missed by prior approaches. It also extrapolates: fitted on $\leq$6.9B Pythia models with $\leq$180B tokens, it predicts the unseen 12B model up to 307B tokens at pooled $R^2{=}0.847$, while monotonic baselines collapse.

</details>


### [101] [Sample-wise Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Test-time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23411)
*Phuc Duc Nguyen,Quang Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对测试时自适应（TTA）的样本级定向攻击方法，通过元学习和优先级感知梯度对齐策略，在保持全局标签分布不变的前提下，仅对携带触发器的样本进行误分类，实现高成功率和低可检测性，并在多个基准数据集上验证了其有效性及对现有防御的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA下的类别级定向攻击由于批量操作会导致目标标签频率异常升高，容易被检测。为捕捉更现实的威胁场景，需要一种能够在TTA场景下实现隐蔽、可检测性低的样本级定向攻击方法。

Method: 提出基于元学习的攻击框架，核心是优先级感知梯度对齐策略。该策略将梯度更新建模为椭球信任域问题，缓解攻击成功与分布隐蔽性之间的梯度不对齐，并提供理论保证以实现攻击目标的有效优化。

Result: 在CIFAR-10-C、CIFAR-100-C和ImageNet-C上，该方法在多种TTA协议下实现了高定向攻击成功率，同时保持标签分布与无攻击基线一致，难以被检测；此外对现有防御方法表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了TTA场景下一种更隐蔽、更现实的定向攻击威胁，即样本级攻击；所提方法在攻击成功率和隐蔽性之间取得了良好平衡，对防御具有鲁棒性，为TTA安全研究提供了新的视角和挑战。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) effectively counters distribution shifts but exposes models to adversarial manipulation via the unlabeled test stream. Existing class-wise targeted attacks remain impractical for stealthy exploitation in this setting: since TTA operates on batches, forcing a subset of samples toward a target label unintentionally pulls similar benign samples along, resulting in a conspicuously high frequency of the target label that is easy to detect. To capture a more realistic threat, we introduce a sample-wise targeted attack. Unlike prior approaches, the attacker aims to misclassify only inputs carrying an attacker-chosen trigger, while preserving the global label distribution of benign queries to evade detection. To achieve this, we propose a meta-learning-based attack with a novel priority-aware gradient alignment strategy that explicitly prioritizes attack success. The strategy formulates the gradient update as an ellipsoidal trust-region problem, mitigating the misalignment between attack success and distributional stealth, while providing theoretical guarantees for effective optimization of the attack objective in the presence of gradient misalignment. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10-C, CIFAR-100-C, and ImageNet-C across TTA protocols demonstrate that our method achieves high targeted success rates while maintaining a label distribution that is consistent with the no-attack baseline, making it difficult to detect in unlabeled TTA deployment scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our attack shows strong robustness against existing defenses.

</details>


### [102] [An Open-Source Training Dataset for Foundation Models for Black-box Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23417)
*Aaron Klein,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Luca Thale-Bombien,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBO-Pile，首个大规模开源黑箱优化轨迹数据集（含50万+优化轨迹、3095个黑箱问题），并基于该数据集训练了从2M到80M参数的系列基础模型，证明大规模预训练能有效模仿黑箱优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑箱优化方法依赖大量超参数调优，泛化性差；虽然基础模型为优化提供了新思路，但缺乏大规模、真实世界、公开可用的预训练数据，限制了该领域的可重复性和泛化能力。

Method: 构建BBO-Pile数据集，包含超过50万条优化轨迹、覆盖3095个不同黑箱问题和多种优化器；训练多个尺度的基础模型（2M-80M参数，200M-20亿训练token），研究其计算扩展行为。

Result: 大规模预训练是可行且有效的黑箱优化模仿方法，模型性能随计算量扩展而提升。

Conclusion: BBO-Pile数据集和基于其训练的基础模型为黑箱优化领域提供了公开基准和可复现的研究基础，为未来研究方向铺平道路。

Abstract: Most black-box optimization methods require extensive hyperparameter tuning, often limiting their ability to generalize across different optimization domains. Foundation models for black-box optimization that learn optimization principles from a large collection of optimization trajectories offer a promising alternative, with the potential to outperform manually designed methods across diverse problem classes. However, prior work has either relied on non-public datasets or on purely synthetic data, limiting reproducibility and generalization to real-world problems. As a result, progress in this area has been constrained by the lack of large-scale, real-world, publicly available pre-training data. We introduce BBO-Pile, the first open-source dataset comprising over 500K optimization trajectories evaluated across 3095 different black-boxes for different optimizers, which represents by far the largest public dataset for this task. Using this dataset, we train a family of foundation models at multiple scales, ranging from 2M to 80M parameters and from 200M to 2B training tokens, and study their scaling behavior with respect to compute. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pre-training is a viable and effective approach to imitate black-box optimization methods, paving the way for future research in this direction.

</details>


### [103] [Hinge Regression Trees and HRT-Boost: Newton-Optimized Oblique Learning for Compact Tabular Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23422)
*Hongyi Li,Jun Xu,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出铰链回归树（HRT）框架，将斜决策树的分裂优化转化为非线性最小二乘问题，并基于此设计了HRT-Boost集成方法。理论证明了HRT是通用逼近器且具有O(δ²)逼近率，HRT-Boost在平方损失下具有阶段式经验风险降低保证。实验表明HRT在单树上具有竞争力，HRT-Boost在集成模型上表现优越且模型更紧凑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜决策树的分裂优化本质上是离散且非凸的，导致学习高质量斜决策树长期面临挑战，现有方法难以有效处理这一优化难题。

Method: 提出HRT框架，将每个斜分裂重构为两个线性预测器的非线性最小二乘问题，其max/min包络产生类似ReLU的表达能力。节点级优化可解释为阻尼牛顿法，并设计了带回溯线搜索的单调递减变体。在HRT基础上，提出HRT-Boost集成扩展，将节点级牛顿更新与阶段式函数梯度下降相结合。

Result: 理论证明了HRT是通用逼近器且具有O(δ²)显式逼近率；HRT-Boost在平方损失下具有阶段式经验风险降低保证。实验上，HRT在单树基线上极具竞争力，HRT-Boost优于强集成基线且模型更紧凑。

Conclusion: HRT框架有效解决了斜决策树的优化难题，HRT-Boost在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了模型复杂度，为决策树集成提供了一种高效且理论完备的新方案。

Abstract: Learning high-quality oblique decision trees remains a significant challenge due to the discrete and non-convex nature of split optimization. We present the Hinge Regression Tree (HRT) framework, which reframes each oblique split as a nonlinear least-squares problem over two linear predictors whose max/min envelope induces ReLU-like representation capacity. We show that the resulting node-level optimization can be interpreted as a damped Newton method, and we establish the monotonic decrease of the node objective for its backtracking line-search variant. We establish, theoretically, that HRT is a universal approximator with an explicit $O(δ^2)$ approximation rate. Building upon this base learner, we propose HRT-Boost, a mathematically synergistic ensemble extension that couples node-level Newton updates with stage-wise functional gradient descent. We show that this ensemble construction admits a stage-wise empirical risk reduction guarantee under the squared loss. Empirical evaluations on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that HRT is highly competitive with established single-tree baselines, and HRT-Boost compares favorably with strong ensemble baselines and often yields substantially more compact models. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Hongyi-Li-sz/HRT-Boost.

</details>


### [104] [Onsager-Machlup Posterior Transport for Deep Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23434)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OM-Path方法，将深度高斯过程（DGP）中的近似推断问题重新定义为后验传输（posterior transport），通过确定性采样器将可处理的参考测度映射到后验相关的诱导变量，并利用来自Doob桥参考扩散的路径先验进行正则化。该方法在UCI回归基准上对大型数据集取得了统计显著的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度高斯过程（DGP）中诱导变量的近似推断是计算瓶颈，现有方法（如DSVI、IPVI、DDVI、DBVI等基于ELBO的变分推断或SGHMC的MCMC采样）在计算效率和推断质量上存在不足，需要一种更高效且理论上更完善的推断框架。

Method: 将DGP推断重新表述为后验传输问题，学习一个确定性采样器将可处理的参考测度映射到后验相关的诱导变量。具体实现（OM-Path）使用Song的概率流ODE应用于DBVI的Doob桥前向SDE；参考漂移项通过桥边际系数闭式可得（无需分数匹配）；路径正则化器采用Onsager-Machlup作用量。在有限ε训练时，目标函数是 tempered Doob桥路径后验的未归一化负对数密度，定理1通过Freidlin-Wentzell大偏差原理将其等同于同一后验的小噪声MAP路径。还推导了两种严格的路径空间ELBO变体（FFJORD log-det; OM-正则化CNF）作为消融实验。

Result: 在七个UCI回归基准上与DBVI进行匹配种子的配对Wilcoxon检验：OM-Path在两个最大数据集上取得统计显著胜利（power: p=0.014, NLL 0.012匹配DSVI基线0.017；protein: p=0.002, RMSE 0.716 vs 0.764, NLL 1.086 vs 1.149）；在yacht/qsar上统计持平；在小样本噪声数据（boston/energy/concrete）上逊于DBVI。严格ELBO变体在所有UCI指标上均未超越DBVI。

Conclusion: 在该实验设定下，降低路径目标函数的方差比精确追踪密度更重要。OM-Path通过后验传输框架为DGP推断提供了一种新的有效途径，特别适合大数据集场景。

Abstract: Approximate inference over inducing variables is the central computational bottleneck of Deep Gaussian Processes (DGPs). Existing methods either fit an explicit density $q_φ(\bU)$ by an ELBO (DSVI, IPVI, DDVI, DBVI) or sample by MCMC (SGHMC). We instead frame DGP inference as \emph{posterior transport}: learn a deterministic sampler that maps a tractable reference measure to posterior-relevant inducing variables, regularised by a path prior derived from the Doob-bridged reference diffusion. Our realisation, \textbf{OM-Path} (formally FBVI-bridge-Path), uses Song's probability-flow ODE applied to DBVI's Doob-bridged forward SDE; the reference drift is closed-form from the bridge marginal coefficients (no score matching) and the path regulariser is the \textbf{Onsager--Machlup action}. At the finite-$ε$ value used at training, the objective is the negative log unnormalised density of a tempered Doob-bridge path posterior, and Theorem 1 identifies it with the same posterior's small-noise MAP path via the Freidlin--Wentzell LDP. Two strict path-space ELBO variants on the same bridge backbone (FFJORD log-det; OM-regularised CNF) are derived as ablations. Under a matched-seed paired Wilcoxon test against DBVI on seven UCI regression benchmarks, OM-Path delivers statistically significant wins on the two largest datasets (\textit{power}: $p\!=\!0.014$, NLL $\mathbf{0.012}$ matching the DSVI baseline of $0.017$; \textit{protein}: $p\!=\!0.002$, RMSE $\mathbf{0.716}$ vs.\ $0.764$, NLL $\mathbf{1.086}$ vs.\ $1.149$), statistical ties on \textit{yacht} / \textit{qsar}, and concedes \textit{boston} / \textit{energy} / \textit{concrete} to DBVI on small-$N$ noisy data. The strict-ELBO variants do not clear DBVI on any UCI metric: in this regime, reducing the variance of the path objective dominates exact-density tracking.

</details>


### [105] [Weisfeiler-Leman Is Incomplete on Simple Spectrum Graphs, so Canonicalize Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23446)
*Snir Hordan,Nadav Dym,Tim Seppelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明具有简单谱的图虽可被立方时间同构测试识别，但k-WL测试及所有对齐GNN无法区分所有非同构的简单谱图。为此提出PRiSM，首个对简单谱特征分解具有完备性保证的规范化方法，结合DeepSets或Transformer可实现通用逼近，实验表现优于现有谱规范化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN（包括所有k-WL对齐的GNN族）无法完全区分所有非同构的简单谱图，而先前的规范化方法缺乏完备性保证。本文旨在解决简单谱图上实现完全表达性的开放问题。

Method: 提出PRiSM（Partition, Refine, Solve, Match）框架，通过分区、精化、求解、匹配四个步骤对简单谱特征分解进行可证明完备的规范化，消除特征向量符号翻转和排列不确定性。

Result: PRiSM是首个对简单谱特征分解具有完备性保证的规范化方法，解决了先前方法缺乏完备性的问题。结合DeepSets或Transformer可在简单谱图上实现通用逼近，在图回归、分类和表达性任务上表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: PRiSM填补了k-WL测试和GNN在简单谱图上的表达性空白，为谱规范化提供了理论上完备的解决方案，实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Graphs with a simple spectrum admit cubic-time isomorphism testing, yet we prove that for every natural number $k$, the $k$-Weisfeiler-Leman ($k$-WL) test cannot distinguish all non-isomorphic graphs with a simple spectrum. As the WL hierarchy upper-bounds the distinguishing power of widely-used Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), this incompleteness applies to all such GNNs, ruling out completeness for every $k$-WL-aligned GNN family. To close this gap, we introduce PRiSM (Partition, Refine, Solve, Match), the first provably complete canonicalization of simple-spectrum eigendecompositions. PRiSM obtains the completeness guarantee that prior canonicalizations provably lack, and resolves the open problem of achieving complete expressivity on simple-spectrum graphs. When composed with DeepSets or a Transformer, PRiSM achieves universal approximation on simple-spectrum graphs, justifying the use of canonicalized Laplacian positional encodings. Empirically, PRiSM performs comparably to or outperforms existing spectral canonicalizations on graph regression, classification, and expressivity

</details>


### [106] [Commutator-Induced Uncertainty in VAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23449)
*Tahereh Dehdarirad,Michael Felsberg,Gabriel Eilertsen,Ziliang Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lie Group VAE框架，通过两阶段训练——先无约束训练并诊断非交换性，再引入形变稳定性约束——来解决VAE难以表示潜在空间中非交换结构的问题，在多个基准上提升了重建质量和解码器行为的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的对称性感知VAE通过代数正则化强制交换性，这适用于交换变换群，但会抑制数据中固有的非交换结构。现有方法缺乏对非交换性的显式诊断和反映机制。

Method: 提出Lie Group VAE框架：第一阶段无约束训练，通过有限BCH偏差测量代数非交换性，通过重建交换测试测量解码器顺序敏感性；第二阶段引入形变稳定性约束，使用数据驱动的校准常数对齐解码器敏感性与代数非交换性。框架还将离散生成因子与连续几何变换分离。

Result: 在dSprites、3DShapes、3DCars和CelebA上，与beta-VAE、CLG-VAE、CFASL等基线相比，该方法改善了重建质量，使解码器行为与非交换潜在结构更一致。在CelebA上重建更忠实，潜在遍历更因子特定，并展现出有意义的顺序依赖交互。

Conclusion: 非交换性应被显式诊断并反映在重建行为中，而非被正则化压制；所提框架能有效对齐代数非交换性与解码器行为。

Abstract: Variational autoencoders (VAEs) often struggle to represent non-commutative structure in learned latent spaces. Symmetry-aware VAEs commonly address this issue by enforcing commutativity through algebraic regularization, which is appropriate for commutative transformation groups but can suppress meaningful non-commutative structure when it is intrinsic to the data. We argue that non-commutativity should instead be explicitly diagnosed and reflected in reconstruction behavior. We introduce a Lie Group VAE framework that combines geometric and algebraic perspectives on uncertainty while separating discrete generative factors from continuous geometric transformations. In a first phase, the model is trained without structural constraints while algebraic non-commutativity is measured through finite Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff deviations and decoder order sensitivity is measured through reconstruction order-swap tests. These diagnostics reveal a scale mismatch between latent non-commutativity and reconstruction behavior under unconstrained training. In a second phase, we introduce a deformation-stability constraint with a data-driven calibration constant that aligns decoder sensitivity with algebraic non-commutativity. We evaluate the framework on dSprites, 3DShapes, 3DCars, and CelebA against generic and symmetry-aware baselines, including beta-VAE, CLG-VAE, and CFASL. Across synthetic benchmarks, the method improves reconstruction quality and yields decoder-level behavior more consistent with latent non-commutative structure. Qualitative analyses show clearer order-dependent latent compositions and more stable reconstructions. On CelebA, the model yields more faithful reconstructions and factor-specific latent traversals than CFASL, while also exhibiting meaningful order-dependent interactions between learned latent directions.

</details>


### [107] [Class-Dependent Hybrid Data Augmentation for Multiclass Migraine Classification under Severe Class Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23453)
*Elvin Somón,Miguel A. Gutiérrez-Naranjo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对偏头痛分类研究中的方法学缺陷（数据泄露、指标偏差）进行修正，提出临床驱动的亚型聚合、基于样本量的混合增强策略以及保比例增长替代完全平衡的概念。在400名患者、七种亚型的数据集上，修正后基线宏F1为0.71，提出框架在八种分类器上平均宏F1达0.862，优于各单独增强方法（0.836/0.815）及无增强基线（0.801），FT-Transformer在保比例增强下取得峰值0.914，但无增强基线已达0.896，表明临床聚合贡献了大部分改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人偏头痛分类研究存在数据泄露和指标偏差等方法学缺陷，导致性能被高估。本文旨在修正这些问题，并探索更合理的分类框架。

Method: (i) 依据ICHD-3 §1.2.3对两种偏瘫亚型进行临床驱动的类别聚合；(ii) 提出基于各类别样本量分配生成方法的类别依赖混合增强策略；(iii) 提出保比例增长替代完全类别平衡。采用两阶段协议，在400名患者、七种偏头痛亚型数据集上评估，使用分层5折交叉验证和宏平均F1。

Result: 修正后宏F1基线为0.71。提出框架在八种分类器上平均宏F1达0.862，优于Gaussian Copula（0.836）、CTGAN（0.815）和无增强基线（0.801）。FT-Transformer在保比例增强下取得峰值0.914，而无增强基线为0.896。

Conclusion: 临床驱动的类别聚合是性能提升的主要贡献因素，框架的主要可衡量贡献在于提升各分类器的平均鲁棒性，问题公式化（problem formulation）的主导作用被凸显。

Abstract: We conducted a reproducibility-oriented re-evaluation of prior migraine classification studies, correcting for data leakage and metric bias. We then introduced (i) a clinically motivated aggregation of two hemiplegic subtypes following ICHD-3 §1.2.3, (ii) a class-dependent hybrid augmentation strategy that assigns generation methods based on per-class sample size, and (iii) the concept of fidelity asymmetry, motivating proportionally constrained growth as an alternative to full class balance. Experiments were performed on a dataset of 400 patients across seven migraine subtypes under a two-stage protocol, including the six-class configuration described above. Models were evaluated using stratified 5-fold cross-validation with macro-averaged F1 as the primary metric.
  Correcting methodological flaws reduces previously inflated performance estimates, with the corrected macro-F1 baseline standing at 0.71. The proposed framework consistently outperformed individual augmenters in macro-F1 averaged across the eight evaluated classifiers (0.862 vs. 0.836 for Gaussian Copula, 0.815 for CTGAN, and 0.801 for the no-augmentation baseline), and achieved its peak result of 0.914 with FT-Transformer under proportional augmentation. The no-augmentation FT-Transformer baseline (0.896) shows that, at the per-classifier ceiling, clinically motivated class aggregation accounts for most of the absolute improvement; the framework's principal measurable contribution is the gain in average robustness across classifiers, highlighting the dominant role of problem formulation.

</details>


### [108] [Unextractable Protocol Models: Collaborative Training and Inference without Weight Materialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23464)
*Alexander Long,Chamin Hewa Koneputugodage,Thalaiyasingam Ajanthan,Yan Zuo,Gil Avraham,Violetta Shevchenko,Hadi Mohaghegh Dolatabadi,Sameera Ramasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unextractable Protocol Models (UPMs)，一种在去中心化训练框架中通过周期性注入时间变化可逆变换来确保模型分片在不同时间步不兼容、防止完整权重被提取的方法，在保持模型性能的同时实现可证明的抗提取性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化协作训练大型神经网络场景中，参与者仅处理模型子集（分片），但完整权重集可能被恶意参与者重建或提取。现有方法缺乏对"不可物化权重"（即没有任何参与者持有完整权重）的形式化保障机制。

Method: 提出UPMs框架：在参与者边界周期性注入时间变化的随机可逆变换，这些变换保持整体网络功能不变，但使得不同时间步的分片权重在跨时间组合时产生不兼容性。设计了变换的生成、注入和同步协议，并分析了不同精度数据类型下的数值稳定性。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5-0.5B和Llama-3.2-1B上，10000次变换后FP32困惑度变化<0.01，Jensen-Shannon散度<4×10^{-5}；每30秒应用一次变换带来推理3%延迟、0.1%带宽和10%显存开销，训练开销降至1.6%时间和<1%内存；基于梯度的拼接分区微调需要从头训练60%以上的token。

Conclusion: UPMs使得模型可以协作训练但不可被提取，为社区驱动的去中心化训练中嵌入程序化激励机制提供了实用基础，实现了训练与所有权分离的新范式。

Abstract: We consider a decentralized setup in which the participants collaboratively train and serve a large neural network, and where each participant only processes a subset of the model. In this setup, we explore the possibility of unmaterializable weights, where a full weight set is never available to any one participant. We introduce Unextractable Protocol Models (UPMs): a training and inference framework that leverages the sharded model setup to ensure model shards (i.e., subsets) held by participants are incompatible at different time steps. UPMs periodically inject time-varying, random, invertible transforms at participant boundaries; preserving the overall network function yet rendering cross-time assemblies incoherent. On Qwen-2.5-0.5B and Llama-3.2-1B, 10,000 transforms leave FP32 perplexity unchanged ($Δ$PPL $< 0.01$; Jensen-Shannon drift $< 4 \times 10^{-5}$), and we show how to control growth for lower precision datatypes. Applying a transform every 30s adds 3% latency, 0.1% bandwidth, and 10% GPU-memory overhead at inference, while training overhead falls to 1.6% time and $< 1$% memory. We consider several attacks, showing that the requirements of direct attacks are impractical and easy to defend against, and that gradient-based fine-tuning of stitched partitions consumes $\geq 60$% of the tokens required to train from scratch. By enabling models to be collaboratively trained yet not extracted, UPMs make it practical to embed programmatic incentive mechanisms in community-driven decentralized training.

</details>


### [109] [S$^3$GNN: Efficient Global Mixing and Local Message Passing for Long-Range Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23467)
*Dai Shi,Luke Thompson,Linhan Luo,Lequan Lin,Andi Han,Junbin Gao,José Miguel Hernández Lobato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MPNN在处理长程依赖时存在信息瓶颈（oversquashing）。现有方法通过光谱滤波缓解该问题，但其雅可比敏感度下界在实践中难以达到。本文提出S³GNN，通过轻量级重新引入被省略的组件，在无需严格假设的情况下缓解oversquashing，并在多个领域（长程基准、KGQA、网格流体动力学）实现高达一个数量级的误差降低和最多50%的参数减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPNN在捕获长程依赖时存在oversquashing问题，现有基于光谱滤波的方法虽然能缓解该问题，但其依赖的雅可比敏感度下界在实际中难以达到，需要更实用的解决方案。

Method: 提出S³GNN，通过轻量级地重新引入被省略的组件（降低计算复杂度），同时保持标准稳定性约束在新的动力学下有效，从而在不依赖强理论假设的情况下缓解oversquashing。

Result: 在长程基准、KGQA和网格流体动力学等多个领域实验中，S³GNN实现高达一个数量级的误差降低，并减少最多50%的参数。

Conclusion: S³GNN通过轻量级方法有效缓解oversquashing问题，无需严格的假设条件，且在多个实际应用中表现优异。

Abstract: Message-passing neural networks (MPNNs) often suffer from an information bottleneck when capturing long-range dependencies, leading to the oversquashing (OSQ) phenomenon. Alongside spatial connectivity enrichment (e.g., rewiring), recent studies have shown that spectral filtering can yield strong long-range learning outcomes, as spectral operators enable global information mixing that alleviates OSQ. These approaches achieve this either by stabilizing the Jacobian energies in deep propagation or by guaranteeing OSQ mitigation under strong theoretical assumptions. We revisit these conclusions and show that the associated Jacobian sensitivity lower bound is generally difficult to achieve in practice. We then propose S$^3$GNN, which mitigates OSQ without such restrictive assumptions by lightweightly reintroducing omitted components with substantially lower computational complexity, while standard stability constraints on feature transformations remain effective under our new dynamics. Extensive experiments across diverse domains (e.g., long-range benchmarks, KGQA, and mesh-based fluid dynamics) demonstrate that S$^3$GNN achieves up to an order-of-magnitude error reduction with up to 50\% fewer parameters. Our code can be found in https://github.com/EEthanShi/S3-GNN.git.

</details>


### [110] [Learning partially observed systems with neural Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23510)
*Sunniva Meltzer,Sølve Eidnes,Alexander Johannes Stasik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NHODE框架，融合哈密顿神经网络和神经常微分方程，用于从部分观测数据学习动力系统，通过嵌入物理结构（能量守恒、对称性、可分离能量）提升预测精度和长期稳定性，在从线性弹簧到混沌三体问题的实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息模型通常假设可访问完整系统状态，这限制了它们在部分观测场景（某些状态变量完全不可观测且无直接监督）中的应用。因此需要一种能从部分观测数据中学习动力系统并嵌入物理结构的方法。

Method: NHODE框架将哈密顿神经网络（HNN）与神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）结合。哈密顿结构通过构造保证能量守恒，神经常微分方程框架允许损失函数仅定义在观测变量上进行灵活训练。同时引入对称感知坐标变换和可分离能量公式以嵌入额外物理约束。

Result: 在从线性/非线性弹簧系统到混沌三体问题的实验中，嵌入更多物理结构可提升预测精度和长期稳定性。NHODE在最具挑战性的场景下也能捕获观测和潜在动力学，而纯数据驱动基线方法变得不稳定。

Conclusion: NHODE框架有效解决了部分观测动力系统的学习问题，通过嵌入物理结构显著提升了模型在复杂和混沌系统中的泛化能力与长期预测稳定性。

Abstract: When learning dynamical systems from data, embedding physical structure can constrain the solution space and improve generalization, but many physics-informed models assume access to the full system state. This limits their use in partially observed settings, where some state variables are completely unobserved and must be inferred without direct supervision. Here, we present neural Hamiltonian ordinary differential equations (NHODE), a framework that combines Hamiltonian neural networks (HNNs) with neural ordinary differential equations (neural ODEs) to learn partially observed dynamical systems from data. The Hamiltonian structure enforces energy conservation by construction, while the neural ODE framework enables a flexible training procedure that allows the loss to be defined only on observed variables. We also incorporate additional physical constraints through symmetry-aware coordinate transformations and separable energy formulations. The framework is evaluated on systems of increasing complexity, from linear and nonlinear mass-spring systems to the chaotic three-body problem. Across all examples, increasing the amount of embedded physical structure improves the accuracy and long-horizon stability of the predictions. Even in the most challenging regimes, the NHODE framework captures both observed and latent dynamics, whereas purely data-driven baselines become unstable.

</details>


### [111] [When One Point Is Not Enough: Addressing Ambiguous Instances in Dimensionality Reduction by Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23540)
*Diede P. M. van der Hoorn,Alessio Arleo,Fernando V. Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出降维可视化中一个被忽视的伪影来源——模糊实例（在原始高维空间中与多个互不相似邻域高度相似的实例），并提出基于图的复制方法，通过在投影中生成多个副本分别放置于对应邻域中，减少部分邻域嵌入失真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降维是有损过程，投影中存在视觉伪影。作者发现模糊实例（ambiguous instances）是伪影的重要来源——这类实例在高维空间中与多个互不相似的邻域高度相似，但标准降维方法只能将其映射为单个点，导致只能部分表示其邻域结构，产生"部分邻域嵌入"失真，这一问题此前被广泛忽视。

Method: 提出一种基于图的方法：(1) 识别高维空间中的模糊实例（与多个互不相似邻域高度相似的实例）；(2) 在投影中将这些模糊实例复制为多个点，每个副本放置在其对应的邻域中。方法基于UMAP实现，但可推广到其他基于局部图的降维技术。

Result: 在多个数据集上，该方法能揭示投影中先前被隐藏的邻域成员关系，减少部分邻域嵌入失真，并通过定量分析提供了进一步支持证据。

Conclusion: 模糊实例是降维可视化中重要的伪影来源，通过复制策略可以有效缓解部分邻域嵌入问题，且该方法具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) methods are widely used to visualize high-dimensional data. One key task in DR-based analysis is discovering neighborhoods, which relies on analyzing the fine-grained local structure of a projection. However, DR is an inherently lossy process; no technique can perfectly preserve the high-dimensional relationships, and projections therefore contain visual artifacts. In this paper, we highlight a typically overlooked source of visual artifacts: ambiguous instances. These are instances that are highly similar to multiple mutually dissimilar neighborhoods in the high-dimensional space.
