Standard DR methods cannot faithfully project such instances, since each data instance is mapped to a single point in the visual space. As a result, such an instance is placed in only one of its neighborhoods (or in none at all), so only part of its neighborhood structure is represented. We call this distortion partial neighborhood embedding. In this paper, we introduce a graph-based approach that identifies ambiguous instances and replicates them as multiple points in the projection, placing each copy within its respective neighborhood. We use UMAP for our results, but our approach also generalizes to other local graph-based DR techniques, and we show that our approach reveals previously hidden neighborhood memberships in projections and reduces partial neighborhood embedding across multiple examples, and is further supported by quantitative analyses.

</details>


### [112] [Is Dimensionality a Barrier for Retrieval Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23556)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Jonathan Kogan,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌入检索模型中，在维度 $d=O(m^{-2}\log n)$ 时即可几乎达到最优间隔 $m$，并证明了对 $k$-稀疏查询，维度 $d = \Theta(k\log (n/k))$ 是必要且充分的。实验表明 sigmoid 损失在产生大间隔嵌入方面优于 InfoNCE 损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释现代低维嵌入（$d\approx 1000$）为何能支撑数十亿甚至万亿规模数据的检索，探究维度与间隔之间的理论关系。

Method: 采用通信复杂度中的最大间隔嵌入模型，通过理论分析建立维度与间隔的充分必要条件，并辅以 InfoNCE 和 sigmoid 损失的实证对比。

Result: 证明在 $d = O(m^{-2}\log n)$ 维度下可几乎达到最优间隔；对 $k$-稀疏查询，$d = \Theta(k\log (n/k))$ 是最优间隔的必要且充分条件；sigmoid 损失优于 InfoNCE 损失。

Conclusion: 低维嵌入之所以有效，是因为所需维度仅与间隔平方的倒数和文档数的对数成正比，而非与数据规模本身成正比。sigmoid 损失更适合生产大间隔嵌入。

Abstract: Why does the low dimensionality of representations, typically $d\approx 1000$, not prevent modern embedding-based retrieval models from scaling to billions, or even trillions, of data points? To answer this question, we study maximal-margin embeddings in the following retrieval model, classically studied in communication complexity [PS86] and more recently in embedding-based retrieval [WBNL26]. Let $A\in \{0,1\}^{N\times n}$ be a matrix indicating whether each of $N$ queries is relevant to each of $n$ documents. We are interested in the largest margin $m>0,$ denoted by $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(d, A),$ for which there exist unit norm embeddings of the queries and documents $\{U_j\}_{j = 1}^N, \{V_i\}_{i = 1}^n$ with the following property. $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \ge m$ whenever $A_{ji} = 1$ and $\langle U_j, V_i\rangle \le -m$ otherwise. A large margin is a key proxy for representation quality: it controls both robustness to perturbations and compositional generalization across queries. Our main theorem establishes that the best possible margin without a restriction on the dimension, $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A),$ can be nearly achieved in dimension $d = O(\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A)^{-2}\log n)$ which improves a theorem of [BDES02]. Together with a matching lower bound in Theorem 1.5, we conclude that when $A\in \{0,1\}^{\binom{n}{k}\times n}$ is the matrix containing all possible $k$-sparse rows once, dimension $d = O(k\log (n/k))$ is necessary and sufficient for the maximal possible margin $\mathsf{m}^{\mathsf{rd}}(+\infty, A) = Θ(k^{-1/2})$ in this setting. This fully resolves the setup of [WBNL26]. We also give several constructions for large margins when $d = o(k\log (n/k)).$ Finally, we empirically test the InfoNCE and sigmoid losses for producing large margin embeddings and demonstrate a clear advantage of the sigmoid loss.

</details>


### [113] [MARS: Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23563)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Afsaneh M. Nejad,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MARS方法，通过在CD图中引入相对边际系数加权离散排名，解决标准CD图忽略性能差距幅度的"量级盲视"问题，实现更真实的模型性能统计表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CD图仅依赖离散排名，丢弃了模型间性能差距的幅度信息，存在"量级盲视"问题，导致统计表征不够准确。

Method: 提出Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS)，引入相对边际系数作为离散排名的权重，该系数基于最佳与最差性能者之间的距离缩放排名，并采用动态投影处理边界情况，随后计算CD值。

Result: MARS能更真实地统计表征模型性能差异，在广泛实验设置下提供更多关于方法实际表现的认识。

Conclusion: MARS通过量级感知的排名统计，有效克服了标准CD图的量级盲视局限，提升了模型评估的统计准确性。

Abstract: Comprehensive evaluation of machine learning models is the key to make sure that they perform as robustly and consistently as desired. In order to summarize the experimental results and pick a winner, Critical Difference (CD) diagrams are used. Standard CD diagrams rely on discrete ranks, discarding the magnitude of performance gaps between models, raising an issue which we call magnitude-blindness. In order to address this issue, we propose Magnitude-Aware Rank Statistics (MARS) that incorporates a relative margin coefficient as a weight for the discrete ranks. This coefficient scales ranks based on the distance between the best and worst performers, with a dynamic projection to handle boundary cases. Followed by the calculation of a CD value, MARS results in a more realistic statistical representation of differences of model performances and more insights on how methods actually perform in vast and extensive experimental settings.

</details>


### [114] [Push Your Agent: Measuring and Enforcing Quantitative Goal Persistence in Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23574)
*Yuandao Cai,Yuzhang Zhu,Liyou Gao,Wensheng Tang,Shengchao Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了长程语言代理在需要完成指定数量目标时的持久性问题，提出了量化目标持久性（QGP）概念，并构建了PushBench基准测试。实验表明，传统控制器在量化目标上表现不佳，而状态追踪检索控制器可消除重复提交并显著提升成功率，即使前沿模型在100工件任务上也仅有33%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程语言代理在局部工具调用上表现良好，但往往无法坚持到外部验证器确认足够数量的有效项目完成，即存在量化目标持久性（QGP）的缺口，需要对这种失效模式进行系统测量与研究。

Method: 提出量化目标持久性（QGP）概念，构建PushBench基准测试（仓库工件收集与验证器驱动的任务单位），直接测量重复工作、重复提交、虚假完成和进度漂移；对比多种控制器（状态追踪检索控制器、积压追踪工作单元控制器、标准控制器、完成门控控制器），并评估Claude Code和Codex CLI等黑箱前沿代理。

Result: 状态追踪检索控制器达到69-78%成功率并消除重复提交；积压追踪工作单元控制器在标准与完成门控控制器完全失败的设置下达到25-50%成功率；前沿模型解决50工件任务表现良好，但在100工件条件下每条件仅3/9任务成功。

Conclusion: 量化目标对代理提出了不同于局部任务能力的可靠性要求：代理必须维持已验证的进度、且仅在请求的工作量完全完成后才停止，这对现有系统构成了显著挑战。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents can make many plausible local tool calls yet fail to persist until a requested count is actually complete. We study this gap as Quantitative Goal Persistence (QGP): whether an agent keeps working until an external verifier confirms enough distinct valid items. PushBench turns this into a benchmark for repository-artifact collection and verifier-backed work units, so repeated work, duplicate submissions, false completion, and progress drift are measured directly rather than hidden behind a final success flag. In matched controller comparisons, a state-tracking retrieval controller reaches 69-78% success while eliminating duplicate submissions, and a backlog-tracking work-unit controller reaches 25-50% success in settings where standard and completion-gated controllers complete no task instances. Black-box frontier-agent evaluations with Claude Code (Sonnet 4.6) and Codex CLI (gpt-5.4) solve many 50-artifact tasks but drop to 3 out of 9 successes per condition at 100 artifacts. The results show that quantitative goals stress a different reliability requirement from local task competence: agents must maintain verified progress and stop only when the requested work is complete.

</details>


### [115] [How Hard is it to Rig a Benchmark? A Social Choice Analysis of Leaderboard Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23628)
*Polina Gordienko,Georg Schollmeyer,Frauke Kreuter,Christoph Jansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将基准测试中的特定训练问题建模为社会选择中的选票贿赂问题，证明该问题在计数规则下是NP难的，并引入实例级鲁棒性概念。实验表明，平均胜率在MMLU和BIG-Bench Hard上最难被操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务基准测试的影响力日益增长，导致了基准测试操纵行为（为提高特定模型排名而采取的策略性行动）。本研究旨在从计算社会选择视角形式化分析这种操纵行为。

Method: 将基准测试中的数据集视为选民、模型视为候选者，将基准特定训练问题映射为计算社会选择中的班次贿赂问题。证明了在Borda计数和平均胜率下的NP难性，并为算术平均、中位数、平均胜率和成对多数投票推导了实例级鲁棒性的解析表达式。在MMLU(HELM)和BIG-Bench Hard(Open LLM Leaderboard)上进行了实验评估。

Result: 在BIG-Bench Hard上，平均胜率的中位鲁棒性为22个任务（92%），远高于算术平均的13个（54%）、中位数和成对多数投票的12个（50%），表明平均胜率最难被操纵。

Conclusion: 本文为基准测试操纵提供了形式化框架，证明平均胜率是最稳健的聚合方法，可有效抵御基准特定训练这类操纵行为。

Abstract: Multi-task benchmarks have become a central pillar of machine learning research, yet their growing influence has incentivised benchmark gaming -- strategic actions taken to improve the leaderboard rank of a specific model. Treating datasets as voters and models as candidates, we consider benchmark-specific training -- the inclusion of benchmark data in training -- as a form of election manipulation. For any ordinal benchmark, the problem of choosing datasets to train on so that a target model becomes top-ranked corresponds to shift bribery, a class of manipulation problems from computational social choice. Leveraging this identification, we show that the benchmark-specific training problem is NP-hard under Borda count and mean win rate. Complementing this worst-case perspective, we introduce the instance-level robustness, the minimum number of datasets a model developer must include in training to top a given leaderboard, and derive expressions for it under arithmetic mean, median, mean win rate and pairwise majority. We evaluate these expressions on MMLU under HELM and on BIG-Bench Hard (BBH) under the Open LLM Leaderboard. Across both suites, mean win rate is hardest to manipulate: this gap is clear on BBH (24 tasks, 4507 models), where its median robustness is 22 tasks (92%), compared with 13 (54%) under arithmetic mean and 12 (50%) under median and pairwise majority.

</details>


### [116] [Valid and Expressive Copulas for Irregular Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23632)
*Christian Klötergens,Tom Hanika,Lars Schmidt-Thieme,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CopFITi是一种用于不规则多元时间序列概率预测的Copula模型，通过归一化流处理边缘分布、高斯混合Copula处理联合依赖结构，实现了边缘化一致性，在联合密度建模上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不规则多元时间序列的概率预测中，现有方法难以同时兼顾边缘分布的准确建模与联合依赖结构的灵活表达，且缺乏满足边缘化一致性的模型。

Method: 采用Copula框架将边缘分布与联合依赖结构解耦：用归一化流建模单变量边缘分布，用高斯混合Copula建模联合依赖关系，确保模型在边缘化下具有一致性。

Result: 实验表明Copula方法能比直接拟合完整联合分布的架构得到更好的边缘模型；CopFITi在不规则多元时间序列联合密度建模上达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: CopFITi是首个满足边缘化一致性的不规则多元时间序列Copula模型，验证了解耦边缘与联合结构在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce CopFITi, a copula model for probabilistic forecasting of irregular multivariate time series (IMTS). Our model combines the expressivity of normalizing flows for univariate marginals with the consistency and flexibility of a Gaussian Mixture Copula for the joint dependency structure. Our experiments show that copula-based approaches, which decouple the marginals from the joint, yield better marginal models than architectures that directly fit the full joint. With CopFITi, we propose the first IMTS copula that is marginalization-consistent by construction and establish a new state of the art in joint IMTS density modeling.

</details>


### [117] [Relevant Walk Search for Explaining Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23673)
*Ping Xiong,Thomas Schnake,Michael Gastegger,Grégoire Montavon,Klaus-Robert Müller,Shinichi Nakajima*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GNN-LRP方法计算相关性路径时指数级复杂度的问题，提出了基于最大积算法的多项式时间算法，能够在神经元级别精确、节点级别近似地找到Top-K最相关路径，大幅提升计算效率，并在流行病学、分子和自然语言等基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN-LRP虽然能提供优于节点/边级别解释的高阶路径级解释，但其识别相关路径的计算复杂度随网络深度呈指数级增长，严重限制了在大规模问题上的应用。

Method: 基于最大积算法，提出多项式时间复杂度的Top-K相关路径搜索算法，在神经元级别实现精确搜索，在节点级别实现近似搜索。

Result: 所提算法大幅降低了GNN-LRP的计算开销，在流行病学、分子和自然语言处理等多个应用领域的基准测试中表现出良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Conclusion: 提出的多项式时间算法有效解决了GNN-LRP指数级复杂度的瓶颈问题，显著扩展了其在大型图分析任务中的适用性。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become important machine learning tools for graph analysis, and its explainability is crucial for safety, fairness, and robustness. Layer-wise relevance propagation for GNNs (GNN-LRP) evaluates the relevance of \emph{walks} to reveal important information flows in the network, and provides higher-order explanations, which have been shown to be superior to the lower-order, i.e., node-/edge-level, explanations. However, identifying relevant walks by GNN-LRP requires {\em exponential} computational complexity with respect to the network depth, which we will remedy in this paper. Specifically, we propose {\em polynomial-time} algorithms for finding top-$K$ relevant walks, which drastically reduces the computation and thus increases the applicability of GNN-LRP to large-scale problems. Our proposed algorithms are based on the \emph{max-product} algorithm -- a common tool for finding the maximum likelihood configurations in probabilistic graphical models -- and can find the most relevant walks exactly at the neuron level and approximately at the node level. Our experiments demonstrate the performance of our algorithms at scale and their utility across application domains, i.e., on epidemiology, molecular, and natural language benchmarks. We provide our codes under \href{https://github.com/xiong-ping/rel_walk_gnnlrp}{github.com/xiong-ping/rel\_walk\_gnnlrp}.

</details>


### [118] [Optimization of randomized neural networks for transfer operator approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23689)
*Mohammad Tabish,Stefan Klus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种优化随机神经网络激活函数的方法，在保持权重和偏置固定的情况下，为复杂动力系统的传递算子逼近提供更优的基函数字典，显著提升RaNNDy框架的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RaNNDy框架中隐藏层的权重和偏置随机初始化后固定不变，其基函数完全由激活函数决定，而初始激活函数的选择对算子逼近效果至关重要。现有方法缺乏对激活函数本身的优化机制，限制了基函数字典的表达能力。

Method: 在保持随机神经网络隐藏层权重和偏置固定的前提下，通过优化激活函数本身来获得更合适的基函数字典；输出层仍通过闭式解训练。

Result: 在随机微分方程和图论随机游走等多个基准问题上验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过优化激活函数而非调整随机权重和偏置，可以显著提升随机神经网络在动力系统传递算子逼近中的性能，且保留了计算成本低的优势。

Abstract: RaNNDy is a randomized neural network architecture for the data-driven approximation of transfer operators associated with complex dynamical systems. The weights and biases of the hidden layers of the network are randomly initialized and kept fixed, only the output layer is trained. This has several advantages over fully optimized neural networks, notably a closed-form solution for the output layer and significantly lower training costs. Despite these advantages, RaNNDy is restricted to the initial selection of weights and biases that parametrize the basis functions required for the operator approximation. Since the basis functions are determined by the activation function, choosing an appropriate activation function for the hidden layers is crucial. In this work, we propose an algorithm that optimizes the activation function itself, while keeping the weights and biases in the randomized neural network fixed, providing a more suitable dictionary. We illustrate the efficacy of the approach using various benchmark problems, including stochastic differential equations and random walks on graphons.

</details>


### [119] [Graph-based Complexity Forecasts in UK En Route Airspace Using Relevant Aircraft Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23696)
*Edward Henderson,George De Ath,Nick Pepper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于概率的空中交通复杂度预测方法，通过改进过滤器算法识别相关飞机对（需管制员监控或解冲突的飞机对），结合图结构路由网络与到达时间不确定性建模，实现对伦敦中部扇区未来45分钟管制员工作负荷的预测，其性能（Spearman ρ=0.68）显著优于传统交通量预测（ρ=0.55）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行业标准模型难以捕捉空中交通复杂性的细微变化，导致对ATCO工作负荷的预测不够准确，影响运行安全与决策质量。

Method: 1) 改进现有过滤器算法，通过ATCO迭代反馈适配伦敦中部扇区的几何与运行特性；2) 构建LMS航线网络的图表示，标准化航段空间保真度；3) 对飞机到达时间不确定性进行概率建模，预测未来45分钟内每个飞机占据航段的概率；4) 结合历史相关交互分布与实时数据流，输出工作负荷预测。

Result: 改进算法F1-score从0.69提升至0.84；工作负荷预测与真实相关交互的Spearman ρ=0.68，显著优于传统交通量预测的ρ=0.55。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动工具可辅助组主管进行扇区配置与管制员排班决策，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Effectively managing Air Traffic Control Officer (ATCO) workload is crucial in maintaining operational safety. Group supervisors use tools that estimate upcoming traffic load to aid decision-making. However, industry-standard models can fail to capture the nuances of upcoming air traffic complexity. This study presents a probabilistic approach to forecast the complexity of an airspace sector using the number of relevant aircraft pairs, i.e., those that require monitoring or deconfliction by a controller, as a proxy measure for ATCO workload. We adapted an existing filter algorithm to make it suitable for use in London Middle Sector (LMS), a complex airspace sector with multiple flows of traffic above some of the busiest airports in Europe. Through iterative feedback with ATCOs, the algorithm was refined and extended to handle specific geometric and operational considerations. The updated algorithm outperformed the original, with an F1-score of 0.84 compared to 0.69 on a labelled set of 50 traffic scenarios. To produce forecasts of future numbers of relevant aircraft pairs in the sector, a graph representation of the LMS route network was constructed, standardising the spatial fidelity of route legs. The forecasting method accounts for uncertainty in aircraft arrival times by modelling the probability of each aircraft occupying route segments at future query times. When combined with historic distributions of relevant interactions and a live operational data stream, predictions of upcoming ATCO workload could be made up to 45 minutes in advance. The proposed method to forecast upcoming workload showed a significantly stronger correlation with actual relevant interactions (Spearman's $ρ= 0.68$) than a standard traffic volume prediction ($ρ= 0.55$). The resulting data-driven tool shows promise for use by group supervisors to inform sector configuration and ATCO rostering decisions.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Dynamic Stability Landscapes in Synchronization Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23708)
*Christian Nauck,Junyou Zhu,Michael Lindner,Frank Hellmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的图到图像预测范式，通过学习稳定性地形图（stability landscapes）来深入理解同步行为，并发布两个各含10000个图的数据集，利用GNN编码器与CNN解码器的端到端架构实现从图拓扑到节点级图像标签的预测，实验证明该方法具有良好的分布内精度、跨图规模泛化能力，并可推广至实际电网拓扑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同步鲁棒性分析依赖标量节点稳定性指标，通过网络科学或图神经网络研究其拓扑依赖性，但这些标量指标对同步行为的刻画有限，缺乏更深层次的结构化理解。作者希望突破标量指标的局限，提出一种能从图拓扑直接学习更丰富的稳定性地形图的新范式。

Method: 提出图到图像预测范式：采用GNN编码图拓扑结构，CNN解码器生成每个节点的图像标签（稳定性地形图），整个模型端到端训练。同时基于概念振子模型（模拟电网同步行为）构建了两个数据集，分别包含20节点和100节点的各10000个图，每个节点带有地形图标签。

Result: 模型在分布内数据上具有良好的预测精度，能够泛化到不同图规模（跨图规模泛化），并可推广至真实电网拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 证明了稳定性地形图虽然传统网络科学方法无法直接获得，但可以从拓扑中学习得到，这为生物学、神经科学和电力系统等领域超越标量稳定性指标提供了新的研究途径。

Abstract: The robustness of synchronization is typically characterized by scalar, per-node stability indices whose dependence on topology is studied via network science or graph neural networks (GNNs). We propose a novel upstream task, learning stability landscapes, which provide deeper insights into synchronization behavior and from which many such scalar indices can be derived. Crucially, we pioneer a graph-to-image prediction paradigm: learning image-like landscapes as per-node targets directly from graph topology, a formulation we are not aware of having been established elsewhere in the literature. To support this task, we release two datasets of 10,000 graphs each at 20 and 100 nodes with per-node landscape labels, based on a conceptual oscillator model, capturing power grid synchronization behavior. A GNN encodes topology and a CNN decoder renders per-node images, learned end-to-end with good in-distribution accuracy, generalizing across graph sizes and to realistic power grid topologies. This demonstrates that stability landscapes, while beyond the reach of conventional network science, are learnable from topology and open new avenues for moving beyond scalar stability indices in biology, neuroscience, and power grids.

</details>


### [121] [Contrast to Detect: Dynamic Graph Contrastive Regularization for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23744)
*Yunhua Pei,Zixing Song,Jin Zheng,John Cartlidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ContrastAD是一种无监督多元时间序列异常检测框架，通过动态图对比学习、频率感知注意力机制和多视角嵌入，在五个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能，平均F1全部最优，三个数据集AUC最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两大问题：(1) 基于重构的检测器会像恢复正常模式一样恢复异常；(2) 图对比方法强制跨视图不变性，假设关系结构是平稳的，但真实系统中的结构漂移会破坏该假设。因此需要一种能利用结构演化本身作为学习信号的方法。

Method: (1) Multi-Perspective Embedder：从时间、属性和结构三个视角编码输入；(2) Frequency-Aware Attention Mixer：谱域top-K滤波后计算注意力，防止噪声泄漏到查询-键相似度中；(3) Dynamic Graph Contrastive Learner：基于批次级DTW距离构建幂律分布的稀疏图快照，对比最发散的一对与稳定锚点，作为软正则化而非强制不变性。

Result: 五个真实基准数据集上，平均F1全部最优；SWaT AUC 93.60、SMD AUC 98.66、PSM AUC 97.79，MSL和SMAP的AUC落后最优基线不到0.7个点但F1领先。消融和敏感性实验证实对比目标作为软正则化效果最佳。

Conclusion: 严格不变性在非平稳动态下是次优的，结构演化本身可作为有效的学习信号，对比学习作为软正则化优于硬不变性约束。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in multivariate time series (MTS) is hindered by dynamic inter-variable dependencies and feature entanglement under spectral noise, and in practice, is further complicated by the absence of anomaly labels. Existing reconstruction-based detectors tend to recover anomalies as faithfully as normal patterns, while prevailing graph contrastive methods enforce invariance across views and thus assume a stationary relational structure, an assumption that breaks under structural drift in real systems. We propose ContrastAD, an unsupervised framework that turns structural evolution itself into a learning signal rather than suppressing it. A Multi-Perspective Embedder encodes inputs from temporal, attribute, and structural perspectives. A Frequency-Aware Attention Mixer then performs spectral top-K filtering before attention, preventing noise from leaking into query-key similarities. The core component, a Dynamic Graph Contrastive Learner, builds power-law-inspired sparse graph snapshots from batch-level DTW distances and contrasts the most divergent pair against a stable anchor, regularizing the latent space without imposing rigid invariance. Across five real-world benchmarks, ContrastAD attains the highest mean F1 on all five datasets and the highest AUC on three (SWaT 93.60, SMD 98.66, PSM 97.79), with statistically significant F1 and AUC margins over the strongest baseline on SWaT and PSM. On MSL and SMAP, it trails the AUC leader by under 0.7 points while still leading on F1. Ablation and sensitivity studies further confirm that the contrastive objective works best as a soft regularizer, supporting our claim that strict invariance is suboptimal under non-stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [122] [Approaching I/O-optimality for Approximate Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23751)
*Pál András Papp,Aleksandros Sobczyk,Anastasios Zouzias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了大语言模型中注意力机制的I/O复杂度，提出了一种在大多数参数范围内I/O成本几乎线性依赖于n的注意力计算方法，并给出了对应的下界证明其接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如FlashAttention）的I/O成本随n呈二次增长，而平凡下界仅需Ω(nd)次I/O。本文旨在缩小这一差距，设计I/O效率更高的注意力计算算法。

Method: 受Alman和Song最近提出的近似注意力框架启发，开发了I/O高效的注意力计算算法，使得I/O成本在大多数参数范围内几乎线性依赖于n。

Result: 所提出的算法在大多数参数范围内实现了几乎线性于n的I/O复杂度，远优于现有方法的二次依赖；同时给出了各参数范围内的匹配下界，证明了算法的接近最优性。

Conclusion: 本文从理论上证明了注意力计算的I/O复杂度可以远低于现有实现，所提算法在I/O层面接近最优，为大语言模型的高效部署提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We revisit the I/O complexity of attention in large language models. Given query-key-value matrices $Q,K,V\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times d}$, and a machine with fast memory size $M$, the goal is to compute the "attention matrix" $A=\text{softmax}(Q K ^{\top}/\sqrt{d}) V$ with the minimal number of data transfers between fast and slow memory. Existing methods in the literature, most notably FlashAttention and its variants, incur an I/O cost that depends quadratically on $n$, while a trivial lower bound only requires $Ω(nd)$ I/O's to read the inputs and write the output. In this work, we present a technique for computing attention where the I/O cost only depends almost-linearly on $n$ in most parameter regimes. This is achieved by developing I/O-efficient algorithms inspired by the recent approximate attention framework of Alman and Song. We also prove corresponding lower bounds in each parameter regime to show that our algorithms are indeed close to I/O-optimal.

</details>


### [123] [SeedER: Seed-and-Expand Retrieval from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23753)
*Hamed Shirzad,Frederik Wenkel,Dominique Beaini,Danica J. Sutherland,Emmanuel Noutahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SeedER是一种基于“种子-扩展”策略的KG检索框架，通过轻量级检索初始化核心节点，再利用强化学习训练的策略进行选择性扩展，在大幅降低计算成本的同时提升多跳组合查询的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱的不规则结构导致传统检索方法（如ego-graph扩展、密集嵌入）在多跳组合查询上效率低或效果差，现有基于智能体的图探索方法虽表达力强但成本过高，亟需一种兼顾效率与效果的KG结构化检索方法。

Method: SeedER采用两阶段策略：1) 种子阶段：利用轻量级密集检索和基于实体的检索获取紧凑的核心节点集；2) 扩展阶段：通过强化学习训练的图感知策略，迭代地选择性扩展节点集，将全局推理分解为可复用的局部决策，并严格控制扩展成本。

Result: 在组合查询上揭示了密集检索的理论局限性，实验证明SeedER在保持紧凑候选集的同时，显著提升了召回率，优于强密集基线和图增强基线。

Conclusion: SeedER作为知识密集型推理系统的有效第一阶段检索器，从组合泛化和图约束子模优化两个理论视角确立了优势，为大规模KG上的高效结构化检索提供了新范式。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs (KGs) offer a rich representation for relational knowledge, but their irregular structure makes retrieval challenging: ego-graph expansion grows rapidly, and dense embedding methods struggle with multi-hop compositional queries. Existing agent-based graph exploration approaches, while expressive, are often too expensive for large-scale retrieval. We introduce SeedER (Seed-and-Expand Retrieval), a retrieval framework that explicitly leverages KG structure through iterative, low-cost expansion. SeedER first seeds a compact set of core nodes using lightweight dense and entity-based retrieval, then selectively expands this set via a learned graph-aware policy trained with reinforcement learning. This design decomposes global reasoning into reusable local decisions, enabling efficient discovery of query-relevant nodes while tightly controlling expansion cost. We show theoretical limitations of dense retrieval on compositional graph queries, and establish advantages of SeedER from both compositional generalization and graph-constrained submodular optimization perspectives. Empirically, SeedER substantially improves recall with compact candidate sets over strong dense and graph-augmented baselines, making it an effective first-stage retriever for knowledge-intensive reasoning systems.

</details>


### [124] [LLM-driven design of physics-constrained constitutive models: two agents are better than one](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23754)
*Marius Tacke,Matthias Busch,Kian Abdolazizi,Jonas Eichinger,Kevin Linka,Roland Aydin,Christian Cyron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个多智能体LLM驱动方法用于本构模型生成：Creator智能体提出模型，Inspector智能体用9项物理约束进行审计，将物理约束满足率从91%提升至100%（Opus），同时保持高精度和良好泛化能力，使LLM生成的本构模型真正可信可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构模型开发需要多年的专业知识和编程经验，现有单智能体LLM方法虽降低门槛但缺乏对模型是否遵守物理定律的系统性检查，导致生成的模型可能物理无效。

Method: 提出Creator-Inspector双智能体框架：Creator智能体根据数据提出本构模型（基于CANNs），Inspector智能体对模型候选进行9项物理约束的严格审计，发现违规则返回Creator进行修正，迭代直至通过所有约束。

Result: 在脑组织、实验橡胶和合成橡胶三个基准上测试，使用Claude Opus 4.7和Kimi K2.5两种LLM后端。Inspector将物理约束满足率：Opus从91%提升至100%，Kimi从37%提升至56%。生成的模型精度与基线相当，且对未见加载路径具有出色的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将生成与审计分离可使LLM驱动的本构模型生成成为真正可信的过程；该方法与具体技术无关，能随LLM能力提升自动扩展，为自动化、物理感知的模型发现开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Developing constitutive models that capture how materials deform under load traditionally requires years of specialized expertise in continuum mechanics, machine learning, and scientific programming. Large language models (LLMs) have recently been shown to lower this barrier by generating constitutive models on demand, but existing single-agent pipelines lack systematic checks that the resulting models respect fundamental physical laws. To close this gap, we introduce the first multi-agent LLM-driven approach for constitutive model generation: a Creator agent proposes a model tailored to the data, while an Inspector agent critically audits each proposal against nine physical constraints and returns it for refinement whenever a violation is detected. We demonstrate this concept with constitutive artificial neural networks (CANNs) and benchmark it on brain tissue, experimental rubber, and synthetic rubber, using two different LLM backbones (Claude Opus 4.7 and Kimi K2.5). Adding the Inspector raises the share of exported models that truly satisfy all physical constraints from 91% to a perfect 100% for Opus and from 37% to 56% for Kimi, while preserving near-baseline accuracy and remarkable generalization to unseen loading paths. In combination, the generated models are physically valid, highly accurate, and extrapolate reliably beyond the training data - properties that together make them directly usable in practice. Separating generation from inspection thus turns LLM-driven constitutive modeling into a genuinely trustworthy process. The paradigm is deliberately technique-agnostic and scales automatically with advances in LLM capability, opening a promising path toward automated, physics-aware model discovery.

</details>


### [125] [Debiased Negative Mining Improves Out-of-distribution Detection with Pre-trained Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23797)
*Bo Peng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang,Zhen Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）的分布外（OOD）检测任务，提出了一种通过蒙特卡洛采样对负标签进行去偏采样以解决假阴性问题的新方法，实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的OOD检测方法依赖启发式规则从未标注的野数据中挖掘负标签，存在严重的假阴性问题（即采样的负标签并非真正的OOD标签），限制了检测性能的提升。

Method: 提出去偏负标签挖掘（Debiased Negative Mining）的理论框架，通过间接近似负标签的真实分布来校正采样偏差，并证明该过程可自然转化为基于ID标签和未标注野数据的蒙特卡洛采样。

Result: 在多种OOD检测设定下，该方法取得了新的SOTA结果，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证表明，解决负标签的假阴性问题能够显著提升VLM在OOD检测任务上的性能，去偏采样策略是释放VLM能力的关键。

Abstract: Aiming at identifying unexpected inputs from unknown classes, out-of-distribution (OOD) detection has emerged as a pivotal approach to enhancing the reliability of machine learning models. This paper focuses on the burgeoning paradigm of post-hoc OOD detection with pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs), where a popular pipeline is to detect OOD inputs by examining their affinities between ID labels and negative labels, i.e., those semantically different from ID labels. Due to the unavailability of target OOD labels, existing works predominantly rely on heuristic rules to mine negative labels from unlabeled wild corpus data. Despite the empirical success, we argue that the power of VLM-based OOD detection has yet to be fully unleashed since the notorious false negative problem is far from addressed in the literature. With this motivation, we are interested in addressing the challenge of mining true negative labels for OOD scoring. To this end, we develop a theoretical framework for correcting the sampling bias of negatives labels by indirectly approximating the distribution of negative labels. Perhaps surprisingly, we show that the debiased negative mining can be naturally converted into Monte-Carlo sampling based on ID labels and the unlabeled wild corpus data. Extensive experiments empirically manifest that our method establishes a new state-of-the-art in a variety of OOD detection setups. Code is publicly available at \href{https://github.com/60pen9/Debiased-Negative-Mining-Improves-OOD-Detection-with-Pre-trained-VLMs}{\textcolor{red}{here}}.

</details>


### [126] [Strong Teacher Not Needed? On Distillation in LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23857)
*Taiming Lu,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 知识蒸馏中教师模型不一定需要强于学生模型：弱教师也能提升大模型效果，且强教师可能导致收益饱和甚至逆转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战知识蒸馏中"强教师必然产生好学生"的传统假设，系统性地研究大语言模型预训练阶段中不同强弱关系的师生对蒸馏效果的影响。

Method: 通过改变架构规模和训练token预算，构建强到弱、同级、弱到强三种师生关系，在语言建模损失与知识蒸馏损失的混合训练下评估蒸馏效果。

Result: 弱教师也能提升大模型；强教师（更多参数或更多训练token）的收益会饱和甚至反转；蒸馏更有利于提升泛化性能（分布外和下游任务）而非域内拟合。

Conclusion: 预训练蒸馏并不总是需要强教师，推翻了"强教师必然更好"的普遍认知。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation generally assumes a strong-to-weak relationship where stronger teachers yield better students. In this work, we examine this assumption about distillation in large language model pretraining. By varying architecture sizes and training token budgets, we create strong-to-weak, same-level, and weak-to-strong teacher-student relationships, and study distillation's effectiveness under each. We find that the teacher need not be strong: with proper mixing of the language modeling and knowledge distillation losses, even small and undertrained teachers improve larger students. At the same time, a stronger teacher is not always better: pushing the teacher further, through more parameters or more training tokens, can saturate or even reverse the distillation gains. We further observe that distillation improves generalization (out-of-distribution and downstream performance) more readily than in-domain fitting. Together, these results challenge the common belief that distillation pretraining always requires a strong teacher.

</details>


### [127] [Complete-muE: Optimal Hyperparameter Transfer and Scaling for MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23893)
*Hongwu Peng,Ohiremen Dibua,Yuanjun Xiong,Yifan Gong,Jianming Zhang,Yan Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Complete-muE框架，通过双桥系统（Bridge I：密集FFN→密集MoE的活跃宽度μP；Bridge II：密集MoE→稀疏MoE的激活专家缩放）实现超参数在密集FFN与任意MoE架构间的迁移，支持专家数量、容量、粒度等变化，实验证明"一次调优密集模型，迁移至所有MoE配置"的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有超参数迁移工具（如μP、SDE）无法解决MoE场景下的迁移问题，因为密集到MoE的转换同时改变了架构和每个专家的token数。需要一种能覆盖密集FFN与任意MoE配置的超参数迁移方法。

Method: 提出Complete-muE双桥系统：Bridge I通过活跃宽度μP和归一化路由器尺度实现密集FFN到密集MoE的映射；Bridge II通过激活专家缩放处理密集MoE到稀疏MoE的映射，其中一阶SDE学习率/权重衰减校正抵消，仅保留有界残差σ₀偏移。覆盖激活专家数、总容量、粒度、共享/分组平衡混合等变化。

Result: 在语言模型和扩散模型预训练实验中，Complete-muE在不同架构和参数量下产生相对稳定的超参数最优值，仅有与Bridge II非严格SDE行为一致的小漂移。实践中该漂移足够小，使得在单个密集参考上调优的超参数可近乎最优地迁移至所有MoE配置。

Conclusion: 实现了"一次调优密集，迁移至所有"的实用方案，使MoE模型在扩展模型容量时无需昂贵超参数搜索即可获得加速收敛速度提升。

Abstract: We propose Complete-muE, a framework which targets hyperparameter transfer across dense FFN and any Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) setups in transformer blocks. Existing tools such as $μ$P (requires fixed architectue) or SDE (requires fixed per-step token count) cannot directly solve the hyperparameter transfer problem in MoE setups because Dense to MoE transfer or MoE total experts scaling changes both architecture and tokens per expert. Complete-muE solves this challenge with a two-bridge system: Bridge~I maps between dense FFN and Dense MoE by active-width $μ$P with a normalized router scale. Bridge~II maps between Dense MoE and sparse MoE by activated-expert scaling, where the first-order SDE LR/WD correction cancels while a bounded residual $σ_0$ shift remains. The resulting transfer rule, which we term as Complete muE, covers changes in activated experts, total capacity, granularity, and shared/group-balanced hybrids for MoE models as well as network width/depth, batch size, and duration changes for general Transformer models. Extensive language model and diffusion model pretraining experiments confirm that complete-muE yields relatively stable hyperparameter optima across model architectures and parameter counts -- with only minor drift consistent with the non-strict SDE behavior of Bridge~II. In practice this drift is small enough that hyperparameters tuned on a single dense reference transfer near-optimally to all MoE configurations -- \emph{tune dense once, transfer to all} is the practical recipe at the core of Complete-muE. This enables MoE models to achieve accelerated convergence speedup over dense models when scaling model capacity without costly hyperparameter search.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [BOHM: Zero-Cost Hierarchical Attribution for Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22866)
*Joss Armstrong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BOHM 提出了一种基于路由权重的层级归因方法，无需访问组件内部或评估任意子集，即可在零边际成本下提供多分辨率归因，与 SHAP 互补且在其路径上路由近似最优时收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Shapley 类方法（如 SHAP）需要评估系统在任意组件子集上的表现，这对于第三方 API、不透明端点以及仅在少数工具上集中路由的智能体编排器不可行，因此需要一种无需访问组件内部即可进行层级归因的方法。

Method: BOHM 从系统中已有的路由权重中提取层级归因树：叶子归因为根到叶子的路径权重乘积；第 k 层归因为深度 k 节点上的诱导分布。该方法零边际成本，无需访问组件内部，同时提供多分辨率归因。

Result: 在 18 个 LLM 的 3 层层级上，BOHM 的 Kendall tau=0.928，SHAP 在 9000 倍评估代价下达到 0.980；在 5 个驱动器的智能体研究中，BOHM 与 SHAP 的单元级别 tau 由驱动器的首选是否是最优工具预测；在 Census 层级上，BOHM 恢复各个层级真实排名（tau 最高 0.722）。

Conclusion: BOHM 满足效率、单调性、对称性和弱抑制性，但不满足 Shapley 的可加性。它作为补充原语，在存在路由状态的任何地方可计算，其与 Shapley 的分歧本身具有诊断价值。

Abstract: Compound AI systems route tasks through hierarchies of specialised components. Attribution is dominated by Shapley-based methods (SHAP), which decompose a coalition value function into per-component marginal contributions and require evaluation of the system on arbitrary component subsets. That requirement fails for third-party APIs, opaque endpoints, and agentic orchestrators that concentrate routing on a few tools, leaving most coalitions un-evaluable from the deployed orchestrator. We introduce BOHM, which extracts a hierarchical attribution tree directly from the routing weights such systems already maintain: leaf attribution is the path product of root-to-leaf routing weights; level-k attribution is the induced distribution over depth-k nodes. The method has zero marginal cost, requires no access to component internals, and provides multi-resolution attribution at every level simultaneously, which flat methods cannot offer at any evaluation budget. BOHM and SHAP answer different questions and converge when the deployed router routes near-optimally. On 18 LLMs in a 3-level hierarchy over 880 LiveCodeBench problems, BOHM yields Kendall tau=0.928; SHAP reaches tau=0.980 at 9,000x more coalition evaluations per seed. On a 5-driver, 7-benchmark agentic study (35 cells, complete coverage), drivers concentrate routing on a single tool (top-share median 0.65), and cell-level tau(BOHM,SHAP) is predicted by whether the driver's top pick is the empirically best tool (mean +0.22 vs ~+0.01). On a US Census hierarchy (475 leaves, 4 levels), BOHM recovers ground-truth rankings at every level (tau up to 0.722). BOHM satisfies efficiency, monotonicity, symmetry, and weak suppression but not Shapley's additivity. It is best understood as a complementary primitive: a multi-resolution decomposition computable wherever routing state exists, whose disagreement with Shapley is itself diagnostic.

</details>


### [129] [NeuroNL2LTL: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Natural Language Translation of Linear Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22874)
*Paapa Kwesi Quansah,Ernest Bonnah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeuroNL2LTL 是一个神经符号架构，通过将自然语言到LTL的翻译路由至结构保持的中间表示，并结合验证器在环训练（强化学习）、可满足性检查与最小编辑修复机制，在200,000+跨领域需求上实现了28%语义等价率和86%可满足输出，证明形式化验证可作为神经规范系统的训练目标和运行时过滤器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言到形式逻辑（如LTL）的翻译需要专业知识，限制了形式化验证在安全关键开发中的应用；现有模板方法牺牲表达力换取可靠性，神经方法虽流畅但缺乏正确性保证。

Method: 提出NeuroNL2LTL神经符号架构：1) 通过结构保持的中间表示将NL映射到LTL；2) 生成规范后执行可满足性和非平凡性检查；3) 最小编辑修复机制修正接近正确的输出；4) 核心创新是验证器在环训练——将验证结果作为强化学习的奖励信号，使神经组件直接针对形式正确性优化。

Result: 在涵盖航空航天、机器人、自动驾驶等十余个领域的200,000+需求上，NeuroNL2LTL达到28%语义等价率，86%输出被验证为可满足。

Conclusion: 形式化验证既可以作为神经规范系统的训练目标，也可以作为运行时过滤器，从而构建以逻辑保证而非统计置信度为基础的可靠神经工具。

Abstract: Effectively translating between natural language (NL) and formal logics like Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) requires expertise that limits formal verification's reach in safety-critical development. Template-based approaches sacrifice expressiveness for reliability; neural methods achieve fluency but provide no correctness guarantees. We present NeuroNL2LTL, a neurosymbolic architecture unifying learned translation with formal verification. NeuroNL2LTL routes translation through an intermediate representation whose mapping to LTL is structure-preserving by construction. Generated specifications undergo satisfiability and non-triviality checking; a minimal-edit repair mechanism corrects near-miss outputs before they reach downstream tools. The central innovation is verifier-in-the-loop training: verification outcomes serve as reward signals for reinforcement learning, producing neural components that optimize directly for formal correctness. On 200,000+ requirements spanning aerospace, robotics, autonomous vehicles, and ten additional domains, NeuroNL2LTL achieves 28\% semantic equivalence with reference specifications while ensuring 86\% of outputs are verified satisfiable. The system also generates contextually grounded explanations from LTL, enabling domain experts to validate specifications without specialized training. This work demonstrates that formal verification can function as both training objective and runtime filter for neural specification systems, allowing us to build neural-based tools whose reliability derives from logical guarantees rather than statistical confidence.

</details>


### [130] [RMA: an Agentic System for Research-Level Mathematical Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22875)
*Zelin Zhao,Bo Yuan,Jaemoo Choi,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Research Math Agents (RMA)框架，用于自动化研究级数学问题的推理，在First Proof基准上解决10个问题中的8个，优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要集中在竞赛数学或形式定理证明，缺乏能够处理需要长程推理、文献支撑和迭代证明改进的研究级数学问题的自动化框架。

Method: RMA将研究级证明求解分解为问题分析、文献检索与理解、公平比较、知识库构建和证明验证等专门模块，由初始化器、提议器和验证器智能体通过共享结构化内存协调，在多角色多轮工作流中协作生成、改进和验证候选证明。

Result: 在包含10个研究级数学问题的First Proof基准上，RMA解决了8个问题，优于GPT-5.2R和Aletheia等基线，生成了逻辑更严谨、可读性更强的证明。消融实验表明性能提升源于结构化推理模块、迭代改进和验证器反馈的协同作用。

Conclusion: RMA框架有效地将智能体协作与结构化模块相结合，显著推进了自动化研究级数学证明的能力。

Abstract: We present $\textbf{Research Math Agents (RMA)}$, an agentic framework for automated reasoning on research-level mathematical problems. Unlike prior studies centered on competition mathematics or formal theorem proving, RMA targets research-level mathematical problems that require long-horizon reasoning, literature grounding, and iterative proof refinement. RMA decomposes research-level proof solving into specialized modules for problem analysis, literature search and understanding, fair comparison, knowledge-bank construction, and proof verification, all coordinated by initializer, proposer, and verifier agents through a shared structured memory. Within this unified framework, these agents operate in a multi-role, multi-round workflow, collaboratively generating, refining, and verifying candidate proofs through iterative feedback. We evaluate RMA on the First Proof benchmark, which consists of ten research-level problems contributed by expert mathematicians across diverse domains. Through comprehensive expert evaluation, RMA outperforms strong baselines on the First Proof benchmark, including GPT-5.2R and Aletheia, solving eight out of ten research problems and producing more logically sound and readable proofs. Our comprehensive ablation studies further show that performance gains arise from the interaction of structured reasoning modules, iterative refinement, and verifier-based feedback, rather than any single component. Our solutions and implementations will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [131] [SciAtlas: A Large-Scale Knowledge Graph for Automated Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22878)
*Shuofei Qiao,Yunxiang Wei,Jiazheng Fan,Bin Wu,Busheng Zhang,Mengru Wang,Yuqi Zhu,Ningyu Zhang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciAtlas是一个大规模多学科异构学术资源知识图谱，包含4300万篇论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组，通过神经符号检索算法实现从语义匹配到确定性关联发现的转变，为AI代理提供结构化拓扑认知基础，降低推理成本并赋能自动化科研全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球学术产出指数级增长导致"信息爆炸"，碎片化、非结构化的知识组织阻碍了深度跨学科融合，而现有学术检索工具依赖浅层关键词匹配或向量空间语义检索，缺乏处理复杂逻辑连接所需的拓扑推理能力；基于代理的深度研究框架易产生逻辑幻觉且推理成本高。

Method: 构建大规模多学科异构学术资源知识图谱SciAtlas，集成26个学科4300万篇论文、1.57亿实体和30亿三元组；提出神经符号检索算法，包含三路径协同召回和图重排序，实现从简单语义匹配到确定性关联发现的平滑过渡。

Result: SciAtlas作为全景科学演化网络，提供了结构化拓扑认知基底，可应用于文献综述、自动化研究趋势综合、思想定位和学术轨迹探索等关键方向，有效赋能自动化科研全流程并显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: SciAtlas可作为有效的"认知地图"，为AI代理提供全局视角，消除学科壁垒，在降低推理成本的同时实现自动化科学研究的全链路支持。

Abstract: The exponential growth of global academic output has confronted researchers and AI agents with an unprecedented ``information explosion,'' where fragmented and unstructured knowledge organization impedes deep interdisciplinary integration. Current academic retrieval tools predominantly rely on superficial keyword matching or vector-space semantic retrieval, which lack the topological reasoning capabilities required to navigate complex logical connections. Agentic deep-research-based frameworks are often prone to logical hallucinations and consuming high inference costs. To bridge this gap, in this report, we introduce SciAtlas, a large-scale, multi-disciplinary, heterogeneous academic resource knowledge graph designed as a panoramic scientific evolution network. By integrating over 43M papers from 26 disciplines, and a total of 157M entities and 3B triplets, SciAtlas provides a structured topological cognitive substrate that dismantles disciplinary barriers and furnishes AI agents with a global perspective. Furthermore, we develop a neuro-symbolic retrieval algorithm featuring tri-path collaborative recall and graph reranking, achieving a seamless transition from simple semantic matching to deterministic association discovery. We also present key application directions of SciAtlas, including literature review, automated research trend synthesis, idea positioning, and academic trajectory exploration, to demonstrate that SciAtlas can serve as an effective ``cognitive map'' to empower the full loop of automated scientific research while significantly reducing reasoning costs. We have released the interfaces for KG retrieval and various downstream tasks in our GitHub repo.

</details>


### [132] [Energy per Successful Goal: Goal-Level Energy Accounting for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22883)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出A-LEMS框架，将AI能耗计量单位从每次推理能耗（energy per inference）重新定义为每个成功目标能耗（Energy per Successful Goal, EpG），并在多个代理任务上验证了代理系统能耗比线性基线高4.33倍，同时提出编排开销指数（OOI）以量化编排结构的能耗成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI能耗基准以单次模型调用或训练运行为粒度衡量能耗，这对经典单轮任务适用，但代理系统（agentic systems）中单个用户目标可能触发多步编排、工具调用、重试和故障恢复，调用次数是实现产物而非任务属性，基于推理层级的归一化会歪曲目标完成的实际能耗成本。

Method: 提出A-LEMS跨层测量框架，核心指标为Energy per Successful Goal (EpG)，聚合所有执行尝试（包括失败和重试）的工作流总能耗，按成功完成的目标数归一化。形式化能耗归因通过时间边界模型、将RAPL信号映射到工作流级能耗的五层观测流水线，以及绑定硬件和运行时配置的可复现协议实现。基于EpG定义Orchestration Overhead Index (OOI)，隔离编排结构相对于相同任务标准下线性执行的能耗成本。

Result: 在五个推理类和三个工具增强类任务族上，代理工作流每个成功目标的平均能耗为线性基线的4.33倍（888.1 J vs 205.3 J）。该开销由编排结构驱动而非推理计算。对于工具增强任务，OOI低于1.0x，即代理执行比线性执行更节能，证实了该指标捕捉的是编排结构而非固定的正向偏差。

Conclusion: 能耗-每次推理（energy-per-inference）不适用于代理AI系统。EpG和OOI为准确基准测试提供了测量基础，编排结构是能耗成本的主要决定因素。

Abstract: Current AI energy benchmarks measure consumption at the granularity of a single model invocation or training run. For classical single-turn workloads this unit remains coherent. For agentic systems - where a single user goal may trigger multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure-recovery cycles - the invocation count is an implementation artifact rather than a task property, and inference-level normalization misrepresents the energy cost of goal completion. We present A-LEMS (Agentic LLM Energy Measurement System), a cross-layer measurement framework that redefines the unit of AI energy accounting from energy per inference to Energy per Successful Goal (EpG). EpG aggregates total workflow energy across all execution attempts, including failures and retries, normalized by successfully completed goals. A-LEMS formalizes energy attribution through a temporal boundary model, a five-layer observation pipeline mapping RAPL signals to workflow-level energy, and a reproducibility protocol binding every measurement to hardware and runtime configuration. Building on EpG, we define the Orchestration Overhead Index (OOI), isolating the energy cost of orchestration relative to linear execution under identical task criteria.
  Across five reasoning and three tool-augmented task families, agentic workflows consume 4.33x higher mean energy per successful goal than linear baselines (888.1 J vs 205.3 J). This overhead is driven by orchestration structure, not inference compute. For tool-augmented tasks, OOI inverts below 1.0x: agentic execution is cheaper than linear, confirming the metric captures orchestration structure rather than a fixed upward bias.
  These findings establish that energy-per-inference is insufficient for agentic AI. EpG and OOI provide the measurement foundation for accurate benchmarking, where orchestration structure is the primary determinant of energy cost.

</details>


### [133] [ImProver 2: Iteratively Self-Improving LMs for Neurosymbolic Proof Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22885)
*Riyaz Ahuja,Tate Rowney,Jeremy Avigad,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ImProver 2是一个神经符号框架，用于在Lean 4中自动优化形式化数学证明。它通过数据高效的专家迭代流程和暴露形式结构及轻量级非正式抽象的脚手架，训练了一个7B参数模型，该模型在证明优化上超越了同系列大数个数量级的模型，并与中端前沿模型竞争力相当。研究表明，通过适当的脚手架和训练，小型模型可以有效重构研究级证明，使证明优化成为可扩展、可学习的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库快速扩展，需要重构验证过的证明以提高可维护性并改善神经证明器的训练数据质量。然而，可扩展的证明优化受到异构且启发式指定的目标、稀缺数据以及高训练和推理成本的阻碍。

Method: 提出了ImProver 2神经符号框架，包含数据高效的专家迭代流程，以及一个暴露形式结构并附带轻量级非正式抽象的脚手架。同时引入了一套捕获结构证明属性的度量指标。

Result: 训练的7B参数模型在同模型家族中优于大数个数量级的模型，与中端前沿模型竞争力相当。神经符号脚手架显著提升了小型和前沿模型的性能。

Conclusion: 通过适当的脚手架和训练，小型模型可以有效重构研究级证明，匹配更大系统的性能，证明优化是一个可扩展、可学习的任务。

Abstract: Formal mathematics libraries are rapidly expanding, creating a growing need to refactor verified proofs for maintainability and to improve training data quality for neural provers. However, scalable proof optimization is hindered by heterogeneous and heuristically specified objectives, scarce data, and high training and inference costs. To overcome these challenges, we introduce ImProver 2, a neurosymbolic framework for automated proof optimization in Lean 4. ImProver 2 combines a data-efficient expert-iteration pipeline with a scaffold that exposes formal structure alongside lightweight informal abstractions. We further introduce a suite of metrics capturing structural proof properties. Using ImProver 2, we train a 7B-parameter model that outperforms orders-of-magnitude larger models within the same model family, and is competitive with mid-tier frontier models across metrics. We additionally demonstrate that our neurosymbolic scaffold significantly improves performance across both small and frontier models. We show that with proper scaffolding and training, small models can effectively restructure research-level proofs over complex and varied metrics, matching substantially larger systems and establishing proof optimization as a scalable, learnable task.

</details>


### [134] [Mediative Fuzzy Logic: From Type-1 Foundations to Type-2, Type-3 and Quantum Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22900)
*Oscar Montiel Ross*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统发展了介导模糊逻辑（Mediative Fuzzy Logic）的理论基础，从类型-1核心扩展到区间类型-2、颗粒类型-3及量子扩展，建立了基于连续双格结构的介导真值模型，提出了扩展t-范数模糊逻辑的命题系统，证明了可靠性和超一致性，并通过自动驾驶传感器融合实例展示了其在处理不完整、异质和轻微矛盾证据时的决策支持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介导模糊逻辑最初用于调和模糊控制与决策中的犹豫或冲突评估，但其逻辑和语义基础（尤其是类型-1操作设置之外）尚未得到充分发展，亟需统一的理论框架。

Method: 将介导算子刻画为由犹豫和矛盾控制的凸聚合；将介导真值建模为连续双格结构中的独立真-假对；引入扩展标准t-范数模糊逻辑的命题系统，添加介导联结词；建立可靠性和超一致性证明；形式化扩展到区间类型-2真值、颗粒索引局部评估、以及希尔伯特空间上的效应算子和密度算子。

Result: 证明了系统的可靠性（soundness）、超一致性（paraconsistency）以及对无介导公式的底层模糊逻辑的保守性（conservativity）；高阶形式化在适当假设下可约化为类型-1情形，阐明了跨层级的连贯性。

Conclusion: 介导模糊逻辑的统一框架支持在不完整、异质和轻微矛盾证据下的透明、保守且安全优先的决策，为未来智能决策系统奠定了可靠的理论基础。

Abstract: Mediative Fuzzy Logic was conceived as a practical scheme for reconciling hesitant or conflicting assessments in fuzzy control and decision-making. However, its logical and semantic foundations remain underdeveloped, especially beyond operational type-1 settings. This article develops a unified account of the type-1 core together with interval type-2, granular type-3, and quantum extensions. We characterize the mediative operator as a convex aggregation controlled by hesitation and contradiction, model mediative truth values as independent truth-falsity pairs in a continuous bilattice-like structure, and introduce a propositional system extending a standard t-norm-based fuzzy logic with a mediative connective. We establish soundness, paraconsistency, and conservativity over the underlying fuzzy base for formulas without mediation, and formulate coherent semantic extensions to interval type-2 truth values, granule-indexed local evaluations, and effects and density operators on Hilbert spaces. An autonomous-braking sensor-fusion example illustrates how the framework supports transparent, conservative, and safety-first decisions under incomplete, heterogeneous, and mildly contradictory evidence. Under suitable assumptions, the higher-level formulations reduce to the type-1 case, clarifying coherence across levels and reliably supporting future work in intelligent decision systems.

</details>


### [135] [EVE-Agent: Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22905)
*Yamato Arai,Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EVE-Agent通过可验证证据机制改进自演化搜索智能体，确保每个训练样本不仅包含答案，还包含可审计的源文本片段，从而避免模型从无法证明的样本中学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自演化智能体在没有可验证证据的情况下，可能会奖励流畅但无依据的样本，导致自生成课程变成不可靠的训练信号。因此需要一种机制确保每个生成实例都有源可溯的证据。

Method: 在提议者-求解者框架中，提议者生成问题、答案和逐字证据片段；证据验证器根据证据提供时的边际准确率增益来奖励该证据，无需人工标注或外部注释。

Result: EVE-Agent在证据锚定的正确性上显著优于之前的自演化搜索智能体，且不改变骨干模型、检索器、搜索工具和优化框架。

Conclusion: 证据可验证性是可信自演化的先决条件；EVE-Agent生成的课程不仅是自生成的，而且是可审计的——每个训练样本都带有可检查的源片段，解释为何该样本值得信任。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents should not train on examples they cannot justify. Data-free self-evolving search agents offer a scalable route to systems that generate their own questions, answer them, and improve from their own feedback without human annotations. Yet, without verifiable evidence, this loop can reward fluent but unsupported examples, turning the self-generated curriculum into an opaque and potentially unreliable training signal. We argue that evidence verifiability is a prerequisite for trustworthy self-evolution in search agents: each generated instance should include not only an answer but also a source-grounded span whose contribution to that answer can be measured. We introduce EVE-Agent, an Evidence-Verifiable Self-Evolving Agent that operationalizes this principle through a modification to the proposer--solver framework. The proposer generates a question, an answer, and a verbatim evidence span. An evidence verifier then rewards the span according to the marginal accuracy gain when the evidence is provided. This produces a training signal that favors evidence that genuinely helps answer the question, without requiring oracle answers, human labels, or external annotations. EVE-Agent leaves the backbone model, retriever, search tool, and optimization framework unchanged. Experiments show that EVE-Agent substantially improves evidence-grounded correctness over prior self-evolving search agents. The resulting curriculum is not merely self-generated but auditable by construction: each training example carries an inspectable source span that explains why it should be trusted.

</details>


### [136] [The Deterministic Horizon: Impossibility Results as Design Specifications for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23024)
*Dongxin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“不可能性-规范”方法论，将AI中的不可能性结果转化为设计规则。核心成果是“确定性视界”——仅由架构（层数、嵌入维度）决定的准确率上限，可部署前计算，在12种Transformer架构中范围为19-31层，超过此深度微调也只能恢复不到4个百分点。此外涵盖偏好学习、多阶段检索、拍卖机制、零知识推理等16个规范，每个规范包含可计算边界、量化违反成本和建设性设计规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将AI领域分散的不可能性结果（图灵、阿罗、没有免费午餐定理）从理论好奇心转化为可操作的设计规则，为可信AI提供建设性指导而非单纯的警告。

Method: 发现Transformer残差流存在容量不变量，推导出“确定性视界”；信息论转换得到超指数级准确率衰减；对模幂运算给出常数深度电路下界；跨子领域（偏好学习、检索、拍卖、零知识证明）统一应用论证框架。

Result: 确定性视界在12种Transformer架构中为19-31层；在最优长度轨迹上微调只能恢复不到4个百分点；零知识验证神经推理的测量开销为每非线性激活110-190倍；共16个规范，其中2个证明组合成立、1个是诚实障碍、4个保持开放。

Conclusion: 每一个AI的根本极限同时也是设计规则。不可能性-规范方法论可作为可信AI生成式研究纲领的基础工具。

Abstract: Large language models now write software, draft legal documents, and produce clinical notes, yet fundamental limits, from Turing and Arrow to the No Free Lunch theorems, shape what computation can do. This thesis turns such impossibility results from curiosities into design rules. Its flagship result proves an accuracy ceiling set by architecture alone: past a critical reasoning depth, no amount of training moves it, at any adapter rank, sample size, or loss function. Computable before deployment from layer count and embedding width, this Deterministic Horizon is measured between nineteen and thirty-one across twelve transformer architectures, and fine-tuning on optimal-length traces recovers under four percentage points. The mechanism is a capacity invariant of the residual stream, and an information-theoretic conversion yields super-exponential accuracy decay past the horizon. An unconditional circuit-complexity lower bound for modular exponentiation against constant-depth prime-modulus circuits complements this result. The same argument recasts across subfields: preference learning under any misspecified model jumps discontinuously in sample complexity; multi-stage retrieval pipelines require at least as many independent metrics as stages; standard truthful auctions fail for agents with prompt-dependent valuations; and zero-knowledge verification of neural inference pays a measured overhead of one hundred ten to one hundred ninety times per non-linear activation. Together these form a catalogue of sixteen specifications, each pairing a computable boundary, a quantified violation cost, and a constructive design rule: two compositions are proved, one pairing is an honest obstruction, and four remain open. The impossibility-specification methodology is offered for the generative research programme that trustworthy AI may need. Every fundamental limit of AI is also a design rule.

</details>


### [137] [PathCal: State-Aware Reflection-Marker Calibration for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23074)
*Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Shuo Xing,Peiran Li,Tsubasa Takahashi,Dengzhe Hou,Zhengzhong Tu,Kazunori Yamada,Fangzhou Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PathCal，一种无需训练的解码控制器，通过区分不同类型的反思标记（如"wait"、"but"、"alternatively"）并在局部不确定状态进行干预，从而校准推理路径，在不依赖外部验证器或额外采样的前提下提升效率-性能平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将大型推理语言模型（LRMs）生成的反思标记视为单一粗粒度类别，忽略了不同标记在功能角色和影响时机上的差异，导致无法精细化控制推理过程。

Method: PathCal：一种无需训练的解码控制器，在每一步解码时利用反思标记的分布来估计当前推理路径与竞争分支之间的局部竞争程度，当竞争分支证据过多时软性重平衡标记logits，且仅在局部不确定状态进行干预。

Result: 在六个推理基准上，PathCal在提升或保持精度的同时缩短了生成长度，实现了更优的效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 不同类别的反思标记对准确率和生成长度有不同影响，且标记选择在模型进入稳定推理轨迹之前影响最大；PathCal通过区分标记类型和局部干预有效校准推理路径。

Abstract: The emergence of Large Reasoning Language Models (LRMs) has paved the way for tackling complex reasoning tasks through test-time scaling by generating long-form Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories during inference. Meanwhile, these trajectories often contain explicit reflection markers such as ``wait'', ``but'', and ``alternatively'', signaling hesitation, revision, and the consideration of alternative explorations, respectively. Recent studies on test-time control leverage such markers as lightweight handles for steering reasoning, typically treating them as a single coarse-grained category rather than distinguishing their distinct functional roles. In this paper, we conduct type-wise suppression and fixed-prefix intervention, revealing that reflection markers differ not only in their functional roles but also in when they exert the greatest influence. Specifically, different marker classes affect accuracy and generation length in distinct ways, and marker choices are most consequential before the model settles into a stable reasoning trajectory. Motivated by these findings, we introduce PathCal, a novel training-free decoding controller that calibrates reasoning paths by distinguishing marker types and intervening only at locally uncertain states. At each decoding step, PathCal utilizes the distribution over reflection-markers to estimate local competition between maintaining the current reasoning trajectory and initiating a competing branch, and softly rebalances marker logits when competing-branch evidence becomes excessive. Experiments across six reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that PathCal achieves a better efficiency--performance trade-off, improving or preserving accuracy while reducing generation length, without relying on external verifiers or additional sampling.

</details>


### [138] [Inductive Deductive Synthesis: Enabling AI to Generate Formally Verified Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23109)
*Shubham Agarwal,Alexander Krentsel,Shu Liu,Mert Cemri,Audrey Cheng,Rui Meng,Tomas Pfister,Chun-Liang Li,Sylvia Ratnasamy,Aditya Parameswaran,Matei Zaharia,Ion Stoica,Mohsen Lesani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出归纳演绎综合（IDS）方法，联合增量地合成实现与证明，使AI agent在分布式键值存储规约上的形式验证成功率从2/7提升至7/7，平均耗时6.8小时、成本106美元，比专家快约200倍，比SOTA agent便宜17%，且生成的实现比已发表已验证系统快3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI agent在需要形式化保证全覆盖的任务（如分布式系统一致性验证）上表现不佳，即使SOTA agent（Codex with GPT-5.4和Claude Code with Opus 4.6）在分布式键值存储规约上的成功率仅为2/7，而传统机械化形式验证需要数月到数年的专家工作量。

Method: 提出归纳演绎综合（IDS）方法，联合且增量地合成实现与证明，从失败的尝试中学习并系统性地探索有前景的策略。该方法构建为agentic LLM系统。

Result: IDS在7个分布式键值存储规约上全部成功（7/7），平均每个规约耗时约6.8小时、成本106美元，比专家快约200倍，比SOTA agent便宜17%。同时，将性能反馈融入同一循环后，生成的实现比已发表的已验证系统快3倍。

Conclusion: IDS是首个有效弥合AI agent在形式化验证方面差距的方法，实现了自动化、低成本、高性能的分布式系统形式化验证与实现合成。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly excel at generating, testing, and refining code. However, they fall short on tasks requiring formal guarantees of full coverage that testing alone cannot provide. Distributed systems are a prime example: properties such as consistency between reads and writes must hold under every possible interleaving of events. Mechanized formal verification can guarantee such correctness, but typically demands months to years of expert effort. As evidence, even SOTA coding agents (Codex with GPT-5.4 and Claude Code with Opus 4.6) succeed on only 2/7 distributed key-value-store specifications. In this paper, we present the first effective approach to addressing this gap, Inductive Deductive Synthesis (IDS), which jointly and incrementally synthesizes implementation and proof, and learns from failed attempts to systematically try promising strategies. Built as an agentic LLM system, IDS achieves 7/7 in about 6.8 hours and $106 per spec on average, roughly 200x faster than expert effort and 17% cheaper than SOTA agents. IDS further incorporates performance feedback into the same loop, yielding implementations up to 3x faster than published verified systems.

</details>


### [139] [Redrawing the AI Map: A Theory of Accountability Boundaries in Agentic Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23179)
*Muhammad Zia Hydari,Farooq Muzaffar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出智能体编排时代下"责任边界"理论，指出虽然AI技术实现了模块化接口，但当AI输出需要证据、审查、签核或可分配责任时，责任边界可能仍保持整合状态。引入"责任资产"概念（使AI输出合法、可审计、可审查、可分配责任的互补资产），并基于验证成本与责任可转移性，识别了三种边界策略：组件型、整合型和双轨型。同时提出"规则债务"概念，即组织决策规则从正式信息系统迁移到无治理的智能体执行环境时产生的治理负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为AI编排器通过降低接口和组装成本加速模块化和组织去整合，但忽略了AI能力的输出需要证据、审查、签核或可分配责任这一现实——技术接口变为模块化，责任边界却可能保持整合。本文旨在解释这种错位的成因及其后果。

Method: 融合数字创新、交易成本、互补资产、数字平台治理和IS控制等多理论视角，通过分析验证成本和责任可转移性两个核心维度，推导出三种边界策略（组件型、整合型、双轨型），提出七个命题，并在文档处理、法律服务、审计、临床决策支持和采购五个领域进行结构化案例说明。

Result: 识别了决定执行边界与责任边界能否协同移动的关键因素（验证成本与责任可转移性）；提出了三种边界策略和规则债务概念；建立了七项命题，连接智能体组装成本降低、责任资产、可占用性、编排者意图捕获和边界错配与边界策略、价值占用和规则债务之间的关系。

Conclusion: 数字模块化何时能扩展到组织去整合、何时责任迫使能力保持整合，取决于验证成本和责任可转移性。当AI能力输出的合法性、可审计性、可审查性和责任可分配性无法通过模块化接口解决时，组织必须维持整合的责任边界，否则将积累规则债务并面临治理风险。

Abstract: Agentic AI orchestrators reduce the interface and assembly costs of composing information systems capabilities across organizational boundaries, seemingly accelerating modularization and organizational disaggregation. Yet AI-enabled capabilities whose outputs require evidence, review, signoff, or assignable responsibility may retain integrated accountability boundaries even when their technical interfaces become modular. We develop a capability-level theory of accountability-boundary placement in agentic ecosystems. We introduce accountability assets: complementary assets that make AI-supported outputs legitimate, auditable, reviewable, and assignable to a responsible party. We argue that verification cost and responsibility transferability determine whether the execution and accountability boundaries can move together. The theory identifies three boundary strategies: component, integrated, and dual-track. It also introduces rule debt, the governance burden that accrues when organizational decision rules migrate from formal information systems into ungoverned agentic execution environments. Integrating digital innovation, transaction cost, complementary-assets, digital platform governance, and IS control perspectives, we develop seven propositions linking agentic assembly-cost reductions, accountability assets, appropriability, orchestrator intent capture, and boundary misconfiguration to boundary strategy, value appropriation, and rule debt. The theory explains when digital modularization extends to organizational disaggregation and when accountability keeps capabilities integrated. Structured illustrations across document processing, legal services, audit, clinical decision support, and procurement discipline the boundary logic.

</details>


### [140] [AutoResearch AI: Towards AI-Powered Research Automation for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23204)
*Guiyao Tie,Jiawen Shi,Dingjie Song,Yixiao Huang,Ziji Sheng,Xueyang Zhou,Daizong Liu,Pan Zhou,Yongchao Chen,Ran Xu,Lifang He,Qingsong Wen,Manling Li,Cong Lu,Shuai Li,Pengtao Xie,Yixuan Yuan,Rui Meng,Lei Xing,Lichao Sun,Caiming Xiong,Philip S. Yu,Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI驱动的科研自动化（AutoResearch）从任务级辅助向工作流级自动化的演进，分析了当前系统的碎片化现状，提出了五个工作流条件和五个评估维度，并指出自主性具有领域依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研正被AI系统重塑，从孤立的辅助工具转向覆盖文献、假设、实验、验证、报告等长周期工作流。然而现有系统在自主性、领域范围、执行环境、验证机制和人类监督等方面高度碎片化，缺乏统一的框架来理解和评估这一转变。

Method: 通过综述现有AI科学家系统、混合主动协作框架、基准测试、领域部署和开源基础设施，围绕五个工作流条件（文献与科研基础、假设形成与规划、实验与工具使用、反馈验证与评审、报告与知识传播）组织分析，并提出五个评估维度（新颖性、有效性、影响力、可靠性、可溯源性）。

Result: 识别出Vibe Research（人类引导的提示驱动辅助）与AI主导系统之间的光谱；发现AutoResearch自主性在结构化、可执行、可快速验证的领域可信度更高，在具身化、延迟反馈、异质性、伦理或机构问责的领域中受到限制。

Conclusion: 科研自动化正向工作流级发展，但完全自主的AI科研尚不成熟；未来需要在证据保存、可重复性、弱方向拒绝、溯源性、跨领域鲁棒性和可问责的科学闭环等关键挑战上取得突破。

Abstract: Scientific research is being reshaped by AI systems that move beyond isolated assistance toward longer-horizon workflows spanning literature grounding, hypothesis generation, experimentation, validation, reporting, and revision. This shift marks a transition from task-level AI for science to workflow-level research automation. Yet current systems remain fragmented, differing in autonomy, domain scope, execution environment, validation mechanism, and human oversight, while still struggling with evidence preservation, reproducibility, weak-direction rejection, provenance tracking, cross-domain robustness, and accountable scientific closure. This survey examines these developments through AutoResearch, defined as the developmental spectrum of AI-powered scientific workflow automation. Within it, Vibe Research denotes the human-steered region of prompt-based assistance and human-verified execution, whereas emerging AI-led systems coordinate larger portions of the discovery loop without achieving robust autonomy. We analyze how research systems redistribute control, evidence, execution, validation, and accountability across workflows and organize the field around five workflow conditions: literature and research grounding; hypothesis formation and planning; experimentation and tool use; feedback, validation, and review; and reporting and knowledge communication. We further synthesize AI scientist systems, mixed-initiative co-research frameworks, benchmarks, domain deployments, and open-source infrastructures. Finally, we propose five evaluation dimensions--novelty, validity, impact, reliability, and provenance--and show that AutoResearch autonomy is domain-conditioned, being more credible in structured, executable, and rapidly verifiable settings but limited in embodied, delayed, heterogeneous, ethical, or institutionally accountable contexts.

</details>


### [141] [Foundation Protocol: A Coordination Layer for Agentic Society](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23218)
*Bang Liu,Yongfeng Gu,Jiayi Zhang,Zhaoyang Yu,Sirui Hong,Maojia Song,Xiaoqiang Wang,Mingyi Deng,Zijie Zhuang,Ronghao Wang,Mingzhe Cao,Yutong Zhu,Xingjian Li,Yifan Wu,Jianhao Ruan,Yiran Peng,Shuangrui Chen,Jinlin Wang,Yizhang Lin,Dongjie Zhang,Dekun Wu,Chen Ma,Lizi Liao,Han Yu,Jian Pei,Heng Ji,Qiang Yang,Yuyu Luo,Chenglin Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Foundation Protocol (FP)，一个面向人机社会的图优先协调层，统一异构实体、支持多方协作、经济原语与审计问责，旨在构建开放、可治理的共享基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主智能体从工具演变为社会基础设施层，瓶颈从模型能力转向协调问题。现有协议缺乏统一异构实体（智能体、工具、人类、机构）、原生多方组织、经济结算及审计问责的一体化方案。

Method: FP 采用图优先的协调层设计，统一异构实体，原生支持多方组织和事件驱动协作，内建计量、收据和结算等经济原语，将策略、溯源和审计作为一等公民，并通过包装/桥接现有协议实现渐进式采用。

Result: FP 提供统一的协调基础设施，降低集成与治理开销，使自主智能体可组合的同时保持问责不可妥协，支持开放、多元、可治理的人机社会。

Conclusion: FP 将协调本身转化为共享基础设施，为人机社会的开放性和可治理性提供了可行的协议层设计。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are moving from tools into a layer of social infrastructure: they browse, purchase, deploy software, manage systems, and increasingly interact with one another. As these systems scale, the bottleneck shifts away from raw model capability toward coordination. Agents need to form reliable relationships, organize multi-agent work, exchange value, support an AI economy, and stay safe and accountable under real-world oversight. This paper introduces the Foundation Protocol (FP), a graph-first coordination layer for an emerging human-AI society. FP unifies heterogeneous entities, including agents, tools, resources, humans, institutions, and organizations, and supports native multi-party organization and event-based collaboration. It also provides economic primitives for metering, receipts, and settlement, and treats policy, provenance, and audit as first-class concerns. FP is designed to wrap and bridge existing protocols rather than replace them, enabling incremental adoption while reducing integration and governance overhead. The aim is to keep autonomous agency composable while keeping accountability non-negotiable, so that coordination itself can become shared infrastructure for a human-AI society that is open, pluralistic, and governable.

</details>


### [142] [GENSTRAT: Toward a Science of Strategic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23238)
*Vartan Shadarevian,Kia Ghods,Alex Kenich,Anany Kotawala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GENSTRAT是一个通过程序生成不完全信息卡牌游戏来评估LLM战略推理能力的基准框架，包含能力剖面分析和锯齿度衡量指标，能在36,000多场对局中揭示模型间细微且部署相关的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有战略推理基准使用固定规范博弈，随着模型能力提升容易饱和，且无法从基准性能可靠推广到实际部署中的多样化、混乱的战略环境。

Method: 提出GENSTRAT，通过程序生成二人零和、不完全信息卡牌游戏分布，支持按需生成新游戏以实现长青评估和抗污染；配套六轴能力剖面（状态空间、时间深度、信息敏感度、对手建模、风险、脆弱性）和锯齿度衡量指标，检测模型在战略相似游戏间的局部不稳定性。

Result: 从2000个游戏池中采样50个基准游戏，对9个前沿和开源LLM进行36,000多场头对头锦标赛。新前沿模型平均得分更高；总分相近的模型表现出不同能力剖面；前三名中gpt-5和claude的局部波动性明显高于gemini-3.1-pro。

Conclusion: 能力剖面和锯齿度指标提供了单一排行榜排名无法提供的部署相关诊断信息，能更精细地刻画模型在实际战略环境中的行为特征和稳定性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as economic agents in marketplaces, auctions, and bidding settings. Anticipating their behavior in any specific deployment is hard. Existing strategic-reasoning benchmarks evaluate models on fixed canonical games. These benchmarks may saturate as the frontier improves, and they do not allow evaluators to generalize with confidence from benchmark performance to the varied and messy strategic environments that actual deployments involve. We introduce GENSTRAT, which uses procedurally generated strategic environments to address these challenges. Concretely, we generate a distribution of two-player zero-sum imperfect-information card games. The generator can draw fresh games on demand, allowing for evergreen evaluation and resistance to contamination. We pair the game distribution with a capability-profile methodology that decomposes model competence across six axes (state space, temporal depth, information sensitivity, opponent modeling, risk, and brittleness). We also introduce a jaggedness measure of within-distribution smoothness that detects when a model's advantage jumps unpredictably between strategically similar games. We sample 50 benchmark games from a 2,000-game generated pool and evaluate nine frontier and open-weight LLMs in a head-to-head tournament with over 36,000 matches. Newer frontier-tier models score higher on average. Beyond that average, models with near-identical overall strength show qualitatively different capability profiles, and two of the top three leaderboard models (gpt-5 and claude) are noticeably more locally volatile than the third (gemini-3.1-pro), despite being close in overall strength. Together, the capability profile and the jaggedness measure give a deployment-relevant diagnostic that the overall ranking alone cannot provide.

</details>


### [143] [Design and Report Benchmarks for Knowledge Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23262)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha,Levi Lian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个三步法框架，用于明确基准测试任务与知识工作声明之间的对应关系，并从工作研究视角审视现有基准设计中的不足，通过三项案例展示基准设计选择如何影响可支持的工作声明强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识工作AI的评估与基准设计仍沿用传统NLP任务的逻辑，导致更高的基准性能并不能可靠反映系统在实际部署中执行知识工作的能力。

Method: 三步法：1) 定义被评估的工作活动；2) 明确测试设置（材料、工具、角色、约束）；3) 对适当的工作产品进行评分。同时从O*NET职业任务数据库中提取18种工作活动清单。

Result: 通过对GDPval、OfficeQA Pro和APEX-SWE三个基准案例的分析，展示了基准设计选择如何塑造其分数所能支持的最强工作声明，以及基准任务、测试设置、评分产品与更广泛工作声明之间的差距。

Conclusion: 知识工作的核心特征（角色与职责、本地化材料与工具、需在下游工作流中保持可用的人工制品）应被纳入基准设计与报告指南，以弥合基准分数与真实工作能力之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: The development of LLM agents has led to a growing body of work on knowledge-work AI, including coding, research, and healthcare. However, current knowledge-work evaluation and benchmark design still largely follow the logic of traditional NLP tasks. As a result, higher benchmark performance does not reliably show that a system can carry out knowledge work in real-world deployment settings. This paper contributes a three-step approach for making explicit how benchmarked tasks represent the work claims attached to their scores: defining the work activity under evaluation, specifying the tested setting, and scoring the appropriate work product. We review work studies showing that knowledge work is organized through roles and responsibilities, local materials and tools, and artifacts that must remain usable in downstream workflows. We then translate these concerns into benchmark design and reporting guidance, covering how tasks should be mapped to work activities, how tested settings should specify materials, tools, roles, and constraints, and how scoring should focus on the work product left by the system. To name the work activity being evaluated and distinguish it from common benchmark tasks, we derive an inventory of 18 work activities from the O{*}NET occupational task database. We demonstrate the approach through three benchmark case analyses: GDPval, a non-code occupational deliverable benchmark; OfficeQA Pro, a grounded document-analysis benchmark scored by final answers; and APEX-SWE, a software-engineering benchmark with executable scored products. These cases show how benchmark design choices shape the strongest work claim a score can support, and where gaps arise between the benchmarked task, tested setting, scored product, and broader work claim.

</details>


### [144] [Parallel Context Compaction for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23296)
*Musa Cim,Burak Topcu,Chita Das,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并行压缩（parallel compaction）方法，用于长程LLM智能体的上下文压缩，相比顺序同步基线，该方法提供细粒度、可预测的摘要体积控制，支持每块定向提示工程，在匹配压缩解码体积时减少端到端耗时并提升压缩吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体对话历史不断增长，超出模型上下文窗口；现有基于LLM的摘要压缩方法存在三大问题：(1) 摘要有损且阻塞调用导致推理延迟数十秒；(2) 操作者无法精细控制摘要体积（提示指令基本被忽略）；(3) 随着上下文增长，输出token数量和信息保留程度在不同运行间波动大，导致智能体知识保留不可预测。

Method: 提出并行压缩（parallel compaction）方法，将长程对话历史分块并行进行摘要压缩，替代传统的顺序同步压缩方式。在HotpotQA多跳QA和LoCoMo长上下文对话两个基准上，使用8B到120B参数的四个骨干模型（涵盖密集和MoE架构、推理和非推理模型）进行实验评估。

Result: 并行压缩为操作者提供了对摘要体积的细粒度、可预测控制；支持更针对性的每块提示工程；在匹配压缩解码体积的条件下，相比顺序基线减少了端到端耗时，并提升了压缩吞吐量。

Conclusion: 并行压缩是一种有效的上下文压缩替代方案，解决了顺序摘要方法的延迟、可控性差和运行间波动问题，在保持信息保留的同时显著提升效率。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate growing conversation histories that eventually exceed the model's context window. Context compaction via LLM-based summarization keeps the conversation bounded, but summarization is inherently lossy and the blocking call stalls agent inference for tens of seconds. Moreover, the operator has no fine-grained control over summary volume since prompt instructions are largely ignored, and as context grows, both the amount of output tokens the model produces and the information it retains fluctuate substantially from run to run, making the agent's retained knowledge unpredictable across runs. We introduce \textbf{parallel compaction} for long-horizon agentic flows and characterize it against the sequential synchronous baseline across four backbones spanning 8B to 120B parameters, mixing dense and MoE architectures with reasoning and non-reasoning models, on the HotpotQA multi-hop QA and LoCoMo long-context dialogue benchmarks. Parallel compaction gives the operator fine-grained, predictable control over summary volume and enables more targeted prompt engineering per block. At matched compaction decode volume, it reduces end-to-end wall time and improves compaction throughput over the sequential baseline.

</details>


### [145] [Ontological Knowledge Blocks: Executable Compliance and Profile-Based Validation for Trustworthy AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23297)
*Aasish Kumar Sharma,Julian M. Kunkel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可编程治理基础设施Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs)，将监管义务编译为机器可检查的约束，通过结构化证据图实现自动化合规验证，在AI-HPC资源分配场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI服务合规治理依赖文档化、静态清单和人工审查，无法扩展到自动化AI系统，缺乏可编程、可自动验证的治理基础设施。

Method: 提出OKBs五元组形式化模型，将规范性义务绑定到RDF/OWL概念模式、SHACL验证规则、证据需求和PROV-O溯源链接；设计确定性监管编译器将结构化IR记录编译为可组合知识库模块，支持基于配置文件的治理重配置。

Result: 在AI辅助HPC资源分配场景中，24次验证运行和4个治理配置文件下实现：配置文件敏感验证、严格累加性违规累积、SHACL验证延迟12.6-100.3ms，配置文件等价测试证实Combined为最全面配置。

Conclusion: OKBs作为可编程治理基础设施，能有效将监管义务转化为机器可执行约束，实现自动化合规验证且性能开销可控，所有工件已开源。

Abstract: AI-enabled services deployed in critical digital infrastructure are subject to governance obligations spanning transparency, accountability, fairness, and traceability. Compliance today remains documentation-centric: obligations are described in prose, audits rely on static checklists, and verification depends on manual review. Such approaches do not scale to automated AI systems. This paper introduces Ontological Knowledge Blocks (OKBs), a programmable governance infrastructure that compiles regulatory obligations into machine-checkable constraints over structured evidence graphs. We formalize an OKB as a 5-tuple that binds normative obligations to an RDF/OWL concept schema, executable SHACL validation rules, explicit evidence requirements, and PROV-O provenance links. A deterministic regulatory compiler translates structured Intermediate Representation (IR) records into composable KB modules, enabling profile-based governance reconfiguration without modifying service code. We implement two prototypes and evaluate them in an AI-assisted HPC resource allocation scenario across 24 validation runs and four governance profiles. Results demonstrate profile-sensitive validation, strictly additive violation accumulation, SHACL validation latency between 12.6 ms and 100.3 ms, and profile equivalence testing confirming Combined as the strictly most comprehensive profile. All artefacts are released as open source.

</details>


### [146] [DART: Semantic Recoverability for Structured Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23311)
*Ke Yang,Panpan Li,Zonghan Wu,Kejin Xu,Huaxi Huang,Xiaoshui Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DART是一个模块化运行时系统，通过语义可恢复性检查，解决结构化工具代理在执行失败时，既不能简单回滚整个任务（浪费），也不能本地恢复（可能破坏下游已提交工作）的困境。实验表明DART能在承诺敏感场景下正确恢复，且无安全违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恢复方法提供机械性回滚，但缺乏判断本地恢复在存在下游已提交工作后是否仍保持语义有效性的标准，即语义可恢复性（semantic recoverability）这一缺口。

Method: DART包含四个关键步骤：1) 定位失败的实例；2) 认证该实例的语义可恢复边界；3) 将检查点对齐到这些边界；4) 在依赖和效应约束下选择可接受的恢复点（admissible restore point），以保证下游已提交工作不受破坏，否则阻止恢复。

Result: 在三个LLM驱动领域及LangGraph基板的外部验证中，DART正确恢复了所有基线本地恢复失败的承诺敏感案例；五领域安全审计未发现任何不安全的回滚行为。
