Conclusion: 控制器合法性（controller legality）不等于语义有效性（semantic validity），合理的本地恢复需要显式的可接受性检查（admissibility check）。

Abstract: When a structured tool agent fails mid-execution, the runtime faces a dilemma: replaying the entire task is safe but wasteful, while restoring from a local checkpoint is efficient but can leave committed downstream work tied to an upstream history that no longer exists. This tension is acute in commitment-sensitive settings, where rollback targets a single failed instance yet downstream consumers have already acted on its output. Existing recovery approaches provide mechanical rollback but no criterion for whether a local restore remains semantically valid after downstream commitment. We formalize this gap as semantic recoverability and address it in DART, a modular runtime that localizes the failed instance, certifies semantically recoverable boundaries of that instance, aligns checkpoints to those boundaries, and selects an admissible restore point that preserves committed downstream work under dependency and effect constraints-or blocks otherwise. Across three LLM-driven domains and external validation on a LangGraph-based substrate, DART correctly recovers all evaluated commitment-sensitive cases where baseline local recovery fails, and a five-domain safety audit finds no unsafe admitted rollbacks. These results show that controller legality does not imply semantic validity, and that sound local recovery requires an explicit admissibility check.

</details>


### [147] [Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Ventilator Decision Support with Contextual Bandit Preference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23320)
*Sijia Li,Xiaoyu Tan,Qixing Wang,Weiyi Zhao,Chen Zhan,Teqi Hao,Xuemin Wang,Lei Gu,Roland Eils,Xihe Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种人类参与的多智能体框架VDSS，通过合约驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件，结合上下文赌博机进行在线偏好自适应，并利用结构化拒绝反馈触发目标重规划，在ICU轨迹回放中实现了更高的推荐可接受性和更少的交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有呼吸机决策支持方法存在不足：基于规则的方法难以泛化个性化需求；端到端强化学习或单一大型语言模型系统难以控制和审计。

Method: 提出VDSS框架：(1) 人类参与的多智能体架构，通过合约驱动的结构化接口协调模块化决策组件，产生可追溯证据；(2) 使用上下文赌博机进行在线偏好自适应，从每次调整周期中最终接受的决策更新临床医生特定偏好；(3) 结构化拒绝反馈触发目标重规划，减少无效迭代。

Result: 回顾性ICU轨迹回放与专家评审表明，VDSS实现了更高的推荐可接受性和更少的交互轮次以达到可接受的计划。

Conclusion: VDSS支持临床可部署的人机协作，在安全性和个性化方面具有优势。

Abstract: Ventilator decision support requires sequential decisions that track evolving physiology and disease trajectories while respecting safety boundaries and clinician specific tuning styles. Rule based approaches rarely generalize personalization, and end to end reinforcement learning or single large language model systems remain difficult to control and audit. We propose the Ventilator Decision Support System (VDSS), a human in the loop multi agent framework that coordinates modular decision components through contract driven structured interfaces and produces traceable evidence for review. VDSS performs online preference adaptation with a contextual bandit, updating clinician specific preferences from the final accepted decision at each adjustment cycle and using them to guide subsequent recommendations. Structured rejection feedback triggers targeted replanning to reduce unproductive iterations and improve interaction stability. Retrospective ICU trajectory replay with expert review indicates higher recommendation acceptability and fewer interaction rounds to reach an acceptable plan, supporting clinically deployable human AI collaboration.

</details>


### [148] [When Planning Fails Despite Correct Execution: On Epistemic Calibration for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23414)
*Zehao Wang,Shilong Jin,Zhao Cao,Lanjun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为EPC-AW的工作流，通过信息一致性评估与动态校准，解决LLM多智能体系统中因认知校准偏差导致的规划失败问题，平均系统级成功率提升9.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统即使行动执行正确，也可能因智能体在评估计划可行性时误判自身知识（即认知校准偏差）而导致失败。这种偏差在规划阶段是潜在且动态的，传统方法难以检测和应对。

Method: 提出EPC-AW工作流，包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于信息一致性的计划选择，在不同信息条件下评估计划的稳定性；(2) 一致性引导的认知状态精炼，利用历史偏差动态调整校准策略以指导未来规划。

Result: 实验表明EPC-AW将系统级成功率平均提升了9.75%。

Conclusion: 通过信息一致性评估而非直接验证可行性的方式，能够有效缓解多智能体规划中的认知校准偏差问题，并实现动态自适应的校准优化。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems can fail even when planned actions are executed correctly because agents may misjudge their knowledge when evaluating plan feasibility, a phenomenon we term epistemic miscalibration in planning. Unlike execution errors, epistemic miscalibration is latent during planning, as generated plans can remain self-consistent and executable without observable errors; the miscalibration is also dynamic, as new information can alter feasibility assessments, potentially obscuring past miscalibration signals and causing them to recur over time. To address this, we propose the Epistemic Planning Calibration Agentic Workflow (EPC-AW), which assesses whether plans remain supported under varying information conditions rather than directly verifying feasibility. EPC-AW employs Information-consistency-based Plan Selection, selecting plans whose evaluations are stable across agents, together with Consistency-guided Epistemic State Refinement to adapt calibration over time by leveraging past discrepancies to guide future planning. Experiments show that EPC-AW improves system-level success by an average of 9.75%.

</details>


### [149] [EDGE-OPD: Internalizing Privileged Context with Evidence Guided On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23493)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Dylan Bates,Aman Sharma,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EDGE-OPD方法，通过引导式rollout和证据掩码解决On-Policy Self-Distillation中特权信息导致的行为偏移问题，在稀有token/身份设定下成功实现知识迁移且不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD虽能利用推理时缺失的特权上下文（如身份、私有事实）提升模型能力，但特权信息可能导致超出预期的行为改变（如修改推理模式、降低通用能力、影响响应长度和风格等），使学生学到副作用而非期望的可迁移行为。

Method: 提出EDGE-OPD，包含两个核心设计：(a) 引导式rollout：在采样时向学生注入特权上下文行为，确保稀有目标行为出现在on-policy数据中；(b) 证据掩码：仅在特权上下文支持采样token的位置更新学生，而非rollout中所有token位置。

Result: 实验表明，OPSD及其变体RLSD（无论是否使用验证器）完全无法学习目标身份，而集成引导式rollout后成功完成学习。掩码区域消融实验显示身份信号定位在正证据尾部，揭示了高效知识迁移与通用能力保持之间的权衡。

Conclusion: EDGE-OPD通过引导式rollout和证据掩码有效解决了OPSD中特权信息导致的行为偏移问题，实现了目标身份的高效学习，同时为理解知识迁移与通用能力保持提供了重要见解。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has gained wide attraction as an LLM post-training paradigm due to its effectiveness in improving capabilities without introducing model distribution drift, and consequently, regression in general tasks. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) is an efficient use-case of OPD, which is appealing as it requires only a single model as a student and teacher, and it also has the benefit of providing privileged context that is a absent at inference time (e.g. a persona, a private fact, or a worked solution) to the teacher during the training process. The challenge in this approach is that the privileged information can change model behavior more than intended: it can modify reasoning, degrade general capabilities, and affect performance indicators like response length, style, or local token preferences. Consequently, OPSD may train the student on side effects rather than a desired, transferable behavior. In this paper, we study this problem in a rare-token/identity setting and propose EviDence GuidEd On-Policy Distillation (EDGE-OPD), a modification of OPSD with two distinct characteristics: a) it uses guided rollouts to inject privileged-context behavior to the student at sampling time, so that the rare target behavior is actually present in the on-policy data, and b) it applies an evidence mask: the student is updated only at token positions where the privileged context supports the sampled token, rather than on every token in the rollout. We empirically show that OPSD (and its variant RLSD, with and without a verifier) completely fail to learn a target identity, while the integration of guided rollouts allows them to succeed. Additionally, mask-region ablations show that the persona signal is localized to the positive-evidence tail, allows us to draw valuable insights about efficient knowledge transfer and preservation of general purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [150] [CP or DP? Why Not Both: A Case Study in the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23569)
*Emma Legrand,Roger Kameugne,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将动态规划(DP)与约束规划(CP)有效结合的混合方法，用于求解部分车间调度问题(PSSP)。其中DP作为主搜索框架，CP作为子程序利用全局约束传播。该方法支持任意时间DP策略和大邻域搜索，能够处理任意前序约束图，验证了DP-CP混合集成的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DP和CP通常是分开使用的两种优化范式，本文旨在证明两者可以有效且优雅地结合，发挥各自优势。

Method: 以DP为主搜索框架，CP作为子程序进行全局约束传播；支持任意时间DP策略（如任意时间列搜索）；利用CP建模的灵活性融入任意前序约束；设计了大邻域搜索方案，在重启时通过部分序调度改进当前解。

Result: 该方法在部分车间调度问题上不敌最先进的纯CP求解器，但成功验证了DP-CP混合集成的可行性。

Conclusion: 主要贡献在于证明了DP与CP混合集成的可行性，该方法灵活且能处理任意前序图结构，具有进一步发展的潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic Programming (DP) and Constraint Programming (CP) are well-established paradigms for solving combinatorial optimization problems. Usually, these two approaches are used separately. This paper aims to show that the two can be combined effectively and elegantly, with DP serving as the primary search framework and CP used as a subroutine to leverage global constraint propagation. This paper presents such an approach for the Partial Shop Scheduling Problem (PSSP), for which a pure DP method has previously been proposed, and efficient CP filtering algorithms are available. The PSSP is a general scheduling problem where each job consists of a set of operations with arbitrary precedence constraints. The approach is flexible enough to accommodate anytime DP strategies, such as anytime column search, whereas the original DP algorithm operated in a strictly layer-wise manner. Moreover, the flexibility of the CP modeling makes it straightforward to incorporate arbitrary precedence constraints. As a result, the model naturally handles any precedence graph and even enables the design of a Large Neighborhood Search (LNS) scheme, in which the DP model is reused, and partial-order schedules are imposed across restarts to improve the incumbent solution. While not competitive with state-of-the-art pure CP solvers for this specific problem, our primary contribution is demonstrating the viability of this hybrid integration.

</details>


### [151] [Co-ReAct: Rubrics as Step-Level Collaborators for ReAct Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23590)
*Jiazheng Kang,Bowen Zhang,Zixin Song,Jiangwang Chen,Xiao Yang,Da Zhu,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Co-ReAct 提出了一种推理时利用评分标准（rubric）引导搜索型智能体逐步决策的框架，通过专门训练的评分生成器（基于 GRPO 和列表式 Spearman 排序奖励）在每一步注入细化指导，显著提升了 ReAct 及多种基线的复杂推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 型智能体在多步搜索推理中依赖内部判断，导致决策浅层、冗余或目标不精准；而先前的评分标准仅用于训练奖励或事后评估，缺乏推理时的逐步行动引导。

Method: 提出 Co-ReAct 框架：在每一步决策时将评分标准注入智能体上下文，指导"推理或行动"的选择；训练专用评分生成器（采用 GRPO 优化列表式 Spearman 秩相关奖励，基于多专家共识排序），确保生成的评分标准具有判别力。

Result: 在 DeepResearchBench 和 SQA-CS-V2 上，Co-ReAct 在基于 8B/14B 开源模型及前沿闭源模型的搜索智能体上，持续优于 ReAct 及代表性的推理时计算基线；训练好的评分生成器可作为即插即用组件提升基线性能。

Conclusion: 推理时注入判别性评分标准能有效引导搜索型智能体的逐步决策，且评分生成器具有良好的迁移性和兼容性，代码已开源。

Abstract: ReAct-style agents for search-intensive, multi-step reasoning tasks rely largely on their own internal judgment to decide what evidence to seek, which reasoning or action step to take next, and when to stop, often producing shallow, redundant, or poorly targeted trajectories. Prior work has explored rubrics as external quality signals, but existing uses are mostly evaluative rather than action-guiding: rubrics typically serve as training-time rewards or post-hoc evaluators of completed outputs, and in deep-research settings they are often coarse-grained and report-level rather than step-level. We introduce Co-ReAct, a rubric-guided action-selection framework that uses rubrics as step-level guidance during inference. At each decision step, Co-ReAct injects a rubric into the agent's context to guide the next Reason-or-Act decision, specifying what the agent should target in evidence seeking, search, reasoning, or self-evaluation. To make this guidance reliable, we train a dedicated rubric generator with GRPO. Unlike prior pairwise or binary preference formulations, our objective optimizes a list-wise Spearman rank-correlation reward against multi-judge expert consensus rankings, encouraging rubrics that are discriminative rather than merely plausible. On DeepResearchBench and SQA-CS-V2, Co-ReAct consistently improves over ReAct and representative test-time compute baselines across search agents built on both 8B/14B open-source and frontier closed-source base models. The trained rubric generator can also serve as a drop-in component that improves these baselines without changing their underlying decision mechanisms. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/ZBWpro/Co-ReAct.

</details>


### [152] [Solving the Aircraft Disassembly Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23592)
*Charles Thomas,Pierre Schaus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究飞机退役拆解调度问题，提出约束规划（CP）和混合整数规划（MIP）两种模型，在包含最多1450个任务的实例上进行了测试，旨在提高拆解流程的效率与盈利性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飞机退役拆解对可持续性至关重要，但利润空间小，高效的拆解调度是确保流程盈利并激励实践的关键。

Method: 分别构建了约束规划（CP）模型和混合整数规划（MIP）模型来解决包含数千个任务、认证要求、优先级约束、飞机平衡约束以及空间容量约束的大规模拆解调度问题。

Result: 在基于工业合作伙伴真实运营数据构建的不同规模实例（最多1450个任务）上测试了两种模型。

Conclusion: 本文为飞机拆解调度问题提供了形式化描述和两种求解方法（CP和MIP），有助于提升拆解流程的经济可行性和可持续性。

Abstract: Dismantling aircrafts reaching their end of life is a complex endeavour that is necessary in terms of sustainability but yields small income margins for air transport companies. An efficient scheduling of the disassembly procedure is thus crucial to ensure the profitability of the process and incentivize practice. This is a large scheduling problem that involves thousands of tasks and many different constraints: Extracting parts that are destined to be reused requires technicians with specific certifications and equipment. Extraction operations might be subject to precedence relations. Furthermore, the aircraft must be kept balanced during the whole process. Finally, some of the locations of the aircraft have a limited space that caps the number of technicians able to work there concurrently. This article presents the problem in details and proposes two approaches to solve the problem: a Constraint Programming model and a MIP model. The models are tested on instances of varying sizes involving up to 1450 tasks, which are based on real operational data provided by an industrial partner.

</details>


### [153] [One Policy, Infinite NPCs: Persona-Traceable Shared RL Policies for Scalable Game Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23652)
*Yoosung Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PCSP是一种基于强化学习的共享策略方法，通过冻结LLM嵌入的条件化，实现大规模、实时、可控制的NPC人格化行为生成，在300人基准测试中达到随机水平17倍的人格识别准确率和22倍于LLM策略的推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生命模拟游戏需要成百上千个具有一致人格且可被设计者通过自然语言控制的NPC，现有方法无法同时满足人格一致性、可控性和实时推理三个约束条件。

Method: 提出PCSP（Persona Conditioned Shared Policy），包含冻结LLM人格嵌入编码、低秩人格投影、神经人格条件化，以及PPO + InfoNCE一致性 + KL散度多样性的联合训练目标。

Result: 在300人格生命模拟基准上，零样本人格识别达到随机水平的17倍，语义-行为对齐Spearman ρ≈0.73，推理速度比LLM策略基线快22倍；消融实验表明InfoNCE轨迹一致性目标不可或缺；在Melting Pot多智能体环境中验证了人格条件化行为分化的泛化能力；UE5部署在64智能体规模下保持亚帧级推理。

Conclusion: 共享RL策略可以在商业游戏引擎中支持可扩展、实时、人格条件化的NPC控制，为大规模生命模拟游戏提供了一种高效可行的技术方案。

Abstract: On a 300-persona life-simulation benchmark, pcsp achieves compositional zero-shot persona identification up to 17x above chance, Spearman rho approx 0.73 semantic-behavioral alignment, and 22x faster inference than an LLM-as-policy baseline. Life simulation games require hundreds to thousands of non-player characters (NPCs) that behave consistently with distinct personalities while remaining controllable through designer-authored natural language. Existing methods fail on constraints like persona consistency, controllability, or real-time inference. We introduce pcsp (Persona Conditioned Shared Policy), a single reinforcement learning policy conditioned on frozen LLM embeddings of free-form persona descriptions. pcsp combines once-per-NPC persona encoding, low-rank persona projection, neural persona conditioning, and a PPO + InfoNCE consistency + KL diversity training objective. Across three experimental settings, ablations show that the InfoNCE trajectory-consistency objective is load bearing: removing it collapses zero-shot persona identification to chance. External validation on Melting Pot 2.4.0 substrates confirms that our method produces persona-conditioned behavioral divergence in multi-agent strategic environments. We distinguish two senses of held-out evaluation: compositional zero-shot and vocabulary-expansion held-out. Finally, a UE5 deployment reproduces the in-engine persona-conditioning ablation at 64 agents with a low failure rate, showing that the sub-frame inference profile survives in a commercial game engine. These results prove that shared RL policies can support scalable, real-time, persona-conditioned NPC control.

</details>


### [154] [MemAudit: Post-hoc Auditing of Poisoned Agent Memory via Causal Attribution and Structural Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23723)
*Zhewen Tan,Yilun Yao,Huiyan Jin,Wenhan Yu,Guoan Wang,Mengyuan Fan,liang lu,Feng Liu,Xiangzheng Zhang,Duohe Ma,Tong Yang,Lin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemAudit，一种针对记忆增强型LLM代理的事后因果记忆审计框架，通过反事实记忆影响分数和记忆一致性图两种互补信号，有效识别导致有害行为的恶意记忆注入。实验证明，该框架在QA和推理代理两种场景下均能将攻击成功率从70%-83.3%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理依赖持久记忆存储历史交互来改进长程任务执行，但这种记忆机制存在安全漏洞：攻击者可通过正常交互注入恶意记录，这些记录在后续被检索时会操纵代理的推理和行为。现有防御主要关注在线干预（如提示过滤或输出拦截），但无法解决有害行为发生后应该追责哪些存储记忆的问题。

Method: MemAudit框架结合两种互补信号：(1) 反事实记忆影响分数，衡量每条记忆对有害输出的因果贡献；(2) 记忆一致性图，识别记忆库中结构异常的记忆。该方法针对MINJA攻击（一种仅通过查询即可实现记忆注入的攻击，恶意记录通过正常代理交互生成和存储，而非直接修改记忆库）进行防御。

Result: 在QA和推理代理两种设置下，MemAudit在现实的事后审计场景中显著降低了攻击成功率。QA场景下攻击成功率从70%降至0%，RAP场景下攻击成功率从83.3%降至0%。

Conclusion: MemAudit框架能够有效识别和防御通过记忆注入实施的后门攻击，为记忆增强型LLM代理提供了一种可行的事后安全审计方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on persistent memory to store past interactions, retrieve relevant demonstrations, and improve long-horizon task execution. However, this memory mechanism also creates a practical security vulnerability: an adversarial user may inject malicious records into the agent's memory through ordinary interaction, and these records can later be retrieved to steer the agent's reasoning and actions. Existing defenses primarily focus on online intervention, such as prompt filtering or output blocking, but they do not address the post-hoc question of which stored memories are responsible after harmful behavior has already been observed. We propose \textbf{MemAudit}, a post-hoc causal memory auditing framework for memory-augmented LLM agents. The framework combines two complementary signals: (1) a counterfactual memory influence score that measures each memory's causal contribution to harmful outputs, and (2) a memory consistency graph that identifies structurally anomalous memories within the broader memory store. We evaluate MemAudit against MINJA, a query-only memory injection attack in which malicious records are generated and stored through normal agent interactions rather than direct memory-bank modification. Across both QA and reasoning-agent settings, MemAudit substantially reduces attack success rates under realistic post-hoc auditing scenarios. The results show that QA attack success is reduced from $70\%$ to $0\%$, while RAP attack success drops from $83.3\%$ to $0\%$.

</details>


### [155] [Agentic Proving for Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23772)
*Alessandro Sosso,Akhil Arora,Bas Spitters*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了Claude Code在CLEVER基准（Lean 4可验证代码生成）上的表现，发现其在规范生成、实现验证和端到端流水线上均达到极高成功率（>80%），并指出现有基准难度已落后于现代智能体证明器能力，需更严格的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估智能体系统在形式化数学中的自动化定理证明能力是否能扩展到程序验证领域，以及现有基准是否仍能有效衡量现代智能体证明器的能力。

Method: 在CLEVER基准（Lean 4）上，采用Claude Code构建智能体证明框架，评估规范生成（含同构评分匹配）、实现验证以及端到端程序生成与验证流水线的成功率，并对自身尝试进行人工反馈质量审查。

Result: 规范生成98.8%有效（81.3%通过同构评分），实现验证87.5%通过，端到端流水线98.1%成功。Claude能提供高质量自反馈，识别失败原因和数据集错误。

Conclusion: 现有程序验证基准难度已不足以匹配现代智能体证明器能力，需更严格、bug弹性的评估方法（尤其应替代同构评分的规范评估）；编译器参与循环的智能体范式是目前基础程序验证最有效的方法。

Abstract: Agentic systems have recently emerged as state-of-the-art approaches for automated theorem proving in formal mathematics. To assess how far these capabilities extend to program verification, we evaluate Claude Code in an agentic proving framework on CLEVER, a Lean 4 benchmark for verifiable code generation. Our results show that Claude generates arguably valid specifications for 98.8% of problems (with 81.3% also accepted by CLEVER's isomorphism-based scoring on the correct portion of the benchmark), certifies implementations against correct ground-truth specifications for 87.5% of problems, and reaches a 98.1% success rate on the end-to-end program generation and verification pipeline over entries with self-consistent premises. Across all stages, Claude further provides high-quality feedback on its own attempts (as confirmed under manual review), identifying underlying causes of failure and lingering bugs in the dataset. These findings highlight a growing mismatch between the difficulty of existing program verification benchmarks and the capabilities of modern agentic provers, and point to the need for more rigorous, bug-resilient evaluation methodologies, and in particular for alternatives to isomorphism-based scoring of generated specifications. More broadly, our results provide empirical evidence that tight compiler-in-the-loop agentic paradigms are currently the most effective approach for foundational program verification.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Binary Edits Robust Multimodal Knowledge Editing with Adversarial Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23780)
*Haoyuan Wang,Xiaohao Liu,Jiajie Su,Jianmao Xiao,Chaochao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）内禀知识编辑泛化性不足的问题，提出LAR（潜在对抗鲁棒化）和RCSL（秩约束子空间学习）方法，通过语义等价知识单元分组和低秩对齐增强编辑的鲁棒泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内禀多模态知识编辑在保持可靠性和局部性方面表现良好，但泛化性严重不足，无法将编辑传播到语义等价的视觉和语言变体上。根本原因在于缺乏显式语义监督、编辑范围僵化以及在高维多模态空间中过度锚定于单个样本。

Method: 1) 将鲁棒性形式化为基于知识单元（语义等价的多模态输入组）的泛化一致性预测；2) 提出潜在对抗鲁棒化（LAR），在联合潜在空间中生成对抗但语义连贯的变体以暴露脆弱语义区域；3) 提出秩约束子空间学习（RCSL），通过基于奇异值的目标函数在编辑层对对抗性表示实施低秩对齐。

Result: 大量实验证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过LAR和RCSL两种机制协同作用，有效解决了内禀多模态知识编辑的泛化瓶颈，实现了鲁棒且可泛化的知识更新。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) need efficient mechanisms to update knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. While intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing achieves strong reliability and locality, it often exhibits limited generality, failing to propagate edits across semantically equivalent visual and linguistic variations. This issue arises from the lack of explicit semantic supervision, rigid editing scopes, and biased anchoring to individual samples in high-dimensional multimodal spaces. We address robust intrinsic multimodal knowledge editing by explicitly targeting generalization. We formalize robustness through knowledge units that group semantically equivalent multimodal inputs and define generality as consistent predictions within each unit. To expose fragile semantic regions, we introduce Latent Adversarial Robustification (LAR), which generates adversarial yet semantically coherent variants in the joint latent space. We further propose Rank-Constrained Subspace Learning (RCSL), enforcing low-rank alignment of adversarial representations at the edit layer via a singular value-based objective. Extensive analysis demonstrates the effectiveness of ASAM empirically.

</details>


### [157] [SPACENUM: Revisiting Spatial Numerical Understanding in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23898)
*Jianshu Zhang,Yijiang Li,Huifeixin Chen,Haoran Lu,Letian Xue,Bingyang Wang,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaceNum框架，系统评估VLM在空间数值理解上的能力，发现当前模型在将数值与空间结构对应方面表现极差，接近随机猜测，主要依赖浅层空间线索，缺乏稳定的坐标感知表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在具身环境中需要输出动作幅度、空间坐标等数值，但这些数值输出是否真正基于空间感知尚不明确，因此需要系统性评估VLM的空间数值理解能力。

Method: 提出SpaceNum统一框架，包含两种互补设置：动态转换（空间探索中的数值变化）和静态布局（空间推理中的数值结构）。设计Num2Space和Space2Num双向任务，通过错误分析、推理轨迹分析和受控干预进行系统评估。

Result: 当前VLMs在空间数值理解任务上表现极差，接近随机猜测水平；模型严重依赖浅层空间线索，难以构建稳定的坐标感知表征，无法从视觉观察中抽象出结构化空间布局。

Conclusion: 显式推理仅带来边际改善，而微调可以部分提升空间数值理解能力并泛化到外部空间推理基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in embodied environments, where they need produce numerical outputs such as action magnitudes and spatial coordinates. Although these numbers appear meaningful, it remains unclear whether these numerical outputs are genuinely grounded in spatial perception. Therefore, in this work, we revisit spatial numerical understanding through SpaceNum, a unified framework that captures two complementary settings: numbers as dynamic transitions during spatial exploration, and numbers as static layouts in spatial reasoning. We formulate two bidirectional tasks, Num2Space and Space2Num, to evaluate how well VLMs map between vision-side spatial structure and language-side numerical representations. We systematically study whether current VLMs truly understand numerical values in spatial settings. Across dynamic transitions and static layouts, we find that models largely fail to ground numbers in spatial meaning and often perform close to random guess. Through error analysis, reasoning trace analysis, and controlled interventions, we show that current VLMs rely heavily on shallow spatial cues, struggle to build stable coordinate-aware representations, and fail to abstract structured spatial layouts from visual observations. We further show that explicit reasoning provides only marginal gains, while tuning can partially improve spatial numerical understanding and transfer to external spatial reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [158] [From Raw Experience to Skill Consumption: A Systematic Study of Model-Generated Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23899)
*Zisu Huang,Jingwen Xu,Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Qihao Yang,Muzhao Tian,Xiaohua Wang,Changze Lv,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Xue Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xiaoqing Zheng,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了技能全生命周期（经验生成→技能提取→技能消费）的效用评估框架，发现模型生成技能平均有益但存在负面迁移，技能效用与模型规模无关。通过深度分析各阶段特征，提出元技能（meta-skill）指导提取过程，一致提升技能质量、减少负面迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能提取方法不断涌现，但缺乏对技能完整生命周期（经验生成、技能提取、技能消费）的系统性理解，不清楚模型生成的技能是否真正有效、何时有效、为何成功或失败。

Method: 构建基于效用的评估框架，在五个多样化智能体任务领域进行系统实验，涵盖多种提取器和目标智能体；随后深度剖析生命周期各阶段，分析经验组成、技能属性、跨消费者迁移等影响因素；最后提出元技能指导提取过程。

Result: 模型生成技能平均有益但存在非平凡的负面迁移；提取器和目标智能体行为不一致，技能效用与模型规模或基线任务强度无关。元技能一致提升技能质量并显著减少负面迁移。

Conclusion: 技能效用具有复杂的非线性特征，需系统评估；元技能可作为实用方案提升技能提取质量，降低负面迁移风险。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly improve by reusing \emph{skills} -- structured procedural artifacts distilled from past experience. In particular, \emph{domain-level} and \emph{model-generated} skills are especially promising. They offer fast adaptation within a domain by encoding domain-specific recurring procedures, and they scale beyond labor-intensive hand-crafting. However, while extraction methods continue to proliferate, understanding remains limited, with no comprehensive study spanning the full skill lifecycle -- \textbf{experience generation}, \textbf{skill extraction}, and \textbf{skill consumption} -- to ask whether such skills actually work, when they work, and what makes them succeed or fail. To close this gap, we build a utility-grounded evaluation framework that provides systematic experimental results across extractors and target agents, covering five diverse agentic task domains. We find that model-generated skills are beneficial on average but exhibit non-trivial negative transfer, and that neither extractors nor targets behave uniformly. A model can be a strong extractor yet a weak consumer, or vice versa, with skill utility independent of model scale or baseline task strength. To explain these patterns, we then dissect each lifecycle stage in depth, analyzing how experience composition shapes skill quality, what properties characterize useful skills, and how the same skill transfers across different consumers. Finally, we translate these findings into a concrete \emph{meta-skill} that guides skill extraction toward the features tied to actual utility, which consistently improves skill quality across domains and substantially reduces negative transfer.

</details>


### [159] [SkillOpt: Executive Strategy for Self-Evolving Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23904)
*Yifan Yang,Ziyang Gong,Weiquan Huang,Qihao Yang,Ziwei Zhou,Zisu Huang,Yan Li,Xuemei Gao,Qi Dai,Bei Liu,Kai Qiu,Yuqing Yang,Dongdong Chen,Xue Yang,Chong Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillOpt，首个系统性的可控文本空间优化器，将agent技能作为冻结agent的外部状态进行训练，通过独立的优化器模型对评分轨迹执行有界编辑，并仅在验证集得分严格提升时接受编辑。在52个评测单元上全部取得最佳或并列最佳，在GPT-5.5上将无技能基线平均提升19.1~24.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent技能生成方法（手工、一次性生成、自修订）缺乏类似深度学习优化器的系统性和可靠性，无法在反馈下稳定改进。本文旨在将技能优化纳入与权重空间优化同等严谨的训练范式。

Method: SkillOpt包含：(1) 独立的优化器模型，基于评分轨迹对技能文档执行有界的增/删/替换编辑；(2) 仅在验证集得分严格提升时才接受编辑；(3) 文本学习率预算、拒绝编辑缓冲区和epoch级慢/元更新机制，保证训练稳定性且推理时零额外模型调用。

Result: 在6个基准、7个目标模型、3种执行框架（直接对话、Codex、Claude Code）的52个评测单元上全部最佳或并列最佳，击败人类、一次性LLM、Trace2Skill、TextGrad、GEPA、EvoSkill等方法。GPT-5.5上分别提升23.5（直接对话）、24.8（Codex）、19.1（Claude Code）个百分点。跨模型规模、跨执行环境、跨相近数学基准的迁移实验均保持有效。

Conclusion: 将技能作为冻结agent的外部状态进行系统性训练是可行的，SkillOpt证明了文本空间优化可以像权重空间优化一样可靠、可控、可迁移，且不增加部署时推理开销。

Abstract: Agent skills today are hand-crafted, generated one-shot, or evolved through loosely controlled self-revision, none of which behaves like a deep-learning optimizer for the skill, and none of which reliably improves over its starting point under feedback. We argue the skill should instead be trained as the external state of a frozen agent, with the same discipline that makes weight-space optimization reproducible. SkillOpt is, to our knowledge, the first systematic controllable text-space optimizer for agent skills: a separate optimizer model turns scored rollouts into bounded add/delete/replace edits on a single skill document, and an edit is accepted only when it strictly improves a held-out validation score. A textual learning-rate budget, rejected-edit buffer, and epoch-wise slow/meta update make skill training stable while adding zero inference-time model calls at deployment. Across six benchmarks, seven target models, and three execution harnesses (direct chat, Codex, Claude Code), SkillOpt is best or tied on all 52 evaluated (model, benchmark, harness) cells and beats every per-cell competitor among human, one-shot LLM, Trace2Skill, TextGrad, GEPA, and EvoSkill skills. On GPT-5.5 it lifts the average no-skill accuracy by +23.5 points in direct chat, by +24.8 inside the Codex agentic loop, and by +19.1 inside Claude Code. Transfer experiments further show that optimized skill artifacts retain value when moved across model scales, between Codex and Claude Code execution environments, and to a nearby math benchmark without further optimization.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Diffusion-based Denoising Beats Vanilla Score Matching in Parameter Estimation: A Theoretical Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22950)
*Benedikt Lütke Schwienhorst,Nadja Klein,Johannes Lederer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于多峰分布，扩散去噪得分匹配估计器（DDSME）通过超参数调优可避免传统得分匹配估计器（SME）误差随模态间距增大而恶化的问题，首次从理论上解释了扩散得分匹配的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统得分匹配估计器在多峰分布（模态间距大）时效率低于最大似然估计，缺乏理论解释。本文旨在从理论上比较并解释扩散去噪得分匹配为何能克服这一缺陷。

Method: 对扩散去噪得分匹配估计器（DDSME）和传统得分匹配估计器（SME）分别建立统计保证，推导误差界，分析其随模态间距变化的渐近行为，并进行理论对比。

Result: 传统SME的误差界随模态间距增大而恶化；而通过合适的超参数调优，DDSME可避免这一现象，保持稳定的估计误差。

Conclusion: 扩散去噪得分匹配在理论上优于传统得分匹配，尤其适用于多峰分布场景，该理论解释为扩散模型的应用提供了统计学习层面的支撑。

Abstract: Score matching is an alternative to maximum likelihood estimation when the normalizing constant is unknown or too costly to evaluate. However, vanilla score matching has shown to be inefficient relative to maximum likelihood estimation for multimodal distributions with well-separated modes, which are commonly encountered in practical applications. We compare a novel diffusion-based denoising score matching estimator (DDSME) to the vanilla score matching estimator (SME) in this scenario. In particular, we prove statistical guarantees for both estimators, showing that the error bound for the vanilla SME worsens when the separation between the modes increases, which can be avoided in case of the DDSME with suitable hyperparameter tuning. This provides a novel theoretical explanation for the superior behavior of diffusion-based score matching over the vanilla version.

</details>


### [161] [KAPLAN: Kolmogorov-Arnold Prognostic Learnable Activation Networks for Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23082)
*Stelios Boulitsakis Logothetis,Angela Wood,Pietro Li ò*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出KAPLAN-HR模型，利用B样条Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）进行非参数生存分析，在避免维度诅咒的同时自动学习协变量与时间的交互作用和非线性效应，在六个临床数据集上达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统生存分析方法（如Cox模型、GAM）需要手动指定交互项和时间变化效应，在临床数据日益复杂的背景下越来越不实用，亟需一种能自动学习这些复杂关系的非参数方法。

Method: 提出KAPLAN-HR模型，基于B样条Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）对条件风险函数进行非参数估计。单层结构恢复GAM，深层结构通过组合自动学习交互和时间变化效应。理论上证明了估计量的收敛率仅依赖于底层KAN表示的平滑性而非协变量维度。

Result: 在六个临床基准数据集上，KAPLAN-HR在预测性能上匹配或超越已有的统计方法和深度学习方法。

Conclusion: KAPLAN-HR通过KAN架构有效缓解了维度诅咒，实现了对生存数据中复杂关系的自动学习，是一种有竞争力的非参数生存分析方法。

Abstract: Survival analysis aims to model how covariates and time jointly shape the time-to-event distribution under right censoring. Classical methods such as the Cox model and generalised additive models (GAMs) require interactions and time-varying effects to be manually specified, which is increasingly impractical on rich clinical datasets. We introduce KAPLAN-HR, a B-spline Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN) for nonparametric estimation of the conditional hazard as a joint function of covariates and time. A single-layer KAPLAN-HR model recovers a GAM, while deeper architectures capture interactions and time-varying effects through composition. We establish a convergence rate for the nonparametric KAN hazard estimator that depends only on the smoothness of the underlying KAN representation and not on the covariate dimension, thereby mitigating the curse of dimensionality for KAN-representable targets. In evaluations over six clinical benchmark datasets, KAPLAN-HR matches or exceeds the predictive performance of established statistical and deep learning survival methods.

</details>


### [162] [LLM Sparsity Prior for Robust Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23102)
*Caleb Skinner,Yihan Guo,Meng Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM Sparsity Prior (LSP)方法，通过分层超先验动态调整LLM生成权重的可信度，将LLM先验信息整合到Spike-and-Slab和Spike-and-Slab Lasso模型中，在高维变量选择中提升鲁棒性和预测精度，并在急性肾损伤医疗数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-Lasso等方法在LLM生成权重质量不佳时性能急剧下降，缺乏对权重质量的量化评估框架，亟需一种能够动态权衡LLM先验信息可靠性的高维变量选择方法。

Method: (1) 建立LLM生成权重质量量化框架；(2) 提出LSP方法，将LLM权重映射为Spike-and-Slab和Spike-and-Slab Lasso模型的先验包含概率，通过全局稀疏度和权重浓度两个可解释超参数实现整合；(3) 采用分层超先验自动折扣不可靠权重；(4) 设计原则性提示工程策略。

Result: 在AKI医疗数据集上，LSP提升了预测精度，识别出基线方法遗漏的临床相关特征，对提示变化具有鲁棒性，在低数据场景下尤其有效。

Conclusion: LSP有效解决了LLM先验信息不可靠时的鲁棒性问题，在高维变量选择中优于现有方法，为LLM与统计模型的可靠融合提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable mechanism to elicit domain-informed prior information for high-dimensional variable selection. However, existing methods such as LLM-Lasso are sensitive to weight quality, with performance degrading substantially when LLM-generated weights are inaccurate. To address this challenge, we first introduce a framework for quantifying the quality of LLM-generated weights, enabling rigorous evaluation of LLM-informed methods across varying weight regimes. We then propose the LLM Sparsity Prior (LSP), which integrates LLM-generated weights into the prior inclusion probabilities of Spike-and-Slab and Spike-and-Slab Lasso models via two interpretable hyperparameters governing global sparsity and weight concentration. Hierarchical hyperpriors on these parameters allow the model to dynamically discount uninformative or misleading weights, improving robustness without sacrificing gains when weights are accurate. Finally, we develop principled prompt engineering strategies and validate the method on a private medical dataset studying Acute Kidney Injury. LSP improves prediction accuracy and identifies clinically relevant features missed by the baselines, with robustness to prompt variation and particular effectiveness in low-data regimes.

</details>


### [163] [Operationalizing Individual Fairness via Gradient Descent and Bradley-Terry Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23145)
*Conlan Olson,Linjun Zhang,Zhun Deng,Pragya Sur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从三元组查询中学习马氏距离度量（Mahalanobis metric）的算法，用于实现个体公平性（individual fairness），即在Bradley-Terry模型下通过谱初始化加梯度下降非凸优化快速收敛到真实度量，并证明基于估计度量的个体公平性足以近似真实度量下的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个体公平性要求"相似的个体应受到相似对待"，但实际部署中最大的障碍是难以学习个体间的相似性度量（similarity metric）。

Method: 采用三元组查询形式（"个体i与j还是k更相似？"），在Bradley-Terry成对比较模型下，先通过谱初始化（spectral initialization）获得初始估计，再通过梯度下降（gradient descent）优化非凸损失函数来学习马氏距离度量。

Result: 算法在非凸损失下仍能快速收敛到真实度量；基于估计度量的个体公平性足以实现与真实度量相近的公平性保证；实验验证了算法收敛性及下游公平预测器的公平性表现。

Conclusion: 该工作为个体公平性在实践中落地提供了可行的度量学习方法，并证明了估计度量下的公平性近似保证，同时讨论了在AI模型调优中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Individual fairness, the notion that "similar individuals should be treated similarly," provides a strong and flexible fairness guarantee for algorithmic decision makers. However, a barrier to implementing individual fairness in practice is the difficulty of learning the similarity metric over individuals. In this work, we present an algorithm for learning a Mahalanobis similarity metric from triplet queries of the form "is individual $i$ more similar to individual $j$ or $k$?" We work in the standard Bradley-Terry model for pairwise comparisons. Our algorithm consists of a spectral initialization step followed by gradient descent. We provide extensive theoretical guarantees on our algorithm, showing that it converges quickly to the ground truth metric despite the non-convexity of the loss in our model. Because our focus is on fairness, we also show that individual fairness with respect to an estimated metric is sufficient to achieve similar fairness with respect to the true metric. We also discuss potential applications of our work to AI model tuning. Finally, we present experimental results that demonstrate the convergence of our algorithm and the fairness performance of downstream fair predictors trained on our estimated metric.

</details>


### [164] [Coupled Training with Privileged Information and Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23268)
*Jiahao Shi,Omar Hagrass,Jason M. Klusowski*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出联合训练方法，在训练阶段利用额外信息但避免其噪声干扰部署模型，通过两模型协同学习提升预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两阶段策略在训练时先用全部信息训练教师模型，再用其伪标签训练仅使用测试时输入的部署模型。但当额外训练信息弱或含噪时，教师模型的错误会误导部署模型甚至降低准确率。

Method: 提出联合训练方法，同时学习两个模型，使部署模型仅在额外信息确实有助时获益，而非继承其错误。分析了适用于大规模高维模型的交替训练算法，并给出联合训练提升预测精度的理论保证。

Result: 在合成数据和真实预测任务上的实验表明，联合训练避免了两阶段方法的失败模式，稳健地优于标准两阶段基线。

Conclusion: 联合训练能有效处理训练时额外信息弱或含噪的情况，让部署模型选择性地利用有用信息而避免错误传播，理论分析和实验验证了一致性优势。

Abstract: In many prediction problems, we have extra information during training (for example, measurements that are expensive or slow to collect) that will not be available when the model is deployed. A common strategy is to first train a model that uses all training information, then use its predictions on unlabeled examples to train a second model that only uses the inputs available at test time. However, when the extra training-only information is weak or noisy, this Two-Stage approach can mislead the deployment model and even hurt accuracy. We propose a joint training method that learns the two models together, so the deployment model can benefit from the extra information only when it actually helps, instead of inheriting its mistakes. We provide guarantees that describe when joint training improves prediction accuracy and analyze a simple alternating training algorithm for large, high-dimensional models. Experiments on synthetic data and real-world prediction tasks show that our approach avoids these failures and robustly outperforms standard Two-Stage baselines.

</details>


### [165] [Concomitant DAG Learning: On the Roles of Noise Adaptivity, Sparsity, and Non-negativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23537)
*Gonzalo Mateos,Samuel Rey,Hamed Ajorlou,Mariano Tepper*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本教程综述了将DAG结构学习重塑为基于分数的连续优化问题的最新信号处理与优化进展，涵盖从组合搜索到连续框架的方法演进，并介绍了噪声自适应联合估计方法，以应对环约束计算挑战和异方差性下的可识别性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果结构未知且干预实验不可行或存在伦理限制，因此需要从观测数据中推断DAG。然而经典方法面临两大障碍：环约束带来的组合爆炸问题（限制可扩展性）以及潜在混淆变量或异方差噪声导致的可识别性挑战。

Method: 将DAG学习重塑为基于邻接矩阵的连续分数估计问题；利用环的光滑表征将组合约束转化为连续优化；采用噪声自适应联合估计方法，同时推断稀疏因果结构和外生噪声水平。

Result: 提出了一套从早期组合搜索、贪婪启发式到现代连续优化框架的完整方法谱系；噪声自适应方法提升了异方差和分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 指出了因果推断、高维统计与可扩展图学习交叉领域的研究机遇，并展望了在线、非线性及神经因果发现等新兴方向。

Abstract: Directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) constitute a central modeling tool to enable principled reasoning about cause-effect interactions in complex systems. However, since the causal structure underlying a group of variables is often unknown and interventions may be infeasible or ethically challenging to implement, there is a need to address the task of inferring DAGs from observational data. However, most classical structure identification approaches face two key obstacles: the combinatorial challenge of enforcing acyclicity, which severely limits scalability, and identifiability challenges arising from latent confounding or heterogeneous noise. This tutorial offers an overview of recent signal processing and optimization advances that address these issues by recasting DAG structure learning as a continuous, score-based estimation problem over adjacency matrices. We begin with a didactic introduction to structural equation models and the formulation of causal graph recovery, followed by a historical survey of score-based methods ranging from early combinatorial search schemes and greedy heuristics to modern continuous frameworks that leverage smooth characterizations of acyclicity. Building on this foundation, we describe concomitant DAG estimation methods that jointly infer sparse causal structure and exogenous noise levels, improving robustness under heteroscedasticity and distribution shifts by rendering the estimator noise adaptive. All in all, the tutorial introduces readers to challenges and opportunities for signal processing research at the crossroads of causal inference, high-dimensional statistics, and scalable graph learning, while outlining emerging directions including online, nonlinear, and neural causal discovery.

</details>


### [166] [Asymmetric Scaling Laws from Sparse Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23591)
*John Sous,Michael Winer*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种稀疏激活下的神经缩放定律模型，发现测试损失由训练输入中从未出现的稀有坐标主导，导致与密集模型不同的瓶颈。研究推导了欠参数化和过参数化 regimes 下的渐近总体损失，揭示了在插值阈值附近的双下降峰，以及由稀疏度决定的缩放指数间隙。还推导了计算最优边界，分析了梯度下降动力学，并证明该效应在非线性激活下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经缩放定律主要针对密集激活模型，而对实际中广泛存在的稀疏激活（如ReLU、Top-k稀疏化等）如何影响缩放行为缺乏理论理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立稀疏激活下神经缩放定律的理论模型。

Method: 构建了一个稀疏激活下的理论模型，分析训练输入中稀有坐标对测试损失的支配作用；分别在欠参数化和过参数化 regimes 下推导渐近总体损失；通过计算最优边界分析数据集大小与模型容量的权衡；研究固定步长梯度下降的不稳定性概率；最后将分析拓展到非线性激活场景。

Result: 稀疏激活导致测试损失在插值阈值附近出现双下降峰；损失曲线由两个不同缩放指数控制，其间隙由稀疏度决定；计算最优边界倾向于在固定预算下增加数据集大小而非模型容量；梯度下降存在不稳定性缩放规律；稀疏诱导效应在非线性激活下依然成立。

Conclusion: 稀疏激活从根本上改变了神经缩放定律的行为模式，引入了一个独特的双指数缩放结构，这对实际中的模型设计和计算资源分配具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We introduce a model for neural scaling laws under sparse activations. In the model, test loss is often dominated by rare coordinates that are never observed in the training input. This mechanism induces a novel bottleneck absent from dense models. We derive the asymptotic population loss in both the underparameterized and overparameterized regimes, and show that the loss exhibits a double-descent peak near the interpolation threshold -- where the number of parameters is just sufficient to fit the training data -- resulting in a loss curve governed by two distinct scaling exponents -- one for the overparameterized regime and one for the underparameterized regime -- with a gap determined by the degree of sparsity. Additionally, we derive a compute-optimal frontier that favors increasing dataset size over model capacity under fixed compute budgets. We also analyze gradient-descent dynamics and identify a scaling law for the probability that fixed-step gradient descent becomes unstable. We further show that the sparsity-induced effect persists under nonlinear activations.

</details>


### [167] [Dirichlet-Based Monte Carlo Dropout for Uncertainty Estimation in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23635)
*Rouaa Hoblos,Noura Dridi,Noureddine Zerhouni,Zeina Al Masry*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将Dirichlet分布框架集成到MC Dropout中，以在保持计算效率的同时提升深度学习模型的不确定性估计质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统神经网络缺乏不确定性估计，而贝叶斯神经网络计算复杂度过高；MC Dropout虽能近似贝叶斯推断但不确定性表征能力有限，需要更高质量的不确定性估计方法。

Method: 在MC Dropout框架中引入Dirichlet分布建模类别概率（基于Sensoy等人2018年的工作），通过多次随机前向传播实现概率建模，在保持计算高效性的同时增强不确定性表征的信息量。

Result: 该方法能生成校准良好的不确定性估计，在不确定性量化质量上优于现有技术。

Conclusion: 所提方法为深度学习中不确定性感知建模提供了实用且高效的解决方案，在不显著增加计算负担的前提下显著提升了不确定性估计的质量。

Abstract: Traditional neural networks provide deterministic predictions without inherent uncertainty estimates. While Bayesian Neural Networks (BNNs) offer a principled approach to uncertainty quantification, their computational complexity limits scalability. Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout, initially introduced as a regularization technique, has been shown to approximate Bayesian inference by enabling probabilistic modeling through multiple stochastic forward passes. In this work, we enhance uncertainty estimation in deep learning by integrating a Dirichlet-based framework within MC Dropout. Specifically, we leverage the formulation proposed by Sensoy et al. (2018), where class probabilities are modeled using a Dirichlet distribution, allowing for a more informative uncertainty representation. The proposed approach maintains the computational efficiency of MC Dropout while improving the quality of uncertainty estimates. We discuss the theoretical foundations of our method and compare it with existing uncertainty quantification techniques. The results highlight the effectiveness of the proposed method in producing well-calibrated uncertainty estimates, offering a practical solution for uncertainty-aware deep learning models.

</details>


### [168] [Learning Kernel-Based MDPs from Episodic Preferential Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23650)
*Nikola Pavlovic,Sattar Vakili,Qing Zhao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于偏好反馈的核马尔可夫决策过程进行了严格的理论分析，证明了在仅有偏好标签（无数值奖励）的情况下，学习算法能够以亚线性遗憾界收敛到最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类反馈通常以偏好形式（而非标量奖励）出现，因此需要从偏好反馈中进行强化学习（RLHF）。然而，现有理论分析大多假设数值奖励可用，缺乏对仅偏好设置下MDP学习的严格理论理解。

Method: 采用基于核的假设对奖励函数和转移函数进行建模，使用Bradley-Terry-Luce模型将偏好概率与累积奖励差关联，设计了基于偏好的值估计方法和置信集，在每集结束时仅利用二元偏好标签进行学习。

Result: 证明了高概率遗憾界随情节数亚线性增长，即学习策略的值收敛到最优策略的值。

Conclusion: 该工作为RLHF提供了严格的理论基础，验证了在一般化的核MDP框架下仅通过偏好反馈即可实现策略优化，拓展了偏好学习在复杂环境中的理论可行性。

Abstract: Human feedback often arrives as preferences rather than calibrated numeric rewards, motivating reinforcement learning from preferential feedback, also referred to as reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF). We present a rigorous theoretical study of preference-only learning in episodic kernel MDPs. In each episode, the learner deploys two policies from a common start state and receives a single binary label indicating which trajectory is preferred, modeled by a Bradley--Terry--Luce link on the difference of cumulative (unobserved) rewards. Under kernel-based assumptions on the reward and transition functions (one of the most general models amenable to theoretical analysis) we develop preference-based value estimation and confidence sets tailored to end-of-episode comparisons.We prove high-probability regret bounds that scale sublinearly in the number of episodes, implying that the value of the learned policy converges to that of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [169] [Move on Muon : A Hamiltonian probability gradient flow perspective of Muon optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23871)
*Aratrika Mustafi,Soumya Mukherjee,Bharath K. Sriperumbudur*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发展了正则化Muon优化器在概率测度空间上的梯度流理论，将其识别为核范数的Fenchel对偶光滑化梯度，推导了惯性连续时间极限，证明了哈密顿耗散恒等式和指数收敛率，并扩展到希尔伯特值特征映射的块状Muon概率流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并理论化Muon优化器的几何结构，将其从单矩阵参数推广到平均场神经网络训练的有限粒子概率目标，建立严格的连续时间极限和收敛性理论。

Method: (1) 将正则化正交化映射识别为核范数的Fenchel对偶光滑化梯度，建立Muon更新与镜像/近端步的联系；(2) 在惯性缩放步长和动量下推导有限粒子连续时间极限，进而过渡到相空间平均场方程；(3) 证明哈密顿耗散恒等式，在梯度支配、有界动量和曲率/对齐假设下导出指数收敛率；(4) 建立平均场极限的适定性并证明传播混沌保证；(5) 扩展到产品矩阵空间上的希尔伯特值特征映射，形成块状Muon概率流。

Result: 获得了正则化Muon的镜像/近端步几何解释；推导了阻尼哈密顿概率动力学，证明了哈密顿能量单调递减；在适当假设下获得了连续和离散时间的指数收敛率；建立了平均场极限的适定性和传播混沌保证；给出了块状Muon概率流在平滑Transformer混合专家模型中的应用。

Conclusion: 本文为正则化Muon优化器提供了深刻的几何视角，将其与平均场理论、镜像下降和哈密顿动力学统一起来，为理解和分析Muon类优化器在神经网络训练中的行为提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We develop a gradient flow on the space of probability measures defined on matrix-valued parameters induced by regularized Muon, an analytically smoothed version of the idealized Muon optimizer. The key observation is that the regularized orthogonalization map is the gradient of a smooth Fenchel-dual smoothing of the nuclear norm. This identifies the (regularized) Muon update as a mirror/prox step in the update variable, with momentum acting as the dual coordinate. We use this structure to lift Muon from a single matrix parameter to finite-particle probability objectives of the form $J(ρ)=R\left(\int F d ρ\right)$, a setting motivated by mean-field descriptions of neural-network training, and derive the inertial continuous-time limit. Using this structure, we derive the finite-particle continuous-time limit under the inertial scaling of step size and momentum, and then pass to a phase-space mean-field equation over probability laws on parameter-momentum pairs. The resulting flow can be shown to be a damped Hamiltonian probability dynamics whose kinetic energy is induced by the regularized Muon mirror potential. We prove an exact Hamiltonian dissipation identity, showing that the Hamiltonian energy decreases monotonically. While the target objective itself need not be monotone along the inertial Muon dynamics, under additional gradient-dominance, bounded-momentum, and curvature/alignment assumptions, we obtain continuous and discrete-time exponential convergence rates for the objective gap. We also study the well-posedness of the mean-field limit equation and establish propagation of chaos guarantees for the interacting particle system. Finally, we extend the formulation to Hilbert-valued feature maps on product matrix spaces, yielding a blockwise Muon probability flow applicable to smooth transformer mixture-of-experts models.

</details>


### [170] [On the Stability of Spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich Flows and Their Implications for Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23879)
*Aratrika Mustafi,Soumya Mukherjee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了球面Hellinger-Kantorovich（SHK）梯度流的微扰理论，量化了不同势函数下流动力学差异的传播，并将其应用于差分隐私中指数机制的近似采样，提供了纯DP和近似DP保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发SHK梯度流的微扰理论，以量化不同势函数下动力学差异随时间传播的行为，并应用于差分隐私的隐私保证分析。

Method: 通过建立均匀微扰界，得到对数似然比和Rényi散度的无维点态控制；在额外结构下进一步导出KL散度界。

Result: 提供了SHK采样器的显式时间依赖纯DP保证（通过似然比控制）和近似DP保证（通过KL界导出的曲棍球stick散度）；分离了指数机制固有次优性与有限时间采样误差的效用界。

Conclusion: SHK梯度流的微扰理论为基于指数机制的隐私保护采样提供了严格的隐私与效用分析框架。

Abstract: Gradient-flow sampling interprets a Gibbs distribution as the minimizer of an energy functional over probability measures and generates dynamics converging to this target. Under spherical Hellinger-Kantorovich (SHK) geometry, the flow couples transport and reaction and coincides with birth-death Langevin dynamics. In this work, we develop a perturbation theory for SHK gradient flows. For two potentials $V$ and $V^{\prime}$, we compare the associated flows from a common initialization and quantify how potential discrepancies propagate over time. A uniform perturbation bound yields dimension-free, pointwise control of the log-likelihood ratio and Rényi divergence, while additional structure allows us to derive bounds for the KL divergence as well. We apply these results to approximate sampling for the exponential mechanism in differential privacy. The likelihood-ratio control provides explicit time-dependent Pure-DP guarantees for SHK-based samplers, while the KL bound yields Approximate-DP certificates via hockey-stick divergence. We also derive a utility bound separating intrinsic exponential-mechanism suboptimality from finite-time sampling error.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [An optimal first-order method for smooth and strongly convex composite optimization and its stationary limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22929)
*Manu Upadhyaya,Daniel Berg Thomsen,Aymeric Dieuleveut,Adrien B. Taylor*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Prox-ITEM，一种用于最小化光滑强凸函数f与凸函数g之和的最优近端梯度方法。Prox-ITEM在光滑情形(g=0)退化为ITEM。作者证明了Prox-ITEM具有与ITEM相同的精确距离收敛率，并且该率在基于span的一阶方法中达到极小极大最优。此外，还识别了Prox-ITEM的平稳极限Prox-TMM，它是三重动量方法(TMM)在复合设置下的近端扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将信息论精确方法(ITEM)和三重动量方法(TMM)从光滑情形扩展到复合优化设置（即可分目标函数f+g），并保持最优收敛率。

Method: 提出Prox-ITEM算法：一种结合梯度Oracle（用于f）和近端Oracle（用于g）的最优近端梯度方法。通过理论分析证明其距离收敛率，并识别其平稳极限Prox-TMM。

Result: Prox-ITEM达到与ITEM相同的精确距离收敛率；该率在基于span的一阶方法中极小极大最优；Prox-TMM在复合设置下达到TMM对应的收敛率。

Conclusion: Prox-ITEM是复合优化中具有最优收敛率的一阶方法，Prox-TMM是TMM在复合设置下的自然扩展。

Abstract: We introduce Prox-ITEM, an optimal proximal gradient method for minimizing $f+g$, where $f$ is smooth and strongly convex, and $g$ is convex, proper, and lower semicontinuous. In the smooth case $g=0$, Prox-ITEM reduces to the information-theoretic exact method (ITEM). We prove an exact distance-to-solution bound for Prox-ITEM with the same distance-convergence rate as ITEM, and show that this rate is minimax optimal among span-based first-order methods using the same number of gradient-oracle calls for $f$ and an arbitrary number of proximal-oracle calls for $g$. We also identify the stationary limit of Prox-ITEM, denoted Prox-TMM, which gives a proximal extension of the triple momentum method (TMM) to the composite setting and achieves the corresponding TMM distance-convergence rate.

</details>


### [172] [Performance Bounds for Rollout Policies in Stochastic Shortest Path Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22965)
*Anders Hansson,Bo Wahlberg*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机最短路径问题中rollout和确定性等价rollout策略的性能保证，证明其最优性损失由值函数近似的均匀精度和到达终端状态的期望时间控制，并给出了性能差异恒等式和确定性尖锐性示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机最短路径问题中，rollout策略被广泛使用，但其非渐近性能保证尚不清晰。本文旨在提供不需要渐近假设的直接性能证书，揭示期望击中时间在无折扣瞬态环境中扮演的角色。

Method: 通过建立性能差异恒等式，将rollout策略的次优性分解为瞬态占据测度上的累积误差；利用均匀值函数近似误差和期望击中时间给出上界；在均匀击中时间和Foster-Lyapunov漂移条件下扩展论证至确定性等价rollout，并引入局部模型失配项。

Result: 非渐近性能证书：rollout策略的损失由值函数近似的均匀精度与期望击中时间的乘积控制；性能差异恒等式精确刻画了次优性的累积机制；确定性示例证明了击中时间因子的不可去除性。

Conclusion: 期望击中时间在无折扣瞬态设置中起到了类似于折扣因子或有限时域参数的作用；该结果统一了近似动态规划中的相关理论，并为rollout策略提供了严谨的误差控制框架。

Abstract: This paper concerns rollout and certainty-equivalent rollout policies for stochastic shortest path problems with absorbing terminal states. The main result provides a direct non-asymptotic performance certificate for a fixed rollout policy: the loss relative to the optimal value is controlled by the uniform accuracy of the value approximation and by the expected time for which the rollout closed loop remains away from the terminal state. Thus, in the undiscounted transient setting, the expected hitting time plays the role of a discount or finite-horizon parameter in more standard approximate dynamic programming bounds. This paper also gives a performance-difference identity showing that suboptimality is exactly accumulated through the transient occupation measure, and a deterministic sharpness example showing that the hitting-time factor is unavoidable. Finally, consequences under uniform hitting-time and Foster-Lyapunov drift conditions are given, and extend the argument to certainty-equivalent rollout by adding a separate local model-mismatch term.

</details>


### [173] [Extremum seeking with exponential convergence via high-order Lie bracket approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23029)
*Victoria Grushkovskaya,Sameh A. Eisa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对极值搜索系统中的李括号近似方法进行扩展，提出一种新型设计，使系统沿高阶李括号方向运动，从而在"平底型"（高次多项式类）代价函数上实现指数收敛，无需海森矩阵信息或极小点处海森非零的假设。数值仿真验证了该设计在四、六、八次代价函数上的有效性，并优于牛顿法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极值搜索系统的李括号近似方法中，对于二次型代价函数可实现指数收敛，但对更高次（>2）的代价函数只能达到多项式衰减速率。本文旨在突破这一局限，实现高次平坦代价函数上的指数收敛，且不依赖海森矩阵信息。

Method: 提出一种新型极值搜索系统设计，通过使系统运动方向与高阶李括号对齐，利用高阶李括号的逼近特性，在无需海森矩阵或极小点处海森非零假设的条件下，对高次多项式类代价函数实现指数收敛。

Result: 数值仿真表明，所提设计分别在四次、六次和八次代价函数上实现了指数收敛，且在与牛顿法的对比中表现更优。

Conclusion: 本文成功将极值搜索系统中李括号逼近方法的收敛性能从二次型推广至高次平坦代价函数，实现了指数收敛速率，且放松了对海森矩阵信息的依赖，为更广泛场景下的极值搜索优化提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper focuses on the further development of the Lie bracket approximation approach for optimization and control via extremum seeking systems. Classical results in this area provide algorithms with exponential convergence rates for quadratic-like cost functions, and polynomial decay rates for cost functions of higher degrees. This paper proposes a novel design that ensures the motion of the extremum seeking system along directions associated with higher-order Lie brackets, thereby achieving exponential convergence for cost functions that are "flat-bottomed", i.e., polynomial-like but of degree greater than two and unlike literature assumptions, we do not require Hessian information or strictly non zero Hessian at the minimum. Numerical simulations are presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed designs and their exponential convergence on fourth-, sixth-, and even eighth-degree cost functions. We include a comparison that shows our design outperforming a Newton-based method.

</details>


### [174] [Proximal DCA for Fréchet Regression on Riemannian Manifolds with Bounded Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23097)
*Yamin Zhou,César A. Uribe*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究完备黎曼流形上带混合符号权重的Fréchet回归问题，提出FRIDA算法（黎曼迭代DC算法），证明了极小值的存在性、内点性、曲率依赖的强凸性以及迭代的收敛性，并给出了次线性复杂度估计和KL型局部收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fréchet回归将线性回归推广到度量空间值响应，但混合符号权重导致目标为有符号重心问题，而非标准凸重心问题。在黎曼流形上，全局测地凸性的缺失和割迹附近距离平方的非光滑性进一步复杂化了问题。现有方法缺乏针对此类有符号Fréchet回归的严格优化理论框架。

Method: 将优化限制在包含响应支撑集的强凸正规球内，利用局部光滑性、Hessian比较定理和Jacobi场估计，将问题建模为局部受控的黎曼近端DC（Difference of Convex）问题。提出FRIDA算法，包括精确和非精确近端DC算法。通过显式有符号权重条件证明极小值的存在性和内点性，建立曲率依赖的近端子问题强凸性，并证明迭代的下降性和收敛到稳定点。

Result: 证明了有符号权重条件下极小值的存在性与内点性；建立了曲率依赖的近端子问题强凸性；证明了FRIDA算法的下降性和收敛到稳定点；推导了次线性复杂度估计；在实解析条件下，获得了全序列收敛及KL型局部收敛率。

Conclusion: 该工作为有界曲率流形上的有符号Fréchet回归提供了严格的优化理论框架，FRIDA算法在理论上保证了收敛性，为流形上带混合符号权重的回归分析奠定了算法基础。

Abstract: Fréchet regression generalizes linear regression to metric-space-valued responses by defining fitted values as minimizers of weighted Fréchet functionals. Since these weights may have mixed signs, the resulting objective is a signed barycenter problem rather than a standard convex barycenter problem. On Riemannian manifolds, this is further complicated by the lack of global geodesic convexity and possible nonsmoothness of squared distances near cut loci. We study signed Fréchet regression on complete manifolds with two-sided bounded sectional curvature. By restricting optimization to a strongly convex normal ball containing the response support, we use local smoothness, Hessian comparison, and Jacobi-field estimates to formulate the problem as a locally controlled Riemannian proximal DC problem. This leads to FRIDA (Fréchet Regression via Riemannian Iterative DC Algorithm), an exact and inexact proximal DC algorithm for computing regression fits. We prove existence and interiority of minimizers under explicit signed-weight conditions, establish curvature-dependent strong convexity of the proximal subproblems, and show descent and convergence of the iterates to stationary points. We also derive sublinear complexity estimates and, under real-analyticity, obtain full-sequence convergence with KL-type local rates. These results provide a rigorous optimization framework for signed Fréchet regression on manifolds with bounded curvature.

</details>


### [175] [Selective Ambulance Dispatch Under Contextual Travel-Time Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23378)
*Zikun Lin,Daniel Zhuoyu Long,Viet Anh Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: IDEAL是一种选择性双派遣框架，通过乐观时间差阈值决定是否派遣第二辆救护车，使用弱监督双层表示网络学习上下文相关行程时间，并通过Burg散度扰动建模不确定性，在OHCA场景中实现了响应时间与资源消耗的更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 院外心脏骤停（OHCA）救护车响应中，静态区域划分和确定性行程时间估计难以应对动态拥堵，而始终双派遣虽能降低风险但消耗过多车队资源，需要在响应及时性与资源有限性之间取得更好平衡。

Method: 提出IDEAL框架：1) 使用弱监督双层表示网络从行程记录中学习上下文相关的边行程时间；2) 通过Burg散度扰动共享度量空间建模不确定性；3) 将乐观时间差计算转化为差凸规划并设计高效预言机；4) 采用小批量保守梯度训练非光滑模型并保证渐近收敛。

Result: 与香港消防处合作，使用历史OHCA记录和实时自适应模拟评估，IDEAL在所有基于区域和基于Google地图的基线方法中取得了更强的响应时间与资源权衡表现。

Conclusion: 选择性双派遣策略能有效平衡OHCA响应时间与车队容量，弱监督学习结合不确定性建模可提升动态交通环境下的派遣决策质量。

Abstract: Ambulance response is time-critical in out-of-hospital cardiac arrest (OHCA), where dispatchers must balance timely arrivals with limited fleet capacity. Static territories and deterministic travel-time estimates are vulnerable to dynamic congestion, while always-dual dispatch adds redundancy but consumes fleet capacity. We propose IDEAL (Intelligent Dual dispatch of Emergency AmbuLances), a selective dual-dispatch framework that sends a second ambulance only when the optimistic gap between primary and secondary paths exceeds a threshold. IDEAL learns context-specific edge travel times from trip-level dispatch records, including unobserved routes, using a weakly supervised bilevel representation network. We train the nonsmooth model with mini-batch conservative gradients and prove an asymptotic convergence guarantee. IDEAL models uncertainty via Burg-divergence perturbations to a shared metric in the learned representation space, thereby inducing correlated changes in edge travel times and learning context-specific radii from historical underprediction errors. For real-time decisions, IDEAL casts optimistic-gap computation as a difference-of-convex program and derives an efficient oracle with complexity guarantees. In collaboration with the Hong Kong Fire Services Department, we evaluate IDEAL using historical OHCA records and real-time adaptive simulations. The results achieve a stronger response-time/resource trade-off relative to all region-based and Google-based baselines.

</details>


### [176] [A Stochastic Implicit Proximal Point Algorithm for Solving Linearly Constrained Stochastic Minimax Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23488)
*Kehan Zhu,Jiani Wang,Yu-Hong Dai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于方差缩减的随机隐式邻近点算法，结合半光滑牛顿法和Armijo线搜索求解带非光滑正则化项的大规模极小极大问题，理论上证明了迭代点列在期望意义下全局q-线性收敛到鞍点、乘子全局r-线性收敛到乘子集，数值实验展示了在机器学习的计算效率和步长选择上的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理带非光滑正则化项和耦合线性方程的大规模强凸-强凹极小极大问题时，存在计算效率低、步长敏感等问题，亟需一种高效且收敛性良好的算法。

Method: 提出随机隐式邻近点算法，引入方差缩减技术；随机 oracle 分为放回与不放回两种采样方式；利用半光滑牛顿法配合Armijo线搜索求解隐式邻近点更新的子问题。

Result: 迭代点列在期望意义下全局q-线性收敛到鞍点，乘子全局r-线性收敛到乘子集；在机器学习问题上，计算效率和步长选择均优于现有最优算法。

Conclusion: 所提算法能高效求解一类带非光滑正则化项和耦合线性约束的强凸-强凹极小极大问题，理论上具有强收敛保证，实际性能优越。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach to solving large-scale minimax problems with nonsmooth regularizers. We propose a stochastic implicit proximal point algorithm with variance reduction techniques where stochastic oracles are selected in two cases -- with or without replacement. The semismooth Newton methods with Armijo line search is used to solve the implicit proximal point update subproblem in each iteration. The algorithm efficiently handles the strongly-convex-strongly-concave objective function with nonsmooth regularizers and coupling linear equations, which is proved to exhibit global q-linear convergence of the iterations to the saddle point and global r-linearly convergence of the multipliers to the multiplier set in expectation. Numerical experiments on machine learning problems demonstrate the superiority of the proposed method over state-of-the-art algorithms in terms of both computational efficiency and selection of the step sizes.

</details>


### [177] [End-to-End Pseudo-Measurement Learning for State Estimation under Limited Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23525)
*J. G. De la Varga,S. Pineda,A. Stratigakos,J. M. Morales*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端学习的配电网状态估计框架，将加权最小二乘估计器作为神经网络层嵌入，通过隐式微分联合优化伪量测生成与物理估计过程，在IEEE 30/33节点系统上显著提升了估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网中分布式能源的接入增加了运行复杂性，但现有监测基础设施有限且异构，导致系统可观测性不足。传统方法将伪量测生成与物理估计分离，无法充分利用两者间的耦合关系。

Method: 提出将WLS估计器作为可微层嵌入NN架构，通过端到端学习框架联合训练伪量测生成网络和状态估计器。利用隐式微分实现梯度反向传播，使NN参数能够直接接收估计误差的反馈信号。

Result: 在IEEE 30节点和33节点系统上的大量实验表明，所提框架在各种负荷条件和量测配置下，状态估计精度均持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将物理模型(WLS)嵌入数据驱动模型(NN)的端到端框架，有效解决了配电网状态估计中伪量测与物理估计脱节的问题，为低可观测性条件下的DSSE提供了新范式。

Abstract: Distribution System State Estimation (DSSE) is becoming increasingly important with the integration of Distributed Energy Resources (DERs) and the active operation of distribution networks (DNs), but it remains challenging due to the limited and heterogeneous monitoring infrastructure available in these networks. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a novel DSSE framework that restores observability through data-driven pseudo-measurements generated by a Neural Network (NN), while preserving the exact non-linear AC power flow model within a classical Weighted Least Squares (WLS) estimator. Unlike conventional approaches that generate pseudo-measurements independently of the physical estimation process, the proposed method explicitly couples both components through an end-to-end learning formulation. Specifically, the WLS estimator is embedded as a layer within the NN architecture, enabling implicit differentiation to propagate the impact of pseudo-measurements on the final estimation error back to the NN parameters. Extensive numerical experiments on the IEEE 30-bus and IEEE 33-bus systems demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in state estimation (SE) accuracy under a wide range of loading conditions and measurement configurations.

</details>


### [178] [RA-DCA: A Randomized Active-Set DCA for Directional Stationarity in Max-Structured DC Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23550)
*Yi-Shuai Niu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出RA-DCA算法，一种基于随机化顶点优先的活跃集DCA方法，通过投影活跃梯度到采样方向并检查采样顶点残差，避免标准DCA收敛到非方向稳定点，同时降低活跃集筛选的计算成本。在正则性、数值活跃集一致性和随机嵌入假设下，算法的每个聚点都几乎必然方向稳定。MATLAB实验验证了该方法在退化模型上的有效性，并通过块Top-k测试展示了该筛选思路在组合枚举困难时的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非光滑差凸规划中，标准DCA迭代可能收敛到非方向稳定的临界点，而精确活跃顶点筛选在活跃集较大或组合结构复杂时计算代价高昂，因此需要一种高效且能保证方向稳定性的随机化活跃集方法。

Method: 提出RA-DCA（随机化活跃集DCA），采用顶点优先策略：将活跃梯度投影到采样方向，检查采样顶点残差，仅在残差较低时使用小规模线性规划作为凸组合后备方案。该方法将随机化筛选层简化为矩阵乘法运算，并设计了安全机制（safeguard）保证收敛性质。

Result: 在正则性、数值活跃集一致性和随机嵌入假设下，算法每个聚点几乎必然方向稳定。MATLAB实验在退化最大仿射、最大二次和稀疏支撑函数模型上验证了算法避免非稳定临界点并紧密跟踪全活跃顶点扫描的效果。块Top-k测试表明该筛选思想在精确聚合枚举组合困难时依然有效。

Conclusion: RA-DCA通过随机化活跃筛选有效解决了标准DCA的非方向稳定收敛问题，同时大幅降低了计算复杂度。不同诊断案例（修剪回归、互补性、QUBO）区分了活跃集选择有效和由多起点搜索、DC分裂或其他问题特征主导的情形。

Abstract: We study nonsmooth difference-of-convex programs whose subtracted convex term is a finite maximum of smooth convex functions. In this setting, standard DCA iterations may converge to critical points that are not directionally stationary, whereas exact active-vertex screening can be expensive when active sets are large or combinatorial. We propose RA-DCA, a vertex-first randomized active-set DCA that projects active gradients onto sampled directions, checks a sampled vertex residual, and uses a small linear program only as a low-residual convex-combination fallback. The method preserves the descent structure of DCA and reduces the randomized screening layer to matrix multiplications. Under the stated regularity, numerical active-set consistency, and random-embedding assumptions, every accumulation point generated by the safeguarded method is directionally stationary with probability one. MATLAB experiments first test the theorem on degenerate max-affine, max-quadratic, and sparse support-function models, where the safeguard avoids nonstationary critical points and closely tracks a full active-vertex scan. Block top-k tests then show that the same screening idea remains useful when exact aggregate enumeration is combinatorial. Trimmed-regression, complementarity, and QUBO diagnostics separate cases where active-set selection helps from cases dominated by multistart search, the DC split, or other problem-specific features.

</details>


### [179] [Coupling optimization algorithms and monotone control systems: Suboptimal model predictive control as an operator splitting scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23581)
*Till Preuster,Hannes Gernandt,Manuel Schaller*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于单调动力系统（如端口-哈密顿系统）互联的次优模型预测控制框架，将优化器与受控对象均建模为动态系统并通过结构化互联耦合，建立了连续时间闭环公式，揭示了次优MPC算法与算子分裂时间离散化之间的直接联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MPC将优化器视为瞬时映射，忽略了优化过程的动态特性。本文旨在通过将优化器也建模为动态系统，建立统一的次优MPC理论框架，并桥接迭代优化算法与动力系统控制理论。

Method: 将受控对象与优化器均建模为动力系统，通过（拟）单调算子的结构化互联形成连续时间闭环系统；利用算子分裂方法对连续时间动力学进行时间离散化，实现次优MPC算法。

Result: 建立了耦合优化器-受控对象动力学的适定性，提供了次优MPC框架的统一解释，证明了标准次优MPC算法可视为连续时间动力学的算子分裂时间离散化。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了次优MPC方案的理论基础，建立了迭代优化算法与动力系统控制理论之间的直接联系，为基于单调算子互联的MPC设计提供了新视角。

Abstract: We propose a framework for suboptimal model predictive control (MPC) based on the interconnection of monotone dynamical systems, such as port-Hamiltonian systems. In contrast to classical MPC formulations, where the optimizer is treated as an instantaneous mapping, we model both the plant and the optimizer as dynamical systems and couple them through a structured interconnection. This leads to a continuous-time closed-loop formulation governed by (quasi-)monotone operators. Within this setting, we establish well-posedness of the coupled optimizer-plant dynamics and provide a unified interpretation of suboptimal MPC schemes. In particular, we reveal a direct connection between iterative optimization algorithms and dynamical control systems theory by showing that standard suboptimal MPC algorithms can be understood as time discretizations of the underlying continuous-time dynamics via operator splitting methods.

</details>


### [180] [Unlocking the Informational Value of Marginal Costs for Exact Time Series Aggregation in Generation Expansion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23589)
*Luca Santosuosso,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对含跨期储能约束的发电扩展规划（GEP）混合整数线性规划模型，提出基于边际成本的启发式时间序列聚合（TSA）方法，通过保留完整模型的有效约束实现精确时间聚合，嵌入迭代求解算法并给出理论支撑的最大误差界，数值实验表明在完整优化不可行时仍能恢复可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GEP问题为NP-hard，计算复杂度随规划期和二元变量数量急剧增长，现有启发式TSA方法缺乏理论保障。

Method: 提出基于边际成本的时间序列聚合方法，通过保留完整规模模型的有效约束实现精确时间聚合；将该方法嵌入可迭代收紧最大误差界的求解算法中，提供形式化的性能保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提算法在完整规模优化不可解时仍能恢复可解性，并保持计算优势。

Conclusion: 基于边际成本的TSA方法可为决策者提供有理论保障的近似最优解，显著提升GEP问题在大规模场景下的可解性。

Abstract: This paper addresses the generation expansion planning (GEP) problem, formulated as a mixed-integer linear programming model with intertemporal storage constraints. Being generally NP-hard, the problem's computational complexity grows sharply with the planning horizon and the number of binary variables. While previous research has tackled this challenge using heuristic time series aggregation (TSA) methods, we propose a theoretically grounded marginal-cost-based TSA, designed to construct an aggregated model that preserves the active constraints of its full-scale counterpart, thereby explicitly targeting exact temporal aggregation. This TSA method is embedded within solution algorithms that iteratively refine theoretically validated bounds on the maximum error introduced by the temporal aggregation relative to conventional full-scale optimization, thus offering a formal performance guarantee to the decision-maker. Numerical results highlight the computational advantages of the proposed algorithms, which notably recover tractability whereas full-scale optimization proves intractable.

</details>


### [181] [Linear quadratic optimal transport and interpolation inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23608)
*Luca Rizzi,Alec Jacopo Almo Schiavoni Piazza*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在线性二次型最优控制框架下研究最优传输问题，建立了Monge问题的适定性，分析了最优传输映射的正则性，并推导了熵泛函的插值不等式，为次黎曼几何中的比较理论提供了模型空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些系统可作为次黎曼几何中比较理论的自然模型空间，因此需要深入理解其最优传输性质。

Method: 在线性二次型最优控制系统的框架下，将最优传输问题与LQ最优控制相结合，通过分析Monge问题的适定性和传输映射的正则性，并研究测度的位移插值与熵泛函的插值不等式。

Result: 建立了Monge问题的适定性，刻画了最优传输映射的正则性，推广了Hindawi等人（2011）关于非负成本的结果，并推导了熵泛函的插值不等式。

Conclusion: 线性二次型最优控制系统可作为次黎曼几何中比较理论的有效模型空间，其最优传输结构具有良好的适定性和正则性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the optimal transport problem within the framework of Linear Quadratic optimal control systems. We establish the well-posedness of the Monge problem and analyze the regularity of the resulting optimal transport map, extending the results obtained in [Hindawi, Pomet, Rifford, 2011] for non-negative costs. Furthermore, we study the displacement interpolation of measures and derive general interpolation inequalities for entropy functionals. Our analysis is motivated by the role of these systems as natural model spaces for comparison theory in sub-Riemannian geometry.

</details>


### [182] [A Non-Iterative Algorithm for Clearing Two-Layer Energy-Sharing Markets with Voltage Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23671)
*Tonghua Liu,Yifan Su,Zhaojian Wang,Feng Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于含电压约束的两层能量共享市场的非迭代出清算法，通过推导下层市场的有效最佳响应函数，将双层MPEC问题转化为单层MISOCP，在IEEE 123节点系统（含12,300个产消者）上实现0.829秒内求解，最大误差低于0.01%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实时分层能量共享市场出清方法大多依赖线性化或直流潮流模型，未能显式处理无功功率和电压安全约束。考虑AC网络约束后，问题变为大规模双层MPEC，难以实时求解。

Method: 首先推导出下层能量共享市场的有效最佳响应函数，利用定价-耦合结构将均衡搜索降为一维；然后将该函数嵌入上层网络约束问题，将双层MPEC重构为单层MISOCP。

Result: 在IEEE 123节点系统（12,300个产消者）上，该方法能将节点电压保持在规定限值内，求解时间0.829秒，最大误差低于0.01%。

Conclusion: 所提非迭代出清算法能有效处理AC网络约束下的双层能量共享市场实时出清问题，兼顾计算效率与电压安全性。

Abstract: Real-time hierarchical energy-sharing markets are promising to coordinate large numbers of prosumers. Still, most existing clearing methods rely on linearized or DC power-flow models and do not explicitly handle reactive power or voltage-security constraints. With AC network constraints, the problem becomes a large-scale bilevel Mathematical Program with Equilibrium Constraints (MPEC) that is difficult to solve in real time. This paper develops a non-iterative clearing algorithm for two-layer energy-sharing markets with voltage constraints. We first derive an efficient best-response function for each lower-layer energy-sharing market and reduce the equilibrium search to one dimension by exploiting the pricing-coupling structure. We then embed this function into the upper-layer network-constrained problem and reformulate the bilevel MPEC as a single-level mixed-integer second-order cone program (MISOCP), which is computationally tractable. Case studies on the IEEE 123-bus system with 12,300 prosumers show that the proposed method preserves nodal voltages within prescribed limits and delivers solutions with maximum errors below 0.01\% in 0.829 s.

</details>


### [183] [Simulation Strategies for an Efficient Local Search to solve Stochastic Scheduling Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23675)
*Philip de Bruin,Bram Elderhorst,Marjan van den Akker,Han Hoogeveen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在局部搜索算法中通过限制模拟次数来加速随机调度问题求解的技术，并提出了一种基于t检验的方法，在随机并行机调度和电动汽车调度两个问题上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调度问题假设确定性的任务持续时间，这与现实不符，导致调度方案对任务时长变化不鲁棒。使用随机任务时长更合理，但将其纳入局部搜索时，随机目标值的期望计算困难，而通过模拟估计又需要大量计算。

Method: 比较现有技术并提出基于t检验的方法，用于在局部搜索算法中限制模拟次数，在保持解质量的同时降低运行时间。

Result: 在随机并行机调度问题和随机电动汽车调度问题上，基于t检验的方法在两种问题中均最有效地减少了运行时间，同时保持了求解质量。

Conclusion: 使用限制模拟次数的技术可以有效降低随机调度问题的求解开销，而t检验方法在效果上优于现有已知技术。

Abstract: In scheduling problems, deterministic task durations are often assumed. This usually does not capture reality and may lead to schedules that are not robust to (small) changes to these task lengths. The use of stochastic task durations therefore seems preferable. Including these in local search, which is the way to find good solutions for difficult scheduling problems, is not straightforward, though. The objective value becomes stochastic then too, and computing the expected value is often not possible. One way out of this it to approximate this value by using simulation. This is quite easy to implement in a local search algorithm, but it may require many simulations each iteration to get a reliable estimate. Hence such an approach comes with a performance penalty.
  In this paper, we study techniques to limit the number of simulations. Besides comparing known techniques, we propose our own method for this, which is based on $t$-tests. We evaluate these techniques on the Stochastic Parallel Machine Scheduling Problem and the Stochastic Electric Vehicle Scheduling Problem. In these case studies, we show the effectiveness of using such methods to reduce runtime while retaining solution quality. Our method using $t$-tests turns out to be most effective in both problems.

</details>


### [184] [Concentration of measure-valued solutions for semilinear parabolic equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23678)
*Charlie Lebarbé,Émilien Flayac,Michel Fournié,Didier Henrion,Milan Korda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于标量半线性反应扩散型抛物偏微分方程，矩-平方和（moment-sum-of-squares）层级结构的松弛间隙不存在，即线性测度公式化问题的解对应于原始非线性PDE的经典物理解。证明通过构造能量测度值（emv）解并证明其集中在非线性PDE的唯一解上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将矩-平方和层级结构从最优控制问题扩展到非线性偏微分方程时，存在一个根本性挑战：基于occupation measures的线性测度公式化问题的解可能与原始PDE的经典物理解之间存在松弛间隙（relaxation gap），即线性松弛的解并不对应真实的物理解。本文旨在证明对于一类重要的反应扩散型半线性抛物PDE，这种松弛间隙并不存在。

Method: 1. 证明线性测度方程的每个解都能在Young测度空间中导出一个满足适当能量恒等式的能量测度值（emv）解；2. 证明当原始非线性PDE的解存在且唯一时，任何这样的emv解都集中（concentrate）在该非线性PDE的解上。

Result: 成功证明了对于标量半线性反应扩散型抛物PDE，矩-平方和层级结构的松弛间隙不存在，即线性测度松弛的解与非线性PDE的经典解之间没有间隙。这是首次针对反应扩散型PDE的测度值解得到此类集中性结果。

Conclusion: 该结果为使用矩-平方和层级结构求解非线性反应扩散型PDE提供了严格的理论基础，表明这类凸半定松弛方法能够可靠地逼近原始非线性问题的真实解，无松弛间隙的困扰。

Abstract: The moment-sum-of-squares hierarchy provides a powerful framework for solving non-convex optimal control problems by constructing a sequence of convex semidefinite relaxations. However, when extending these methods to nonlinear partial differential equations (PDEs), a fundamental challenge is the potential existence of a relaxation gap, where the solution to the linear measure formulation using occupation measures fails to correspond to a classical physical solution of the original PDE. In this paper, we prove the absence of a relaxation gap for scalar semilinear parabolic PDEs of the reaction-diffusion type. We do so by showing that each solution to the linear measure equation gives rise to an energy measure-valued (emv) solution in the space of Young measures satisfying suitable energy identities. We then prove that any such emv solution concentrates on the solution to the nonlinear PDE, provided the latter exists and is unique. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first concentration result of this kind for measure-valued solutions of reaction-diffusion PDEs.

</details>


### [185] [Distributionally Robust Complex Chance-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23757)
*Raneem Madani,Abdel Lisser,Zeno Toffano*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向复变量的机会约束优化框架，将复线性规划中的个体与联合概率约束转化为确定性凸二阶锥问题，并扩展至分布鲁棒（基于矩、支撑集和数据驱动）设定，在信号处理的MVDR波束成形问题中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机会约束优化主要针对实变量，缺乏对复变量情形（如信号处理中的复值信号）的系统处理方法，需要将概率约束在复域中进行合理转化以保证求解可行性与理论严谨性。

Method: 1) 假设随机参数服从复椭圆对称分布，分析密度设定下的3CP模型；2) 扩展到分布鲁棒设定（矩已知/有界、支撑集有界、数据驱动矩估计）；3) 将个体约束转化为确定性二阶锥问题；4) 利用Copula理论对联合概率约束推导上下界近似。

Result: 在MVDR波束成形问题上的实验表明，所提出框架的经验样本外违反率与预设的概率保证高度吻合。
